### [1] [Self-Orthogonal $τ$-Tilting Modules and Tilting Modules III: Finitistic Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00105)
*Xiaojin Zhang,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究Artin代数上的τ-倾斜模与经典倾斜模的关系，证明在特定有限维数条件下τ-倾斜模即为经典1-倾斜模，并建立自正交τ-倾斜猜想与有限维数猜想之间的蕴含关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: τ-倾斜模是倾斜理论的重要推广，自正交τ-倾斜猜想是表示论中的核心问题之一。作者希望揭示τ-倾斜模退化为经典倾斜模的条件，并探究该猜想与有限维数猜想之间的深刻联系。

Method: 设A为Artin代数，T为τ-倾斜右A-模，令C=A/Ann_A(T)。利用C^op的有限小有限生成维数条件，结合T自扩张消失的有限范围，通过同调代数与模论方法分析T的性质。

Result: 证明C^op的有限小有限维数加上T自扩张消失的有限范围，迫使T成为经典1-倾斜模；若有限维数猜想对所有Artin代数成立，则自正交τ-倾斜猜想对所有Artin代数成立；自正交τ-倾斜猜想的反例将给出有限维数猜想的反例；对radical立方为零的Artin代数，自正交τ-倾斜猜想成立。

Conclusion: τ-倾斜模在有限维数条件下退化为经典倾斜模，且自正交τ-倾斜猜想弱于有限维数猜想，后者成立可推出前者；该结果对radical立方为零的代数验证了猜想。

Abstract: Let $A$ be an Artin algebra and let $T$ be a $τ$-tilting right $A$-module. Put $C=A/{\rm Ann}_A(T)$. We prove that finite little finitistic dimension of $C^{\rm op}$, together with a finite range of self-extension vanishing for $T$, forces $T$ to be a classical $1$-tilting module. We also show that if the finitistic conjecture holds for all Artin algebras then the self-orthogonal $τ$-tilting conjecture holds for all Artin algebras. Moreover, a counterexample to the self-orthogonal $τ$-tilting conjecture will give a counterexample to the finitistic dimension conjecture. As an application, the self-orthogonal $τ$-tilting conjecture holds for Artin algebras with radical cube zero.

</details>


### [2] [A characterization of triangulated structures induced by premutation triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00135)
*Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明(MT4)兼容条件对于Iitsuka典范预三角结构成为三角结构是充要条件，并将其与Nakaoka的兼容条件等价化，最后构造出非变异三元组的可约三元组，否定回答了Iitsuka的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Iitsuka引入预变异三元组作为多种变异构造的统一框架，并证明商范畴Z/[I]具有典范预三角结构，但该结构何时成为三角结构尚缺乏精确刻画，本文旨在给出充要条件并厘清其与既有工作的关系。

Method: 在扩展三角范畴(S,Z,V)中设I=S∩Z=Z∩V，研究兼容条件(MT4)对Iitsuka典范预三角结构的三角性的作用；进一步对同心双余挠对，比较Nakaoka构造三角商时所用的兼容条件与(MT4)；并显式构造可约三元组。

Result: (MT4)不仅是充分的也是必要的，即它精确刻画了Iitsuka典范预三角结构成为三角结构的条件；对同心双余挠对，Nakaoka的兼容条件与(MT4)等价；构造出可约但非变异的三元组。

Conclusion: (MT4)是三角商结构的关键刻画条件，沟通了Iitsuka与Nakaoka的两类构造；可约非变异三元组的存在否定回答了Iitsuka提出的问题。

Abstract: Let $(\mathcal{S},\mathcal{Z},\mathcal{V})$ be a premutation triple in an extriangulated category. Set $\mathcal{I}=\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{Z}=\mathcal{Z}\cap\mathcal{V}$. Iitsuka introduced premutation triples as a unified framework for various mutation constructions and showed that the quotient category $\mathcal{Z}/[\mathcal{I}]$ admits a canonical pretriangulated structure. In this paper, we prove that the compatibility condition $(\mathrm{MT4})$ is not only sufficient but also necessary for this structure to be triangulated. Therefore, $(\mathrm{MT4})$ precisely characterizes when Iitsuka's canonical pretriangulated structure becomes triangulated. Furthermore, we prove that, for concentric twin cotorsion pairs, the compatibility condition used by Nakaoka in the construction of triangulated quotients is equivalent to $(\mathrm{MT4})$. Finally, we construct an explicit reducible triple which is not a mutation triple. This gives a negative answer to a question posed by Iitsuka.

</details>


### [3] [Explicit Transgression Primitives on Flag Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00300)
*Greg Weiler*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了K/T上相对超渡原语α_j，满足d_m α_j = −p_j(F_t)，并给出所有j的统一闭式；揭示了从α_2、α_3到j=4的结构转变，在A_2格点上给出几何与Koszul DGA解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Borel定理以不变代数同构刻画旗流形上同调，但仅非构造性地说明特征形式p_j(F_t)=tr(F_t^j)的消失；从Weil代数投影得到的K上闭原语无法下降到K/T。本文旨在显式构造可下降的相对原语，弥补这一构造性缺口。

Method: 构造相对超渡原语α_j∈(Λ^{2j−1}m^∨)^T≅^KΩ^{2j−1}(K/T)，使d_m α_j=−p_j(F_t)。引入连接θ_s=θ_t+sθ_m，曲率分解为F_s=(1−s)(F_t−sθ_m^2)；用非交换字和与Euler Beta积分展开，导出所有j≥1的普适闭式。并在A_2根格点上以T不变上链、闭环及Koszul DGA极小分解进行分析。

Result: 得到α_j的通用闭式公式。结构上：α_2=−(1/3)tr(θ_m^3)无曲率项；α_3关于F_t线性；j=4时[F_t,θ_m^2]≠0破坏循环迹对称性，分解为独立necklace，产生不可约不变量tr(θ_m∧F_t∧θ_m^2∧F_t)。在A_2（sl_3(C)）上，α_2=Ω_−−Ω_+为三角形有向差，d_m为边分裂算子，见证c_2(E)的恰当性；k=4时Specht分解遇重数障碍，而极小Koszul DGA分解K_•(p_2,…,p_k)消解余不变代数，恢复Poincaré多项式P_{K/T}(t)=∏_{j=2}^k(1−t^{2j})/(1−t^2)与Euler特征χ(K/T)=k!。

Conclusion: 相对超渡原语α_j显式实现了特征形式的消失，避免矩阵秩歧义；其结构随j的升高从曲率无关、线性演化到不可约necklace不变量；A_2情形下与Koszul DGA消解一致，恢复余不变代数的Poincaré多项式与Euler特征。

Abstract: Borel's theorem presents the flag manifold cohomology as the coinvariant algebra $H^*(K/T; \mathbb{C}) \cong S(\mathfrak{h}^\vee)/I_W$, establishing the vanishing of characteristic forms $p_j(F_\mathfrak{t}) = \mathrm{tr}(F_\mathfrak{t}^j)$ non-constructively; projection from the Weil algebra $W(\mathfrak{k})$ yields closed primitives on $K$ failing to descend.
  We construct relative transgression primitives $α_j \in (Λ^{2j-1}\mathfrak{m}^\vee)^T \cong {}^KΩ^{2j-1}(K/T)$ satisfying $d_\mathfrak{m} α_j = -p_j(F_\mathfrak{t})$ on $K/T$. Via connections $θ_s = θ_\mathfrak{t} + sθ_\mathfrak{m}$, the curvature factors as $F_s = (1-s)(F_\mathfrak{t} - sθ_\mathfrak{m}^2)$; expanding via non-commutative word sums and Euler's Beta integral yields a universal closed formula for all $j \ge 1$.
  These primitives exhibit a structural transition: $α_2 = -\frac{1}{3}\mathrm{tr}(θ_\mathfrak{m}^3)$ is curvature-free, $α_3$ is linear in $F_\mathfrak{t}$, while at $j = 4$, $[F_\mathfrak{t}, θ_\mathfrak{m}^2] \neq 0$ shatters cyclic trace symmetry into independent necklaces, producing the irreducible invariant $\mathrm{tr}(θ_\mathfrak{m} \wedge F_\mathfrak{t} \wedge θ_\mathfrak{m}^2 \wedge F_\mathfrak{t})$.
  On the $A_2$ root lattice ($\mathfrak{sl}_3(\mathbb{C})$), $T$-invariant cochains correspond to closed loops, $α_2 = Ω_- - Ω_+$ is the oriented difference of triangles, and $d_\mathfrak{m}$ acts as an edge-fracturing operator witnessing the exactness of $c_2(E)$. While character theory encounters a multiplicity barrier at $k=4$ in Specht decompositions, a minimal Koszul DGA resolution $K_\bullet(p_2, \dots, p_k)$ resolves the coinvariant algebra, recovering the Poincaré polynomial $P_{K/T}(t) = \prod_{j=2}^k \frac{1-t^{2j}}{1-t^2}$ and Euler characteristic $χ(K/T) = k!$ without matrix-rank ambiguities.

</details>


### [4] [Magnitude of module categories and special biserial algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00413)
*Haruhisa Enomoto*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文证明了 Børve、Horiatakis 与 Kalck 关于表示有限代数模范畴 magnitude 的猜想：在代数闭域上，magnitude 至少等于单模个数，且取等号当且仅当代数为特殊双列代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Børve 等人将表示有限代数模范畴的 magnitude 定义为不可分解模之间 Hom 维数矩阵之逆的元素和，并证明它是 Auslander–Reiten 箭图的欧拉特征，进而猜想 magnitude ≥ 单模个数，且等号仅对特殊双列代数成立。本文旨在证明该猜想。

Method: 对表示有向代数，建立模范畴、去掉给定单合成因子的子范畴与其理想商三者 magnitude 之间的关系；借助 Ringel–Vossieck 的 hammock 理论，将该商等价于某有限偏序集表示范畴并计算其 magnitude；一般情形通过"标准形"分级所得代数化归到表示有向情形。

Result: 在代数闭域上证明了对任意表示有限代数，模范畴的 magnitude 不小于其单模个数；且 magnitude 等于单模个数当且仅当代数为特殊双列代数。

Conclusion: 该猜想在代数闭域情形下完全成立，从而给出了 magnitude 达到下界 的代数类刻画。

Abstract: Børve, Horiatakis, and Kalck defined the magnitude of the module category of a representation-finite algebra as the sum of the entries of the inverse of the matrix of Hom dimensions between indecomposable modules. They showed it is an Euler characteristic, the alternating count of vertices, arrows, and meshes of the Auslander--Reiten quiver, and conjectured that it is at least the number of simple modules, with equality precisely for special biserial algebras. We prove this conjecture over an algebraically closed field. For a representation-directed algebra, we relate the magnitudes of the module category, the subcategory of modules without a given simple composition factor, and the ideal quotient by it. By the theory of hammocks of Ringel and Vossieck, this quotient is equivalent to a category of representations of a finite poset, whose magnitude we compute. The general case reduces to the representation-directed case through algebras obtained by grading a standard form.

</details>


### [5] [Big Finitistic Dimensions of Radical-Cube-Zero Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00433)
*Liang Chen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 构造了一个十维代数A（rad³A=0），满足findim A=2、Findim A=∞、Findim(A^op)=0，从而推翻了大finitistic维数猜想；并给出模的嵌入分类、维数向量刻画及若干有限性与wildness结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大finitistic维数猜想（Findim）是代表示论中长期未决的问题。本文旨在通过构造反例来检验并推翻该猜想，同时研究与此相关的模的投射维数结构、维数向量分布以及表示型问题。

Method: 在任意域上显式构造十维代数A，满足(rad A)³=0、findim A=2、Findim A=∞、Findim(A^op)=0；利用逆syzygy构造逐层识别投射覆盖的核，并用非零Ext判定分辨率长度；用可逆线性算子分类可嵌入投射模根中且pd≤1的模；对任意Artin代数给出通过根零化理想与合适corner控制有限投射分解的递归充分条件；对两顶点各带一个loop的radical-cube-zero初等代数证明大finitistic维数有限并给出界为1或2的乘法映射判据；最后用精确表示嵌入证明pd=2类的wildness及存在超可分解纯内射模。

Result: 得到Findim A=∞而Findim(A^op)=0的十维代数，推翻大finitistic维数猜想；构造出每个正有限投射维数的可数生成右模；分类了pd≤1且嵌入投射根中的模；确定pd=2有限维模的全部维数向量并分类达到尖锐维数界的模（含全部14维pd=2模）；对两顶点各带一个loop的radical-cube-zero代数证明Findim有限并得到界为1或2的判据；证明pd=2类wild且存在超可分解纯内射模。

Conclusion: 大finitistic维数猜想不成立；rad³=0的有限维代数即可同时表现出findim小、Findim无限及左右不对称的极端行为；pd≤2模的结构可由可逆线性算子与维数向量精细刻画，相关模类具有wild表示型并含超可分解纯内射模。

Abstract: We construct, over any field, a ten-dimensional algebra $A$ with $(\operatorname{rad}A)^3=0$ such that $\operatorname{findim}A=2$, $\operatorname{Findim}A=\infty$, and $\operatorname{Findim}(A^{\mathrm{op}})=0$. An explicit inverse-syzygy construction gives countably generated right modules of every positive finite projective dimension, disproving the big finitistic dimension conjecture. At each step we identify the full projective-cover kernel, and nonzero Ext detects the exact resolution length. For this algebra, we classify the modules of projective dimension at most one that embed in radicals of projective modules, with no generation restriction, in terms of invertible linear operators. We determine all dimension vectors of finite-dimensional modules of projective dimension two and classify those attaining the sharp dimension bounds, including all fourteen-dimensional modules of projective dimension two. For arbitrary Artin algebras, we give recursive sufficient criteria that control finite projective resolutions through radical-annihilated ideals and suitable corners. For finite-dimensional elementary radical-cube-zero algebras, we prove finiteness of the big finitistic dimension in the two-vertex case with one loop at each vertex and obtain multiplication-map criteria for bounds of one or two. Finally, an exact representation embedding yields wildness of the projective-dimension-two class and, over every field, a superdecomposable pure-injective module in that class.

</details>


### [6] [Endomorphism algebras of Gorenstein-projective modules over string algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00937)
*Yu-Zhe Liu,Zheng Xin*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了字符串代数的Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对，给出了其与极大好集合的对应及有限相容图，并在G条件下构造了End_A(T)的带标签瓦片曲面模型，证明了表示有限性的等价刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字符串代数的τ-倾斜理论与曲面模型是表示论的重要工具，但Gorenstein-投射情形下的支撑τ-倾斜对结构及其与曲面模型、表示有限性的联系尚待建立。

Method: 对字符串代数A，将其Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对与一类特殊的极大好集合对应起来，并构造有限相容图；在G条件下，对任意非零基本Gorenstein-投射模T，显式构造End_A(T)的带标签瓦片曲面模型。

Result: 建立了Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对与极大好集合的对应及有限相容图；在G条件下给出了End_A(T)的显式带标签瓦片曲面模型；并证明A表示有限当且仅当所有基本Gorenstein-投射模T对应的End_A(T)表示有限。

Conclusion: 该工作将字符串代数的Gorenstein-投射τ-倾斜理论、曲面模型与表示有限性统一起来，为相关代数的分类与模型构造提供了新途径。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a string algebra. We identify its Gorenstein-projective support $τ$-tilting pairs with a distinguished class of maximal good collections and give a finite compatibility graph for them. Under the G-condition, we construct an explicit labelled tiled-surface model for $\mathrm{End}_A(T)$ for every nonzero basic Gorenstein-projective module $T$. Under the same condition, we show that $A$ is representation-finite if and only if $\End_A(T)$ is representation-finite for every basic Gorenstein-projective module $T$.

</details>


### [7] [Homology and higher-order intermediate categories for proper abelian subcategories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01155)
*Zheng-Gang Wu,Xianhui Fu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 在不依赖t-结构的前提下，本文在具有ℓ+1层扩张闭真阿贝尔子范畴的有限扩张窗口上建立了自然长正合同调序列，条件为1至ℓ+1次负自扩张消失；证明了该界的统一锐性；并在Krull-Schmidt情形下用相容序列分类了对纯同调层取层封闭的中间子范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典长正合同调序列通常依赖于t-结构或阿贝尔范畴的特定设置。作者希望在更弱、更一般的框架（不需要t-结构）下构造同调序列，并厘清负自扩张消失条件与序列长度之间的定量关系，同时刻画相应的中间子范畴结构。

Method: 在有限扩张窗口上，取ℓ+1层扩张闭的、真（proper）阿贝尔子范畴，假设次数1到ℓ+1的负自扩张消失，构造自然长正合同调序列；通过例子或构造论证该界的锐性；在Krull-Schmidt范畴中，利用相容序列对稳定于纯同调层的中间子范畴进行分类。

Result: 建立了有限扩张窗口上的自然长正合同调序列而无需t-结构；证明了所给消失条件所界（ℓ+1）是统一锐的；在Krull-Schmidt情形下给出了中间子范畴按相容序列的完整分类。

Conclusion: 结果表明同调长正合序列可以在较弱的范畴假设下获得，负自扩张消失的层数界不可改进，且中间子范畴的结构可由相容序列精确刻画，为相关同调代数与子范畴分类问题提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Without needing a $t$-structure, we establish natural long exact homology sequences on finite extension windows with $\ell+1$ layers of extension-closed proper abelian subcategories, assuming vanishing of negative self-extensions in degrees $1,\ldots,\ell+1$, and prove uniform sharpness of this bound. In the Krull-Schmidt setting, we classify intermediate subcategories stable under taking pure homology layers by compatible sequences.

</details>


### [8] [Central algebraic cohomology of Galois gerbes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01159)
*Taeyeoup Kang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 研究局部域与数域上Galois gerbe的中心代数上同调，得到abel化上同调纤维积公式及满上同调集的笛卡尔方块刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Galois gerbe的中心代数上同调（系数取自连通约化群及不一定有限或连通的中心子群）在Langlands纲领相关构造中具有重要意义，但缺乏对其abel化与满上同调集结构的系统描述，本文旨在给出这类上同调的计算公式与结构定理。

Method: 在特征零局部域和数域上研究Galois gerbe的中心代数上同调；对存在Kaletha刚性gerbe比较态射的gerbe，用有限中心商代数的基本群与带（band）到中心子群的同态给出abel化上同调的纤维积公式；证明带状有限型时比较态射存在；在数域上绕过比较假设，通过笛卡尔方块由abel化与实局部化重构满上同调集；并将公式特殊化到Kottwitz局部与整体gerbe。

Result: 对存在比较态射的gerbe，得到abel化上同调的纤维积公式，其因子为有限中心商代数的代数基本群和band到中心子群的同态；带为有限型时此类比较存在。数域上无需比较假设，满上同调集由其abel化与实局部化经笛卡尔方块还原。非阿基米德局部域及全虚数域上abel化映射为双射。

Conclusion: 给出了Galois gerbe中心代数上同调的abel化计算公式与满上同调集的结构定理，澄清了比较态射的存在条件，并将结果应用于Kottwitz局部和整体gerbe。

Abstract: We study central algebraic cohomology of Galois gerbes over local fields of characteristic zero and number fields, with coefficients in a connected reductive group and an algebraic central subgroup, not necessarily finite or connected. For gerbes admitting a comparison morphism from Kaletha's rigid gerbe, we obtain fiber product formulas for abelianized cohomology in terms of algebraic fundamental groups of finite central quotients and homomorphisms from the band to the central subgroup. Such comparisons exist whenever the band is of finite type. Over number fields, independently of the comparison hypothesis, the full cohomology set is recovered from its abelianization and its real localizations through a cartesian square. Over non-Archimedean local fields and totally imaginary number fields, abelianization is bijective. We also specialize the formulas to Kottwitz's local and global gerbes.

</details>


### [9] [Harmonic Norms on Quiver Representations over Non-Archimedean Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01694)
*Oren Ben-Bassat*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 论文证明了任意完备非阿基米德域上有限箭图的每个多稳定表示都承认 HKKP 意义下的分裂调和范数，回答了 HKKP 预印本中的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答 Haiden–Katzarkov–Kontsevich–Pandit 在《Towards Categorical Kähler Geometry》中提出的问题：多稳定表示是否承认分裂调和范数。该问题位于非阿基米德几何与范畴 Kähler 几何交叉处，且此前结果多依赖球完备或值群限制。

Method: 在任意完备非阿基米德域上工作：用 Moret-Bailly 闭像定理获得与去稳定子对象表示的一致分离；对范数权重作有限估计，得到稳定表示的线性强制性界，从而在全体范数的完备 CAT(0) 空间上给出扩展 HKKP 势的最小值；通过一阶变分计算将分裂极小元等同于 HKKP 调和范数；用严格支撑铰链把分裂达到性归约为带任意实半径标签箭图的半稳定约化；通过提升分裂的微小改变使反向扩张非分裂，并用有限斜率–秩下降证明终止。

Result: 完备范数空间中的每个极小元都由一个分裂范数达到，且具有相同的箭头奇异轨迹，对值群和箭图均无限制；非分裂极小元在一、二半径修正中展示分裂达到性；双参数 Berkovich 解析族将所选分裂范数的多稳定性、半稳定性和调和性区分开来。

Conclusion: 在任意完备非阿基米德域（无需球完备）上，有限箭图的每个多稳定表示都承认 HKKP 分裂调和范数，正面回答了 HKKP 的问题，并取消了以往对值群与箭图的限制。

Abstract: Let $K$ be any complete non-Archimedean field, not necessarily spherically complete. We prove that every polystable representation of a finite quiver over $K$ admits a split harmonic norm in the sense of Haiden--Katzarkov--Kontsevich--Pandit (HKKP), answering a question from their preprint \emph{Towards Categorical Kähler Geometry}. Moret-Bailly's closed-image theorem gives a uniform separation from representations with destabilizing subobjects. A finite estimate on norm weights then yields a linear coercivity bound for stable representations and hence a minimum of the extended HKKP potential on the complete CAT(0) space of all norms. A direct first-variation computation identifies split minimizers with HKKP harmonic norms. Strict supporting hinges reduce split attainment to semistable reduction for quivers with arbitrary real radius labels. Small changes of the lifted splitting make each reversed extension nonsplit; a finite slope--rank descent proves termination. Consequently, every minimum in the completed norm space is attained by a split norm with the same arrow singular profiles, without restrictions on the value group or the quiver. Nonsplit minimizers illustrate split attainment in one and two radius modifications, and a two-parameter Berkovich analytic family separates polystability, semistability, and harmonicity of a chosen split norm.

</details>


### [10] [Rotation fixed points of undecorated braid varieties and fusion rings of affine Lie algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02034)
*Yuma Mizuno*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明：在 Coxeter 元素幂对应的未装饰辫子簇上，由词循环旋转诱导的有限阶自等价（Zamolodchikov 变换）的不动点栈的粗模空间，同构于相应仿射 Lie 代数融合环的谱；该结论可推广到扭曲 Coxeter 元素，并能以此获得所有仿射 Lie 代数的融合环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立辫子簇（及其上的 Zamolodchikov 自等价）与仿射 Lie 代数融合环之间的几何—代数对应关系，并寻求用统一构造实现所有仿射 Lie 代数融合环。

Method: 设 G 为单连通半单代数群；取 Coxeter 元素（及其推广：扭曲 Coxeter 元素）的幂所对应的未装饰辫子簇，利用词循环旋转诱导的有限阶自等价——Zamolodchikov 变换，研究其不动点栈。

Result: 证明该自等价的不动点栈的粗模空间同构于相应仿射 Lie 代数融合环的谱；结论对扭曲 Coxeter 元素亦成立，并由此得到所有仿射 Lie 代数的融合环。

Conclusion: 辫子簇上的 Zamolodchikov 变换为仿射 Lie 代数融合环提供了一种几何实现，统一覆盖（扭曲）Coxeter 元素情形乃至所有仿射 Lie 代数。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a simply connected semisimple algebraic group. On the (undecorated) braid variety associated with a power of a Coxeter element, there is a finite-order autoequivalence, called the Zamolodchikov transformation, induced by cyclic rotation of words. In this paper, we show that the coarse moduli space of the fixed-point stack of this autoequivalence is isomorphic to the spectrum of the fusion ring of the corresponding affine Lie algebra. The above results also extend to twisted Coxeter elements, and the fusion rings of all affine Lie algebras are obtained in this way.

</details>

### [11] [Bounded-Fidelity Sim-as-Demo-Stage: Mocap Handoff for Governance Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00008)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 bounded-fidelity sim-as-demo-stage 设计模式:在显式括定的交接包络内抑制接触物理,其他地方保持完整动力学,从而为 LLM 驱动机器人的治理基准提供审计链可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sim-to-real 研究通常以物理保真度为核心目标,但用于 LLM 驱动机器人治理基准时,高保真接触动力学反而成为负担:物体交接(抓取、搬运、放置)处的接触力积分噪声会注入与所测治理属性无关的审计链分歧。作者希望在不牺牲其他动力学的前提下消除这种结构性分歧,同时明确该模式的适用边界,避免误用。

Method: 采用有界保真度的 sim-as-demo-stage 设计模式:在明确括定的交接包络内抑制接触物理,包络外保留完整动力学。实现基于 MuJoCo 的 mocap-body 原语,配合一个 220 行 Python 适配器,由治理桥通过结构化意图调用。将审计链稳定性形式化为重放间哈希事件日志的字节相等,并给出两个可推出该性质的包络结构属性。实验包括每姿势 N=1000 次重放、1/2/5/10 ms 时间步扫描、包络边缘 ±10 仿真步时序抖动(1400 次重放),以及 K∈{1,2,3} 个顺序交接物体的审计链测试(1500 次重放)。

Result: mocap 变体每次姿势仅产生一个不同审计链哈希(1000/1000 字节相同;Wilson 95% CI [0.997, 1.000]);接触力基线产生 584 个不同哈希(993/1000 分歧;CI [0.987, 0.998])。时间步扫描显示分歧是结构性的而非调参产物,各时间步下均为 0.985。包络边缘 ±10 步时序抖动下分歧为 0;K∈{1,2,3} 顺序交接时审计链仍字节相等,且每次取放开销为亚线性。

Conclusion: 该模式以近乎零工程成本为基准设计者带来审计链可复现性,并明确了其有害场景(sim-to-real 验证、策略训练、接触丰富任务),防止误部署。

Abstract: Sim-to-real research pursues physics fidelity as a primary objective: simulators are judged by how closely they reproduce real-world contact dynamics. For governance benchmarking of LLM-driven robots, where the simulator demonstrates that an admission/policy/contract/audit pipeline behaves correctly, contact fidelity at object handoffs (grasp, carry, place) becomes a liability: contact-force integration noise injects audit-chain divergence that is structurally unrelated to the governance property under test. We propose bounded-fidelity sim-as-demo-stage, a design pattern that suppresses contact physics within explicitly bracketed handoff envelopes while preserving full dynamics elsewhere. The construction uses MuJoCo's mocap-body primitive driven by a 220-line Python adapter that the governance bridge invokes via structured intents. We formalise audit-chain stability as byte-equality of the hashed event log across replays and identify two structural envelope properties that imply it. Across N=1000 replays per posture, the mocap variant produces one distinct audit-chain hash (1000/1000 byte-identical; Wilson 95% CI [0.997, 1.000]); the contact-force baseline produces 584 distinct hashes (993/1000 diverged; CI [0.987, 0.998]). A timestep sweep (1, 2, 5, 10 ms) shows the divergence is structural, not a tuning artefact: it stays at 0.985 at every timestep. Envelope-edge timing jitter (+/-10 simulation steps, 1,400 replays) produces 0 divergence, and audit chains remain byte-equal across K in {1, 2, 3} sequentially handed-off objects (1,500 replays) with sub-linear per-pick-and-place overhead. The pattern gives benchmark designers audit-chain reproducibility at near-zero engineering cost; we also map where it is harmful (sim-to-real validation, policy training, contact-rich tasks) so it is not mis-deployed.

</details>


### [12] [Multi-Reference Path Tracking Control for an Agricultural Tractor with Nonlinear Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00057)
*Marcel Moll,Timo Oksanen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）的拖拉机路径跟踪控制器，将分段线性参考路径的多段直接纳入目标函数，并给出从完整路径中选取可行参考段的方法；在配备拖拉机机具管理（TIM）转向接口的拖拉机上开展田间试验，求解器平均3.45 ms收敛，弯曲路径下的平均绝对横向偏差为6.1 cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沿预定参考路径引导拖拉机是精准农业的关键环节，需要高精度、可实时求解的路径跟踪控制方法。

Method: 构建基于NMPC的路径跟踪控制器，将分段线性参考路径的多段直接写入目标函数，并提出从完整路径中筛选可行参考段的选择方法；通过TIM转向接口在实际拖拉机上实施控制并进行田间试验。

Result: NMPC求解器平均在3.45 ms内收敛；对弯曲参考路径的跟踪平均绝对横向偏差为6.1 cm。

Conclusion: 该NMPC路径跟踪方案兼顾实时性与跟踪精度，在基于TIM转向接口的拖拉机田间作业中验证有效，适用于精准农业的路径跟踪需求。

Abstract: Guiding a tractor along a predefined reference path is a key component of precision agriculture. This study develops a path tracking controller based on Nonlinear Model Predictive Control, which incorporates multiple segments of a piecewise-linear reference path directly into the objective function. In addition, methods for selecting viable reference segments from the full path are presented. The control system is evaluated during a field test with a tractor controlled via the Tractor Implement Management steering interface. The NMPC solver converged on average after 3.45 ms and tracked the curved reference path with a mean absolute cross-track error of 6.1 cm.

</details>


### [13] [Probabilistic Plan Legibility with Off-the-shelf Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00065)
*Michele Persiani,Thomas Hellström*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向任意PDDL域的可读性规划方法，通过扩展经典规划的可读性研究避免构建专用规划器，并利用二阶心智理论估计观察者视角，在多个PDDL基准域验证可读性与效率之间存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可读性规划旨在从观察者视角使计划的目标最易与其他候选目标区分开；现有可读性研究多局限于特定场景或需定制规划器，难以推广到经典规划与任意PDDL域。

Method: 在经典规划框架下扩展既有可读性研究，面向任意PDDL域生成可读计划，无需构建专用（ad-hoc）规划器；通过连接规划者与观察者任务空间的二阶心智理论来估计观察者视角。

Result: 在若干PDDL规划域上进行基准测试；结果显示计划可读性与计划效率总体呈权衡关系，但不同规划域提升可读性的方式并不一致，且需要引入正则化因子来平衡可读性与效率。

Conclusion: 该方法可应用于人-机器人协作场景，使自主机器人通过生成可读计划隐式传达目标；可读性提升具有域依赖性，需借助正则化因子在可读性与效率间取得平衡。

Abstract: Legible planning is the creation of plans that best disambiguate their goals from a set of other candidates from an observer's perspective. In this paper we propose a method for legible planning for arbitrary PDDL domains, by extending previous research on legibility to classical planning without requiring to construct ad-hoc planners. We also discuss how the observer perspective may be estimated through a second order theory of mind that connects the planner's and the observer's task spaces. Our solution can for example be deployed in human-robot teaming scenarios, where an autonomous robot in a team can implicitly communicate its goal by producing legible plans. We present benchmark results on several PDDL planning domains. Our results generally show that plan legibility is a trade-off with plan efficiency, however, not all planning domains allows to increase legibility in the same way and a regularizing factor to balance legibility and efficiency was proved necessary.

</details>


### [14] [HumanoidTTT: Test-Time Capability Reuse for Efficient Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00198)
*Jingtai Yang,Yining Wu,Yanjun Li,Zeyu Zhang,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HumanoidTTT框架，实现持续人形控制中已验证运动能力的可靠复用与自适应保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人持续部署中运动能力会被反复请求，但中间运动改变进入状态，使旧运动盲目重放不安全；同时已验证能力不断累积，而存储有限需决定保留哪些。

Method: 提出Selective Full-Motion Reuse：仅从经认证的适用入口状态授权直接复用完整已验证运动，使被接受的复用绕过重新生成；并提出Test-Time Capability Consolidation：以后续部署复用为反馈，在有限Full-Motion Store中自适应决定哪些合格能力留存。

Result: 实现零不安全接受，端到端相较重新生成加速16.4倍；在线整合相较冻结版本每200次请求多避免13.2次生成器调用。

Conclusion: HumanoidTTT可在持续部署中可靠、高效复用已验证运动能力，并自适应保留有用能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in motion generation and whole-body tracking have enabled humanoid robots to execute increasingly diverse motions, yet the same motion capabilities may be requested repeatedly during continual deployment. Reliable reuse is challenging because intervening motions can change the robot's entry state, making previously successful motions unsafe to replay blindly. Meanwhile, validated capabilities accumulate during deployment, while bounded storage requires deciding which ones are worth retaining. To address these challenges, we present HumanoidTTT, a framework for test-time capability reuse in continual humanoid control. Specifically, we introduce Selective Full-Motion Reuse, which authorizes direct reuse of validated complete motions only from certified applicable entry states, allowing accepted reuse to bypass fresh generation. We further introduce Test-Time Capability Consolidation, which adapts which qualified capabilities persist in a bounded Full-Motion Store using subsequent deployment reuse as feedback. Experiments demonstrate zero unsafe accepts and a 16.4$\times$ end-to-end speedup over fresh generation, while online consolidation improves avoided generator calls by 13.2 per 200 requests over its frozen counterpart. Overall, HumanoidTTT enables reliable and efficient reuse of validated motion capabilities while adaptively retaining useful capabilities throughout continual deployment. Code: https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/HumanoidTTT. Website: https://aigeeksgroup.github.io/HumanoidTTT.

</details>


### [15] [Real-Time Whole-Body Safe Motion Generation for Multi-Segment Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00220)
*Fangju Yang,Siyi Ma,Tonghao Guan,Tingcong Liu,Hang Yang,Zhengqiang Zhang,Jian S. Dai,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向平面多段腱驱动连续体机器人的统一驱动空间框架，结合能量法变曲率建模与多点CBF-QP，实现实时全身无碰撞运动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动连续体机器人的实时运动生成需要同时处理非均匀弯曲建模与全身避碰问题，现有方法难以兼顾建模精度、约束完备性与控制实时性。

Method: 构建能量法变曲率模型，刻画空间变化的腱间距与弯曲刚度，并给出解析雅可比用于微分逆运动学与安全监控；设计多点CBF-QP，在障碍运动与驱动速度边界下保证骨架间距，其决策维度仅取决于独立驱动段数。

Result: 模型与GVS参考吻合，最大曲率误差为5.223×10⁻² m⁻¹；100次MuJoCo试验中，静态与动态场景成功率分别为96%与100%，无CBF约束时约为60%与80%；穿洞测试验证受限环境安全性；600个骨架监测点下平均控制步耗时6.66 ms。

Conclusion: 该框架在保证建模精度的同时实现全身安全实时运动生成，具备在受限动态环境中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Real-time motion generation for tendon-driven continuum robots requires accurate modeling of nonuniform bending and whole-body collision avoidance. This paper presents a unified actuation-space framework for planar multi-segment tendon-driven continuum robots. An energy-based variable-curvature model captures spatially varying tendon spacing and bending stiffness and provides analytical Jacobians for differential inverse kinematics and safety monitoring. A multipoint CBF-QP enforces backbone clearance under obstacle motion and actuation-velocity bounds, while its decision dimension depends only on the number of independently actuated segments. The model closely agrees with GVS references, with a maximum curvature error of $5.223 \times 10^{-2}\,\mathrm{m}^{-1}$. Over 100 MuJoCo trials, the proposed method achieves collision-free success rates of 96% and 100% in static and dynamic scenarios, respectively, compared with approximately 60% and 80% without CBF constraints. Hole-traversal tests further demonstrate safe motion in constrained environments. With 600 backbone monitoring points, the mean control-step time is 6.66 ms, demonstrating real-time whole-body safe motion generation.

</details>


### [16] [DriftOPD: Sequence-Level Reverse-KL Distillation for One-Step VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00317)
*Youngjun Jun,Kyumin Choi,Youngmin Kim,Seonghyun Jin,Sunwoo Park,Jangho Park,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriftOPD 提出一种无教师、无 rollout 的序列级同策略蒸馏框架，将序列级反向 KL 分解为 chunk 级反向 KL 项与未来势能项，仅用离线数据和一步动作生成即可对连续 VLA 动作专家实现长程优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型的动作专家在滚动时域控制下生成短动作 chunk，chunk 级训练虽便于跨具身迁移，但只优化局部动作似然，未显式考虑长程任务成功率。序列级强化学习可弥补该缺陷，却通常依赖策略 rollout 与闭环交互，对真实机器人操作代价高昂。

Method: 作者证明序列级反向 KL 散度可分解为 chunk 级反向 KL 项与刻画当前动作长程影响的未来势能项。DriftOPD 分别用一步 drifting 目标优化前者、用从离线示范中学习的 Q 函数 critic 优化后者，从而仅凭离线数据与一步动作生成实现序列级优化，且无需教师策略与在线交互。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界操作任务中，跨多种 VLA 架构，DriftOPD 总体优于现有一步蒸馏基线，任务成功率与多步教师策略相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无需在线交互或独立教师，即可将长程行为有效蒸馏进一步 VLA 动作专家。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models increasingly rely on action experts that generate short action chunks under receding-horizon control. While chunk-level training is convenient across robot embodiments, it optimizes local action likelihood without explicitly accounting for long-horizon task success. Sequence-level reinforcement learning can address this limitation, but typically requires policy rollouts and closed-loop interaction, which are costly for real-robot manipulation. We introduce DriftOPD, a teacher-free, rollout-free framework for sequence-level on-policy distillation of continuous VLA action experts. We show that the sequence-level reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence decomposes into a chunk-level reverse-KL term and a future-potential term that captures the long-horizon effect of the current action. DriftOPD optimizes these two terms using a one-step drifting objective and a Q-function critic learned from offline demonstrations, respectively, enabling sequence-level optimization with only offline data and one-step action generation. Across multiple VLA architectures in simulation and real-world manipulation, DriftOPD generally outperforms existing one-step distillation baselines while achieving task success performance comparable to multi-step teacher policies. These results demonstrate that long-horizon behavior can be effectively distilled into one-step VLA action experts without online interaction or a separate teacher.

</details>


### [17] [Retrospective Open-Vocabulary Memory for Long-Term Object Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00330)
*Jiaming Wang,Zhiwei Xue,Chen Jizhuo,Peng Shiqi,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将长期目标搜索中的记忆问题形式化为对删失观测的概率推断，提出ECROM方法，依据机器人观测机会对每次检测/未检测证据加权，估计开放词汇概念的长期出现概率，并转化为主动搜索先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期目标搜索需要从对变化环境的重复但不均匀观测中学习物体的常见出现位置；现有记忆方法未考虑观测机会的不均匀性。

Method: 将retrospective open-vocabulary memory建模为删失观测下的概率推断，核心原则是按机器人观测对应位置的机会大小来加权证据；提出ECROM，用于估计仅在查询时指定的概念的长期流行度，并将所得信念直接转换为主动搜索先验。

Result: 在十个HM3D家庭中构建受控长期基准，独立变化物体摆放和观测机会；ECROM在保留查询上的support-level AP提升4.5点，search SPL提升4.2点。

Conclusion: ECROM通过按观测机会加权证据的概率推断，有效提升了开放词汇长期目标搜索的记忆与主动搜索性能，基准、数据集与代码将开源。

Abstract: Long-term object search requires learning where objects usually appear from repeated but uneven observations of a changing environment. We formulate retrospective open-vocabulary memory as probabilistic inference from censored observations, where the key idea is to reason with evidence per opportunity: a detection or non-detection should influence belief only in proportion to the robot's opportunity to observe the corresponding location. We introduce ECROM, which uses this principle to estimate long-term prevalence for concepts specified only at query time and converts the resulting belief directly into an active-search prior. To evaluate this problem, we introduce a controlled long-term benchmark in ten HM3D homes that independently varies object placement and observation opportunity across repeated traversals. ECROM improves support-level AP on held-out queries by 4.5 points and search SPL by 4.2 points over the strongest competing memory in each metric. The benchmark, dataset, and code will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [18] [IndoorBEV: A Lightweight Real-Time LiDAR BEV Perception System for Indoor Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00355)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IndoorBEV是一种轻量级室内LiDAR感知框架，通过高度感知BEV表示与几何条件特征融合，在紧凑BEV表示中显式编码垂直几何，实现精度、延迟与内存的有利权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内LiDAR感知需在严格延迟与内存约束下理解杂乱三维环境。现有点云/体素方法计算开销大，而传统BEV表示虽提升效率却丢弃垂直几何信息，存在效率与信息量之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出高度感知BEV表示，用统计高度特征与多频高度编码汇总每个BEV单元的垂直点分布，使三维线索可由二维卷积高效处理；轻量编码器融合互补几何特征、多尺度局部表示与紧凑全局场景上下文；解耦密集预测头联合输出语义BEV图与有向目标框。

Result: 模型仅0.6M参数、2.3 MB存储；NVIDIA AGX Orin上单次推理GPU内存21.52 MB，200 ms感知截止期下平均延迟169.6 ms，截止期错失率1.8%。在模拟场景、真实机器人扫描及开源室内点云数据集上验证了精度、延迟与内存的有利权衡。

Conclusion: 在紧凑BEV表示中显式编码垂直几何，是资源高效室内LiDAR感知的有效途径。

Abstract: Efficient indoor LiDAR perception is challenging because mobile robots must understand cluttered three-dimensional environments under strict latency and memory constraints. Existing point-based and voxel-based methods often incur substantial computational overhead, whereas conventional bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations improve efficiency at the cost of discarding vertical geometric information. We present IndoorBEV, a lightweight LiDAR perception framework that mitigates this tradeoff through a height-aware BEV representation and geometry-conditioned feature fusion. IndoorBEV summarizes the vertical point distribution in each BEV cell using statistical height features and multi-frequency height encoding, allowing informative three-dimensional cues to be processed efficiently by two-dimensional convolutions. A lightweight encoder then integrates complementary geometric features with multi-scale local representations and compact global scene context. Decoupled dense prediction heads jointly produce semantic BEV maps and oriented object bounding boxes. IndoorBEV contains only 0.6M parameters and requires 2.3 MB of model storage. On an NVIDIA AGX Orin, it uses 21.52 MB of GPU memory per inference and achieves a mean latency of 169.6 ms under a 200 ms perception deadline, with a deadline miss ratio of 1.8\%. Evaluations on simulated scenes, real-world robot scans, and an open-source indoor point-cloud dataset demonstrate a favorable tradeoff among perception accuracy, latency, and memory consumption. These results indicate that explicitly encoding vertical geometry within a compact BEV representation provides an effective approach to resource-efficient indoor LiDAR perception.

</details>


### [19] [DexPolicy: Scheduled Exploration for Trajectory-Guided Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00360)
*Haoyu Wang,Siyuan Qian,Yanjun Li,Zeyu Zhang,Yandong Guo,Boxin Shi,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将动作噪声从训练步数的显式函数出发进行退火（DexPolicy），在轨迹引导的灵巧操作RL中统一控制探索尺度，从而显著提升各类策略优化设置下的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作RL需先发现手指-物体接触、再精细控制物体，而服务前者的动作噪声会干扰后者。在ViViDex等轨迹引导设置中，基线PPO训练5M步后仍停留在初始动作噪声附近，说明缺乏对探索尺度的显式控制。

Method: DexPolicy将探索尺度显式表示为训练步数的函数，由宽到窄退火，同时固定损失、架构、奖励与优化器设置。研究三种策略优化设置：PPO、无critic的GRPO续训、流参数化PPO变体（FPO）。在5个YCB物体、3个随机种子上评估；并在RealMan RM75机械臂配Inspire/RH56手上对3个物体做360次试验（每物体-方法条件一个训练模型）。另做PPO组件消融，比较噪声控制与优化器收缩，并与相同端点的线性衰减调度对比。

Result: 仿真中确定性Target成功率均值由49.4%升至68.1%（FPO）、14.1%升至45.4%（GRPO）、32.0%升至35.7%（PPO）。真机上均值由25.0%升至85.0%（FPO）、10.0%升至63.3%（GRPO）、8.3%升至43.3%（PPO）。PPO组件筛选表明噪声控制优于所测优化器收缩；所选调度在三个测试物体上平均Target成功率高于相同端点的线性衰减。

Conclusion: 训练回报、确定性Target成功率与执行噪声容忍度三者相互解耦；调度应以预期执行条件下的最终任务成功率来评判，且需按任务与策略优化设置分别评估。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for dexterous manipulation must discover finger-object contacts and then control the object precisely; the action noise that serves the first goal can interfere with the second. In trajectory-guided settings such as ViViDex, where RL refine hand-object trajectories from human video, our baseline PPO runs end near their initial action noise after 5M steps, motivating explicit control of exploration scale. DexPolicy makes that scale an explicit function of training steps, annealing from broad to narrow exploration while holding loss, architecture, reward, and optimizer settings fixed. We study three policy-optimization settings: PPO, critic-free GRPO continuation, and a flow-parameterized PPO variant (FPO). Across five YCB objects and three training seeds, mean deterministic Target success rises from 49.4% to 68.1% (FPO), 14.1% to 45.4% (GRPO), and 32.0% to 35.7% (PPO). On a RealMan RM75 arm with an Inspire/RH56 hand, 360 trials over three objects raise mean Target success from 25.0% to 85.0% (FPO), 10.0% to 63.3% (GRPO), and 8.3% to 43.3% (PPO), with one trained model per object-method condition. PPO component screening favors noise control over the tested optimizer contraction; the selected PPO schedule yields higher mean Target success than linear decay with the same endpoints on three tested objects. Training return, deterministic Target success, and tolerance to execution noise dissociate; schedules should therefore be judged by terminal task success under the intended execution conditions, per task and policy-optimization setting. Code: https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/DexPolicy. Website: https://aigeeksgroup.github.io/DexPolicy.

</details>


### [20] [DeepJEPA: Scaling World Models from Within](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00368)
*Zijian Jin,Yunbei Zhang,Yuanzhe Liu,Ming Liu,Baian Chen,Weirui Ye,Shilong Liu,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeepJEPA将世界模型中每个转移的计算深度视为测试时的内部扩展轴，通过权重绑定与自适应递归更新，在决策关键转移处投入更多计算，从而以更少算力提升或匹配最强固定深度规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型规划器通常通过滚动更远、采样更多轨迹或优化更久来向外扩展，却对每个想象转移分配相同计算量。而真正有价值的精化集中在少数决策关键事件上，统一加深每个转移既浪费计算，也可能因过度处理而降低规划质量。

Method: 提出DeepJEPA，一种权重绑定的联合嵌入预测世界模型，将转移深度作为测试时内部扩展轴。模型学习在每个候选与每个展开步骤上判断是否需要再进行一次递归更新，从而使额外计算自适应地集中到关键时刻。

Result: 在五个视觉控制设定中，DeepJEPA优于或匹配最强固定深度规划器，同时每个转移平均仅需1.00–1.26次更新。额外计算集中于接触发生和持续物体交互阶段，此时潜在修正可改变进入精英集的候选与最终动作。表征探针显示，规划改善并不依赖于物体状态可解码性的全面提升。

Conclusion: DeepJEPA将世界模型扩展重新定义为内部计算的分配问题：应在能改变规划器决策的转移处思考更深，而非让所有展开统一更深或更长。

Abstract: World-model planners typically scale outward by rolling farther, sampling more trajectories, or optimizing longer, while assigning the same computation to every imagined transition. We show that making every transition uniformly deeper wastes computation and can degrade planning because useful refinement is concentrated at a small set of decision-critical events. We introduce DeepJEPA, a weight-tied joint-embedding predictive world model that treats transition depth as an inner test-time scaling axis and learns when another recurrent update is worth computing for each candidate and rollout step. Across five visual-control settings, DeepJEPA improves or matches the strongest fixed-depth planner while averaging only 1.00-1.26 updates per transition. Its additional computation concentrates at contact onset and sustained object interaction, where latent corrections can change which candidates enter the planner's elite set and which action is selected. Representation probes further show that improved planning does not require uniformly better object-state decodability. DeepJEPA therefore reframes world-model scaling as a problem of allocating internal computation where it can change the planner's decision: think deeper at decision-critical transitions instead of making every rollout uniformly deeper or longer.

</details>


### [21] [Whole-Body Aerial Grasping and Lifting via Partial Visual Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00404)
*Jiaye Jin,Rui Jin,Xinhang Xu,Haotian Jin,Ruiyang Liu,Yi Wang,Jiayan Zhao,Kun Cao,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出循环教师-学生框架，在仿真中学习单一策略，联合控制飞行、机械臂运动与夹爪闭合，无需显式任务阶段输入；教师通过关键状态课程强化学习，蒸馏至基于双视角点云与本体感知的循环视觉学生。8,996个仿真回合中，标称、物理/控制随机化、附加相机随机化条件下全任务成功率分别为99.97%、97.14%、95.84%，标称条件下采集对齐误差第90百分位均值为8.12 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中抓取-提升任务需在部分目标观测下完成接近、采集与提升的全身体协调。训练早期接近阶段的失败会限制智能体接触后续任务阶段；执行阶段可见性变化又使对齐与闭合时序难以把握。因此需要一种无需任务阶段输入、能统一控制飞行/机械臂/夹爪并具备闭环视觉反馈的学习方法。

Method: 采用循环教师-学生框架。教师为特权策略，通过强化学习训练，并使用关键状态课程：先暴露采集与提升状态，再将其接入正常接近轨迹。随后将教师行为蒸馏为循环视觉学生，用双视角点云与本体感知替代特权目标状态，并整合观测历史实现闭环控制。另设专门的闭合目标函数，依据模型定义的持续就绪序列监督闭合时序。训练与主评估使用带条件触发锁闩、虚拟附着与基于力旋量载荷加载的仿真采集-载荷模型，任务为短距离提升。

Result: 在该模型下完成8,996个仿真回合，冻结后的学生策略全任务成功率在标称、物理/控制随机化、附加相机随机化条件下分别为99.97%、97.14%、95.84%。标称条件下，各随机种子采集对齐误差第90百分位的锁闩计数加权均值为8.12 mm。

Conclusion: 该循环教师-学生框架可在无显式任务阶段输入条件下，以单策略统一控制飞行、机械臂与夹爪，并通过关键状态课程与闭合时序监督有效缓解早期失败与可见性变化问题；蒸馏后的视觉学生在多类随机化条件下均保持高成功率与毫米级对齐精度，验证了方法的鲁棒性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Aerial grasp-and-lift tasks require whole-body coordination across approach, acquisition, and lifting under partial target observations. Early approach failures can limit exposure to later task stages during training, while changing visibility complicates alignment and closure timing during execution. We present a recurrent teacher-student framework that learns a single policy in simulation to jointly command flight, arm motion, and gripper closure without an explicit task-phase input. A privileged teacher learns through reinforcement learning with a critical-state curriculum that exposes acquisition and lifting states before connecting them to normal approach trajectories. Its behavior is distilled into a recurrent visual student that replaces privileged target states with dual-view point clouds and proprioception, integrating observation history for closed-loop control. A dedicated closure objective supervises closure timing from sustained model-defined readiness sequences. Training and primary evaluation use a simulated acquisition-and-payload model with condition-triggered latching, virtual attachment, and wrench-based payload loading for short-distance lifting. Across 8,996 completed simulation episodes under this model, the frozen student achieves full-task success rates of 99.97%, 97.14%, and 95.84% under nominal, physics/control-randomized, and additional camera-randomized conditions, respectively. The nominal latch-count-weighted mean of per-seed 90th-percentile alignment errors at acquisition is 8.12 mm.

</details>


### [22] [Towards a General Humanoid Loco-Manipulation Model via Egocentric Whole-Body Human Data Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00438)
*Chongyang Xu,Zhao Wu,Jin Chen,Yiming Jiang,Jinhui Ye,Yuming Jiang,Shifeng Zhang,Ziliang Feng,Mu Xu,Yilun Chen,Li Lu,Steven C. H. Hoi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出500小时人形全身移动操作数据集HumanVerse-500，并基于其训练三阶段视觉-语言-动作策略λ0，在全球开放环境中利用人类视频经验实现可扩展的人形机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形全身操作发展迅速，但现有第一视角人类视频监督对全身运动与手-物交互协调的覆盖有限，而通过人形遥操作获取此类监督成本高且难以扩展。因此需要探索如何利用人类经验支持人形移动操作的可扩展学习。

Method: 构建轻量级可穿戴系统，同步采集第一视角视频与身体、手部运动，形成500小时开放世界人类移动操作数据集HumanVerse-500。基于该数据集开发全身人形视觉-语言-动作策略λ0，采用三阶段训练：先从多样第一视角数据集中学习交互，再用HumanVerse-500协调身体与手部运动，最后适配下游任务与机器人本体。策略学习共享表征空间以迁移人类经验，并通过领域特定接口处理人机状态与动作差异。

Result: 在SIMPLE及4个真实世界移动操作任务上评估λ0，取得当前最优性能；进一步分析其扩展行为、泛化能力及各训练阶段贡献，以理解人类数据如何支持下游全身人形控制。

Conclusion: 研究证明人类经验可有效支持人形移动操作的可扩展学习，λ0在三阶段训练框架下实现全身协调与最优性能，并将开源代码、模型与数据以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body manipulation has advanced rapidly, enabling policies to coordinate locomotion, posture, bimanual interaction, and dexterous hand movements. Meanwhile, egocentric human videos provide diverse examples of everyday interactions across objects and scenes, offering scalable supervision without robot operation. However, existing supervision from these videos provides limited coverage of whole-body movement and coordination with hand-object interaction, while obtaining such supervision through humanoid teleoperation is also costly and difficult to scale. We therefore explore how human experience can support scalable learning of humanoid loco-manipulation. To support this study, we introduce HumanVerse-500, a 500-hour dataset of diverse human loco-manipulation behaviors in open-world environments, collected with a lightweight wearable system that synchronizes egocentric video with body and hand motion. Building on this dataset, we develop $λ_0$, a whole-body humanoid vision-language-action policy, through three-stage training that first learns interaction from diverse egocentric datasets, then coordinates body and hand motion using HumanVerse-500, and finally adapts the policy to downstream tasks and robot embodiments. Across these stages, $λ_0$ learns a shared representation space for human experience transfer, while domain-specific interfaces handle differences between human and robot states and actions. We evaluate $λ_0$ on SIMPLE and 4 real-world loco-manipulation tasks, achieving state-of-the-art performance, and further analyze its scaling behavior, generalization, and training-stage contributions to understand how human data support downstream whole-body humanoid control. We will release our code, models, and data to support further research.

</details>


### [23] [ScaffoldM3C: A Multimodal Sequential Monte Carlo Framework for Generative Stable Construction Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00487)
*Gadiel Sznaier Camps,Chengyang He,Guillaume Sartoretti,Eduardo Montijano,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ScaffoldM3C，将积木式 3D 构建建模为概率式“下一块”生成任务，引入多模态条件与脚手架辅助 token，并结合序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）维护多组装配序列；模型比基线小 4 倍、推理快 5–20 倍，构建质量可比且整体稳定性更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主构建物理可实现的 3D 结构存在动作空间组合爆炸、零件可互换、装配序列等效以及构建过程严格稳定性要求等难题。现有方法微调大语言模型做文本生成式构建，但存在三点不足：不支持多模态（文本、图像、草图）条件；忽视脚手架在稳定中间结构中的实际作用；推理速度慢。

Method: 将构建问题形式化为带多种潜在装配动作与多种任务条件模态的概率式下一块生成任务；显式引入辅助脚手架块 token 以建模脚手架效用。提出 ScaffoldM3C——一种多模态、轻量级、自回归的积木构建模型，输出一组下一步候选块；基于这些候选使用序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）维护可能装配序列的种群，从而同时考虑多个（可能不同的）装配方向。训练上，将 StableText2Brick 数据集扩展为包含图像条件提示与脚手架稳定化构建序列。

Result: ScaffoldM3C 参数量比竞争基线小 4 倍，推理速度提升 5–20 倍，构建质量与 SOTA 方法相当，且整体稳定性更高。通过仿真与真实机器人装配演示验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 以多模态轻量自回归生成结合 SMC 搜索与脚手架建模，可在显著降低模型规模与推理成本的同时，实现兼具高质量与高稳定性的物理可装配 3D 结构构建，并具备真实机器人落地能力。

Abstract: Autonomously constructing physically realizable 3D structures remains a significant challenge due to combinatorial action spaces, interchangeable components, equifinal assembly sequences, and strict stability requirements during construction. State-of-the-art methods fine-tune large language models for text-based generative construction. However, these approaches do not allow for Multimodal (text, image, sketch) conditioning, overlook the practical role of scaffolding for stabilizing intermediate structures, and suffer from slow inference speeds. Therefore, we formulate construction as a probabilistic next-block generation task with multiple potential assembly actions and multiple potential task conditioning modalities. Concurrently, we explicitly consider the utility of scaffolding by introducing an auxiliary scaffold block token. We present Scaffold Multimodal Monte Carlo (ScaffoldM3C), a multimodal, lightweight, auto-regressive model for stable block-based construction, that proposes a set of next-step candidate blocks. Leveraging these candidates, we utilize Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) to maintain a population of possible assembly sequences, allowing us to consider multiple, potentially different, assembly directions simultaneously. We train our multimodal architecture by extending the StableText2Brick dataset to contain image conditioning prompts and scaffold-stabilized build sequences. ScaffoldM3C is 4x smaller than competing baselines, yielding a 5x to 20x speedup during inference, while achieving comparable construction quality to state-of-the-art methods and higher overall stability. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach through simulations and real-world robot assembly demonstrations.

</details>


### [24] [Same Scene, Different Task: Skill Alignment for Compositional Generalization in VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00524)
*Taegeun Yang,Youngju Na,Yoonki Cho,Sung-Eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CRAFT，通过反事实配对与技能表征迁移监督，缓解 VLA 模型在未演示技能组合上的视觉捷径问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型即便已学会各个组成技能，仍难以泛化到微调演示中未出现的技能组合，其失败模式之一是"视觉捷径"：视觉观察成为指令的代理，导致策略依据相似观察执行已演示组合而非指令指定的组合。

Method: 构建反事实配对：固定某条演示的观察，将指令改为未演示的组合。由于这些配对缺少对应动作目标，且同一技能在不同观察下所需动作不同，故提出 CRAFT，利用可在同一技能多次执行间复用的技能表征，将已演示执行中的监督迁移到反事实配对。

Result: 在三种 VLA 模型与两个仿真基准上，CRAFT 提升了未演示组合的成功率，同时保持演示组合的高成功率；并在真实机器人上提升了组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: 技能表征迁移能有效利用反事实配对消除视觉捷径，为 VLA 模型的组合泛化提供可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models often struggle to generalize to skill combinations absent from their fine-tuning demonstrations, even when every constituent skill has been demonstrated. We focus on a vision shortcut as one failure mode: during fine-tuning, visual observations can serve as a proxy for the instruction, so a policy may execute a demonstrated combination associated with similar observations rather than the instructed combination. This motivates training with counterfactual pairs formed by holding a demonstration observation fixed while changing the instruction to specify an undemonstrated combination. These pairs, however, lack corresponding demonstrated action targets. Crucially, the currently required skill has already been demonstrated, but actions from those executions cannot serve as direct targets because the same skill can require different actions across observations. We propose CRAFT, which transfers supervision from demonstrated executions of the required skill to counterfactual pairs using skill representations that can be reused across executions of the same skill. Across three VLA models and two simulation benchmarks, CRAFT improves success on undemonstrated combinations while maintaining high success on demonstrated ones; it also improves compositional generalization on a real robot. Project website: https://taegeunyang.github.io/craft/

</details>


### [25] [Does Continual Imitation Learning Remain Grounded? A Language-Perturbed Benchmark for Robotic Task Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00542)
*Siddeshwar Raghavan,Ziqin Yuan,Fengqing Zhu,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基准协议与诊断指标，用于研究机器人持续模仿学习中语言引导行为随任务累积的变化，揭示持续学习性能强并不等于语言基础可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续模仿学习通常只评估任务性能是否保持，但保留任务表现不代表行为仍真正由语言引导——策略可能依赖场景线索、物体关联或记忆化的任务结构，因此需要专门衡量语言基础的诊断方法。

Method: 为LIBERO的Goal、Spatial、Object、Long套件构造保义与变义两种指令变体；策略实验聚焦LIBERO-Goal，在每一持续学习阶段后评估原始与改写（Paraphrase）指令；在保持各方法原始假设的前提下比较代表性持续模仿学习方法，并将任务胜任度与语言敏感性分离，提出语义鲁棒性、目标适应性和语言敏感性等诊断指标。

Result: 实验显示，强的持续学习表现并不总能转化为可靠的语言基础，所提诊断指标能够判断保留下来的技能是否仍被其指令正确引导。

Conclusion: 语言基础应与任务性能分开评估；所提诊断可作为标准学习与遗忘指标的补充，用于检验持续模仿学习中技能的行为是否仍受指令正确引导。

Abstract: Continual imitation learning evaluates whether a robot can learn new knowledge without forgetting previously learned skills. However, retaining task performance does not ensure the behavior remains grounded in language because policies may rely on scene cues, object associations, or memorized task structure. We introduce a benchmark protocol to study how language-guided behavior changes as robotic policies learn successive tasks. We construct meaning-preserving and meaning-changing instruction variants for the Goal, Spatial, Object, and Long suites of LIBERO. Policy experiments focus on LIBERO-Goal, evaluating Original and Paraphrase instructions after each continual-learning stage. We compare representative continual imitation learning methods under their original assumptions while separating task competence from language sensitivity. The proposed diagnostics complement standard learning and forgetting metrics by measuring semantic robustness, goal adaptation, and language sensitivity. Results show that strong continual-learning performance does not always translate to reliable language grounding, and our diagnostics help determine whether retained skills remain correctly guided by their instructions. Additional materials are available at https://sites.google.com/view/stillgrounded

</details>


### [26] [Token-World: World Modeling in Vision-Language Model Token Space for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00575)
*Chuyao Fu,Xiaowei Chi,Yuhan Rui,Yu-kai Wang,Zezhong Qian,Xiaojie Zhang,Yunfan Lou,Kevin Zhang,Kuangzhi Ge,Chak Wing Mak,Zhiyang Chen,Athena Zhuoming Zhong,Hongyang Chen,Haoran Li,Yike Guo,Sirui Han,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Token-World，一种动作条件化的世界模型：将 VLM 视觉 token 压缩为紧凑 token 状态，在低维空间学习未来动力学，再映射回策略面向的原始表示，用于 VLA 系统的世界模型仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 世界模型仿真通常预测未来 RGB 观测并重新编码为策略输入，导致仿真与下游策略执行之间存在间接接口；本文探索能否在由 VLM 视觉 token 导出的紧凑、面向策略的状态中建模世界动力学。关键难点是原始 VLM 视觉 token 维度高，难以高效准确地进行自回归动力学建模。

Method: Token-World 为动作条件化世界模型，包含三步：将 VLM 特征压缩为紧凑 token 状态；在该降维空间中学习未来动力学；将预测状态映射回原始策略面向表示以供下游使用。并分析了紧凑表示设计与维度对预测的影响。

Result: 在操作基准上，Token-World 相比近期世界模型模拟器提升了开环特征保真度与策略-动作一致性，长时域 rollout 退化更慢；闭环评估中，其仿真策略性能与参考策略性能的相关性高于 Ctrl-World（r=0.794 对 0.583），且仿真延迟更低。

Conclusion: 在面向策略的紧凑 VLM token 空间中建模世界动力学是可行且有效的，可提升仿真保真度、长时域稳定性与闭环评估的可靠性，同时降低延迟。消融表明紧凑表示设计及维度显著影响未来状态预测。代码将开源。

Abstract: A common approach to world-model simulation for vision-language-action (VLA) systems is to predict future RGB observations and then re-encode them into policy inputs, introducing an indirect interface between simulation and downstream policy execution. We instead investigate whether world dynamics can be modeled in a compact, policy-oriented state derived from VLM visual tokens. A key challenge is that raw VLM visual tokens are high-dimensional, making efficient and accurate autoregressive dynamics modeling challenging. To address this, we introduce Token-World, an action-conditioned world model that compresses VLM features into a compact token state, learns future dynamics in this reduced space, and maps predicted states back to the original policy-facing representation for downstream use. Across manipulation benchmarks, Token-World improves open-loop feature fidelity and policy-action consistency over recent world-model simulators, with slower degradation over long rollout horizons. In closed-loop evaluation, its simulated policy performance correlates more strongly with reference policy performance than Ctrl-World ($r=0.794$ vs.\ $0.583$), while requiring lower simulation latency. Ablations further show that compact-representation design and dimensionality substantially affect future-state prediction. Code will be available at https://chuyaofu.github.io/Token-World/.

</details>


### [27] [When Reasoning Helps Action: Monitoring and Steering Chain-of-Thought in Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00601)
*Sathwik Karnik,Joseph JR. Lee,Aryaman Gupta,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出评估推理型VLA策略中CoT作为运行时安全接口的两个维度——可纠正性(correctability)与可执行性(actionability)，并给出TRUST方法(离线训练的价值模型，用前缀预测推理正确性以监控和选择性引导生成)。在Alpamayo 1.5驾驶VLA上监控准确率88.9%，推理正确率从75.9%提升至90.0%，碰撞率降低30.4%、最大轨迹误差降低11.5%；而在DeepThinkVLA操作任务上推理正确率显著提升但闭环性能基本不变，说明推理正确性提升不必然带来自主体性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型VLA策略会输出CoT痕迹，看似能解释并引导动作，因而为运行时安全监控与纠正提供了潜在接口。但此前缺乏明确框架来判断这种接口何时能真正改善自主体行为，即推理层面的纠正能否转化为行为层面的收益尚不清晰。

Method: 定义并操作化两个评估轴：correctability(不可靠推理能否在生成过程中被检测并改进)与actionability(推理纠正是否在预期方向上带来行为上有意义的改变)。为实现correctability，提出TRUST(Token-level Reward for Utility-Steered Chain-of-Thought)：一个离线训练的价值模型，从部分前缀预测最终推理正确性，并用该估计在冻结的VLA策略中监控并选择性引导推理生成。

Result: Alpamayo 1.5驾驶VLA：TRUST监控正确性准确率88.9%，推理正确率75.9%→90.0%；在AlpaSim困难子集上碰撞率降低30.4%、最大轨迹误差降低11.5%，优于算力匹配的Best-of-4基线。DeepThinkVLA操作VLA：抓取状态断言正确率69.3%→90.2%，动作选择断言68.8%→85.9%，但LIBERO-Plus闭环任务性能基本不变。经验分析显示Alpamayo 1.5存在意图一致的行为效应，而DeepThinkVLA效应有限，可解释上述任务级结果差异。

Conclusion: 推理正确性的提升并不自动意味着自主体性能的提升。将CoT用作运行时安全接口时，应同时评估correctability与actionability两个维度，以判断推理纠正能否真正转化为行为收益。

Abstract: Reasoning-enabled VLA policies expose chain-of-thought (CoT) traces that appear to explain and guide their actions, creating a potential interface for runtime safety through reasoning monitoring and correction. In this work, we define and operationalize two evaluation axes for assessing when this interface can improve embodied behavior: correctability, which measures whether unreliable reasoning can be detected and improved during generation, and actionability, which measures whether reasoning corrections produce behaviorally meaningful changes in the intended direction. To enable correctability, we introduce Token-level Reward for Utility-Steered Chain-of-Thought (TRUST), an offline-trained value model that predicts eventual reasoning correctness from partial prefixes and uses these estimates to monitor and selectively steer reasoning generation in frozen VLA policies. On the Alpamayo 1.5 driving VLA, TRUST monitors correctness with 88.9% accuracy and improves reasoning correctness from 75.9% to 90.0%. On a baseline-defined challenging subset in AlpaSim, TRUST reduces collision rate by 30.4% and maximum trajectory error by 11.5% relative to the unsteered policy, outperforming a compute-matched Best-of-4 baseline. On the DeepThinkVLA manipulation VLA, TRUST improves the correctness of grasp-state claims from 69.3% to 90.2% and action-choice claims from 68.8% to 85.9%, yet closed-loop task performance on LIBERO-Plus remains largely unchanged. Empirical analysis reveals intent-consistent behavioral effects in Alpamayo 1.5 but limited effects in DeepThinkVLA, helping interpret these different task-level outcomes. Together, our results show that gains in reasoning correctness do not automatically imply gains in embodied performance, motivating evaluation of correctability and actionability when using CoT as a runtime safety interface.

</details>


### [28] [TacDyn-WAM: Learning Implicit Tactile Dynamics in a Heterogeneous Visuo-Tactile World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00638)
*Enyi Wang,Mingxin Wang,Quan Shi,Hetian Guo,Hongyu Wang,Xi Wang,Bin Qian,Yupeng Zheng,Wenxuan Song,Houde Liu,Yong Xu,Cheng Chi,Wenchao Ding,Yilun Chen,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacDyn-WAM：一种异构视触觉世界动作模型，预测隐式触觉动力学而非重建未来触觉观测，在UniVTAC达81.5%成功率，真机五任务达71.0%（预训练后85.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉世界动作模型多沿用视频生成范式，通过迭代去噪重建未来触觉观测；在部署漂移下，接触位置或力的微小变化会显著改变触觉像素，即使底层接触演化本身可预测，导致预测不可靠。

Method: 提出TacDyn-WAM：学习TacRep——通过触觉片段上的掩码时空预测训练、并以关系结构蒸馏正则化的动力学感知触觉目标空间；由视觉专家与隐式触觉动力学专家在各自目标空间预测未来表征，并通过联合注意力交互；触觉专家单次前向预测多时域未来表征及其变化，只读触觉记忆提供当前触觉状态。

Result: UniVTAC上仅用给定演示达平均成功率81.5%，达SOTA水平，与大规模视触觉轨迹预训练模型相当；消融验证两条触觉通路及TacRep优于像素重建与静态替代方案；五个真机任务平均成功率71.0%，适度规模预训练提升至85.0%。

Conclusion: 以隐式触觉动力学预测替代触觉观测重建，可提升视触觉世界动作模型在部署漂移下的鲁棒性与数据效率。

Abstract: World action models improve robotic manipulation by conditioning actions on predicted futures, yet existing tactile variants largely inherit video-generation pipelines that reconstruct future tactile observations through iterative denoising. Such prediction can become unreliable under deployment drift: small changes in contact position or force may substantially alter tactile pixels even when the underlying contact evolution remains predictable. We introduce TacDyn-WAM, a heterogeneous visuo-tactile world action model that predicts implicit tactile dynamics rather than reconstructing future tactile observations. It learns TacRep, a dynamics-aware tactile target space trained through masked spatio-temporal prediction on tactile clips and regularized by relational structure distillation. A visual expert and an Implicit Tactile Dynamics Expert predict future visual and tactile representations in separate target spaces while interacting through joint attention; the tactile expert predicts future representations and their changes at multiple horizons in a single forward pass, and a read-only tactile memory supplies the current tactile state. On UniVTAC, TacDyn-WAM achieves an average success rate of 81.5% using only the provided demonstrations, reaching state-of-the-art-level performance and remaining competitive with models pretrained on large-scale visuo-tactile trajectories. Ablations confirm the benefits of both tactile pathways and TacRep over pixel-reconstruction and static alternatives. On five real-robot tasks, TacDyn-WAM reaches 71.0% average success, and modest-scale pretraining raises it to 85.0%, further validating our method.

</details>


### [29] [Toward Humanoid Robots in Construction: A Teleoperation Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00718)
*Parastoo Ali Pour,David R. Martin,Chang Min Hur,Bo Zhang,Tommy Zhou,Brandon Thomas Lichter,Shane Stanfield,Pramod Khargonekar,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向 Unitree G1 人形机器人的遥操作系统，将基于扩展现实（XR）的上半身控制与脚踏式移动控制相结合，使单一操作员能够同时进行操作与行走，并在两项建筑任务上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受持续劳动力短缺、危险作业环境以及人形机器人自主性不足等问题驱动，作者将遥操作视为一种近期可行的实用方案，既能减轻工人身体负担，又能采集高质量的演示数据。

Method: 系统由两部分组成：基于 XR 的上半身控制用于操作，脚踏式（pedal-based）控制用于移动，从而支持单一操作员同时完成操作与移动。评测任务取自 O*NET 职业数据库中的两项代表性建筑任务，指标为任务成功率与完成时间，并以人工直接作业为基线进行对比。

Result: 工具搬运任务成功率为 100%，表面喷涂任务成功率为 80%；但与人工基线相比，遥操作所需时间显著更长。

Conclusion: 遥操作在建筑任务中具备可行性，尤其在搬运类任务上表现良好，但作业效率仍明显低于人工直接操作，未来需在速度与效率上进一步优化。

Abstract: We present a teleoperation system that enables a single operator to perform construction tasks on a Unitree G1 humanoid, combining extended reality (XR) based upper body control with pedal-based locomotion to enable simultaneous manipulation and locomotion. Motivated by persistent labor shortages, hazardous working conditions, and challenges in humanoid autonomy, we investigate teleoperation as a practical near-term approach for reducing physical strain on workers while generating high quality demonstration data. We evaluate the system on two representative construction tasks drawn from O*NET occupational database, and report task success and completion time relative to a manual baseline. The system achieved 100% success on tool transport and 80% success on surface painting, with teleoperation requiring substantially more time compared to manual execution.

</details>


### [30] [CF-JEPA: Improving Robustness of JEPA World Models via Controllability Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00727)
*Morgan Byrd,Robert Wright,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Controllability Factorized JEPA（CF-JEPA），一种将潜在空间分解为可控与不可控子空间的JEPA式世界模型，用于在视觉控制中隔离干扰信息并避免潜在崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉智能体控制需要从无关背景信息中分离有用信息。JEPA式潜在世界模型因不进行像素级重建而看似天然适合该任务，但它们对干扰信号仍然敏感，并会经历潜在崩溃（latent collapse）。

Method: 提出CF-JEPA：一种JEPA式世界模型，将潜在空间拆分为可控子空间和不可控子空间。该因子化使所有干扰信息被捕获到不可控区域，而任务使用与控制相关的潜在信息。

Result: 在2D和3D控制任务中，标称条件下性能相当；在受干扰条件下性能提升，且CF-JEPA是唯一未经历潜在崩溃的模型。并在受干扰条件下的模拟机器人任务中验证了该模型，体现了该方案的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 通过对潜在空间的可控性因子化，CF-JEPA能有效将干扰信息隔离至不可控子空间，在干扰环境下提升控制性能并避免潜在崩溃，具备实际机器人任务的应用潜力。

Abstract: Controlling an agent with vision requires being able to separate useful information from irrelevant background information. JEPA-style latent world models seem like a natural approach for this, as they do not perform pixel-level reconstruction; however, they are still sensitive to these distractor signals and experience latent collapse. In this work, we introduce Controllability Factorized JEPA (CF-JEPA), a JEPA-style world model which splits the latent space into controllable and uncontrollable subspaces. This factorization allows us to capture all the distractor information into the uncontrollable region, while we use the control-relevant latent information for our task. With this, we show comparable performance across 2D and 3D control tasks under nominal conditions and improved performance under distracted conditions, where CF-JEPA is the only model that does not experience latent collapse. We also validate our model under distracted conditions for a simulated robot task, highlighting the practical application of such a scheme.

</details>


### [31] [Measuring Asset and Scene Reconstruction Effects in Real-to-Sim Robot Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00731)
*Sanya Verma,Luca Cilio,Velissarios Christodoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过对比两种仿真重建方案（自建度量尺度+手工物理 vs 开源默认配方），评估其与真机策略评分的吻合度，发现高质量重建显著提升仿真与真机的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真评估因成本低、易重复而被广泛用于机器人策略评估，但其价值取决于仿真结果与真机结果的一致程度。作者希望验证：结合度量尺度物体几何、手工物理参数与场景重建的流水线，是否能比开源默认配方更好地缩小仿真与真机之间的评分差距。

Method: 针对同一双臂机器人单元构建两个仿真版本：自建重建（采用估计度量尺度的物体几何、投影纹理、手工物理参数及自研场景splat）与基线重建（采用开源配方：单图生成网格、引擎默认物理、Gaussian-splat场景），两者使用相同的物体照片和场景视频。2个策略×5个任务=10个任务-策略组合（cell），每个cell在每种重建下以固定其余设置运行20次。两种重建均与同一批真机试验对比评分，由第三方评估者打分。

Result: 10个cell仿真与真机均值的Pearson相关系数：自建重建r=0.90，默认重建r=0.51。平均评分误差：自建重建6.97个百分点，默认重建17.54个百分点，误差减少10.56个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过更高的视觉保真度、手工物理参数和度量尺度来提升环境重建质量，能使仿真更忠实于真实世界，缩小sim-to-real差距。作者公开了测试框架、逐次试验评分、所有报告运行的配置以及两种重建的资产与场景。

Abstract: Simulated evaluation is increasingly used alongside real-world evaluation of robot policies because it is cheaper and easier to repeat; however, its value depends on how closely its outcomes track the real robot's. We test whether our reconstruction pipeline, combining metrically scaled object geometry, authored physical parameters and scene reconstruction, reduces disagreement between simulated and real robot scores relative to a default open-source recipe. We constructed two simulated versions of one bimanual robot cell: an authored reconstruction, using object geometry at estimated metric scale, projected textures, authored physics and our own scene splat; and a baseline, referred to as the default reconstruction, using the open-source recipe of a generative single-image mesh, engine-default physics and a Gaussian-splat scene. Both reconstructions use the same object photographs and scene video. Two policies ran five tasks each, giving ten task-policy pairs, which we call cells; each cell was run twenty times in each reconstruction with all other settings held fixed. Both reconstructions were scored against the same real trials, graded by a third-party evaluator. Pearson correlation between the ten simulated and real cell means is r = 0.90 for the authored reconstruction and 0.51 for the default. Mean score error is 6.97 percentage points for the authored reconstruction and 17.54 for the default, a reduction of 10.56 percentage points. These results show that improving the quality of the environment reconstruction through higher visual fidelity, authored physics and metric scale makes the simulation more faithful to the real world and narrows the sim-to-real gap. We release the harness, the per-trial scores, every reported run's configuration, and the assets and scenes of both reconstructions.

</details>


### [32] [DITTO-X: Forward and Reverse Teleoperation for Dexterous Manipulation and Human Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00781)
*Zhanpeng He,Joaquin Palacios,Zhangyu Wang,Chenhao Li,Katelyn Lee,Matei Ciocarlie,C. Karen Liu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DITTO-X：一种与手型无关的灵巧遥操作接口，通过机器人手部既有传感器渲染关节级力与指尖接触事件，驱动三种商用灵巧手，并支持反向遥操作使操作者入环即与机器人状态匹配，提升演示质量与吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作系统多仅依赖视觉闭环且围绕平行夹爪构建，限制了机器人可执行的动作与操作者可表达的信息。在共享自主场景中尤为严重：操作者仅能通过被遮挡的相机观察场景，且需在任务中途接管已抓取物体的灵巧手。

Method: DITTO-X为与手型无关的灵巧遥操作接口：利用机器人手部已有的传感渲染关节级力与指尖接触事件，无需针对每种手重新设计即可驱动Sharpa、Wuji、Inspire三种商用灵巧手；外骨骼具备驱动能力，因此支持反向遥操作——由机器人反向驱动操作者手指至自身构型，再移交控制权，使操作者入环时即与其继承的状态匹配。

Result: 在常规数据采集以及在策略部署期间针对接触丰富操作任务的人类干预中，DITTO-X相较商用手势追踪手套均提升了演示质量与吞吐量。

Conclusion: DITTO-X作为手型无关的灵巧遥操作接口，结合力/接触反馈与反向遥操作，可有效改善遥操作演示质量与部署干预效率。更多信息见 https://tml.stanford.edu/ditto-x/。

Abstract: Teleoperated demonstrations are a primary source of data for robot manipulation, and teleoperated interventions are a primary mechanism for correcting policies at deployment. Yet most teleoperation systems close the loop through vision alone and are built around parallel-jaw grippers, limiting both what the robot can execute and what the operator can express through it. This is most damaging in shared autonomy, where the operator sees the scene only through occluded cameras and must take over a dexterous hand mid-task, often with an object already grasped. We present DITTO-X, a hand-agnostic dexterous teleoperation interface that renders joint-level force and fingertip contact events from sensing already on the robot hand, and drives three commercial dexterous hands (Sharpa, Wuji, and Inspire) without per-hand redesign. Because the exoskeleton is actuated, DITTO-X also supports reverse teleoperation, in which the robot back-drives the operator's fingers into its own configuration before control is transferred, so the human enters the loop already matched to the state they inherit. Our results show that DITTO-X improves demonstration quality and throughput over a commercial hand-tracking glove, both in regular data collection and in human intervention during policy deployment for contact-rich manipulation tasks. More information can be found from our website: https://tml.stanford.edu/ditto-x/.

</details>


### [33] [ECoMEM: Explicit Concept Memory for Memory-Dependent Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00801)
*Yize Liu,Ke Wang,Mac Schwager,Yiqing Xu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出显式概念记忆（ECoMEM），用可复用的、基于证据的“概念库”代替隐式记忆，将“记住什么”与“如何行动”解耦，在16个RoboMME任务中15项领先，并在2个真实机器人任务上以86.1%成功率远超无记忆VLA的8.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人行为（如物体暂时离开视野、回忆先前演示、追踪任务进度）需要记忆；但现有VLA策略在关键信息从当前观测中消失后往往失效。已有方法多提供更长历史或从观测-动作轨迹中学习隐式记忆，而动作监督只告诉策略如何行动，并不说明哪些过去事实应被保留、以及应如何随新证据更新，因此隐式记忆缺乏明确的记忆内容规范。

Method: 将“维护基于证据的过去记录”与“学习如何据此行动”分离。ECoMEM用可复用的 grounded concept 库表示任务相关历史：一个基于证据的 Writer 负责选择并更新这些记忆记录；一个学习得到的 Reader 将其转换为 memory tokens，直接作为条件输入给 VLA 策略。

Result: 在16个 RoboMME 任务上，ECoMEM 在15个任务中优于所评估的机器人策略。在两个新的真实机器人任务上，同一记忆库要么可直接迁移，要么仅需新增一个概念，取得 86.1% 成功率，而无记忆 VLA 仅 8.6%。

Conclusion: 显式概念可为机器人控制提供可复用、可扩展的记忆接口，弥补隐式记忆在长时程任务中的不足。项目网站：https://ecomem.github.io/

Abstract: A robot may lose sight of an object it must later retrieve, need to recall what a person demonstrated earlier, or track which steps of a task it has already completed. Current vision-language-action (VLA) policies often fail once the information needed for action disappears from the current observation, making memory critical for long-horizon robot behavior. Existing approaches typically provide longer histories or learn implicit memory from observation-action trajectories. But action supervision tells a policy how to act, not what to remember: it does not specify which past facts should persist or how they should change as new evidence arrives. We therefore separate maintaining an evidence-grounded account of the past from learning how to act on it. This insight motivates Explicit Concept Memory (ECoMEM), which represents task-relevant history with a reusable library of grounded concepts. An evidence-based Writer selects and updates these records, while a learned Reader turns them into memory tokens that directly condition the VLA. Across 16 RoboMME tasks, ECoMEM leads the evaluated robot policies on 15 tasks. On two new real-robot tasks, the same memory library either transfers directly or requires only one new concept, achieving 86.1% success versus 8.6% for a no-memory VLA. These results show that explicit concepts provide a reusable and extensible memory interface for robot control. Project website: https://ecomem.github.io/

</details>


### [34] [Getting Out and Getting Back: World and Behavior Grounding in Real2Sim2Real Co-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00821)
*Samuel Liu,Youngsun Kim,Martin Matak,Gilwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出real2sim2real流水线，独立变化"世界接地"与"行为接地"两个维度生成仿真数据用于协同训练，在动态灵巧抓取分拣任务上验证二者的互补作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真可扩展稀缺的真实演示用于协同训练，但世界保真度与对人类行为相似度如何影响策略性能尚不清楚，需厘清仿真数据的哪些属性真正起作用。

Method: 区分两种接地：世界接地对齐仿真与真实系统，行为接地对齐仿真轨迹与人类运动。构建real2sim2real流水线，独立变化这两个维度以生成协同训练数据，并通过潜在空间分析考察部署策略的行为构成。

Result: 在动态灵巧抓取分拣任务上，完全接地的协同训练将成功率从52%提升至86%；跨配置平均，世界接地提升18个百分点，行为接地提升10个百分点。部署策略表现为真实来源与仿真来源策略的混合体：在真实数据覆盖的状态模仿真实演示，其余状态依赖仿真行为。

Conclusion: 二者角色互补：世界接地使策略能利用超出真实数据覆盖范围的仿真经验，行为接地主要在世界接地不完美时才重要。接地仿真对协同训练基础模型依然有益。

Abstract: Simulation can expand scarce real demonstrations for co-training, yet how world fidelity and similarity to human behavior affect policy performance remains unclear. We distinguish world grounding, which aligns simulation with the real system, and behavior grounding, which aligns simulated trajectories with human motion. We build a real2sim2real pipeline that varies these axes independently to generate data for co-training. On a dynamic dexterous pick-and-sort task, fully grounded co-training raises success from 52% to 86%; averaged across configurations, world grounding improves success by 18 percentage points and behavior grounding by 10. Deployed policies behave like a mixture of real-derived and simulation-derived policies, imitating real demonstrations in covered states and relying on simulated behavior elsewhere, which we examine through latent-space analysis. Together, these results suggest complementary roles: world grounding lets policies use simulated experience beyond real-data coverage, while behavior grounding matters mainly when world grounding is imperfect. Grounded simulation remains beneficial when co-training foundation models.

</details>


### [35] [TRACE: Privacy-Preserving Next-Best-View Selection over Distributed 3D Gaussian-Splat Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00822)
*Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Athanasios Cosse,Qiyu Sun,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TRACE协议，使多机器人团队在不共享各自3DGS地图的前提下，通过只传递两束光线聚合量完成去中心化的下一最佳视角选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人各自维护私有的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）地图时，下一最佳视角（NBV）选择所需的期望信息增益（EIG）依赖全局汇聚地图：他机高斯会遮挡本机高斯、也会在其后方透出，而任何单机都无法获得该汇聚地图。核心问题是：如何在不共享地图、不暴露splats的情况下，仍能准确计算EIG及其梯度。

Method: 作者从渲染方程出发证明：地图耦合仅通过两个光线量传递——位于某高斯前方的透射率（transmittance）与其后方的辐射度（radiance），且二者均为该射线上各命中点的可加和。基于此，聚合量可按机器人分解：每台机器人沿候选视角射线，在自己的地图上按深度分箱求和，并把求和值连同其对位姿的导数一起发送。规划视角的机器人据此在SO(3)上重建EIG及其梯度。由于通信内容恰为Transmittance and Radiance Aggregates for the EIG，协议得名TRACE。全程不传输任何splats，消息尺寸不随地图规模增长。理论上证明了重构的精确性条件（仅当某高斯后方的深度箱混入两台机器人的命中点时出现误差），并给出误差上界。

Result: 在Habitat-Sim中执行超过100次NBV决策，TRACE在83.3%的情况下所选朝向与中心化方案相差在15度以内，其视角所达到的EIG为中心化方法的97.9%。

Conclusion: TRACE表明，多机器人3DGS建图中的NBV选择无需共享地图即可近似中心化性能：耦合结构仅由两项可分解的光线聚合量决定，从而在保持隐私与恒定通信开销的同时，获得接近最优的视角与EIG。误差分析给出了精确重构条件与有界误差，仿真结果验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Share the light, not the map. We study next-best-view selection for a team of robots, each of which builds its own 3D Gaussian Splatting map and keeps it private. A robot picks the view with the largest expected information gain (EIG) about the splats along its own path. This gain depends on the other maps. Their splats occlude its own and shine behind them, so the gain has to be evaluated against the pooled map. No robot has this map. We show that the coupling passes through only two ray quantities, the transmittance in front of a splat and the radiance behind it, and that both are sums over the hits of the ray. Hence, they decompose across the robots, and each robot sums them over depth bins in its own map, along the rays of a candidate view, and sends the sums with their pose derivatives. The robot planning the view turns them into its EIG and gradient on SO(3). Transmittance and Radiance Aggregates, communicated for the EIG, give the protocol its name: TRACE. No robot shares its splats, and the message size does not grow with a map. We prove that the reconstruction is exact unless a depth bin behind a splat mixes hits of two robots, and we bound the error otherwise. Over 100 next-best-view decisions in Habitat-Sim, TRACE picks a heading within 15 degrees of the centralized one in 83.3% of the cases, and its views reach 97.9% of the centralized EIG.

</details>


### [36] [Reactive Humanoid Multi-Contact Using Learned Stability Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00823)
*Stephen McCrory,Beomyeong Park,Nicholas Kitchel,Nehar Poddar,Robert Griffin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用手部接触的反应式规划控制方法，结合学习到的CoP区域模型快速评估候选接触点，提升人形机器人低稳定性场景的冲量恢复能力，并在仿真与硬件上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在低稳定性场景下仅依靠足部接触可能无法避免摔倒，需要引入手部接触（bracing）来增强抗扰与稳定能力；而传统基于优化的候选接触点评估计算量大，难以满足反应式快速决策的需求。

Method: 在机器人可达工作空间内采样候选接触点，通过滚动展开质心动力学（涵盖pre-impact、impact、post-impact三阶段）进行预览计算；以post-impact阶段的CoP控制权限对采样点打分。核心是学习得到的post-impact阶段机器人CoP区域模型，用于快速评估候选接触点。规划器分两阶段：先选择最优支撑区域，再在区域内计算最优支撑点。

Result: 仿真中冲量恢复能力相比无手部接触恢复平均提升89%，相比朴素规划策略（最近可达区域）提升17%。硬件实验中，站立推动测试相比朴素手部放置平均减少43%的稳定时间；行走测试相比无手部接触的基线恢复减少18%。

Conclusion: 学习到的CoP区域模型可替代传统优化实现候选接触点的快速评估，两阶段规划策略有效提升了人形机器人在低稳定性场景下的冲量恢复与稳定效率，且方法在仿真与真实硬件上均得到验证。

Abstract: We present a planning and control approach to reactively use hand contacts to stabilize a humanoid in low stability scenarios, where only using feet contacts may result in a fall. Candidate contacts are sampled within the robot's reachable workspace, and a preview is computed by rolling out the centroidal dynamics through pre-impact, impact and post-impact phases. Sampled points are scored based on the Center of Pressure (CoP) control authority at the post-impact phase. Central to our approach is a learned model of the robot's CoP region during post-impact, which enables rapid evaluation of candidate contact points compared to traditional optimization-based methods. The presented planner has two stages: the first selects an optimal bracing region and the second computes an optimal bracing point within the region. Our simulation results demonstrate an average increase in impulse resilience of 89% over recovery without hand contacts and 17% over a naive planning strategy (closest reachable region). We validate our framework on hardware, performing push tests while standing and walking. The standing trials show an average 43% reduction in stabilization time compared to naive hand placement and the walking trials demonstrate a 18% reduction compared to baseline recovery (without hand contacts).

</details>


### [37] [Are Frontier VLM Agents Ready to Be Robot Generalists? An Empirical Study with the Embodied Agent Arena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00854)
*Haojian Huang,Pukun Zhao,Zexi Li,Yehang Zhang,Yangkai Wei,Wenqian Li,Han Yang,Kaiwen Zhou,Ying-Cong Chen,Yinchuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Embodied Agent Arena 基准，用 1000 个案例（32 个来源 + 新基准 GeoProbe）系统评估 7 个视觉语言模型在几何估计、空间推理、可供性、任务规划与操作五类机器人能力上的表现，发现局部能力优势明显但完整任务执行仍是核心缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿视觉语言模型（VLM）已整合场景估计、交互定位与可执行动作，但其各项能力能否支撑完整机器人任务尚不清晰；需要统一框架检验"局部能力"与"整体任务成功"之间的关系，以评估其作为机器人通用智能体的成熟度。

Method: 构建 Embodied Agent Arena 评估平台，涵盖 Geometry、Spatial Reasoning、Affordance、Task Planning、Manipulation 五个维度；汇集 32 个既有来源的 1000 个案例，并提出新基准 GeoProbe（基于 Blender 渲染与真实场景图像的几何估计）。设计极简测试骨架（minimal harness），在保留原始观测与操作的同时，将度量精度、功能定位与原生目标完成三者分离评估。对 7 个 VLM 进行测评，分析 Astra 的任务特定优势，并比较更丰富观测的执行协议与多轮审查机制。

Result: Astra 的优势在精确估计与可用接触定位（usable-contact localization）上最为突出；但在需要协调、目标导向的动作执行方面表现不足。更丰富观测的执行协议与多轮审查在一定程度上影响评估结果。

Conclusion: 现有 VLM 在局部感知与定位子任务上已具备可用能力，但完成协调一致的目标导向动作仍是通向机器人通用智能体的关键瓶颈；评估需区分局部能力与整体任务成功。

Abstract: Frontier vision-language models (VLMs) combine scene estimation, interaction grounding, and executable actions. Understanding how these abilities support complete robotic tasks is central to evaluating their readiness as robot generalists. We introduce Embodied Agent Arena to examine where local competence supports, or falls short of, complete task success across Geometry, Spatial Reasoning, Affordance, Task Planning, and Manipulation. The arena contains 1,000 cases drawn from 32 established sources and GeoProbe, our new benchmark for geometric estimation on Blender renders and real-scene images. A minimal harness preserves source observations and operations while separating metric precision, functional grounding, and native goal completion. We evaluate seven VLMs, analyze Astra's task-specific advantages, and compare richer-observation execution protocols and multi-round review. Across the arena, Astra's advantage is strongest in precise estimation and usable-contact localization; completing coordinated, goal-directed actions remains the key gap to robot generalism.

</details>
