### [38] [Kinematic MeanFlow: One-Step Action Generation Policy for Robotic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00864)
*Jiawei Fan,Sifeng Wang,Yuqing Hou,Anbang Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Kinematic MeanFlow（K-MF），一种面向机器人基础模型（RFM）的一步动作生成策略，通过运动学恒等式解耦 MeanFlow 时间导数项以缓解性能崩溃，在多数任务上超越多步 flow matching，并大幅降低推理时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步 flow matching 在机器人基础模型中推理时延高，而直接套用 MeanFlow 实现一步动作生成会导致性能崩溃。作者发现崩溃源于 RFM 速度场的两种动力学特性：局部加速度在去噪早期稳定、末期急剧上升；其幅值在样本间的离散度随去噪推进而扩大。

Method: 基于运动学恒等式，K-MF 将 MeanFlow 公式中的时间导数项解耦为以中间点为界的两个子区间项，分别捕捉早期与晚期去噪动力学，并缓解跨过程误差放大。该方法支持从零训练与微调两种范式。

Result: K-MF 使 RFM 在多种任务上实现一步动作生成，且在多数设置下优于多步 flow matching。推理效率方面，在 L40 与 Jetson Orin 上，eager 与 compiled 模式下使 GR00T-N1.6 的动作头时延降低 67.5%~74.4%，端到端时延降低 30.3%~54.9%。

Conclusion: K-MF 通过解耦式 MeanFlow 建模有效解决了 RFM 一步动作生成中的性能崩溃问题，兼顾精度与推理效率，为机器人基础模型的实时部署提供了可行方案。代码将开源。

Abstract: In this paper, we study how to achieve one-step action generation in Robotic Foundation Models (RFMs), aiming to overcome the high inference latency of multi-step flow matching. MeanFlow provides a promising framework for this goal, yet its direct application leads to performance collapse. We discover that this stems from two distinctive dynamics exhibited in the RFM velocity field: (1) the ``local acceleration" exhibits stability early on, but surges sharply towards the end of the denoising process, and (2) the spread of its magnitudes across samples widens as denoising progresses. To address these issues, we introduce Kinematic MeanFlow (K-MF), a novel one-step action policy tailored for RFMs. Specifically, grounded in a kinematic identity, K-MF decouples the time derivative term in the MeanFlow formulation into two sub-interval terms separated by an intermediate point. This decoupled formulation enables the two terms to capture early-stage and late-stage denoising dynamics, respectively, while mitigating the error amplification across the process. As a result, our K-MF empowers RFMs to achieve one-step action generation in both training from scratch and fine-tuning paradigms across diverse tasks, while outperforming multi-step flow matching in most settings. In terms of inference efficiency, K-MF reduces action-head latency of GR00T-N1.6 by 67.5%~74.4% across L40 and Jetson Orin in eager and compiled modes, yielding end-to-end latency reductions of 30.3%~54.9%. Code will be available at https://github.com/IntelChina-AI/K-MF.

</details>


### [39] [UniTrackPLA: Unified Panorama-Language-Action Model for Instruction-Guided Navigation and Dynamic Person Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00878)
*Pengfei Qi,Haoran Lin,Sizhuang Chen,Kai Luo,Sirui Zhang,Xinqi Liu,Fei Cheng,Wenrui Chen,Liming Yin,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniTrackPLA：统一全景-语言-动作模型，实现指令导航与动态人物跟踪，配合OmniTrackNav-Bench数据集，显著提升跟踪与导航性能，并在真实机器人上完成闭环验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身机器人在语言指定目标导航与动态人物跟踪任务上，通常依赖前向观测并采用分离策略，限制了全向感知与统一闭环控制能力。论文希望用一个统一模型同时支持任意初始目标方位下的导航与跟踪。

Method: 提出UniTrackPLA统一全景-语言-动作模型。通过Panoramic-Aware Encoding（PAE）保留每个全景投影视角的时间与方位结构，使透视预训练视觉编码器可处理全向观测；采用共享视觉-语言主干，将指令落地到全景上下文并预测连续机器人中心航点块；引入World-Action Consistency（WAC），预测动作条件未来视觉状态并在线上验证航点前缀，一致时复用可靠动作，不一致时触发重规划。同时构建OmniTrackNav-Bench，含5,000条模拟跟踪轨迹、10,000条模拟VLN路线、96条真实路线，共919,978个航点监督实例。

Result: 跟踪整体SR由23.50%提升至35.00%；Omni-VLN的SR/SPL由13.00%/12.77%提升至19.75%/19.29%；加入76条真实路线后，held-out EP@0.2m由42.92%提升至92.08%。在Go2-W机器人上完成室内外统一全景跟踪与导航闭环实验。

Conclusion: UniTrackPLA以统一全景感知与动作生成框架整合导航与人物跟踪，并通过WAC实现重规划，实验证明其性能与真实环境闭环部署有效性。项目页面：https://tw5775.github.io/UniTrackPLA。

Abstract: General-purpose embodied robots should support both navigation toward language-specified destinations and dynamic person tracking under arbitrary initial target azimuths. However, existing methods typically rely on forward-facing observations and address these tasks with separate policies, limiting omnidirectional perception and unified closed-loop control. We present UniTrackPLA, a unified panorama-language-action model for instruction-guided navigation and dynamic person tracking. Its Panoramic-Aware Encoding (PAE) preserves the temporal and azimuthal structure of perspective views projected from each panorama, enabling perspective-pretrained visual encoders to process omnidirectional observations. A shared vision-language backbone grounds instructions in the panoramic context and predicts continuous robot-centric waypoint chunks for both tasks. World-Action Consistency (WAC) further predicts action-conditioned future visual states and verifies waypoint prefixes online, allowing reliable actions to be reused while triggering replanning upon inconsistency. We also introduce OmniTrackNav-Bench, comprising 5,000 simulated tracking trajectories, 10,000 simulated VLN routes, and 96 verified real-world routes, providing 919,978 waypoint-supervision instances. UniTrackPLA improves overall tracking SR from 23.50% to 35.00% and Omni-VLN SR/SPL from 13.00%/12.77% to 19.75%/19.29%. Incorporating 76 real-world routes further improves held-out EP@0.2m from 42.92% to 92.08%. Closed-loop experiments on a Go2-W robot demonstrate unified panoramic tracking and navigation across indoor and outdoor environments. The project page is at https://tw5775.github.io/UniTrackPLA.

</details>


### [40] [Real-Time Human-Adaptive Task Allocation for Multi-Human Multi-Robot Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00897)
*Seabin Lee,Sujeong Park,Nayoung Kim,Sungjin Park,Haechan Jung,Changjoo Nam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种考虑人为因素的多操作员机器人监督任务分配方法，依据操作员实时认知状态动态调整监督容量，以平衡脑力负荷、防止过载并提升团队绩效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多操作员偶尔远程操作多机器人时，监督任务分配需考虑个体实时认知状态。但现有方法多假设每个操作员监督容量固定，忽略工作负荷与疲劳等人为因素波动，导致负荷分配不均，部分操作员过载而其他人闲置。

Method: 采用考虑人为因素的分配方法：动态调节监督容量并分配任务，以维持脑力负荷平衡。分配使用贪心策略，在任务优先级下最小化估计的操作员工作负荷；依据当前监督容量将机器人分配给操作员，所需投入取决于任务类型。

Result: 用户研究中，跨预定义时间区间的分析表明，相比不考虑人为因素的基线方法，所提方法持续获得更高绩效且疲劳行为迹象更低。

Conclusion: 结果表明，自适应容量调节是维持长时间、高需求环境下操作员绩效的有效预防机制。

Abstract: We propose a human-factor-aware method of allocating robot supervision tasks to multiple human operators. In scenarios where multiple operators occasionally teleoperate multiple robots to help the robots overcome difficulties, the allocation of the supervisory control tasks to humans needs to consider the real-time cognitive states of individual operators. However, most existing methods assume fixed supervisory capacity per operator and overlook fluctuations in the human factors such as workload and fatigue. As a result, workload distribution can be unbalanced where some operators become overloaded while the others remain underused. Our method dynamically regulates supervisory capacity and allocates tasks in a way that maintains balanced mental workload, prevents overload, and improves overall team performance. The allocation method uses a greedy strategy that minimizes estimated operator workloads with task prioritization. Robots are assigned to operators by reflecting their current supervisory capacity where the required effort depends on the types of tasks. In the user study, the analysis across predefined time intervals shows that the proposed method consistently achieves higher performance and lower behavioral signs of fatigue compared to a baseline method that does not consider human factors. These results highlight adaptive capacity adjustment as an effective preventive mechanism for sustaining operator performance in long-duration, high-demand settings.

</details>


### [41] [TOAST: Stochastic Robot Action Tokenization for Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00899)
*Keisuke Shirai,Tomohiro Motoda,Hanbit Oh,Ryoichi Nakajo,Roman Mykhailyshyn,Ryo Hanai,Shotaro Miwa,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TOAST：对同一量化动作序列随机采样多种分词方式以增强自回归VLA策略学习，在数据稀缺时提升尤为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归VLA模型将连续动作离散为token序列，FAST等方法通过压缩编码减少动作token数量，但压缩并未必然提升有限示教下的策略学习效率。FAST对每个量化动作序列只赋予单一确定性分词，而同一机器人运动实际上可由多种token序列表示并解码得到，这种表示冗余未被利用。

Method: 提出TOAST（TOkenization of Action sequences with STochastic sampling）：在策略训练过程中，对同一量化动作序列随机采样替代分词方案，从而在保持底层机器人动作不变的前提下多样化离散监督信号，且不依赖额外示教数据。

Result: LIBERO实验中TOAST持续优于确定性基线，且训练数据越少提升越明显，仅用1/16训练数据时成功率提升6.8点；在四个真实机器人操作任务上，平均成功率较确定性基线提升15.8点。

Conclusion: 随机动作分词能有效改善自回归机器人策略学习，尤其在训练数据受限场景下优势突出。

Abstract: Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action models often represent continuous robot actions as discrete token sequences, enabling action prediction with standard next-token objectives. FAST has substantially improved this representation by compactly encoding action containing diverse temporal frequencies into relatively few tokens. However, while such compression reduces the number of action tokens required for autoregressive prediction, it does not necessarily improve the efficiency of policy learning from limited demonstrations. In particular, FAST typically assigns a single deterministic tokenization to each quantized action sequence, although multiple token sequences can represent and decode to the same robot motion. We investigate whether exploiting this representational redundancy can improve policy learning. In this paper, we propose TOkenization of Action sequences with STochastic sampling (TOAST), a stochastic action tokenization method that samples alternative tokenizations of the same quantized action sequence during policy training. This diversifies the discrete supervision while preserving the underlying robot action and requires no additional demonstrations. Experiments on LIBERO show that TOAST consistently improves over its deterministic counterpart, with the improvement increasing as training data decreases, achieving a 6.8 point gain in success rate when only 1/16 of training data is available. Across four real-robot manipulation tasks, TOAST further improves mean success rate by 15.8 points over the deterministic counterpart. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of stochastic action tokenization for autoregressive robot policy learning, particularly when training data are limited.

</details>


### [42] [Screw Attention: Rigid-Body Algebra Inside a Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00904)
*Aly Magassouba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Screw Attention：一种以空间变换（相对位姿/关节螺旋）为关系载体的Transformer层，消息在接收者坐标系中传输、注意力分数仅用坐标无关量，天然满足逐token独立换系的等变性，并可单层表达刚体力学速度递推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习式操作策略需从数据中重新发现刚体力学本可闭式给出的空间关系，导致数据成本高、对场景几何变化脆弱。作者希望将几何关系作为结构先验直接嵌入网络。

Method: 每个token表示一个刚体并携带位姿；token对之间携带相对位姿，机器人关节对额外携带关节螺旋（screw）。消息沿该空间关系变换到接收者坐标系，注意力分数只使用坐标无关（frame-invariant）量。构造上保证对每个token独立换系的等变性，单层即可表达刚体速度递推。

Result: 在仿真操作任务上匹配或超越同规模对照（图网络、Transformer、平坦网络）；在LIBERO-Spatial上仅用16162参数、仅输入物体位姿（无图像、无语言）达97.3%，超过参数多27倍的平坦网络。更换每连杆坐标系约定后成功率不变，其他学习方法均低于3%；作为门控残差叠加在解析控制器上使插入成功率提升17.3个百分点；对10mm以内位姿噪声和Franka机械臂出厂标定范围内的关节偏置不敏感。

Conclusion: 几何是决定性的：当任务需要系统其他部分无法提供的坐标系间关系时，显式几何结构带来数据效率、泛化性与鲁棒性优势。代码与训练策略将开源。

Abstract: Learned manipulation policies rediscover from data the spatial relations that rigid-body mechanics supplies in closed form. This costs data, and it leaves the policies fragile to geometric changes in the scene. We present Screw Attention, a transformer layer in which the relation between two bodies is a spatial transform rather than a graph edge. Every token is a body with a pose. Each pair of tokens carries the relative pose and, for robot joints, the joint screw. Messages are transported along this relation into the receiver's frame, while the attention scores see only frame-invariant quantities. By construction, the messages are equivariant to an independent change of frame at every token, and a single layer can express the velocity recursion of rigid-body mechanics. On simulated manipulation tasks, Screw Attention matches or outperforms controls of the same size, including graph, transformer and flat networks on LIBERO-Spatial. With 16,162 parameters it reaches 97.3% on LIBERO-Spatial from object poses (without images or language), above a flat network with 27x more parameters. Under a change of per-link frame convention its success is unchanged, while every other learned network falls below 3%. Placed on an analytic controller as a gated residual, it raises insertion success by 17.3 points. It is unaffected by pose noise up to 10,mm and by joint offsets within the factory calibration of a Franka arm. These results suggest a criterion: geometry is decisive when the task requires relations between frames that no other part of the system supplies. Code and trained policies will be released.

</details>


### [43] [eRLT: Efficient VLA Reinforcement Learning via Action-Relevant Token Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00913)
*Dehao Huang,Jianbang Liu,Jianpan Gao,Chao Tang,Zilang Cen,Zedong Dan,Jiaheng Wang,Tingguang Li,Yue Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出eRLT，通过跨token与跨层的路由机制从冻结VLA中提取任务相关的动作相关信息构建RL状态表示，在7个LIBERO/RoboTwin任务及真实机器人任务上显著提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有冻结VLA在线强化学习方法的状态表示存在不足：使用VLA无关的视觉编码器，或对VLA内部表示做固定压缩，均未显式提取对下游动作精修与动作价值估计最有用的任务特定、动作相关的VLA特征，从而限制了样本效率。

Method: 提出eRLT：利用可学习路由token在冻结VLA的多个深度上动态聚合视觉-语言特征，并由轻量级层路由器将多层摘要组合为固定维度的RL token；路由模块先用专家演示初始化，以捕获对专家动作具有预测性的特征，再利用在线交互中的critic反馈进行精化，服务于动作价值估计。

Result: 在7个LIBERO与RoboTwin任务上，平均归一化学习曲线AUC相较代表性基线最高提升23.7%；在USB连接器插入与主板排线插入的真实机器人实验中，AUC分别较最强基线提升108.9%与46.7%。

Conclusion: 跨token与跨层路由的任务相关动作特征提取能有效改善冻结VLA适配下游任务时的状态表示质量，显著提升在线RL的样本效率，并在真实机器人精细操作任务中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide strong behavioral priors for robotic manipulation, yet efficiently adapting them to downstream tasks remains challenging. Recent work addresses this challenge by adapting frozen VLAs through online reinforcement learning (RL), whose sample efficiency depends on the quality of the state representation used by the actor and critic. Existing methods construct such representations either with VLA-independent visual encoders or through fixed compression of internal VLA representations. Neither design explicitly extracts the task-specific action-relevant VLA features most useful for downstream action refinement and action-value estimation, therefore limiting sample efficiency. To address this limitation, we introduce eRLT, which constructs an effective state representation by routing task-specific action-relevant information across both tokens and layers of the frozen VLA. Specifically, learned routing tokens dynamically aggregate visual-language features at multiple depths, while a lightweight layer router combines these summaries into a fixed-dimensional RL token. The routing module is initialized using expert demonstrations to capture features predictive of expert actions and then refined using critic feedback from online interactions for action-value estimation. Across seven LIBERO and RoboTwin tasks, eRLT improves mean normalized learning-curve AUC by up to 23.7% over representative baselines. Real-robot experiments on USB connector insertion and motherboard ribbon-cable insertion further show AUC improvements of 108.9% and 46.7%, respectively, over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [44] [A Survey on End-to-End Autonomous Driving Training from the Perspectives of Data, Strategy, and Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00926)
*Chengkai Xu,Yiming Cui,Jiaqi Liu,Yicheng Guo,Cheng Qin,Geyuan Zhang,Xinwei Dong,Shiyu Fang,Peng Hang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了端到端自动驾驶（E2E-AD）的训练方法与生态，提出"数据-策略-平台"三层分类体系，系统梳理数据管线、学习范式与训练基础设施的进展及其相互作用，并展望从数据数量到数据价值、从孤立优化到基座驱动泛化、从静态训练到闭环训练-测试的未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端学习已成为自动驾驶的变革性范式，通过统一可微模型直接将多模态感知输入映射为驾驶动作；然而其有效性最终取决于训练生态的质量。目前缺乏对E2E-AD训练方法与生态的系统性综述，亟需从系统视角整合数据、策略与平台层面的研究。

Method: 采用综述方法，提出"数据-策略-平台"（Data-Strategy-Platform）分类体系，将训练视为相互依赖的系统：数据层界定可学习的内容，策略层决定学习如何对齐驾驶目标，平台层支撑可扩展性与持续演进。在此框架下，调研数据中心管线、学习范式与训练基础设施的研究进展，并分析三者在塑造模型性能、鲁棒性与可部署性方面的相互作用。

Result: 在数据-策略-平台框架下系统梳理了E2E-AD训练生态的代表性工作，分析了各层要素的相互依赖关系及其对模型性能、鲁棒性与可部署性的影响，并建立持续更新的文献与工作追踪仓库。

Conclusion: 反思现有局限并提出前瞻愿景：从数据数量转向数据价值，从孤立优化转向基座驱动泛化，从静态训练转向训练-测试一体化闭环，以迈向鲁棒、可扩展且可信的自动驾驶系统。

Abstract: Autonomous driving is a cornerstone technology for the future of intelligent transportation, where end-to-end learning has emerged as a transformative paradigm that directly maps multimodal sensory inputs to driving actions through unified differentiable models. While offering advantages, the effectiveness of end-to-end autonomous driving (E2E-AD) is ultimately determined by the quality of its training ecosystem. This paper provides a comprehensive review of training methods and ecosystem for E2E-AD. We introduce a Data-Strategy-Platform taxonomy that conceptualizes training as an interdependent system. The data layer defines what can be learned, the strategy layer governs how learning aligns with driving objectives, and the platform layer supports scalability and continuous evolution. Within this framework, we survey recent advances across data-centric pipelines, learning paradigms, and training infrastructures, and analyze their interplay in shaping model performance, robustness, and deployability. Finally, we reflect on current limitations and articulate a forward-looking vision that emphasizes a shift from data quantity to data value, from isolated optimization to foundation-driven generalization, and from static training to integrated training-testing loops, aiming toward robust, scalable, and trustworthy autonomous driving systems. We maintain a continuously updated repository tracking cutting-edge literature and works at \href{https://github.com/Jiaaqiliu/Awesome-Training-Ecosystem-for-E2E-AD}{Our Project Page}.

</details>


### [45] [ShowerFlex: Achieving Pseudo-Static Balancing in a Continuum Shower Hose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00936)
*Zhiyu Ren,Girish Krishnan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于辅助老年人淋浴的高关节度、伪静平衡连续体机构，结合摩擦锁定球窝关节与弹簧卷收器实现重力补偿，通过递归正运动学、静力平衡分析、蒙特卡洛仿真与物理实验验证其"推即停"的稳定性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球人口快速老龄化，维持日常生活活动（ADL）尤其是淋浴时的独立性成为关键挑战。现有坐姿淋浴者只能依赖刚性顶置花洒或需持续握持的手持软管，操作不便且增加跌倒风险。因此需要一种低致动成本、本质安全的辅助淋浴机构。

Method: 1）提出模块化连续体架构：由摩擦锁定球窝关节（loc-line）构成，并集成可伸缩弹簧卷收器实现有效重力补偿；2）建立递归正运动学框架构建几何模型；3）开展完整静力平衡分析，评估51自由度结构的保持能力；4）采用蒙特卡洛方法进行数值仿真，验证标准浴室空间内各种极端姿态下的形态适应性与稳定性；5）通过物理实验验证原型性能。

Result: 仿真验证了系统在标准浴室内多种极端位置下的形态适应性与稳定性；物理实验证实原型具备抗重力的内在静态稳定性，且致动所需力保持在老年人人体工效能力范围内；实现了低致动力的"推即停"交互，无需外部电子动力。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种低成本、本质安全的淋浴喷头设计，减少体力负担，帮助恢复个人卫生中的尊严与自主性。

Abstract: With the global population rapidly aging, maintaining independence in Activities of Daily Living (ADLs), particularly bathing or showering, has become a critical challenge. Older adults who sit while showering currently have limited options, often relying on rigid overhead showerheads or flexible hand-held hoses that require continuous gripping and precarious user maneuvers, increasing the risk of falls. To address this need, this paper introduces a highly articulated, pseudo-static balanced continuum mechanism designed specifically for accessible bathing assistance. The proposed mechanism features a modular continuum architecture composed of friction-locked ball-and-socket joints (loc-line), seamlessly integrated with a retractable spring-loaded reel for effective gravity compensation. This hybrid design achieves an intuitive "Push-and-Stay" interaction logic, maintaining an approximate static equilibrium with ergonomically low actuation force, without any external electronic power, while significantly minimizing the force needed for repositioning. Theoretically, we established a recursive forward kinematics framework to construct the geometric model of the structure, coupled with a comprehensive static equilibrium analysis to evaluate the system's holding capacity across its 51-DOF structure. Numerical simulations utilizing Monte Carlo methods validate the system's morphological adaptability and stability at various extreme positions within a standard bath space. Physical experiments further validate the prototype's real-world performance, confirming its intrinsic static stability against gravity and ensuring that the actuation force remains well within the ergonomic capabilities of older adults. Ultimately, this research provides a low-cost, intrinsically safe showerhead design that reduces physical strain, restoring dignity and autonomy in personal hygiene.

</details>


### [46] [NarrativeFlow: Flow-Based Vision-Language-Action Model Using Robot Velocity Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00981)
*Shota Kobayashi,Koki Seno,Daichi Yashima,Komei Sugiura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 NarrativeFlow，将语言条件下的机器人操作建模为连续速度场（flow matching），实现跨本体的运动中心表示并提升操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言条件下的操作对实用机器人系统至关重要，但机器人基础模型的扩展受限于特定本体的数据采集成本。跨多平台数据复用需要与本体无关、以运动为中心的表示，即机器人流（速度场）。现有方法要么用稀疏关键点位移粗略近似机器人流，要么无法处理语言条件下的操作。

Method: NarrativeFlow 以语言为条件，采用 flow matching 公式将机器人流建模为连续速度场，从而生成与真实操作物理一致的机器人流。在标准语言条件操作数据集上进行实验，并通过真实世界实验在多个操作任务上验证。

Result: 在标准语言条件操作数据集上，NarrativeFlow 在标准评测指标上优于代表性基线方法；真实世界实验中，在多个操作任务上取得比基线更高的成功率。

Conclusion: 将机器人流建模为语言条件下的连续速度场可行且有效，可作为跨本体、运动中心的表示，提升语言条件操作在仿真与真实场景中的性能。

Abstract: We focus on language-conditioned flow-based manipulation, where robot flows (robot velocity fields) serve as embodiment-agnostic, motion-centric representations for leveraging data collected from multiple robot platforms. This task is crucial because language-conditioned manipulation is essential for practical robotic systems, yet scaling robot foundation models remains limited by the labor-intensive collection of embodiment-specific data. Existing methods either coarsely approximate robot flows with sparse keypoint displacements, or cannot handle language-conditioned manipulation. To address this limitation, we propose NarrativeFlow, which models robot flows as continuous velocity fields using a flow-matching formulation conditioned on language. Accordingly, NarrativeFlow generates robot flows that are physically consistent with real-world manipulation. To validate NarrativeFlow, we have conducted experiments on standard datasets for language-conditioned manipulation. The experimental results show that NarrativeFlow outperforms representative baseline methods on standard evaluation metrics. Furthermore, through real-world experiments, we show that NarrativeFlow achieves higher success rates than baseline methods across multiple manipulation tasks. The project page is available at https://shota0520.github.io/NarrativeFlow-project-page/

</details>


### [47] [Divide-and-Remember: Recursive Action-Relevant Memory for Long-Horizon VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00982)
*Xuehui Yu,Eason Yu,Meiyi Wang,Haozhe Du,Stefano V. Albrecht,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Divide-and-Remember (D&R) 递归记忆方法，以信息论视角将"记住什么"建模为优化问题，在长时程操作任务上以64 token预算取得一致最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在历史依赖的操作任务上表现不佳，仅凭当前观测无法确定动作。已有记忆方法（如保留像素变化大的帧）依赖人工设计，跨任务增益不稳定，因此需要将"记住什么"从设计问题转化为优化问题。

Method: 基于模仿学习的 POMDP 形式化，推导出最优记忆应最大化给定当前观测下动作与记忆的条件互信息 I(a_t; m_t | o_t)，即保留历史中当前观测未包含的动作相关信息。据此提出 D&R：学习记忆函数 m_t = M(h_t)，含两项策略——(1) 将全历史上的选择递归分解为对 2K 个 token 做 top-K 选择的子问题，使固定大小的轻量选择器可端到端训练并支持无界历史；(2) 所有递归块共享同一选择器，捕捉各块共有的选择规则并保持高效。

Result: 在 RoboMME（16 个长时程操作任务，需记忆何时、何地、做什么、如何行动）上，D&R 在仅 64 token 预算下于四个套件均取得一致增益并达到最优平均成功率；真机实验同样有效。

Conclusion: 将记忆内容选择建模为最大化条件互信息的优化问题，并通过递归共享选择器的 D&R 实现长上下文、低计算开销的记忆，在仿真与真机上验证了方法的有效性与一致性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models struggle on history-dependent manipulation tasks, where the current observation alone does not determine the action, and the policy needs a memory of the history. Existing memory methods decide what to remember by design, for example, keeping frames with large pixel changes, and show inconsistent gains across tasks. We view what to remember as an optimisation problem. From the POMDP formulation of imitation learning, we show that the optimal memory maximises the conditional mutual information $I(a_t; m_t \mid o_t)$ between the action and the memory given the current observation. Intuitively, this means preserving the action-relevant information in the history that is not already contained in the current observation. Based on our analysis, we propose Divide-and-Remember (D&R), a recursive memory method that learns a memory function $m_t = M(h_t)$ and scales to long contexts while staying compute-light. It involves two strategies: (1) the selection over the full history is divided recursively into subproblems of top-$K$ selection over $2K$ tokens, so that fixed-size, lightweight selectors learned end-to-end support an unbounded history; (2) all recursion blocks share one selector, which captures the selection rule common to every block and keeps the method efficient. On RoboMME, a benchmark of 16 long-horizon manipulation tasks that require remembering when, where, what, and how to act, D&R achieves a state-of-the-art average success rate with consistent gains across all four suites under a budget of only 64 tokens; real-robot experiments show the same gain. Code, checkpoints and more results are at https://dnr-memory.github.io/

</details>


### [48] [Online Planning for Sparse Ground Target Search from a High-Altitude UAV under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01067)
*Ashik E Rasul,Hyung-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将无人机利用PTZ相机进行地面目标序列化探索建模为POMDP，并用POMCP求解，结合基于目标尺度的感知可靠性与选择性集成检测，在仿真中实现变尺度目标检测，步骤更少且对传感器分辨率依赖小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高空无人机搜索地面目标时，目标虽在视场内但因表观尺度小而实际不可观测；分辨率下采样和上下文受限导致常规检测器性能差。主动目标搜索可通过调整位姿获取更丰富视觉信息，但城市空域飞行法规常限制无人机物理移动，因此需要替代性的主动感知方案。

Method: 将利用PTZ机制的序列化探索形式化为POMDP，智能体维护对目标真实位置的信念状态；采用POMCP求解，将感知可靠性以目标物体尺度为条件进行建模，并在识别出候选位置后引入选择性集成检测作为额外推理步骤，在固定尺度下进行更精细推理。

Result: 在照片级仿真器中、不同环境条件与载具状态下进行实验验证，方法能够在变尺度条件下检测地面目标，相比基线方法所需步骤显著更少，且对传感器分辨率的依赖最小。

Conclusion: 将PTZ主动感知与POMDP/POMCP规划及尺度条件化感知、选择性集成检测相结合，可在不依赖物理移动的前提下有效解决高空小尺度地面目标检测问题，并降低对传感器分辨率的依赖。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) searching for ground targets from a high altitude face a unique challenge, particularly when the target is already within the field of view but effectively unobservable because of its small apparent scale. Standard object detectors often underperform in such scenarios because of resolution downscaling and limited context. In contrast, active object search frameworks address this challenge by directing the agent to a suitable pose to gather richer visual information. However, flight regulations in urban airspace often restrict such physical movements for UAVs. As an alternative active sensing approach, the UAV can leverage the pan-tilt-zoom (PTZ) mechanism of the onboard camera to dynamically adjust its field of view and sequentially gather enhanced visual information from specific regions of interest. Once the candidate locations of the target are identified, it can deploy more expensive object detection schemes, such as an ensemble of multiple models, to get better reasoning at a fixed scale. In this work, we formulate the sequential exploration with PTZ operation as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), in which the agent maintains a belief state over the target's true location. To solve the POMDP, we deploy partially observable Monte Carlo planning (POMCP), where we condition the sensing reliability on target object scale and deploy selective ensemble detection as an additional reasoning step.
  We validate our methodology with experiments in a photorealistic simulator under different environmental conditions and vehicle states, showing detection of ground targets at variable scales with significantly fewer steps and minimal dependence on sensor resolution compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [49] [quARtet Marker: A 3D-Printable Multi-Tag Fiducial for Robust Near-Frontal Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01072)
*Araki Wakiuchi,Hikaru Sasaki,Takamitsu Matsubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 quARtet 三维可打印基准标记：将多个 AprilTag 倾斜布置于紧凑平面内，使正面视角下各标签仍处于非正视角，从而提升机器人物体定位与抓取的姿态估计精度，同时以三种布局权衡估计一致性与可抓取性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明或反光实验器皿难以识别与定位；平面编码基准标记虽易打印、不破坏可抓取平面，但单一平面标记在近正视角下透视线索消失、位姿估计最不可靠；非平面几何虽恢复透视线索，却侵占平行夹爪所需的平坦接触面。

Method: 设计 quARtet 标记：在单一紧凑占位内倾斜布置四个 AprilTag，任一标签在标记正对相机时仍以非正视角被观测；将全部检测角点纳入同一次 PnP 求解，并用统一配置同时定义制造几何与检测器模型；由于倾斜占用平坦面积，给出三种布局以权衡位姿估计一致性与可抓取性。

Result: 在机器人参考系、同装置固定相机实验中，三种布局将正面朝向平均误差由单一平面标记的 2.18° 降至 0.24–0.47°，位置 RMSE 由 1.50 mm 降至 0.17–0.20 mm；机载相机闭环视觉反馈的位姿保持测试验证了该差异；在相同条件下的下摆抓取试验中，带平坦接触条带的两种布局抓取滑移约 2 mm，而无平坦条带布局滑移约 100 mm。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下，结果支持一条选择规则：当位姿估计一致性优先时，选用无平坦条带的布局；当标记面需保持可抓取时，选用带平坦条带的布局。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation of labware is difficult when transparent or reflective objects must be identified and localized. Coded planar fiducials are a practical retrofit: easy to print, they leave the marked face flat and graspable. Yet a single planar tag is least reliable in near-frontal views, where perspective cues fade. Non-planar geometries restore those cues but intrude on the flat face that a parallel-jaw gripper must contact. Our idea is to tilt multiple tags within one compact footprint, so that each tag is seen at a non-frontal angle even when the marker faces the camera. We propose the quARtet marker, a 3D-printable fiducial embodying this idea: all detected corners of its four tilted AprilTags enter one Perspective-n-Point solve, and a shared configuration defines the fabricated geometry and the detector model. Because tilting consumes flat area, its three layouts trade pose-estimation consistency against graspability. In robot-referenced, same-setup fixed-camera experiments, all three layouts reduced the mean frontal orientation error from 2.18 degree for a single planar tag to 0.24-0.47 degree and the root-mean-square position error from 1.50 to 0.17-0.20 mm. A robot-mounted-camera pose-hold test confirmed this separation under closed-loop visual feedback. In swing-down trials under identical conditions, the two layouts with flat contact strips retained the object with about 2 mm of in-grasp slip, whereas the layout without flat strips slipped by roughly 100 mm. For the tested conditions, the results support a rule: the layout without flat strips when pose-estimation consistency dominates, a layout with flat strips when the marked face must remain graspable.

</details>


### [50] [WBAG: A Whole-Body and Attached-Geometry Safety Framework for Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01083)
*Samuel Zhen,Siwon Jo,Yanze Zhang,Wenhao Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WBAG：面向VLA策略的全身与抓取相关几何安全框架，在SafeLIBERO上取得97.38%场景安全与59.38%安全成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA推理期安全框架多采用以末端执行器为中心的简化表示，未显式建模完整关节式机器人及所抓取物体的几何，导致机器人在真实部署中仍面临机器人各部位、被操作物体与周围环境之间的碰撞风险。

Method: WBAG建模机器人全身与依赖抓取状态的附着几何：构建随抓取状态自适应的“抓取条件安全集”，将演化几何转化为可微CBF约束，在此基础上对VLA原生的六维操作空间动作做最小修改以实现避碰。

Result: 在SafeLIBERO（LIBERO的安全增强变体，含障碍物）上，采用监控所有非任务物体的场景级安全评估器，WBAG在总体安全性与安全任务成功率上均优于所评估方法，达到97.38%的聚合场景安全率与59.38%的安全成功率。

Conclusion: 显式建模全身与抓取相关几何并结合可微CBF约束，可在仅小幅修改VLA原生动作的前提下显著提升机器人在含障碍场景中的安全性与安全任务完成率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have demonstrated impressive capabilities in generalizable robotic manipulation, but their deployment in the real world remains challenging due to potential collisions involving different parts of the robot, manipulated objects, and the surrounding environment. Existing inference-time VLA safety frameworks typically rely on simplified end-effector-centered representations that do not explicitly model the full articulated robot and attached-object geometry. In this paper, we present WBAG, a safety framework that models the robot's whole-body and grasp-dependent attached geometry. WBAG constructs a grasp-conditioned safe set that adapts the protected geometry as objects are grasped, then converts this evolving geometry into differentiable CBF constraints that minimally modify the VLA's native six-dimensional operational-space action for collision avoidance across robot, scene, and attached geometry. On the SafeLIBERO benchmark, a variant of LIBERO augmented with obstacles for safety evaluation, WBAG achieves the best overall safety and safe task success among the evaluated methods under a scene-level safety evaluator that monitors all eligible non-task objects, reaching 97.38\% aggregate Scene Safety and 59.38\% Safe Success.

</details>


### [51] [OrbitTAMP: Grounding Language Models for Task and Motion Planning in Spacecraft Rendezvous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01093)
*Yuji Takubo,Daniele Gammelli,Marco Pavone,Simone D'Amico*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层框架，将LLM的语义推理与可复用行为图及领域规划模块相结合，用于航天器RPO的TAMP，实现自然语言到动态可行轨迹的转化，意图恢复率达98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器交会与近距操作（RPO）目前依赖专家密集型流程，工程师需将高层操作意图转化为安全且动态可行的轨迹，形成规模化瓶颈。LLM智能体可提供直观接口，但其输出本身并不基于轨道动力学、操作约束或可行机动结构。

Method: 提出分层TAMP框架，将LLM推理锚定在可复用行为图与领域特定规划模块上。预训练LLM将自然语言指令映射为部分任务规范，规划器在可行操作空间内补全未定决策，再通过轨迹优化生成动态可行轨迹。

Result: 数值实验表明该架构显著提升意图恢复能力，在主流LLM支持下所有评测划分的部分任务规范精确恢复率达98%。测试时计算实验显示，对紧凑9B模型，验证器引导的修订将精确恢复率从75%提升至88%；更广的行为规划搜索可独立改善可行轨迹实现的选择。

Conclusion: 该结果为语言驱动的航天器RPO智能体规划建立了可扩展且可审计的基础。

Abstract: Spacecraft rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) are currently planned through an expertise-intensive process in which engineers translate high-level operational intent into safe, dynamically feasible trajectories, creating a bottleneck to scalable operations. Large language model (LLM)-based agents could offer an intuitive interface for this process, although their outputs are not inherently grounded in orbital dynamics, operational constraints, or the structure of admissible spacecraft maneuvers. To exploit their semantic reasoning while ensuring the generated plan's physical validity, this paper presents a hierarchical framework for spacecraft task-and-motion planning (TAMP) that grounds LLM reasoning in a graph of reusable behaviors and domain-specific planning modules. Within this framework, a pretrained LLM maps a natural-language command to a partial mission specification. The associated planners then resolve unspecified decisions within the admissible operational space. Finally, trajectory optimization converts the completed mission specification into a dynamically feasible trajectory. Numerical experiments demonstrate that this architecture substantially improves intent recovery over direct LLM generation, achieving 98% exact recovery of partial mission specifications across all evaluated splits when backed by frontier LLMs. Additional test-time-compute experiments show that, for a compact 9B model, verifier-guided revision increases exact recovery from 75% to 88%, while broader behavior-plan search independently improves selection among admissible trajectory realizations. Overall, these results establish a scalable and auditable foundation for language-driven agentic planning of spacecraft RPO.

</details>


### [52] [MASkillBlender: Decentralized Whole-Body Coordination for Multi-Humanoid Loco-Manipulation via Skill Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01102)
*Yifan Hu,Luhang Hong,Mingkang Long,Danning Wang,Chengfeng Jia,Rong Su,Junjie Fu,Guanghui Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MASkillBlender——通用多智能体强化学习框架，通过可复用预训练单体技能上的共享分散高层策略，实现去中心化多类人机器人全身协调，仅需任务级奖励；并引入基于置换的数据增强及理论证明提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多类人机器人协同移动-操作前景广阔但极具挑战：全身控制维度高、决策需去中心化、可扩展性差。现有强化学习虽提升了单体全身控制，但扩展到多体设定仍困难，常需大量奖励工程或任务定制设计。

Method: 1) MASkillBlender框架：在多体设定下学习一个共享的、去中心化的高层策略，底层为可复用的预训练单体技能，从而仅用任务级奖励、无需任务特定运动参考即可涌现协调行为；2) 面向同构多类人系统的基于置换的数据增强策略，并在同构马尔可夫博弈形式化下从理论上证明置换样本保持原样本的策略梯度方向。

Result: 在两种类人机器人本体上的多项多类人协调任务中评估，仿真结果表明该框架一致地取得强任务性能，并能跨不同任务与本体的实现协调行为。

Conclusion: MASkillBlender为去中心化多类人全身协调提供了通用、免任务定制奖励设计的方案，结合置换数据增强提升样本效率，验证了跨任务与跨本体的协调能力。

Abstract: Coordinated multi-humanoid loco-manipulation is promising yet challenging due to high-dimensional whole-body control, decentralized decision making, and scalability. While recent reinforcement learning methods have improved single-humanoid whole-body control, extending them to the multi-humanoid setting remains nontrivial and often requires substantial reward engineering or task-specific design. We propose MASkillBlender, a general multi-agent reinforcement learning framework to achieve decentralized multi-humanoid whole-body coordination. By learning a shared decentralized high-level policy over reusable pre-trained single-humanoid skills, MASkillBlender enables coordinated behaviors using only task-level rewards, without requiring task-specific motion references. To improve learning efficiency, we further introduce a permutation-based data augmentation strategy for homogeneous multi-humanoid systems, and theoretically show that the permuted samples preserve the policy-gradient direction of the original samples under the homogeneous Markov game formulation. We evaluate MASkillBlender on multiple multi-humanoid coordination tasks across two humanoid embodiments. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently achieves strong task performance and enables coordinated behaviors across different tasks and humanoid embodiments.

</details>


### [53] [Extreme Length Generalization in a Compact Recurrent Architecture for One-Shot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01105)
*Izen Thornton,Aaron Shey,William Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FRANK，一个507K参数的循环架构（tau门控循环模块+内容可寻址记忆+前馈反射通路），在算法序列任务上训练长度5-20、外推至两百万token，10个种子中6个保持100.0%准确率，而50个基线配置无一做到；消融显示四种任务依赖分布，仿真策略可迁移到实体地面车辆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人单次任务的时间跨度远超训练时所见轨迹，且机载算力预算固定，现有循环、状态空间及模块化架构在长时外推上难以维持可靠性能。作者希望解决"训练短、运行极长"这一长度外推难题，并在真实平台上验证。

Method: 提出FRANK（507K参数）循环架构，整合tau门控循环模块、内容可寻址记忆与前馈反射通路。在四个算法序列任务上，与参数规模匹配的循环、状态空间及精简模块化基线对比；训练长度5-20，评测外推至两百万token。开展跨任务的靶向损伤实验以刻画组件依赖；并将仿真训练的策略部署到实体地面车辆上执行避障航点导航。

Result: 在最大训练长度的100,000倍处，10个FRANK种子中有6个保持恰好100.0%准确率，50个基线配置（五种架构各十种子，无缺失）无一达到该水平（Fisher精确检验，双侧p=4.2E-6）。四任务靶向损伤得到四种不同的组件依赖分布，说明循环、记忆与反射通路的任务相关分配。仿真训练的FRANK策略无需遥操作即可驱动实体地面车辆穿越障碍抵达指定航点。

Conclusion: FRANK以极小参数预算实现了极端长时外推下的稳定高精度，且其循环/记忆/反射三通路可按任务动态分工；策略可零遥操作地从仿真迁移到真实车辆，表明该架构适用于长时程自主机器人任务。

Abstract: Autonomous robots on one-shot missions run over horizons far longer than the trajectories seen during training, under a fixed onboard compute budget. We present FRANK, a 507K-parameter recurrent architecture that combines tau-gated recurrent modules, content-addressable memory, and a feedforward reflex pathway. We evaluate it against recurrent, state-space, and reduced modular baselines at matched parameter count on four algorithmic sequence tasks, trained at length 5-20 and evaluated out to two million tokens. At 100,000x the maximum training length, 6 of 10 FRANK seeds retain exactly 100.0% accuracy, while none of the 50 baseline configurations does, five architectures at ten seeds each with none left incomplete (Fisher exact, two-sided p = 4.2E-6. Targeted lesions across the four tasks yield four distinct component-reliance profiles, consistent with task-dependent allocation across the recurrent, memory, and reflex pathways. Separately, a FRANK policy trained in simulation drives a physical ground vehicle to commanded waypoints through obstacles without teleoperation.

</details>


### [54] [Experience-Based Feasibility-Aware Generative Adversarial Imitation from Observation under Embodiment Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01171)
*Yoshiki Takebayashi,Giovanni Perantoni,Hikaru Sasaki,Matteo Saveriano,Takamitsu Matsubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EF-GAIfO方法，从机器人自身经验估计仅状态演示的可行性，并让可行域随策略学习动态扩展，实现自适应可行性感知的观察模仿学习，在仿真运动任务与真实四足机器人抓取任务上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无动作标签的演示接口日益普及，从观察中模仿成为学习机器人行为的重要途径。但人与机器人在形态与动力学上存在差异，人类演示的运动对机器人可能不可行，直接使用会降低策略性能。现有方法多依赖显式动力学模型或大规模先验探索数据集来判定可行性，成本高且难以适应学习过程。

Method: 提出基于经验的可行性感知生成对抗观察模仿（EF-GAIfO）：利用机器人自身经验而非显式动力学模型或大规模先验探索数据来估计仅状态演示的可行性。其关键特性是可行性概念随策略学习演化——策略改进、状态转移覆盖范围扩大时，可行域逐步扩展，从而纳入更多演示；实现随学习阶段自适应的可行性感知模仿，而非依赖预设的可行性判据。

Result: 在仿真中的运动任务以及真实四足机器人执行物体接近与抓取任务上验证了EF-GAIfO的有效性。

Conclusion: EF-GAIfO通过从自身经验动态估计并扩展可行域，避免了显式动力学建模和大规模预探索，使观察模仿能够随策略学习阶段自适应地利用演示数据，在仿真与真实机器人任务中均表现有效。

Abstract: With the increasing use of robot-free demonstration interfaces that provide state trajectories without action labels, imitation from observation has become a promising approach for learning robot behaviors from human demonstrations. However, due to differences in embodiment and dynamics between humans and robots, demonstrated human motions may not be feasible for the robot, potentially degrading policy performance. In this study, we propose Experience-Based Feasibility-Aware Generative Adversarial Imitation from Observation (EF-GAIfO), which estimates the feasibility of state-only demonstrations from the robot's own experience rather than relying on explicit dynamics models or large prior exploration datasets. A key feature of EF-GAIfO is that the notion of feasibility evolves with policy learning: as the policy improves and the robot experiences a broader range of state transitions, the feasible region is progressively expanded, allowing additional demonstrations to be incorporated into learning. This enables feasibility-aware imitation that adapts to the current stage of policy learning, rather than relying on a pre-designed feasibility criterion. We validate the effectiveness of EF-GAIfO on a locomotion task in simulation and on a real quadruped robot performing a object-reaching-and-grasping task.

</details>


### [55] [Recova: Agent-Guided Failure Recovery for Autonomous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01178)
*Isabella Liu,An-Chieh Cheng,Johan Bjorck,Zhiding Yu,Hongxu Yin,Jan Kautz,Linxi Fan,Yuke Zhu,Sifei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Recova：智能体引导的"孪生内探索+真机验证"框架，将操作失败转化为可复用恢复能力，显著提升鲁棒性并逐步消除人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作失败会使场景进入任务策略无法恢复的状态，而学习纠错行为依赖可扩展的失败探索与物理落地；现有方法难以兼顾失败探索的规模性与真机物理有效性。

Method: 在重建的数字孪生中由智能体诊断失败、测试纠正程序，并分别收集任务与恢复的成功轨迹以训练独立策略；部署阶段监控进度、调用学习式或程序化恢复、验证场景恢复后继续执行。无可用恢复时由人类示范解决并接入学习回路；真机轨迹与人类示范按类型路由至对应策略进行 DAgger 训练。

Result: 六个 LIBERO-Pro 设置与四类 MolmoSpaces 类别上平均成功率 78.8% 与 64.9%，最强基线为 71.7% 与 38.0%；四台真机工作站并行采集下，DAgger 微调将平均成功率从 23.8% 提升至 77.5%，恢复技能进一步提升至 87.5%；单任务四轮采集中人工干预率由 87.5% 降至 0%。

Conclusion: 智能体引导的恢复机制可将失败转化为可复用能力，在提升鲁棒性的同时渐进式减少人工干预。

Abstract: Manipulation failures can leave scenes in states from which a task policy cannot recover. Learning corrective behaviors requires scalable failure exploration and physical grounding. We present Recova, an agent-guided framework that jointly develops task execution and recovery in a reconstructed digital twin, then verifies and refines both through real-world experience. In the twin, the agent diagnoses failures, tests corrective programs, and collects successful task and recovery rollouts for separate policies. During deployment, it monitors progress, invokes a learned or programmatic recovery, verifies scene restoration, and resumes execution. When no suitable recovery is available, a human demonstration resolves the failure and enters the learning loop, allowing the system to expand its recovery capabilities. Physical rollouts and human demonstrations are routed to the corresponding policy for DAgger training. Across six LIBERO-Pro settings and four MolmoSpaces categories, Recova achieves 78.8% and 64.9% mean success, compared with 71.7% and 38.0% for the strongest baselines. With parallel collection across four real-robot workstations, DAgger fine-tuning raises mean success from 23.8% to 77.5%, and recovery skills further raise it to 87.5%. Over four collection rounds on one task, observed human intervention falls from 87.5% to 0%. Together, these results show how agent-guided recovery turns failures into reusable capabilities, improving robustness while progressively reducing human intervention. Project page: https://www.liuisabella.com/Recova

</details>


### [56] [AFD-CAMLs: Agile Force-Distribution-Aware Planning and Control for Cable-Suspended Aerial Multi-Lifting Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01185)
*Antreas Kourris,Sihao Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合规划-控制框架，通过零空间探索与导纳滤波改善多无人机协同吊运中的绳索张力分布不均问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机协同吊运重载时需同时控制负载的位置与姿态。基于轨迹的方法灵活性高且满足系统约束，但当张力-力旋量分配存在冗余或病态时，尤其在几何失配和底层跟踪误差下，会产生不均匀的力分布。

Method: 采用混合规划与控制框架：全局规划器在给定内力设定下探索分配零空间，生成负载轨迹与绳索力参考；这些参考用于增强集中式局部规划器的代价函数，在生成所有无人机轨迹的同时促进可行的力分布；随后引入导纳滤波器，将规划力与机载绳索张力估计进行比较，并调整运动学参考，以在退化或近退化构型下改善力跟踪。

Result: 涉及4至10架无人机的仿真与实验表明，在悬停及高机动飞行中均实现了更均衡的张力分布，且未牺牲灵活性与负载跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 该框架有效缓解多无人机协同吊运中的张力分配不均问题，兼顾灵活性与跟踪性能。

Abstract: Multiple UAVs can cooperatively transport heavy payloads while controlling their position and orientation. Trajectory-based methods offer high agility while satisfying system constraints, but can produce uneven force distributions when the tension-to-wrench allocation is redundant or ill-conditioned, particularly under geometric mismatch and low-level tracking errors. We propose a hybrid planning-and-control framework to address this problem. A global planner generates payload trajectories and cable-force references by exploring the allocation null space under a prescribed internal-force setting. These references augment the cost of a centralized local planner, promoting feasible force distributions while generating trajectories for all UAVs. An admittance filter then compares the planned forces with onboard cable-tension estimates and adjusts the kinematic references to improve force tracking in degenerate or near-degenerate configurations. Simulations and experiments involving four to ten UAVs demonstrate more balanced tension distributions during both hovering and demanding agile maneuvers, without compromising agility or payload-tracking performance.

</details>


### [57] [EIDA: Execution-Interface Dynamics Adaptation for Real-to-Sim-to-Real Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01219)
*Yiwei Qian,Shanze Wang,Qingyuan Hu,Xinming Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出执行接口动力学适配（EIDA），用目标平台执行数据拟合速度指令下的运动与反馈响应，无需重建执行器动力学，即可提升导航策略的仿真到真机迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真到真机迁移时，速度指令产生的运动与反馈往往偏离策略训练时所建模的情况，导致迁移失败。传统做法依赖详细的执行器动力学建模，成本高且难以精确匹配策略所观测的反馈。

Method: EIDA 直接利用目标平台的执行数据拟合响应：一个模型预测机体坐标系下的位姿增量以更新仿真器几何，另一个模型预测策略所观测的速度反馈，并将短历史速度反馈作为策略输入；拟合模型嵌入轻量级 GPU 并行仿真器中。

Result: 在 Jackal 与 Go2 完整验证集上，拟合模型较仿真器预定义运动模型降低了位置与偏航预测误差；在另一基于物理的仿真器的 100 个导航基准环境中，EIDA 在有/无全局引导下均取得最高成功率与导航分数；反馈消融验证了匹配策略侧速度估计的必要性；物理 Unitree Go2 上 20 次静态场景试验全部无碰撞到达目标，基线仅 4/20。

Conclusion: 执行接口适配可在不进行详细执行器仿真的前提下改善导航迁移性能。

Abstract: Simulation-to-robot transfer can fail when velocity commands produce motion and feedback that differ from those modeled during policy training. We present execution-interface dynamics adaptation (EIDA), which fits these responses from target-platform execution data without reconstructing actuator dynamics. A model of body-frame pose increments updates simulator geometry, while a separate model predicts the velocity feedback observed by the policy; a short history of velocity feedback is included in the policy input. The fitted models are used within a lightweight GPU-parallel simulator. On the full Jackal and Go2 validation sets, the fitted models reduced position and yaw prediction errors relative to the simulator's predefined motion model. Across 100 benchmark navigation environments evaluated in a separate physics-based simulator, EIDA achieved the highest success rate and navigation score among the compared learned policies, both with and without global guidance. Feedback ablations further supported the need to match policy-facing velocity estimates. On a physical Unitree Go2, EIDA reached the goal without collision in all 20 static-scene trials, compared with 4 of 20 for the baseline. These results show that execution-interface adaptation can improve navigation transfer without detailed actuator simulation.

</details>


### [58] [ColoACT: Multi-Cue Action Chunking for Smooth Autonomous Colon Navigation on a Self-Propelled Endoscopic Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01258)
*Jian Hu,Shujing He,Leixin Chang,Zongze Li,Ding Huang,Chaoyang Shi,Chengzhi Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ColoACT自主结肠镜导航系统，结合RGB-D-E动作分块Transformer策略与自驱动内镜机器人BGER，在离体猪结肠中实现直线/弯曲段85.4%/72.5%成功率，并验证90°转弯与多弯道场景可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主结肠镜导航可降低操作者负担并减少肠袢形成或组织损伤风险，但面临可变形解剖结构、弱纹理与镜面高光的内镜视觉、以及接触丰富的黏弹性交互等挑战。既有方法中，几何驱动流程高效可解释但对人工特征与切换逻辑依赖强、鲁棒性差；学习型策略在弱纹理与高光下推断的深度/几何易出现时间不一致或过度平滑，基于仿真训练的方法（如深度强化学习）还存在仿真到现实的差距。

Method: 提出ColoACT：面向紧凑自驱动斜齿轮内镜机器人（BGER）的自主导航系统，集成基于RGB-D-E的动作分块Transformer策略（ColoACT策略）。该策略在RGB基础上引入估计的相对深度与基于梯度的伪高程图，以增强褶皱-脊线显著性和高频几何线索；通过预测重叠动作块并经时间集成融合，实现对BGER平滑连续控制。

Result: 在不同离体猪结肠（约60 cm）上，系统在直线段和弯曲段成功率分别为85.4%和72.5%；90度转弯成功率为70%，双弯序列为60%；并进一步在具有挑战性的三弯段中验证了可行性。

Conclusion: ColoACT通过融合RGB-D-E多模态几何增强与动作分块时间集成，缓解了弱纹理/高光下的几何不一致问题，在离体猪结肠多类复杂路径上取得较高成功率，展示了自主结肠镜导航的可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous colonoscopic navigation can reduce operator burden and the risk of loop formation or tissue trauma, but remains challenging due to deformable anatomy, weak-texture and specular endoscopic visuals, and contact-rich viscoelastic interactions. Existing methods either rely on geometry-driven pipelines, which are efficient and interpretable yet brittle due to manually engineered features and switching logic, or adopt learning-based policies, whose inferred depth/geometry can become temporally inconsistent or overly smooth under weak texture and specular highlights while simulation-trained variants (e.g., deep reinforcement learning) may further suffer from a sim-to-real gap. We propose ColoACT, an autonomous navigation system that integrates an RGB-D-E based Action Chunking Transformer policy (ColoACT policy) for a compact self-propelled Bevel-Gear-Based Endoscopic Robot (BGER). The ColoACT policy augments RGB with estimated relative depth and a gradient-based pseudo-elevation map to enhance fold-ridge saliency and other high-frequency geometric cues, and enables smooth continuous control of the BGER by predicting overlapping action chunks and fusing them via temporal ensembling. In different \textit{ex-vivo} porcine colons (approximately 60 cm), our system achieves success rates of 85.4\% and 72.5\% in straight and curved segments, respectively, and achieves 70\% success in 90-degree turns and 60\% in double-bend sequences, with feasibility further demonstrated in challenging triple-bend segments. The project page is available at: https://Adamhu1.github.io/ColoACT/.

</details>


### [59] [PROMO: Preference-conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01260)
*Amr Mousa,Rifny Rachman,Neil Karavis,Michele Caprio,Richard Allmendinger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PROMO提出一种偏好条件化的多目标强化学习方法，将步态权衡作为运行时输入交给单一策略，在仿真中实现广泛Pareto覆盖，并零样本迁移至Unitree Go2实机，显著改善能耗、位置误差与姿态偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足运动需平衡指令跟踪、稳定性与能效等相互冲突的目标，但传统强化学习在训练时将这些优先级固化为固定标量奖励函数，无法在部署阶段动态调整权衡，导致操作者意图被奖励塑形项掩盖。

Method: PROMO将权衡显式建模为运行时输入：策略以部署时的语义偏好为条件，同时保持与本体相关的运动先验固定，从而分离操作者意图与生成可行步态所需的奖励塑形项；训练单一策略而非独立专家。

Result: 在仿真中采样100个偏好，67种行为在精确Pareto支配下非受支配，偏好-目标平均相关性达0.843；同一策略零样本迁移到Unitree Go2，相对均衡偏好，偏好调整可分别将比能耗降低30.4%、位置误差降低38.7%、峰值机体姿态偏差降低59.0%。

Conclusion: 偏好条件化多目标强化学习可作为自适应腿式运动的实用运行时接口，将多目标RL的作用从离线Pareto集构建扩展到在线部署阶段。

Abstract: Quadrupedal locomotion requires balancing conflicting objectives such as command tracking, stability, and energy efficiency, yet conventional reinforcement learning (RL) hardcodes these priorities into a fixed scalar reward at training time. We present PROMO (Preference-Conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning), a semantic multi-objective approach that makes this trade-off an explicit runtime input to a single locomotion policy. PROMO conditions the policy on deployment facing preferences while keeping embodiment-specific locomotion priors fixed, thereby separating operator intent from reward shaping terms required for viable gait generation. Compared with fixed-objective controllers, multi-objective baselines, and independently trained specialists, PROMO achieves objective specialization and robustness from a single deployable policy. Across 100 sampled preferences in simulation, 67 behaviors are non-dominated under exact Pareto dominance, with a mean preference-objective correlation of 0.843, demonstrating broad Pareto coverage and predictable preference response. The same policy transfers zero-shot to a Unitree Go2, where preference changes alone reduce specific energy by up to 30.4%, position error by 38.7%, and peak body-attitude deviation by 59.0% relative to the balanced preference. These results establish preference-conditioned multi-objective RL as a practical runtime interface for adaptive legged locomotion, extending its role beyond offline Pareto-set construction. Open-source code and videos are available at https://amrmousa.com/promo/.

</details>


### [60] [Interaction-Stiffness-Guided Basis Allocation in Dynamic Movement Primitives for Efficient Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01288)
*Chan Xu,Silu Chen,Dehao Wang,Xiyu Chen,Dexin Jiang,Chi Zhang,Guilin Yang,Chenguang Yang,Zaojun Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出阶段关键性引导的动态运动基元（SC-DMPs），通过自适应基函数分配提升精度关键技能学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典DMPs的基函数布局固定，无法根据各阶段不同的精度需求分配逼近能力。

Method: 融合操作者-机器人交互刚度与跨示教任务空间变异性的轨迹一致性线索，构建阶段关键性指标；据此通过逆累积关键性在归一化时间上重新分布基中心并映射到规范相位域，同时细化带宽调整局部逼近支撑。

Result: 在手写轨迹和三个真实机器人任务上，推断的关键性集中于几何要求高和任务受约束区域；与DMPs、ProMPs、ProDMP、GP-MP、KMP相比，轨迹复现、端点泛化与任务关键精度更优，同时保持紧凑模型与经典DMP稳定结构。

Conclusion: SC-DMPs通过关键性引导的自适应基分配，在高关键阶段获得更密集灵活表示，实现精度关键技能学习的性能提升。

Abstract: Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) provide a compact and stable formulation for trajectory representation and generalization in robot skill learning. However, their predefined basis layout limits the allocation of approximation capacity according to stage-dependent precision requirements. To address this issue, this article proposes Stage-Criticality-Guided Dynamic Movement Primitives (SC-DMPs) with adaptive basis allocation for precision-critical skill learning. Operator-robot interaction stiffness and a trajectory-consistency cue derived from cross-demonstration task-space variability are integrated to construct a stage-criticality index. Guided by this index, basis centers are redistributed in normalized time through inverse cumulative criticality and mapped to the canonical phase domain, while their bandwidths are refined to adjust local approximation support. This enables denser and more flexible representation at high-criticality stages while retaining sparser allocation elsewhere. Experiments on handwriting trajectories and three real-robot tasks show that the inferred criticality is concentrated in geometrically demanding and task-constrained regions. Comparisons with DMPs, ProMPs, ProDMP, GP-MP, and KMP demonstrate improved trajectory reproduction, endpoint generalization, and task-critical accuracy while retaining a compact model and the stable structure of classical DMPs.

</details>


### [61] [Continual Learning for 6-DoF Grasp Synthesis via Experience and Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01301)
*Giulio Schiavi,Andrei Cramariuc,Michael Pantic,Roland Siegwart*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向平行夹爪、单视角6-DoF抓取合成的持续学习框架，通过嵌入空间记忆实现在线适应，无需微调大模型即可在新物体上持续提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取合成系统多为离线训练、部署后固定不变。当部署条件与训练数据相似时表现良好，但遇到未见过物体等新条件时性能下降，缺乏在线适应能力。

Method: 不微调大型参数化模型，而是在学习到的嵌入空间中通过记忆进行适应：抓取结果用于更新未来抓取评分；可选的用户示范被召回并迁移到新场景中作为额外候选抓取。

Result: 在仿真和超过1500次真实抓取试验中评估。在适应前即匹配现有6-DoF抓取基线；对训练中缺失或欠代表类别的新物体可在线提升；支持有限遗忘的长时程持续学习。真实实验中，仅50次在线抓取后，多个困难类别成功率超过90%。

Conclusion: 基于嵌入空间记忆的持续学习框架可在不微调大模型的前提下实现在线适应，兼顾基线性能与对未见物体的持续改进，并具备可扩展、低遗忘的长时程学习能力。

Abstract: Most current grasp synthesis systems are trained offline and remain fixed during deployment. While this works well when deployment conditions resemble the training data, performance can degrade when robots encounter conditions they have not seen before, such as unfamiliar objects. In this work, we present a continual-learning framework for single-view 6-DoF grasp synthesis for a parallel-jaw gripper in cluttered scenes. Rather than finetuning a large parametric model, our method adapts through memory in a learned embedding space: grasp outcomes update future grasp scores, while optional user demonstrations are recalled and transferred to new scenes as additional candidate grasps. We evaluate our method in simulation and in extensive real-world experiments comprising over 1500 grasp trials. We show that our method matches the performance of existing 6-DoF grasping baselines even before adaptation, improves online on unseen objects from categories absent or underrepresented during training, and supports long-horizon continual learning with limited forgetting. In real-world experiments, our method reaches over 90\% success rates on several challenging object categories after only 50 online grasp attempts. Videos and code at https://giuschio.github.io/cl_grasping/.

</details>


### [62] [Ex vivo breach detection using electrical conductivity during robotic pedicle drilling in the spine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01310)
*Jorge Andrés Pérez Velásquez,François Teyssere,Thibault Chandanson,Quentin Grimal,Brahim Tamadazte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合SpineGuard本体感知电阻抗传感器的机器人椎弓根钻孔方案，通过实时分析电阻抗信号检测电导率突变，实现穿孔的实时预防，无需外部设备或传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊柱侧弯手术中椎弓根螺钉置入技术要求高：视野受限、解剖变异大、并发症风险高，对精度要求极高。现有机器人系统虽能辅助基于CT的规划与执行，但仍依赖术中电离辐射成像和复杂配准，因此需要一种无辐射、无需复杂配准的实时穿孔预防方法。

Method: 将机器人钻孔方式与配备SpineGuard本体感知电阻抗传感器的椎弓根钻具相结合；设计实时检测算法，在钻孔过程中分析电阻抗信号，识别与潜在向椎管穿孔相关的电导率突变；该方法仅依赖工具自身信号，不使用外部设备或传感器。

Result: 离体实验表明，在51例钻孔案例中该方法全部避免了穿孔（摘要中"100 of the 51"为笔误，正文后句明确为全部51例），证明系统能在钻孔过程中检测到潜在危险事件并及时中止操作。

Conclusion: 本工作证明了结合电阻抗传感的机器人椎弓根钻孔实现实时穿孔预防的可行性；仅使用工具信号即可免除对外部传感系统的需求，有助于更安全地完成椎弓根螺钉置入。

Abstract: Purpose: Pedicle screw placement is technically demanding in scoliosis treatment. High precision is required due to limited visibility, anatomical variability, and the risk of complications. Although robotic systems assist CT-based planning and execution, they still rely on ionizing intraoperative imaging and complex registration. This study proposes robotic pedicle drilling with real-time preventive breach detection using electrical bioimpedance sensing.
  Methods: We developed a robotic approach combined with a pedicle-drilling tool equipped with a proprioceptive electrical bioimpedance sensor developed by SpineGuard. A real-time detection algorithm was designed to analyze the electrical bioimpedance signal during drilling and identify abrupt changes in conductivity associated with potential breaches towards the spinal canal. The method operates without external devices or sensors.
  Results: The ex vivo experiments showed that the proposed method prevented breaches in 100 of the 51 drilling cases. These findings demonstrate the system's ability to detect potentially hazardous events during drilling and to stop the procedure before. The ex vivo experiments demonstrated that the proposed method prevented breaches in all 51 drilling cases.
  Conclusions: This work demonstrates the feasibility of robotic pedicle drilling with electrical bioimpedance sensing for real-time breach prevention. Using only the tool signal, the method eliminates the need for external sensing systems and supports safer pedicle screw placement.

</details>


### [63] [Cross-entropy optimization with prioritized constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01319)
*Francisco Roldan Sanchez,Pau de las Heras Molins,David Fridovich-Keil,Georgios Bakirtzis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 TierCEM，一种将严格约束优先级直接嵌入精英选择过程的交叉熵方法变体，无需为每个约束设置重要度权重，并在导航与接触丰富推动任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当约束之间发生冲突时，优化器必须决定保留哪些要求、放宽哪些要求。优先级排序能指定哪些要求优先，而基于惩罚的方法通过数值权重编码相对重要性。但依赖这些权重时，解可能在违反既定优先级的情况下提升其加权得分。因此需要一个不依赖逐约束重要度权重、又能严格尊重约束优先级的方法。

Method: 提出 TierCEM，它是交叉熵方法（CEM）的变体，将严格的约束优先级直接纳入精英选择。其做法是按从最高到最低优先级的顺序，对采样候选者进行逐级过滤；当某个约束淘汰了所有剩余候选者时，方法回退到最后一个非空集合，并选择对该阻塞约束违反最小的精英，从而递归地保持所有更高优先级约束的满足。

Result: 在二维导航和接触丰富的推动任务上，于本体感知与学习到的世界模型两种设置中进行评估。实验显示，颠倒约束排序会改变冲突下被违反的约束；同时，将任务目标的推进设为优先项，使 TierCEM 能够在低优先级约束妨碍继续推进时对其放宽。

Conclusion: TierCEM 通过将严格约束优先级直接嵌入精英选择，规避了逐约束重要度权重带来的优先级违背问题，并在冲突场景下能够按优先级有序地放宽低优先级约束，为处理冲突约束的优化提供了一种有效机制。

Abstract: When constraints conflict, an optimizer must determine which requirements to preserve and which to relax. On the one hand, a priority ordering specifies which requirements take precedence. On the other hand, penalty-based formulations encode their relative importance through numerical weights. Depending on these weights, a solution can improve its weighted score while violating intended priorities. We introduce TierCEM, a variant of the cross-entropy method that incorporates strict constraint priorities directly into elite selection without requiring per-constraint importance weights. TierCEM works by sequentially filtering sampled candidates, from highest- to lowest-priority constraint. If and when a constraint eliminates all remaining candidates, TierCEM returns to the last nonempty set and selects elites with the smallest violations of that blocking constraint, recursively preserving satisfaction of all higher-priority constraints. We evaluate TierCEM on 2D navigation and contact-rich pushing tasks in proprioceptive and learned world-model settings. Experiments show that reversing the constraint ordering changes which constraints are violated under conflict. Prioritizing progress toward the task objective also enables TierCEM to relax lower-priority constraints when they would otherwise prevent further progress.

</details>


### [64] [Is Success All You Need? Investigating the Impact of Input Perturbations on VLA Behaviour in Tabletop Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01351)
*Sophie Higham,Riccardo Andrea Izzo,Matteo Matteucci,Alessandro Suglia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个与基准无关的行为鲁棒性评估框架，通过刻画成功轨迹在扰动下的执行方式来评估VLA模型，而不仅依赖任务成功率（TSR），并在LIBERO/LIBERO-Plus上验证了TSR无法反映行为层面的鲁棒性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型鲁棒性评估主要依赖任务成功率（TSR），但TSR只反映任务是否完成，无法刻画成功轨迹在执行过程中的行为变化与变异性，因此可能掩盖扰动对机器人行为的影响，导致鲁棒性评估不充分。

Method: 提出一个与基准无关的行为鲁棒性评估框架，刻画成功轨迹在扰动下的执行方式；通过扩展广泛使用的LIBERO与LIBERO-Plus基准实现该方法；在3个SOTA VLA模型、4个LIBERO任务套件和7种扰动条件下，评估典型成功行为及其变异性的变化，指标涵盖运动平滑度、效率与夹爪行为。

Result: 实验发现扰动会改变成功轨迹的行为，而这一现象无法仅从TSR推断；在多个LIBERO套件中，SOTA VLA模型在相同扰动条件下TSR相当，但成功轨迹上的行为差异显著。

Conclusion: 为更稳健地评估任务性能，鲁棒性度量应不仅关注任务是否完成，还应关注机器人在完成任务过程中的行为方式；建议在评估VLA模型鲁棒性时，将TSR与刻画成功任务执行性质及变异性的行为评估指标互补使用。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved high task success rates on robot manipulation task benchmarks. More recently, there has been an emphasis on evaluating the robustness of VLA models to perturbations. However, this robustness is still predominantly measured through Task Success Rate (TSR). In this work, we propose a benchmark-agnostic evaluation framework to measure the behavioural robustness of models by characterising how successful trajectories are executed under perturbation. We implement this methodology by extending the widely-used LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus benchmarks. Across three state-of-the-art VLA models, four LIBERO task suites and seven perturbation conditions, we evaluate changes in both typical successful behaviour and its variability, including metrics of motion smoothness, efficiency and gripper behaviour. We find that perturbations can alter the behaviour of successful trajectories, a phenomenon which cannot necessarily be inferred from TSR alone. Across LIBERO suites, we identify cases where state-of-the-art VLA models achieve comparable TSR under the same perturbation condition, yet behaviour on successful trajectories diverges substantially. Therefore, to have a more robust assessment of task performance, we argue that suitable measures of robustness should capture not only whether a task is completed, but also how the robot behaves while completing it. When evaluating the robustness of VLA models, TSR may be complemented by behavioural evaluation metrics that characterise the nature and variability of successful task execution by robots.

</details>


### [65] [Continue, Abort, or Fall: Viability-Aware Policy Selection (VAPS) for Safe Humanoid Acrobatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01397)
*Siwei Ju,Lu Liu,Jan Peters,Oleg Arenz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VAPS：将人形机器人动态动作的安全问题建模为策略条件化的滚动时域决策，通过在线评估跟踪策略与中止策略的可行性，按最小牺牲层级选择行为，显著降低硬件损伤风险并帕累托优于端到端基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人执行翻转等动态动作时，次优策略、扰动或仿真到现实差距易导致硬件损坏。运动跟踪策略一旦偏离参考轨迹便无退路，而安全接管需要在"切换到哪个备用策略"与"何时切换"两方面同时做出正确选择，现有方法缺乏这一系统性决策机制。

Method: VAPS将安全性视为策略条件化的滚动时域决策：除保护性跌倒策略外，额外训练可随时中止动作并双脚落地的abort策略；每个控制步用学习到的预测器估计名义跟踪策略与abort策略在短时域内的可行性，再由最小牺牲层级（least-sacrificial hierarchy）保留仍然可行且任务进取性最高的行为。在Unitree G1与LimX Oli上进行随机扰动仿真，并在LimX Oli上验证可行性预测器与完整VAPS控制器（侧翻动作）。

Result: 在随机扰动仿真中，VAPS显著降低头部与手部接触（硬件损伤的主要来源）；在LimX Oli上，可行性预测器与完整控制器均通过侧翻动作验证；VAPS在任务成功率与头部冲击两项指标上均帕累托优于可训练的最强单网络方案，包括端到端安全跟踪策略以及从VAPS自身oracle路由决策蒸馏出的学生策略。

Conclusion: VAPS为动态人形动作提供了一种可行且有效的安全框架：通过策略条件化可行性预测与分层策略选择，在保持任务进取性的同时保护硬件，并可作为监督训练不足策略、保护硬件的通用手段。

Abstract: Dynamic humanoid motions such as flips risk hardware damage due to suboptimal policies, disturbances or sim-to-real gaps. A motion tracking policy offers no way out once the maneuver leaves its reference, and a backup policy needs to take over to protect the hardware for a minimum-damage landing. Which backup to use matters as much as when to switch. We present Viability-Aware Policy Selection (VAPS), which treats safety as a policy-conditioned, receding-horizon decision. Besides a protective fall policy, we also train an abort policy which can abort the motion at any time, landing on its feet. At every control step, learned predictors estimate whether the nominal tracking policy and the abort policy remain viable over a short horizon, and a least-sacrificial hierarchy keeps the most task-ambitious behavior that remains viable. In simulation with randomized disturbances, VAPS sharply reduces head contact and hand contact, which are the dominant sources of hardware damage, with both a Unitree G1 and a LimX Oli; on the LimX Oli, we validate the viability predictors and the full VAPS controller for side-flip motions. VAPS Pareto-dominates the strongest single-network alternatives we could train, including an end-to-end safe-tracking policy and students distilled from VAPS's own oracle-routed decisions, in both task success and head impact. We also show that VAPS is a powerful framework to supervise undertrained policies and protect the hardware.

</details>


### [66] [ALFRED: Requirement-driven development of an open-source mobile manipulator for long-term plant monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01477)
*Ciarán Miceal Johnson,Christopher Quail,Garry Ellard,Alistair McConnell,Steve Tonneau,Fernando Auat Cheein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ALFRED 是一个基于商用组件构建的开源移动操作机器人，经四次迭代满足六项长期野外部署需求，实现了臂部可用位姿占比从34.0%提升至66.1%，并完成一整年每月森林调查（528次穿越）无遗漏采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跟踪作物与森林的季节性变化需要重复观测同一植株，地面机器人可近距离作业，机械臂能提供更多传感视角；但长期野外数据集背后的机器人很少公开设计文件，且机器人自身结构对臂展的限制与对传感器的遮挡缺乏跨版本对比。

Method: 构建开源移动操作机器人ALFRED：采用Ackermann转向底盘，搭载六自由度机械臂、LiDAR、RGB-D相机、RTK GNSS与IMU，安装于可重构铝型材框架，运行容器化ROS软件；围绕六项重复野外部署需求（耐久性、模块化、可维修性、传感覆盖范围、续航、可复现性）历经四次迭代；对后三个版本开展基于模型的分析，用射线投射评估框架安装式LiDAR的水平视野遮挡。

Result: 后三个版本的机械臂可用可达位姿占比依次为34.0%、60.0%、66.1%；射线投射显示仅最终版本能同时保持框架安装式LiDAR前向与后向水平视野无遮挡；ALFRED完成一年每月森林调查共528次穿越，未遗漏任何一次计划采集，期间经历电池衰减、为其他研究者研究而重配置，以及面向自主作物行作业的ALFRED 2.0并行开发，每次版本切换约需六小时。

Conclusion: ALFRED验证了以商用组件构建开源移动操作机器人并长期野外部署的可行性，机械结构迭代可显著提升臂部可用位姿比例与传感器视野；同时表明机械模块化的可靠性取决于与之同步维护的机器人描述文件。

Abstract: Tracking seasonal change in crops and forests requires observing the same plants repeatedly. Ground robots can do this at close range, and a manipulator gives their sensors more viewpoints. Yet the robots behind long-term field datasets are rarely released with their design files, and how a robot's own structure limits arm reach and occludes its sensors is seldom compared between builds. We present ALFRED, an open-source mobile manipulator built from commercially available components. It carries a six-degree-of-freedom arm, LiDAR, RGB-D cameras, RTK GNSS and an IMU on an Ackermann-steered base, all mounted on a reconfigurable aluminium strut frame, and runs containerised ROS software. It was developed through four builds against six requirements for repeated outdoor deployment: durability, modularity, repairability, sensing reach, endurance and reproducibility. Model-based analysis of the last three builds shows the usable share of the arm's reachable poses rising from 34.0% to 60.0% and then 66.1%, and ray casting shows that only the final build keeps the frame-mounted LiDAR's horizontal view clear both forwards and backwards. ALFRED completed a year of monthly forest surveys (528 traversals) without missing a scheduled collection. This was despite battery degradation, reconfiguration for another researcher's study, and the parallel development of ALFRED 2.0 for autonomous crop-row operation, with each switch between builds taking about six hours. The deployment also showed that mechanical modularity is only as dependable as the robot description that tracks it.

</details>


### [67] [OpenSpace Lab Solution to the IROS 2026 Indoor Exploration Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01505)
*Yuxuan Zhang,Dong Li,Zezhou Sun,Yuxuan Xu,Siyu Teng,Yuchen Li,Jianjian Yang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenSpace Lab 团队在 IROS 2026 智能信息采集竞赛中提出单/多机器人探索方案：单机器人用预训练地图补全预测做全局规划，并引入基于剩余时间的探索策略；多机器人用效用驱动的目标选择。单机器人公共赛道覆盖率 61.04%（第 1 名），单/多机器人私有赛道覆盖率分别为 39.53% 和 39.91%（均第 3 名）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛任务要求机器人在有限时间内探索未知室内环境并最大化地图覆盖率。核心难点在于：如何利用有限的先验信息引导探索方向，以及如何在时间预算受限时平衡"继续探索"与"返回起点"的冲突；多机器人场景下还需避免重复搜索、实现高效协同。

Method: 单机器人框架：利用预训练的地图补全（map completion）预测结果进行全局规划，优先前往未探索区域；提出基于剩余时间的探索策略，将返航（homing）约束纳入决策过程，以协调地图覆盖与有限作业时间。多机器人框架：采用效用驱动的目标选择策略，权衡观测增益、移动代价与预算约束，并共享地图与意图信息以消除冗余搜索、提升协同效率。

Result: Single-Robot Public Track 覆盖率达 61.04%，获该赛道第 1 名；Single-Robot Private Track 覆盖率 39.53%，Multi-Robot Private Track 覆盖率 39.91%，两个私有赛道均获第 3 名。

Conclusion: 该方案通过地图补全引导的全局规划与时间感知的探索决策，在单机器人与多机器人赛道上均取得领先成绩，验证了所提策略的有效性。基于本报告扩展的完整论文正在准备投稿，源代码将在全文被接收后于 GitHub 仓库发布。

Abstract: This report presents the \textbf{OpenSpace Lab}'s solution to the Competition on Intelligent Information Gathering for Single and Multi-Robot Systems Workshops, organized as part of the IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS) 2026. Our team reached 1st place in the Single-Robot Public Track and 3rd place in both the Single- and Multi-Robot Private Tracks. The single-robot framework utilizes pre-trained map completion predictions for global planning to prioritize unexplored areas. To reconcile map coverage with limited operation time, we introduce a remaining-time-based exploration strategy that integrates homing constraints into the decision-making process. For multi-robot exploration, we utilize a utility-driven target selection strategy that balances observation gains, movement costs, and budget constraints, leveraging shared map and intent data to eliminate redundant search and maximize coordination efficiency. Our solution reached a 61.04\% coverage rate in the Single-Robot Public Track, while reaching 39.53\% and 39.91\% coverage in the Single- and Multi-Robot Private Tracks, respectively. An extended full-length paper based on this report is currently being prepared for submission, and the source code will be released upon acceptance of the full manuscript at https://github.com/OpenSpace-Lab/Indoor-Exploration-IROS2026.

</details>


### [68] [Completion Aware Guidance for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01559)
*Seungyeon Kim,Junhoo Lee,Baekseung Kim,Minkyu Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出世界行动模型（WAMs）的"任务未完成幻觉"并非世界模型主干的固有缺陷，而是短片段控制适配带来的问题，并提出免训练采样方法CAG予以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAMs虽能预测视觉未来与机器人动作，但存在"任务未完成幻觉"：预测合理且动作一致，却缺少完成任务所需的过渡。作者旨在证明该失败源于短片段控制的适配，并加以解决。

Method: 提出完成任务感知引导（Completion Aware Guidance, CAG），一种免训练的采样方法，在生成过程中引导采样朝向任务完成。

Result: 在代表性WAMs上，CAG将RoboTwin 2.0子集成功率从64%提升至70%，零样本仿真中从69%提升至75%，并将任务未完成幻觉从79%降至40%。

Conclusion: 任务未完成幻觉是短片段控制适配的产物，而非世界模型主干所固有；通过免训练采样引导CAG可有效提升任务完成率并显著减少该类幻觉。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) predict visual futures and robot actions, yet they remain susceptible to task-incomplete imagination, where plausible, action-consistent predictions omit the transition needed for task completion. In this paper, we show that this failure is not inherent to the world model backbone, but emerges when adapted for short-chunk control, which can repeatedly favor plausible local continuations over task-completing transitions. To address this, we introduce Completion Aware Guidance (CAG), a training-free sampling method that guides generation toward task completion. Across representative WAMs, CAG improves success from 64% to 70% on a RoboTwin 2.0 subset and from 69% to 75% in zero-shot simulation, while reducing task-incomplete imagination from 79% to 40%.

</details>


### [69] [LiDARFlow: Real-Time Panel-Based MAV Guidance in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01573)
*João Machado,Zeynep Bilgin,Matthieu Verdoucq,Murat Bronz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于气动势流面板法的轻量级机载制导算法，利用机载LiDAR在线构建障碍表示并生成光滑无碰撞制导向量，室内飞行实验验证了其实时避障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型飞行器需在未知、杂乱的室内环境中仅依靠机载感知自主飞行，现有制导方法在计算负担与环境未知性方面存在局限。本文旨在探索将该面板法（源自气动势流理论）推广至未知环境，实现轻量、实时的机载制导。

Method: 以源自气动势流理论的面板法为基础，从局部感知的障碍生成光滑、无碰撞的制导向量；通过机载LiDAR测量在线构建并更新障碍表示，从而将方法扩展至未知环境；将所得避障场与标称制导向量场叠加，形成最终控制输入。

Result: 在室内飞行实验中，针对航点导航与方向引导两种场景进行验证；两种情况下飞行器均仅依靠机载感知成功完成任务并实时避开所有障碍。方法计算量小，适合机载部署；点云处理被识别为主要实际限制。

Conclusion: 结果支持在未知环境中采用轻量级机载制导的可行性；未来工作需改进点云处理效率。

Abstract: This paper presents a guidance algorithm for micro aerial vehicles operating in unknown, cluttered environments using only onboard sensing. The method is based on a panel formulation originally derived from aerodynamic potential-flow theory and generates smooth, collision-free guidance vectors from locally perceived obstacles. The approach is extended to unknown environments by constructing and updating the obstacle representation online from onboard LiDAR measurements. The resulting obstacle-avoidance field is integrated with a nominal guiding vector field to produce the final control input. The system is experimentally validated in indoor flight tests under two scenarios: waypoint navigation and directional guidance. In both cases, the vehicle successfully completes its task while avoiding all obstacles in real time using only onboard perception. The results demonstrate that the method is computationally lightweight and suitable for onboard implementation, with pointcloud processing identified as the main practical limitation. These results support the feasibility of lightweight onboard guidance in unknown environments.

</details>


### [70] [ReCo: Response-Consistent Locomotion with Policy-Aware MPC for Legged Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01612)
*Kuankuan Sima,Yichao Gao,Chenxi Gu,Kefan Zhao,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReCo：将"响应一致的运动策略"与"感知策略的MPC"耦合，用于腿足机器人连续移动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续腿足操作要求末端执行器精准跟踪的同时基座持续行走。RL+MPC是合适方案：学习策略提供鲁棒运动，MPC协调基座与机械臂补偿跟踪误差。但MPC只能补偿其可预测的基座运动，且学习策略的指令响应随步态相位、接触状态与负载变化，导致协调困难。

Method: 提出ReCo框架，包含两部分：(1) 响应塑形（response shaping），在随机化动力学下训练策略，使其对指令的响应一致且可重复；(2) 基于辨识得到的闭环响应模型，构建感知策略的MPC（policy-aware MPC），联合规划运动指令与机械臂动作。

Result: 在仿真基准上，相比各指标最优基线，位置RMSE降低28.7%，姿态RMSE降低27.4%。真实世界实验展示了机载的连续腿足操作，基座与机械臂协调运动。

Conclusion: 通过统一策略响应一致性与MPC的可预测性，ReCo实现了机载连续腿足移动操作，验证了响应塑形与策略感知MPC耦合的有效性。

Abstract: Continuous legged manipulation requires accurate end-effector tracking while the base keeps walking. Combining reinforcement learning (RL) with model predictive control (MPC) suits this task: the learned policy provides robust locomotion, while MPC coordinates the base and arm to compensate for tracking errors. However, MPC can compensate only for base motion that it can predict, and a learned policy's command response varies with gait phase, contact, and payload. We present ReCo, a framework that couples response-consistent locomotion with policy-aware MPC for legged manipulation. Response shaping trains the policy to respond to commands consistently and repeatably across randomized dynamics. An identified closed-loop response model then lets MPC jointly plan locomotion commands and arm motion. On the simulation benchmark, ReCo reduces position and orientation root-mean-square error (RMSE) by 28.7% and 27.4% relative to the best baseline for each metric. Real-world experiments demonstrate onboard continuous legged manipulation with coordinated base and arm motion.

</details>


### [71] [SonarVoxNet: Diver Detection in 3D Bounding Box using 3D Sonar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01644)
*Eugene Park,Jiwon Lee,Seyoung Kan,Trung Dong,Xiaomin Lin,Jane Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出SonarVoxNet（首个支持全9-DoF有向包围盒的3D声呐潜水员检测器）与Diver3D（首个带完整3D姿态标注的公开3D声呐潜水员数据集），证明从3D声呐中可恢复潜水员全身姿态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助潜水员的水下自主航行器需连续跟踪潜水员的三维位置与全身姿态。然而水下视觉感知不可靠；前视声呐虽广泛应用却丢失姿态估计所需的高度信息，构成根本性限制。近年商用化的3D声呐虽保留高度信息，但回波稀疏且含噪，而现有检测器面向稠密LiDAR数据、且假设目标保持直立、仅绕偏航轴旋转（如车辆、行人），无法表征自由俯仰与翻滚的潜水员。这一空白亟待填补。

Method: 作者提出两项贡献：其一，SonarVoxNet将基于体素的编码器与无锚框的中心式检测头适配至3D声呐数据，用连续6D旋转参数化替代传统仅偏航的旋转表示，以预测完整9-DoF有向包围盒；其二，构建Diver3D数据集，在自然洞穴潜水场景采集，是首个为处于多样非直立姿态的潜水员提供完整3D姿态标注的公开3D声呐数据集。此外，通过针对骨干网络与检测头的受控消融实验进行分析。

Result: 消融实验表明，3D声呐潜水员精确检测的主导因素是旋转表示从仅偏航（yaw-only）向完整SO(3)的转变。该转变显著提升检测精度并降低姿态误差。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅凭3D声呐即可恢复潜水员全身姿态，为后续潜水员姿态估计与潜水员-机器人交互研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) assisting human divers must continuously track not only the diver's 3D position but also their full-body orientation. However, vision-based perception is unreliable underwater, and forward-looking sonar -- despite being widely used -- discards the elevation information needed for orientation estimation, posing a fundamental limitation. Recently commercialized 3D sonar preserves elevation but produces sparse, noisy returns, and existing detectors are built for dense LiDAR data and for targets that remain upright and rotate only about the yaw axis (e.g., vehicles, pedestrians), making them unable to represent a freely pitching and rolling diver. To address this gap, we present two contributions. First, SonarVoxNet adapts a voxel-based encoder and an anchor-free center-based detection head to 3D sonar data, replacing the conventional yaw-only rotation representation with a continuous 6D rotation parameterization to predict full 9-DoF oriented bounding boxes -- to our knowledge, the first 3D sonar diver detector to do so. Second, Diver3D is the first public 3D sonar dataset with full 3D orientation labels for divers in diverse, non-upright poses, collected at a natural cave-diving site. Through controlled ablations over the backbone and detection head, we show that the dominant factor behind accurate 3D sonar-based diver detection is the transition from yaw-only rotation to full-SO(3) rotation. This transition substantially improves detection accuracy and reduces orientation error. These results demonstrate that full-body diver orientation is recoverable from 3D sonar alone, laying the groundwork for future work on diver pose estimation and diver-robot interaction.

</details>


### [72] [Learning a Resolution-Consistent Jacobian Field for Bio-Inspired Rigid-Soft Finger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01668)
*Tianyou Liang,Haisen Zeng,Shanjun Chen,YiMing Zhu,Zhongyue Lu,Zirong Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Jacobian Flow Matching (JFM) 框架，基于条件流匹配学习分辨率一致的雅可比场，将驱动-运动映射建模为动力学流，在腱驱动刚柔耦合手指上显著提升单步与长时程预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动的刚柔耦合灵巧手指具有强非线性与构型依赖的灵敏度，精确建模困难；离散时间控制中基于雅可比的运动学算法依赖逐点局部线性近似，性能对传感器采样频率与控制器更新频率敏感。

Method: 提出Jacobian Flow Matching (JFM)，一种基于Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) 的结构化学习框架，学习分辨率一致的雅可比场，将驱动到运动的转换建模为动力学流；支持单步预测，并可通过ODE积分实现连续rollout，从而在不同时间分辨率下保持一致的推理结果。
