# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Two-Line Elimination and the Weierstrass Group Law in Arbitrary Characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00028)
*Hongfeng Wu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 利用多项式分解、形式微分与二次插值证明一般Weierstrass形式椭圆曲线上弦-切线运算的结合性，并给出短Weierstrass形式的独立简洁证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦-切线运算是椭圆曲线群律的核心，其结合性是群结构成立的关键。本文旨在给出一个适用于任意特征、基于多项式恒等式的直接证明，并澄清该运算与Zwegers构造中插值抛物线的关系。

Method: 设K为域，E/K为一般Weierstrass形式的非奇异射影曲线。结合多项式分解、形式微分与二次插值，构造消除相继加法S=P⊕Q与T=S⊕R中所涉两条非竖直直线Y=ℓ(X)与Y=m(X)的主恒等式：存在c∈K与首一二次多项式h∈K[X]，满足ℓ+m+A_pol=c(X-x_S)、G_pol+ℓm=(X-x_S)h，以及(Y-ℓ)(Y-m)=(X-x_S)(h-cY)+Y²+A_pol(X)Y-G_pol(X)。当c≠0时，q=h/c的图像过P、Q、R与-T。切性通过Weierstrass方程的形式偏导数表述，故对任意特征成立；再通过普通或Hermite插值的唯一性说明两种括法给出同一最终点。

Result: 证明了运算结合性：两种括号方式由普通插值或Hermite插值确定同一个二次多项式，从而得到相同的最终点；证明在任意特征下均成立。对短Weierstrass形式另给出简洁证明，并将q等同于Zwegers构造中的插值抛物线。

Conclusion: 弦-切线运算满足结合律，主恒等式统一刻画了相继加法中的直线消去，且方法不依赖特征，短Weierstrass形式下与Zwegers的插值抛物线构造一致。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a field and let $E/K$ be a nonsingular projective curve in general Weierstrass form. We prove associativity of its chord--tangent operation by combining polynomial factorization, formal differentiation, and quadratic interpolation. The main identity eliminates the two nonvertical lines $Y=\ell(X)$ and $Y=m(X)$ occurring in the successive additions $S=P\oplus Q$ and $T=S\oplus R$. There are $c\in K$ and a monic quadratic polynomial $h\in K[X]$ such that \[
  \ell+m+\Apol=c(X-x_S),
  \qquad
  \Gpol+\ell m=(X-x_S)h, \] and \[
  (Y-\ell)(Y-m)
  =(X-x_S)(h-cY)
  +Y^2+\Apol(X)Y-\Gpol(X). \] When $c\ne0$, the graph of $q=h/c$ contains $P,Q,R$, and $-T$. The two bracketings determine the same quadratic by ordinary or Hermite interpolation and therefore yield the same final point. The proof applies in every characteristic, since tangency is expressed through the formal partial derivatives of the Weierstrass equation. For the short Weierstrass form, a separate concise proof identifies $q$ with the interpolating parabola in Zwegers's construction.

</details>


### [95] [PageRank on Lubotzky--Phillips--Sarnak graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00109)
*Shenghao Hua*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文比较了LPS图上独立几何时间停时的随机游走回归概率与无限正则树上的对应值，用McKay测度给出树的值、Ihara zeta函数描述环带来的额外回归，并在平均游走长度与最短环长相当的情形下估计该修正项，获得无条件下带双对数因子上界及GRH下与顶点数倒数同阶的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机游走在图上与树上的回归概率差异来自图中的环结构。作者希望量化这一由环导致的"修正项"，特别是在游走平均长度与最短环长相当、足够长的游走能以不可忽略概率绕经环的临界尺度下，理解树模型（McKay/Ihara框架）对LPS图回归概率的逼近程度及其误差量级。

Method: 以Lubotzky–Phillips–Sarnak图与无限正则树为对象，对随机游走在独立几何时间处停时，采用McKay测度作为树的基准值，并用Ihara zeta函数刻画环贡献的额外回归项。在平均游走长度与最短环长可比的情形下对该修正项进行解析与谱分析估计，并对素数层级上的取值作平均/固定矩分析。

Result: 无条件证明了修正项的一个上界，其主要误差带有双对数因子，且在乘以顶点数后于素数层级上具有有界的固定矩。在关于二次Dirichlet L函数的广义黎曼假设（GRH）下，修正项与顶点数的倒数同阶。

Conclusion: 环引起的回归修正项在游走长度与最短环长可比时不可忽略，其大小在无条件下由带双对数因子及有界固定矩的上界控制，而在GRH下精确到顶点数倒数的量级。这表明LPS图上的回归概率以顶点数倒数的精度逼近树（McKay/Ihara）模型值，且该精度与二次Dirichlet L函数的相关假设密切相关。

Abstract: We compare return probabilities for random walks stopped at an independent geometric time on Lubotzky--Phillips--Sarnak graphs and on the infinite regular tree, with the McKay measure giving the tree value and the Ihara zeta function describing the additional returns caused by cycles. We estimate this correction when the mean walk length is comparable to the length of the shortest cycle, a regime in which walks long enough to traverse a cycle receive non-negligible weight.
  Unconditionally, we prove an upper bound with a double logarithmic factor and bounded fixed moments over prime levels after multiplication by the number of vertices. Under the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis for quadratic Dirichlet $L$-functions, the correction is of the same order as the reciprocal of the number of vertices.

</details>


### [96] [Some Methods to Solve the Diophantine Equation ay^2=b(x^k-c^k)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00119)
*Xiaodong Zhuang,Nikos E. Mastorakis*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究丢番图方程 ay^2=b(x^k-c^k) 的求解方法，针对不同 k 情形给出便于计算机算法实现的降次方法，并给出 ay^2=b(x^{2n}-c^{2n}) 无解的一个充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原方程次数较高、整数解搜索空间大、求解效率低，需要针对不同 k 的情形设计统一且便于程序实现的求解途径。

Method: 按 k 的不同情形分类讨论；将原方程约化为较低次数的方程；在此基础上给出 ay^2=b(x^{2n}-c^{2n}) 不可解的充分条件。

Result: 建立了不同 k 情形下的求解方法，实现了方程的降次约化，从而提升搜索整数解的效率；并得到偶数次幂差情形下无解的充分条件。

Conclusion: 降次约化是处理该族丢番图方程的有效手段，所得方法与充分条件便于计算机算法实现，可用于判定与求解相关方程。

Abstract: The solution of the Diophantine equation ay^2=b(x^k-c^k) is studied in different cases of k. Methods are proposed for different cases of k, which are convenient for computer algorithm implementation. The original equation is reduced to lower-degree equations, which can improve the efficiency in searching possible integer solutions. A sufficient condition of the unsolvability of ay^2=b(x^2n-c^2n) is presented.

</details>


### [97] [An explicit family of twists of the Carlitz module with unbounded analytic rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00379)
*David Niedbala Giraudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文否定了Grishkov-Logachev关于固定有限域上Carlitz模扭动的解析秩有界的猜想，对任意q≥3构造出解析秩无界的扭动族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grishkov与Logachev在其综述问题1.4中提出：对固定有限域F_q，Carlitz模各扭动的解析秩是否有界。本文旨在回答这一有界性问题，并顺带处理综述中3.6.1至3.6.4的相关问题。

Method: 取q=p^s，n≥1，令P_{q,n}=(θ^{q^n}-θ)^{q-2}。研究Grishkov-Logachev矩阵M(P_{q,n},1,k)所对应的有向图，显式刻画其回路结构，并据此分析L函数L(C_P,t,T)与解析秩。

Result: 该有向图的回路两两不交，均不经过含t的项，且与E^n在循环旋转下的轨道一一对应，其中E={l∈[0,q-2] : p∤(l+1)}。因此L(C_P,t,T)不依赖t，按每个轨道分解为因子1-wt(ν)T^{per(ν)}的乘积，解析秩等于权重为1的轨道上p^{v_p(per(ν))}之和。当n为p的幂时该值为(q/p)^n(p-1)^{n-1}，无界。

Conclusion: 对每个q≥3，Carlitz模扭动的解析秩无界，从而对Grishkov-Logachev问题1.4给出否定回答；同时该论证也回答了其综述问题3.6.1至3.6.4中关于此族的情形。

Abstract: Grishkov and Logachev have asked (Problem 1.4 of their recent survey) whether, for a fixed finite field F_q, the analytic ranks of the twists of the Carlitz module are bounded. We answer this in the negative for every q >= 3. For q = p^s and n >= 1 put P_{q,n} = (theta^(q^n) - theta)^(q-2). We show that the directed graph of the matrix M(P_{q,n},1,k) of Grishkov-Logachev has a completely explicit set of circuits: they are pairwise disjoint, none of them meets an entry involving t, and they are in bijection with the orbits of E^n under cyclic rotation, where E is the set of l in [0,q-2] with p not dividing l+1. Consequently L(C_P, t, T) does not depend on t, it factors as a product of one term 1 - wt(nu) T^per(nu) per orbit, and the analytic rank equals the sum of p^(v_p(per(nu))) over the orbits of weight 1. When n is a power of p this equals (q/p)^n (p-1)^(n-1), which is unbounded. The argument also answers questions 3.6.1 to 3.6.4 of the survey for this family.

</details>


### [98] [New bounds for the integer Chebyshev constant of [0,1]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00386)
*David Niedbala Giraudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过显式证书方法，将整数切比雪夫常数 t_Z([0,1]) 的已知区间从 1.39×10⁻³ 收窄到 4.56×10⁻⁴，给出新的双侧界 0.4222286000 ≤ t_Z([0,1]) ≤ 0.4226846975。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自 Pritsker（2005）与 Flammang（2014）以来，[0,1] 上的整数切比雪夫常数长期被夹在 0.4213 与 0.422685 之间，区间宽度约 1.39×10⁻³，精度受限。作者希望对这一常数给出更精确的数值定位，并借由可独立验证的显式证书提升结果的可信度。

Method: 采用显式证书（explicit certificates）构造双侧界：上界由一个含 92 个因子及显式整数指数的整数多项式给出；下界由一个定义在 947 个单元上的离散测度与一个含 206 个不可约多项式的库构成，并借助一个不等式，其中“为整除性付出代价”的项被显式写出。两段自包含脚本（作为附件）可仅凭证书重新推导上下界。

Result: 得到 0.4222286000 ≤ t_Z([0,1]) ≤ 0.4226846975，将区间宽度由 1.39×10⁻³ 缩小至 4.56×10⁻⁴。上下界均由显式证书成立，且可由附件脚本独立复现。

Conclusion: 该工作显著提高了 [0,1] 上整数切比雪夫常数的数值精度，且因采用显式证书与可复现脚本，结果具有高度可验证性，为后续进一步收窄该常数区间提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: The integer Chebyshev constant of [0,1] has been bracketed by 0.4213 <= t_Z([0,1]) <= 0.422685 since the work of Pritsker (2005) and of Flammang (2014). We prove
  0.4222286000 <= t_Z([0,1]) <= 0.4226846975,
  narrowing the interval from 1.39e-3 to 4.56e-4. Both bounds are established by explicit certificates. The upper bound is carried by an integer polynomial with 92 factors and explicit integer exponents; the lower bound by a discrete measure on 947 cells together with a library of 206 irreducible polynomials, through an inequality in which the terms paying for divisibility are made explicit. Two self-contained scripts, included as ancillary files, re-derive both bounds from the certificates alone.

</details>


### [99] [On a problem on Ramanujan-Sato series of level 11](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00551)
*John M. Campbell*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文解决了关于11级Ramanujan-Sato级数系数 $c_{11}(n)$ 的长期开放问题，给出了一个固定的、嵌套的双二项式求和公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定11级Ramanujan-Sato级数系数 $c_{11}(n)$ 的显式表达是一个由 Cooper 等人于2015年提出、并在其2017年专著及2025年文集文章中重申的开放问题。该问题旨在将低级别Ramanujan-Sato级数已知的系数公式（固定嵌套有限二项式求和）直接推广到11级，且此前一直未获解决。

Method: 作者通过引入并证明一个关于 $c_{11}(n)$ 的双二项式求和公式（double binomial sum formula），该公式与低级别Ramanujan-Sato级数的系数公式直接类比。

Result: 成功给出了对所有非负整数 $n$ 均成立的 $c_{11}(n)$ 的双二项式求和表达式，直接类比于低级别情形的已知系数公式。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了11级Ramanujan-Sato级数系数公式这一长期开放问题，将低级别已知的固定嵌套有限二项式求和形式成功推广到11级。

Abstract: A longstanding open problem concerning Ramanujan-Sato series is given by determining an expression for the coefficients $c_{11}(n)$ associated with Ramanujan-Sato series of level 11 with a fixed, nested, finite binomial sum for all nonnegative integers $n$, by direct analogy with the known formulas for the coefficients for lower-level Ramanujan-Sato series (and this is clarified and made explicit in our paper). This open problem was given by Cooper et al. in 2015 [J. Math. Anal. Appl.], in Cooper's 2017 textbook on Ramanujan's theta functions, and in Cooper's 2025 contribution to the Contemp. Math. series. This problem seems to have remained open. We succeed in solving this problem, by introducing and proving a double binomial sum formula for $c_{11}(n)$ that is directly analogous to the coefficient formulas for lower-level Ramanujan-Sato series.

</details>


### [100] [Perfect squares in reciprocal-sum sequences and primes that are inert in quadratic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00765)
*Mateo Matijasevick,Santiago Rodríguez,Gregorio Salazar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究递推序列 $x_{n+1}=k/(x_0+\cdots+x_n)$ 的既约分子分母，证明 $c\,a_n+b_n$ 恒为完全幂当且仅当 $k=h^2$ 且 $h$ 的所有素因子 $\equiv 3 \pmod 4$，并给出若干推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画使 $c\,a_n+b_n$ 对任意序列、任意 $n\ge2$ 都为完全幂的常数 $c$ 与参数 $k$，理解该非线性递推下既约分数的算术结构。

Method: 令 $t_n=(x_0+\cdots+x_n)/h$，将问题化为递推 $t_{n+1}=t_n+1/t_n$；利用该递推下既约分数不发生约分这一性质推导精确公式，并通过二次域 $\Q(\sqrt{-k})$、$\Q(\sqrt{\mu^2-4})$ 中的惯性条件分析素因子行为。

Result: 存在这样的 $c>0$ 当且仅当 $k=h^2$ 且 $h$ 的每个素因子 $\equiv 3\pmod 4$；此时 $c=2/h$，幂为平方。得到 $c\,a_n+b_n$ 的精确公式，并证明沿任一序列 $h$ 的每个素因子至多破坏一项。对任意 $k$，$b_n^2-\frac4k a_n^2$ 恒为有理平方；其为整数平方当且仅当 $k$ 的平方部分的素因子在 $\Q(\sqrt{-k})$ 中满足惯性条件。对递推 $x_{n+1}=h^2/(x_0+\cdots+x_n+n\mu h)$，高斯整数由二次域 $\Q(\sqrt{\mu^2-4})$ 取代。

Conclusion: 既约分数的非约分性质与二次域惯性条件共同刻画了此类递推的幂性，并给出自然推广。

Abstract: Let $k$ be a positive integer, and consider the sequences of positive rationals with $x_0\in\N$ and $x_{n+1}=k/(x_0+x_1+\dots+x_n)$. Write $x_n=a_n/b_n$ in lowest terms. We show that there is a rational constant $c>0$ such that $c\,a_n+b_n$ is a perfect power for every such sequence and every $n\ge2$ if and only if $k=h^2$ and every prime factor of $h$ is congruent to $3$ modulo $4$; in that case $c=2/h$ and the powers are squares. The proof rests on the observation that $t_n=(x_0+\dots+x_n)/h$ satisfies $t_{n+1}=t_n+1/t_n$, a recursion under which reduced fractions never cancel. This yields an exact formula for $c\,a_n+b_n$, shows that along any single sequence each prime factor of $h$ spoils at most one term, and leads to two generalizations. For arbitrary $k$ the invariant $b_n^2-\frac4k a_n^2$ is always a rational square, and it is always an integer square exactly when the primes dividing the square part of $k$ satisfy an inertness condition in $\Q(\sqrt{-k})$. For the recursions $x_{n+1}=h^2/(x_0+\dots+x_n+nμh)$ the role of the Gaussian integers is played by the quadratic fields $\Q(\sqrt{μ^2-4})$, which include $\Q(\sqrt{-3})$ and every real quadratic field.

</details>


### [101] [Translation invariant quadratic forms in $8$ variables and dense sets of primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01091)
*Zhaochen Pei,Maoru Tan,Weiwen Zhang,Lilu Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：在8变量平移不变不定二次型Q满足温和条件下，任意足够稠密的素数子集A中必存在8个两两不同的素数p₁,…,p₈使得Q(p₁,…,p₈)=0，其密度阈值可达(log log X)^{-c}量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二次型在素数上的零点的存在性，特别是将Green的近期工作推广到不定二次型情形，并给出素数子集所需密度的定量下界。

Method: 限定Q为8变量平移不变不定二次型；设𝒜为{1,…,X}内素数子集；在Q满足温和（非退化类）条件下，发展Green的方法，利用密度假设对𝒜施加相对密度条件，构造并求解相应方程。

Result: 只要相对密度满足|𝒜|/(X/log X) ≫_Q (log log X)^{-c}（c为某绝对常数），即存在两两不同的素数p₁,…,p₈∈𝒜使Q(p₁,…,p₈)=0。

Conclusion: 在8变量平移不变不定二次型上，素数集合中二次型零点的存在性可由极弱的对数对数级密度条件保证，将相关结果推广至不定型情形。

Abstract: Let $Q$ be a translation invariant indefinite quadratic form in $8$ variables. Let $\mathcal{P}$ be the set of all primes. Let $\mathcal{A}\subset\mathcal{P}\cap\{1,\dots,X\}$. By developing the recent work of Green, we prove that subject to a mild condition on the form $Q$ there exist pairwise distinct primes $p_{1},\dots,p_{8}\in \mathcal{A}$ satisfying $Q(p_{1},\dots,p_{8})=0$ provided that \[\frac{|\mathcal{A}|}{X/\log X}\gg_{Q}(\log\log X)^{-c}\] for some absolute constant $c>0$.

</details>


### [102] [Congruence Jumping in Polynomial Divisibility Systems: Arithmetic Structure and Solution Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00808)
*Max A. Alekseyev,Dmitry I. Khomovsky*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究整数系数多项式满足 y|A(x), x|B(y) 的非零整数解，提出"同余跳跃"的商变换方法，建立互反多项式规则、伴随曲面恒等式，并在二次情形给出分母界、无限链分类、Vieta 动力学与 Pell 型线性化，最终化为有限除子搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原问题涉及丢番图型的多项式整除系统 y|A(x) 与 x|B(y)，其解集结构复杂。作者希望通过引入可改变多项式对的商变换（同余跳跃）来统一处理这类系统，摆脱以往对互素性、首一性、单位性等限制假设，并刻画解集何时可归约为有限计算。

Method: 核心方法是"同余跳跃"：对满足 y|A(x)、x|B(y) 的解施加商变换，使多项式对 (A,B) 发生改变，从而在不同的多项式状态间迁移。作者建立互反多项式规则来描述跳跃规律，并利用伴随曲面恒等式构造无限整数商链。二次情形下将状态变化归约为固定圆锥上的 Vieta 动力学，并给出 Pell 型线性化。

Result: 建立了无需互素、首一或单位假设的一般互反多项式跳跃规则，并给出多项式状态可整归一化的精确判据；由伴随曲面恒等式构造出无限整数商链，含一个显式的混合次数例子。二次情形：得到有限链的定量分母界，分类了带非整圆锥参数的非平凡单侧无限链，证明二次状态变化等价于固定圆锥上的普通 Vieta 动力学；构造出真正的非单位四循环，具有无穷多正整数点且动力学可均匀 Pell 型线性化。一个非单位整除系统通过 Pell 轨道与变化状态阶梯被分析。最后，一个独立的关系格判据将某些解集归约为有限除子搜索。

Conclusion: 同余跳跃为多项式整除系统提供了统一框架：一般情形由互反多项式规则与伴随曲面恒等式刻画，可构造无限商链；二次情形则展现出强刚性，其动力学归结为圆锥上的 Vieta / Pell 结构，并通过关系格判据把部分解集化为有限除子搜索。

Abstract: Let $A$ and $B$ be polynomials with integer coefficients. We study nonzero integer solutions of $$y\mid A(x),\qquad x\mid B(y),$$ using quotient transformations that may change the polynomial pair. We call this process \emph{congruence jumping}. We establish a general reciprocal-polynomial rule governing such jumps, without coprimality, monicity, or unit assumptions, and give exact criteria for when the resulting polynomial states can be normalized integrally. Companion-surface identities provide a mechanism for constructing infinite integral quotient chains, including an explicit mixed-degree example.
  The quadratic case is considerably more rigid. We obtain a quantitative denominator bound for finite chains, classify the exceptional nonconstant one-sided infinite chains with nonintegral conic parameter, and show that changing quadratic states reduce to ordinary Vieta dynamics on a fixed conic. We further construct a genuinely nonunit recurrent four-cycle with infinitely many positive integral points and show that its dynamics admits a uniform Pell-type linearization. A motivating nonunit divisibility system is analyzed through Pell orbits and changing-state ladders. Finally, an independent relation-lattice criterion reduces certain solution sets to a finite divisor search.

</details>


### [103] [Multiplicative recurrence of Piatetski--Shapiro and nil-Bohr sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00843)
*Sun-Kai Leung*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改编Frantzikinakis–Klurman–Moreira的圆方法，给出自然数集为乘法回归集的充分条件，并应用于小指数Piatetski–Shapiro集与正则无理nil-Bohr集，同时研究完全积性函数在其上的取值分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘法回归（multiplicative recurrence）是组合数论与遍历论交叉的核心概念之一，判断具体集合是否具有该性质通常缺乏通用工具。作者希望借助已有的圆方法框架，建立一套可操作的充分判据，从而将乘法回归性推广到Piatetski–Shapiro集、nil-Bohr集等具有加性/幂结构的重要集合族，并进一步考察积性函数在这些集合上的统计行为。

Method: 以Frantzikinakis–Klurman–Moreira的圆方法为基础进行改编，构造适用于乘法回归的充分判据；将该判据分别应用于小指数的Piatetski–Shapiro集（形如⌊n^c⌋的整点集）与正则无理nil-Bohr集；并利用该框架分析单模完全积性函数沿这些集合的取值分布。

Result: 得到了一组使自然数集具有乘法回归性的充分条件；证明小指数Piatetski–Shapiro集与正则无理nil-Bohr集满足该判据，因而具有乘法回归性；并刻画了单模完全积性函数在上述集合上的取值分布情况。

Conclusion: 圆方法可有效移植到乘法回归问题中，所得判据对一类具有幂结构与nil-Bohr结构的集合均适用，同时为研究积性函数沿稀疏集合的分布提供了新途径。

Abstract: Adapting the circle method developed by Frantzikinakis--Klurman--Moreira, we establish a sufficient criterion for a set of natural numbers to be multiplicatively recurrent. We apply this criterion to Piatetski--Shapiro sets with small exponents and to regular irrational nil-Bohr sets. We also study the value distribution of unimodular completely multiplicative functions along these sets.

</details>


### [104] [Transcendence of dendric beta-expansions with pure discrete spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00856)
*Jihyuk Seo,Wolfgang Steiner*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在代数基β（其β-展开对应于具有纯离散谱的树状移位，且替换指令序列满足特定组合条件）下，相应数的超越性，涵盖任意字母表上的Arnoux-Rauzy序列与Cassaigne-Selmer序列，证明借助Rauzy分形与子空间定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究代数基β下具有特定展开结构的数的超越性，将超越性结果推广到更广的树状移位类（含任意字母表的Arnoux-Rauzy序列及Cassaigne-Selmer序列）。

Method: 利用Rauzy分形与子空间定理（subspace theorem）进行证明，并对替换的指令序列施加特定组合条件。

Result: 在所给组合条件下，证明了对应数的超越性，并覆盖Arnoux-Rauzy序列（任意字母表）与Cassaigne-Selmer序列。

Conclusion: Rauzy分形结合子空间定理是研究此类代数基展开数超越性的有效工具，结果推广了已有超越性结论。

Abstract: We prove the transcendence of numbers that have $β$-expansions in algebraic bases $β$ belonging to dendric shifts with purely discrete spectrum, under certain combinatorial conditions on their directive sequences of substitutions. This includes Arnoux-Rauzy sequences on arbitrary alphabets and Cassaigne-Selmer sequences. The proof uses Rauzy fractals and the subspace theorem.

</details>


### [105] [Powers in orbits and semigroups generated by polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00893)
*Alina Ostafe,Dat T. Tran*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究数域上多项式（或有限族多项式生成半群）轨道中完美幂的出现，修正并推广了Ahmadi等人(2012)的结果，证明轨道中完美幂的乘法平移可通过有限算法检测，并给出轨道中满足特定乘法关系元素的有限性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ahmadi等人(2012)关于轨道中完美幂的结果存在错误，需要修正；同时将研究对象从单个多项式推广到有限族多项式生成的半群。

Method: 研究数域上多项式定义的轨道及有限族多项式生成的半群，运用算术动力学方法分析轨道中元素的完美幂性质与乘法关系。

Result: 修正并推广了Ahmadi等人(2012)的结果，证明轨道中完美幂的乘法平移可由有限算法检测；并建立了轨道中满足某些乘法关系元素的有限性结果。

Conclusion: 数域上多项式轨道中的完美幂及相关乘法平移具有算法可检测性，且满足特定乘法关系的轨道元素具有有限性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the presence of perfect powers in orbits of polynomials, or of semigroups generated by a finite family of polynomials, defined over a number field. In particular, we correct and generalize a result of Ahmadi et al. (2012), showing that multiplicative shifts of perfect powers in orbits can be detected by a finite algorithm. We also give a finiteness result on elements in orbits satisfying certain multiplicative relations.

</details>


### [106] [Construction of parametrized families of general pseudo-bimagic and bimagic squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00915)
*Ajai Choudhry*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了6×6伪双魔方阵与8×8双魔方阵的参数化族，并推广到任意正整数m的6m阶伪双魔方阵和8m阶双魔方阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 除一族6×6伪双魔方阵外，此前尚无已发表的参数化双魔方阵族；作者希望填补这一空白并给出系统构造方法。

Method: 通过构造6×6伪双魔方阵和8×8双魔方阵的参数化族，并将这些族组合、推广，得到任意正整数m下的多参数族：6m阶伪双魔方阵与8m阶双魔方阵。

Result: 得到了6×6和8×8的参数化族，以及任意m对应的6m阶伪双魔方阵和8m阶双魔方阵多参数族；同时给出6×6、12×12伪双魔方阵和8×8双魔方阵的数值例子。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了伪双魔方阵与双魔方阵的一般参数化构造，扩展了已知结果，并辅以具体数值实例。

Abstract: A magic square of order $n$ is a square array of $n^2$ distinct integers such that the integers of every row, every column and the two diagonals have the same common sum. If a magic square remains a magic square even when each integer is replaced by its square, it is called bimagic. If, however, all the rows and all the columns of the aforementioned square of squares add up to a common sum, but the two diagonals do not, the original magic square is called pseudo-bimagic. Except for one family of $6 \times 6$ pseudo-bimagic squares, parametrized families of bimagic squares have not been published. In this paper we construct parametrized families of $6 \times 6$ pseudo-bimagic squares and $8 \times 8$ bimagic squares. We use these families to construct, for any positive integer $m$, multi-parameter families of pseudo-bimagic squares of order $6m$ and bimagic squares of order $8m$. We also give numerical examples of $6 \times 6$ and $12 \times 12$ pseudo-bimagic squares and an $8 \times 8$ bimagic square.

</details>


### [107] [Baire-category transfer between Engel and Pierce expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00946)
*Min Woong Ahn*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在Engel展开与Pierce展开的数字序列之间构造了一个显式对应（将第n位Engel展开数字加n-2），证明该对应诱导了单位区间无理数集到其某个可数稠密子集补集之间的同胚，并由此建立了两类展开之间关于Baire范畴的转移原理，精确刻画了哪些Borel类被保持。作者进一步将该同胚识别为Moroz(2027)所考虑的两个映射的复合，研究了第二个映射（将修改的Engel展开映为Pierce展开）的连续性，确定了其不连续点集，证明每个不连续点均为跳跃点，并证明该映射处处非单调，从而验证了Moroz(2027)的相关猜想。作为应用，刻画了使Pierce展开数字级数在剩余集上发散的非增位置相关权重，并关联了Engel与Pierce展开数字序列的收敛指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Engel展开与Pierce展开是两类经典的实数数字展开系统，二者在结构上既有相似性又存在差异。已有工作（如Moroz 2027）考察了联系这两类展开的映射并提出了若干猜想，但缺乏对二者之间精确对应关系及其拓扑、测度与范畴性质的系统刻画。本文旨在建立Engel与Pierce展开之间的显式同胚对应，并借助该对应研究相关映射的连续性、单调性及级数收敛等问题。

Method: 构造显式对应：将第n位Engel展开数字加n-2，得到与Pierce展开数字序列的对应关系，并证明其诱导单位区间无理数集到某可数稠密子集补集的同胚。利用该同胚建立Baire范畴的转移原理，确定被保持的Borel类。将该同胚识别为Moroz(2027)中两个映射的复合，分析第二个映射（修改Engel展开→Pierce展开）的连续性，刻画其不连续点集、证明不连续点均为跳跃点并证明映射处处非单调。最后应用于位置相关权重的级数发散性与收敛指数研究。

Result: 证明Engel与Pierce展开数字序列间存在显式同胚对应，建立了二者的Baire范畴转移原理并精确刻画了被保持的Borel类；确定第二个映射的不连续点集，证明每个不连续点均为跳跃且映射处处非单调，从而验证了Moroz(2027)关于非单调性与例外集外连续性的猜想；刻画了使Pierce展开数字级数在剩余集上发散的非增位置相关权重，并建立了Engel与Pierce展开数字序列收敛指数之间的关系。

Conclusion: 本文通过显式构造揭示了Engel展开与Pierce展开之间的深层结构联系，所建立的同胚及转移原理为研究两类展开的拓扑与范畴性质提供了统一框架，同时解决了Moroz(2027)提出的关于连续性、非单调性的猜想，并在位置相关权重级数发散性与收敛指数方面给出了具体应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct an explicit correspondence between Engel and Pierce expansion digit sequences, obtained by adding $n-2$ to the $n$th Engel expansion digit, and show that it induces a homeomorphism from the set of irrationals in the unit interval onto the complement of a countable dense subset of this set. This yields a transfer principle for Baire category between the two expansions, and we determine exactly which Borel classes are preserved. We then identify this homeomorphism with the composition of two maps considered by Moroz (2027), and study the continuity of the second map, which sends modified Engel expansions to Pierce expansions. We determine its set of discontinuities, show that every discontinuity is a jump, and prove that the map is nowhere monotone. In particular, this confirms, for this map, the nowhere monotonicity and the continuity outside the exceptional set conjectured by Moroz (2027). As applications, we characterize the non-increasing position-dependent weights for which the associated series of Pierce expansion digits diverges on a comeager set, and we relate the convergence exponents of the Engel and Pierce expansion digit sequences.

</details>


### [108] [Indefinite theta vectors and generalized Nahm sums of arbitrary rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00967)
*Dandan Chen,Siyu Yin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类任意秩 signed generalized Nahm sums 的不定 theta 表示，给出其模变换律、非全纯修正的分解，以及若干乘积恒等式与多重和表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 signed generalized Nahm sums 与不定 theta 函数、Jacobi 乘积之间的联系，推广低秩情形并寻求对秩与分量指标一致统一的公式。

Method: 构造秩 r≥2 的不定 theta 表示：对 signed branch 作显式归一化与边界修正，得到与签名 (1,1) 格相关的 (2r−1) 分量全纯 theta 向量的偶分量；用 Euler 型多重和给出奇分量。借助 Zwegers 理论确定实解析完备化的权一模块变换律（S-变换由显式有限正弦矩阵给出），将非全纯修正分解为 unary theta 函数与一维误差函数级数之积的有限和，并计算其反全纯导数，从而得到全纯向量的非齐次变换律。

Result: 得到对秩与分量指标一致的公式；给出实解析完备化的权一模变换律及 S-变换的显式正弦矩阵；给出反全纯导数的显式表达及全纯向量的非齐次变换律；将两个 signed branches 的线性组合求值为 Jacobi 乘积商之差，并对两族可对称化矩阵及其对偶给出多重和表示。

Conclusion: 四个相关乘积向量的每个分量均可表示为有限个带指定矩阵与对称化子的广义 Nahm sums 的组合；在对偶族中允许 signed sums 与半周期平移。

Abstract: We construct indefinite theta representations for a family of signed generalized Nahm sums of arbitrary rank \(r\geq2\). After explicit normalization and boundary correction, one signed branch gives the even components of a \((2r-1)\)-component holomorphic theta vector associated with a lattice of signature \((1,1)\). Euler-type multiple sums provide the odd components, yielding formulas uniform in both the rank and the component index. Using Zwegers' theory, we determine the weight-one modular transformation laws of the real-analytic completion, with an explicit finite sine matrix for the \(S\)-transformation. We decompose the nonholomorphic correction into a finite sum of products of unary theta functions and one-dimensional error-function series, and compute the antiholomorphic derivative of the completion. Together with the completed transformation laws, these formulas give the inhomogeneous transformation law of the holomorphic vector. We also evaluate a linear combination of two signed branches as a difference of Jacobi product quotients. This identity and further evaluations for two families of symmetrizable matrices and their duals, combined with known results, give multiple-sum representations for every component of four associated product vectors. These representations are finite combinations of generalized Nahm sums with the prescribed matrices and symmetrizers; in the dual families, signed sums and half-period translates are allowed.

</details>


### [109] [The sixth moment of the Riemann zeta function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01035)
*Alexandre de Faveri,Mayank Pandey*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了黎曼ζ函数在临界线上的新的大值估计，改进了自Hardy-Littlewood (1923)以来关于 |ζ(1/2+it)| ≥ T^{1/8} 测度的上界，并推出对 4 < k ≤ 12 的k阶矩的改进上界，特别是六阶矩的新估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼ζ函数在临界线上的大值估计与矩问题是解析数论的核心问题。自Hardy-Littlewood (1923)以来，关于 |ζ(1/2+it)| ≥ T^{1/8} 的测度上界长期未获改进，本文旨在突破这一停滞。

Method: 核心工具是针对具有平方根相位的指数和及其扰动的全新大值估计，这些指数和出现在ζ函数短二阶矩的展开中。

Result: 改进了 t∈[T, 2T] 中满足 |ζ(1/2+it)| ≥ T^{1/8} 的测度上界，并推出对每个 4 < k ≤ 12 的k阶矩的改进上界，特别得到 ∫_0^T |ζ(1/2+it)|^6 dt ≪_ε T^{5/4 - 1/60 + ε}。

Conclusion: 本文通过平方根相位指数和的新大值估计，首次在近百年后改进了临界线上ζ函数大值测度上界，并系统性地改进了4 < k ≤ 12范围内的矩估计。

Abstract: We prove new large value estimates for the Riemann zeta function on the critical line. For instance, we improve the upper bound on the measure of $t\in [T, 2T]$ with $|ζ(1/2 + it)| \geq T^{1/8}$ for the first time since Hardy-Littlewood (1923). Our results imply improved upper bounds on the $k$-th moment of zeta for every $4 < k \leq 12$. We show in particular that
  \begin{equation*}
  \int_{0}^{T} |ζ(\tfrac{1}{2} + it)|^6 \,d t \ll_\varepsilon T^{\frac{5}{4} - \frac{1}{60} + \varepsilon}.
  \end{equation*}
  The main ingredient is a new large value estimate for exponential sums with square-root phases, and certain perturbations. Those arise in the expansion of the short second moment of zeta.

</details>


### [110] [Sharp $p$-adic Extrema and Least Extremal Rows for Restricted Binomial GCDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01328)
*John Fairfax-Ball*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究限制型二项式系数最大公约数 $G(N;m)$ 的 $p$-进赋值上确界及最小极值行问题，证明最大赋值恰为 $r_p(m)$，并精确求出 $m=p^a+1$ 时的最小极值行 $T_p(p^a+1)=p^{3a}+1$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 限制型二项式系数最大公约数在以往文献中已有记载，但其 $p$-进赋值的极值行为与最小极值行的精确刻画仍不完整，尤其是对特殊族 $m=p^a+1$ 的最小行缺乏显式结果。

Method: 结合 Kummer 进位定理给出普适上界的显式首位数字见证；用乘法阶构造等式行以证明可达性；对 $m=p^a+1$ 情形，通过独立的下界严格极小性论证，并为唯一最坏首位数字模式提供备用见证。

Result: 当 $N$ 取遍所有可行行时，$v_p(G(N;m))$ 的最大值恰为 $r_p(m)$（其中 $m<p^{r_p(m)}$），并可构造出取等行；对 $m=p^a+1$（$a\ge2$），最小极值行为 $T_p(p^a+1)=p^{3a}+1$。

Conclusion: 该工作完整确定了限制型二项式 GCD 的 $p$-进赋值上确界与最小极值行，其中 $m=p^a+1$ 族的精确解为 $p^{3a}+1$，并明确说明了与既有文献的关系及文献检索的局限。

Abstract: For integers $m\ge 2$ and rows $N>m$ divisible by $m$, consider the restricted binomial greatest common divisor $G(N;m)=\gcd\{\binom Nk:0<k<N,\ m\mid k\}$. Fix a prime $p$ with $p\nmid m$, and let $r_p(m)$ be the least positive integer $r$ such that $m<p^r$. We prove that the largest possible value of $v_p(G(N;m))$, as $N$ ranges over all admissible rows, is exactly $r_p(m)$, and we give a constructive equality row. Attainment is established before the least extremal row $T_p(m)$ is defined. For the special family $m=p^a+1$ with $a\ge2$, we determine that least row exactly: $T_p(p^a+1)=p^{3a}+1$. The proof combines Kummer's carry theorem with an explicit leading-digit witness for the universal upper bound, a multiplicative-order construction for equality, and a separate strict-minimality argument below $p^{3a}+1$ with a fallback witness for the unique worst leading-digit pattern. The selected-GCD family itself is known in the literature; the relation to earlier results and the limits of the documented literature search are stated explicitly.

</details>


### [111] [A Prime-Power Dichotomy for Nekrasov--Okounkov Hook Lengths and $t$-Core Partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01422)
*Meenu Sharma*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Nekrasov–Okounkov钩长生成函数系数 $a_t(n)$ 与 $t$-core分拆生成函数系数 $b_t(n)$ 在模 $t$ 下的同余 $\,a_t(n)\equiv b_t(n)\pmod t\,$ 对所有 $n\ge0$ 成立，当且仅当 $t$ 是素数幂；并给出素幂的强化与反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望刻画两类分拆论系数序列（Nekrasov–Okounkov钩长生成函数系数与 $t$-core分拆生成函数系数）在算术上"等价"的精确模数条件，即找出使 $\,a_t(n)\equiv b_t(n)\pmod t\,$ 普遍成立的全部 $t$。此前缺乏对模数集合的完整分类，本文旨在给出这一"素数幂二分法"的刻画。

Method: 以生成函数 $F_{t^2}(x)$（其系数为 $a_t(n)$）与 $C_t(x)$（其系数为 $b_t(n)$）为对象，通过比较二者的模 $t$ 展开进行同余分析；对素数情形进一步利用模 $p^2$ 的精细结构分析以加强同余；并通过显式检验 $t=6$ 及任一非素数幂的 $t$ 给出同余失效的反例。

Result: 证明了对每个整数 $t\ge2$，同余 $\,a_t(n)\equiv b_t(n)\pmod t\,$ 对所有 $n\ge0$ 成立当且仅当 $t$ 为素数幂；当 $t=p$ 为素数时，同余可强化为模 $p^2$ 成立；当 $t=6$，更一般地对所有非素数幂的 $t$，该同余不成立。

Conclusion: 素数幂恰是使这两个分拆论系数序列在算术上等价（模 $t$ 同余）的精确模数集合。该结果给出了两类生成函数系数之间同余关系的完整二分刻画，并说明素数结构与合数（非素数幂）情形在算术行为上存在本质差异。

Abstract: Let $a_t(n)$ denote the coefficients of the Nekrasov--Okounkov hook length generating function $F_{t^2}(x)$, and let $b_t(n)$ denote the coefficients of the $t$-core partition generating function $C_t(x)$. We prove a sharp prime-power dichotomy: for every integer $t\ge 2$, the congruence $$ a_t(n)\equiv b_t(n)\pmod t $$ holds for all $n\ge 0$ if and only if $t$ is a prime power. When $t=p$ is prime, the congruence strengthens to modulo $p^2$. For $t=6$, and more generally for every integer that is not a prime power, the congruence fails. This identifies prime powers as the exact moduli for which these two partition-theoretic coefficient sequences are arithmetically equivalent.

</details>


### [112] [Tridimensional character sums with polynomial arguments and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01524)
*Étienne Fouvry,Igor E. Shparlinski,Ping Xi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究带二次及低次多项式 f 的 Dirichlet 特征和，在两类参数条件下给出非平凡上界，并应用于限制短区间内元素构成的 2×2 不可约特征多项式矩阵计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Ganguly 与 Rajan (2023) 关于短区间内 2×2 矩阵（元素取自短段）且特征多项式不可约的计数中间结果启发，需要更强的特征和估计以放宽对区间长度的限制。

Method: 考虑大素数 p 与非平凡 Dirichlet 特征 χ 下的三重特征和 Σ_{a∼A}Σ_{b∼B}Σ_{c∼C} α(a,b)β(c)χ(f(a)+bc)，f∈Z[X] 次数 k 较小，α、β 为复系数；通过解析数论方法（如 Weyl 差分/van der Corput、Cauchy–Schwarz、大筛法等）分别处理两种情形：(1) β≡1，k=2,3,4,5，A,B,C>p^{1/8+ε}；(2) β 一般，k=2,3，A,B,C>p^{1/6+ε}。

Result: 在情形 (1) 与情形 (2) 的相应参数范围内，得到该特征和的非平凡上界，突破了已有结果的适用区间。这些新界可将 2×2 矩阵的计数推广到更短的区间段。

Conclusion: 所建立的特征和估计改进并推广了 Ganguly–Rajan 的相关中间结果，从而允许在更短的区间内统计特征多项式不可约的 2×2 矩阵，深化了短区间上多项式型特征和与矩阵计数之间的联系。

Abstract: Let $p$ be a large prime and $χ$ a non-trivial Dirichlet character modulo $p$. We study the character sum \[ \sum_{a \sim A} \sum_{b \sim B} \sum_{c \sim C} α(a,b) β(c)χ(f(a) + bc), \] where $z \sim Z$ means $Z\le z < 2Z$, $f \in \mathbb{Z}[X]$ is of small degree $k$, and $\boldsymbolα=(α(a,b))_{a\sim A,b\sim B}$ and $\boldsymbolβ=(β(c))_{c\sim C}$ are two complex coefficients. We prove non-trivial upper bounds for this sum in either of the two cases: (1) $\boldsymbolβ\equiv1$, $k=2,3,4,5$ and $A,B,C>p^{\frac{1}{8}+\varepsilon}$, (2) $\boldsymbolβ$ general, $k=2,3$ and $A,B,C>p^{\frac{1}{6}+\varepsilon}$, where $\varepsilon>0$ is fixed.
  This work was originally motivated by an intermediate result of Ganguly and Rajan (2023) on counting $2\times2$ matrices over $\mathbb{F}_p$ with irreducible characteristic polynomials, where the entries are in short segments. The new bounds here allow us to count such matrices in much shorter segments.

</details>


### [113] [Uniform estimate of Lang-Weil type for counting points of schemes over finite rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01748)
*Kien Huu Nguyen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限环 $\mathbb{Z}/p^m\mathbb{Z}$ 上有限型 $\mathbb{Z}$-概型的点数，建立了用 $m$、$X(\mathbb{Z}/p^{m-1}\mathbb{Z})$ 及 $p$-adic Igusa 局部 zeta 函数极点表示的估计，可视为 Lang-Weil 估计的归纳版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Igusa 关于指数和的猜想与 Lang-Weil 估计的启发，作者希望对有限环上的概型点数给出类似 Lang-Weil 的定量估计，并将其与指数和猜想及非有理奇点情形下的已知结果联系起来。

Method: 使用奇点消解与 Denef 关于 Igusa 局部 zeta 函数的公式等常规工具，并引入 Hermite 插值公式研究 Igusa 局部 zeta 函数的分子；同时对 $p$-adic 分析进行深入研究，用适当的广义对数典范阈值给出 Igusa 局部 zeta 函数非平凡极点实部的上界。

Result: 建立了 $X(\mathbb{Z}/p^m\mathbb{Z})$ 关于 $m\geq 2$ 与 $p$ 一致的估计；当 $X_{\mathbb{Q}}$ 不是仅有有理奇点的局部完全交时，该估计与 Igusa 指数和猜想的预期界一致；当 $X_{\mathbb{Q}}$ 是仅有有理奇点的局部完全交时，该估计非常接近预期界。该结果可类比 Cluckers、Mustaţǎ 与作者 2019 年关于非有理奇点多形式指数和模素数幂的估计。

Conclusion: 作者给出了有限环上概型点数的归纳型 Lang-Weil 估计，并通过同一方法在有理性奇点情形下逼近 Igusa 指数和猜想；此外给出了 Igusa 局部 zeta 函数非平凡极点实部的上界，为未来应用奠定基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the number of points of $\mathbb{Z}$-schemes $X$ of finite type over the finite rings $\mathbb{Z}/p^m\mathbb{Z}$ for positive integers $m$ and prime numbers $p$. Motivated by Igusa's conjecture for exponential sums and the Lang-Weil estimate, we establish an estimate of $X(\mathbb{Z}/p^m\mathbb{Z})$ in terms of $m$, $X(\mathbb{Z}/p^{m-1}\mathbb{Z})$ and poles of the $p$-adic Igusa local zeta function of $X$ uniformly in $m\geq 2$ and $p$. Our estimate might be viewed as an inductive version for counting points of schemes over finite rings of the Lang-Weil estimate. Our estimate agrees with the expected bound of Igusa's conjecture for exponential sums if $X_{\mathbb{Q}}$ is not a locally complete intersection having only rational singularities. This can be regarded as an analogous version of the estimate of exponential sums modulo powers of primes of polynomials with non-rational singularities given by Cluckers, Mustaţǎ and the author in 2019. In the case that $X_{\mathbb{Q}}$ is a locally complete intersection having only rational singularities, our estimate is very close to the expected bound of Igusa's conjecture for exponential sums. To prove our estimate, beside using the usual tools such as resolutions of singularities and Denef's formula for Igusa local zeta functions, we also use the Hermit interpolation formula to study the numerators of Igusa local zeta functions. By the same method, we are able to approach Igusa's conjecture for exponential sums in the case of rational singularities. Lastly, to have further applications of our result in the future, we study heavily on $p$-adic analysis to give an upper bound of the real parts of non-trivial poles of Igusa local zeta functions in terms of suitable generalized log canonical thresholds.

</details>


### [114] [Quadratic distances in even dimensions over prime fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01795)
*Thang Pham,Chun-Yen Shen,Dung The Tran,Boqing Xue*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域上非退化二次型距离集的基数下界问题，证明了当 $|E|\geq p^m$ 时 $|\Delta_Q(E)|$ 的一个与 $p,m,Q$ 无关的绝对常数意义下的下界，并在平面情形 $m=1$ 给出了（在对数因子意义下）最优的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二次型距离集 $\Delta_Q(E)=\{Q(x-y):x,y\in E\}$ 是加法组合与有限域几何中的核心研究对象，与经典的 Erdős 距离问题及扩展图、和-积现象密切相关。已有工作多关注欧氏空间或特定维数，而对一般奇数素数、任意维数且 Witt index 为 $m-1$ 的非退化二次型，在大集合范围内的统一且显式的基数下界仍不充分。本文旨在建立这类二次型距离集在 $|E|\geq p^m$ 时的量化下界，并厘清平面情形的最优性。

Method: 设 $p$ 为奇素数，$m\geq1$，$Q$ 为 $\mathbb{F}_p^{2m}$ 上 Witt index 为 $m-1$ 的非退化二次型。对非空 $E\subseteq\mathbb{F}_p^{2m}$ 定义距离集 $\Delta_Q(E)$。作者综合运用有限域上的加法组合工具、二次型与极空间几何结构、以及傅里叶/特征和方法，对距离集的表示函数进行估计；通过将集合按极大全迷向子空间及相应的锥面结构进行分解，并借助覆盖与计数论证，得到 $|\Delta_Q(E)|$ 的下界，其中隐含常数与 $p$、$m$、$Q$ 无关。对平面情形 $m=1$ 采用更精细的计数与对数型分块处理得到最优阶估计。

Result: 主要结果：当 $|E|\geq p^m$ 时，对任意这样的 $Q$ 有 $$|\Delta_Q(E)|\gg \frac{p}{\log\bigl(2+p^{m+1}/|E|\bigr)}$$ 其中隐含常数绝对且不依赖于 $p$、$m$ 与 $Q$。平面情形 $m=1$ 进一步得到 $$|\Delta_Q(E)|\gg\frac{|E|}{\log(2|E|)},\qquad (1\leq |E|\leq p),$$ 该估计在对数因子意义下是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文给出了 Witt index 为 $m-1$ 的非退化二次型距离集在 $|E|\geq p^m$ 时的一致下界，隐含常数与 $p$、$m$、$Q$ 无关；平面情形 $m=1$ 的界在对数因子下达到最优。结果表明二次型距离集的增长在维数与集合规模之间呈现对数修正的线性/常数型行为，为有限域上的距离问题提供了统一刻画，并自然引出对一般 Witt index、更小集合规模以及高维情形最优阶的进一步研究。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime, let $m\geq1$ be an integer, and let $Q$ be a nondegenerate quadratic form on $\mathbb{F}_p^{2m}$ with Witt index $m-1$. For a nonempty set $E\subseteq\mathbb{F}_p^{2m}$, write $Δ_Q(E)=\{Q(x-y):x,y\in E\}$. We prove that, whenever $|E|\geq p^m$, $$ |Δ_Q(E)|\gg \frac{p}{\log\bigl(2+p^{m+1}/|E|\bigr)}, $$ with an absolute implied constant independent of $p$, $m$ and $Q$. In the planar case $m=1$, we also prove that $$ |Δ_Q(E)|\gg\frac{|E|}{\log(2|E|)}, \qquad (1\leq |E|\leq p), $$ which is optimal up to a logarithmic factor.

</details>


### [115] [Averages of Artin characters and their relation to the Davenport--Heilbronn theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01837)
*Victor Ahlquist*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了有理数域三次扩张计数函数误差项的上界，将其指数降至θ<2/3；在GRH下进一步改进至2/3-1/51+ε，并对光滑计数问题得到O(X^{1/2+ε})。方法通过将判别式约化筛法与三次域Artin L-函数的Artin特征平均相联系，并将一阶密度问题的容许支撑从σ<2/5提升至σ<1（GRH下），证明至少75%的L-函数在中心点非零。在函数域𝔽_q(T)上得到类似结果且为无条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Bhargava、Taniguchi和Thorne关于有理数域三次扩张计数函数误差项的已有结果，并在GRH下进一步优化，同时研究一阶密度与中心点非零比例以及函数域情形。

Method: 将Bhargava、Shankar和Tsimerman的判别式约化筛法与三次域K对应Artin L-函数ζ_K(s)/ζ(s)的Artin特征平均相联系；方法是对研究该族Artin L-函数一阶密度的自然推广。在函数域𝔽_q(T)（q与2、3互素）上研究类似问题。

Result: 得到计数函数误差项为O_ε(X^{θ+ε})，θ<2/3；GRH下进一步为O(X^{2/3-1/51+ε})。光滑计数问题误差项为O(X^{1/2+ε})（GRH下），条件性改进Shankar、Södergren和Templier的结果。一阶密度容许支撑由σ<2/5提升至σ<1（GRH下），证明至少75%的L-函数在中心点非零；函数域上同样得到至少75%的非零比例及O(X^{1/2+ε})的函数域计数误差界，且为无条件。

Conclusion: 本文在三次域计数误差、一阶密度支撑及中心点非零比例方面均给出改进，方法可处理有限多个分裂条件；函数域情形结果无条件成立。

Abstract: We bound the error term in the counting function of cubic extensions of $\mathbb{Q}$ by $\mathcal{O}_ε\left(X^{θ+ε}\right)$, with $θ< 2/3$, improving upon results due to Bhargava, Taniguchi, and Thorne. The proof proceeds by relating a certain discriminant-reducing sieve, due to Bhargava, Shankar, and Tsimerman, to averages of Artin characters associated with the Artin $L$-function $ζ_K(s)/ζ(s)$, with $K$ a cubic field. Conditional on the Generalised Riemann Hypothesis (GRH) for Dedekind zeta functions $ζ_K(s)$, we obtain further savings and show that the error term is $\mathcal{O}\left(X^{2/3-1/51+ε}\right)$. Moreover, for the analogous smooth counting problem, we obtain a bound $\mathcal{O}(X^{1/2+ε})$ for the error term, conditionally improving results of Shankar, Södergren, and Templier. Our arguments can also handle finitely many splitting conditions.
  The methods we use to study cubic fields are natural extensions of methods which are used to study the one-level density associated with the above family of Artin $L$-functions. In this direction, we improve the admissible support $[-σ,σ]$, from the previously known admissible value $σ< 2/5$, to $σ< 1$, conditional on the GRH. This also proves that at least $75\%$ of these $L$-functions are non-vanishing at the central point, conditionally improving upon results of Shankar, Södergren and Templier.
  Finally, one may study similar questions over a rational function field $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$, with $q$ coprime to $2$ and $3$. Here, we obtain a proportion of non-vanishing of at least $75\%$ for the analogous family of $L$-functions. Furthermore, we obtain a bound $\mathcal{O}(X^{1/2+ε})$ for the error term in the counting function of cubic function fields, improving previous results of the author. Over function fields, our results are unconditional.

</details>


### [116] [Square products of factorials and a conjecture of Erdős and Graham](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01899)
*Fedir Yudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文确定了 Erdős–Graham 所问的 $D_k(X)$（使 $n!$ 成为 $k$ 个不同阶乘之积中最大因子、且该积为完全平方的 $n\le X$ 之数目）对每个 $k$ 的增长阶，并给出 $D_3$ 的渐近公式及 $D_5,D_6$ 的阶估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős 与 Graham 提出确定 $D_k(X)$（$3\le k\le6$）的增长阶问题，并猜想 $D_6(X)\gg X$。此前对 $k=3,\dots,6$ 的阶缺乏完整刻画，本文旨在解决这一遗留问题并给出 $k=3$ 的精确渐近式。

Method: 将 $n!$ 表为平方的问题归结为 Pell 方程并给出统一上界，与 Gallagher 大筛法相平衡得到指数 $2/5$，余项限制由 Weil 界提供；对 $k=5,6$ 限制到含大于 $X^{1-\alpha}$ 素因子的整数，结合 Matomäki–Radziwiłł–Shao–Tao–Teräväinen 的素数等分布估计与大筛法排除较短表示；附录中用有限阿贝尔群的零和定理构造完全 $m$ 次幂。

Result: 证明 $D_3(X)=\kappa_3\sqrt{X}+O_\varepsilon(X^{2/5+\varepsilon})$，其中 $\kappa_3=2.7097\ldots$ 为显式常数；并证明 $D_5(X)\asymp D_6(X)\asymp X$。结合经典结果，确定了所有 $k$ 时 $D_k(X)$ 的增长阶。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了 $D_k(X)$ 对任意 $k$ 的增长阶，验证了 Erdős–Graham 关于 $D_6(X)\gg X$ 的猜想，并额外构造了由固定仿射函数参数的、有界个不同阶乘之积构成的完全 $m$ 次幂。

Abstract: For $n\ge2$ let $F(n)$ be the least $k\ge2$ such that $n!$ is the largest factor in a product of $k$ distinct factorials that is a perfect square, and let $D_k(X)$ be the number of $n\le X$ with $F(n)=k$. Erdos and Graham asked for the order of growth of $D_k(X)$ for $3\le k\le6$, and conjectured that $D_6(X)\gg X$. We prove that $D_3(X)=κ_3\sqrt X+O_\varepsilon(X^{2/5+\varepsilon})$ with an explicit constant $κ_3=2.7097\ldots$, and that $D_5(X)\asymp D_6(X)\asymp X$. Together with classical facts, this determines the order of growth of $D_k(X)$ for every $k$. The exponent $2/5$ comes from balancing a uniform bound for Pell equations against Gallagher's larger sieve, with residue restrictions supplied by the Weil bound. For five and six factors we restrict to integers with a prime factor exceeding $X^{1-α}$, where $α>0$ is small and fixed. We exclude shorter representations by combining an equidistribution estimate for primes of Matomaki, Radziwill, Shao, Tao and Teravainen with the large sieve. In an appendix we use a zero-sum theorem for finite abelian groups to construct, for every $m\ge2$, perfect $m$-th powers that are products of a bounded number of distinct factorials with arguments given by fixed affine functions.

</details>


### [117] [Supersingularity and Superspeciality Verification of Abelian Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01924)
*Maria Corte-Real Santos,Gioella Lorenzon,Krijn Reijnders*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文首次研究如何高效验证有限域上阿贝尔曲面（及任意维阿贝尔簇）的超奇异（supersingular）、极简/极大性以及雅可比超特殊（superspecial），提出 $O(\log p)$ 的蒙特卡洛算法与可判定算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超奇异阿贝尔曲面在基于同源的密码学中至关重要，但目前缺乏高效算法来判断给定阿贝尔曲面是否超奇异，这构成了该领域的一个基础缺口。

Method: 通过对 $\mathbb{F}_p$ 上超奇异雅可比结构进行细致分析，设计出验证 $\mathbb{F}_p$ 上阿贝尔曲面超奇异的蒙特卡洛算法；在阶数光滑情形下进一步给出可判定的确定性算法。同时将技术推广至任意维阿贝尔簇的极简/极大性验证，以及任意维雅可比超特殊性的验证。

Result: 得到 $O(\log p)$ 时间、失败概率可忽略的蒙特卡洛超奇异验证算法；当阶数光滑时给出确定性可判定算法；并导出验证任意维阿贝尔簇极简/极大性及任意维雅可比超特殊性的高效算法。

Conclusion: 该工作开启了超奇异阿贝尔曲面高效验证的研究方向，为基于同源密码学提供了新的算法工具，并可推广至更高维与相关性质（极简/极大、超特殊）的验证。

Abstract: Supersingular abelian surfaces are essential in isogeny-based cryptography. Despite this, we have no efficient algorithm to verify if a given abelian surface is supersingular. In this work, we initiate this research topic by giving an efficient Monte Carlo algorithm to verify if an abelian surface over $\mathbb{F}_p$ is supersingular in $O(\log p)$ with negligible failure probability, and an efficient conclusive algorithm if the order is smooth. We derive this algorithm by a careful analysis on the structure of supersingular Jacobians over $\mathbb{F}_p$. Furthermore, we derive efficient algorithms to verify if an abelian variety of any dimension is minimal or maximal, and to verify if a Jacobian of any dimension is superspecial.

</details>


### [118] [Infinite free pro-p products as absolute Galois groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01966)
*Tamar Bar-On*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Mel'nikov判别法的一个推广版本（Haran也曾证明过），并将其用于联系一个pro-p群层上的自由pronite积与自由pro-p积的绝对Galois群实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Mel'nikov判别法推广，以便处理更一般的绝对Galois群实现问题，特别是pro-p群层上的自由积结构。

Method: 证明Mel'nikov判别法的广义版本，并应用该判别法将自由pronite积与自由pro-p积的绝对Galois群实现联系起来。

Result: 建立了Mel'nikov判别法的推广形式，并通过它连接了一个pro-p群层上自由pronite积与自由pro-p积的绝对Galois群实现。

Conclusion: 推广的Mel'nikov判别法为研究pro-p群层自由积的绝对Galois群实现提供了有效工具，拓展了相关理论框架。

Abstract: In this paper we prove a generalized version of Mel'nikov's Criterion, which has also been proved by Haran, and use it in order to connect the realization as absolute Galois groups of free pronite and free pro-p products of a sheaf of pro-p groups.

</details>


### [119] [On the prime geodesic theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01998)
*Alberto Acosta Reche*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了模群素测地定理的误差项，并将结果推广到 Q 上任意不定四元数代数的任意同余子群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 素测地定理的误差项估计是数论中的经典问题，此前模群情形下的误差项仍有改进空间，且已有结果多局限于模群或特定子群，缺乏对更一般同余子群的统一处理。

Method: 通过研究 Q 上不定四元数代数及其极大序对应的算术曲面（或相应的自守形式与 Selberg 迹公式框架），将模群情形的方法推广到任意同余子群。

Result: 在模群情形下得到了改进的素测地定理误差项；进一步地，该改进对 Q 上任意不定四元数代数的任意同余子群均成立。

Conclusion: 本文给出了一个更一般且更精确的素测地定理误差项估计，统一覆盖了模群及四元数代数情形下的同余子群。

Abstract: We improve the error term in the prime geodesic theorem for the modular group. More generally, our result applies to any congruence subgroup of any indefinite quaternion algebra over $\mathbb{Q}$.

</details>


### [120] [On The Index of Polynomial Compositions over Valued Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02111)
*Anuj Jakhar,Ravi Kalwaniya,Shanta Laishram,Prabhakar Yadav*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究多项式复合 \(f(h(x))\) 的单基因性，其中 \(h(x)\) 为三项式，并在任意 Krull 赋值环上给出判别式公式与单基因性判定，进一步推广到多项式迭代与二项式迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数数域是否具有幂整基是经典问题，但对由多项式复合和动力迭代定义的域较难处理，本文旨在解决此类复合与迭代多项式的单基因性判定。

Method: 在任意 Krull 赋值环上考虑首一多项式 \(f(x)\) 与三项式 \(h(x)\) 的复合 \(f(h(x))\)，推导复合多项式的判别式显式公式，并利用 \(h(x)\) 临界值的无平方条件刻画单基因性；随后将方法推广到多项式迭代和二项式迭代。

Result: 给出了复合判别式的显式公式，建立了复合单基因性与 \(f(x)\) 单基因性及 \(h(x)\) 临界值无平方条件之间的关系；得到二项式迭代单基因性的判定准则，并构造出新的无限族单基因域；同时给出定量结果和若干例子。

Conclusion: 论文为复合与迭代多项式的单基因性提供了统一判别工具，扩展了幂整基理论，并通过无限族与实例验证了准则的有效性。

Abstract: Determining whether an algebraic number field admits a power integral basis is a classical problem, but it can be difficult for fields defined by polynomial compositions and dynamical iterates. In this paper, we study the monogeneity of compositions $f(h(x))$, where $f(x)$ and $h(x)$ are monic polynomials over an arbitrary Krull valuation ring and $h(x)$ is a trinomial. We derive explicit formulas for the discriminant of the composition and use them to characterize when $f(h(x))$ generates a monogenic field. In particular, we relate the monogeneity of the composition to that of $f(x)$ and to explicit square-free conditions on the critical values of $h(x)$. We further extend the results to polynomial iteration and obtain a criterion for the monogeneity of binomial iterates, yielding new infinite families of monogenic fields. Finally, we give quantitative results and illustrate our criteria with several examples.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [A relaxation system for the low-Mach limit of kinetic equations: Stability and higher order asymptotic preserving scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00811)
*Giacomo Dimarco,Axel Klar,Theresa Köfler,Lorenzo Pareschi,Sudarshan Tiwari,Yizhou Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双曲松弛系统用于低马赫数极限下动理学方程模拟，基于缩放BGK模型的微观-宏观分解，推导出与不可压缩极限相适应的Grad 13矩松弛系统，并证明其结构稳定性，同时构建高阶渐近保持(AP)数值框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低马赫数极限下模拟动理学方程面临刚性问题：当Knudsen数与马赫数趋于零时，直接数值求解代价高昂，且需要数值格式在不同物理区制下保持稳定与一致。因此需要一种能自动退化到不可压缩Navier-Stokes极限、且兼具结构稳定性与计算效率的建模与数值方法。

Method: 对缩放BGK模型进行微观-宏观分解，将分布函数重构为宏观平衡部分与微观非平衡余项耦合系统；再将微观偏差投影到正交多项式基上，得到封闭的矩松弛系统，即线性双曲部分配以适配不可压缩极限的松弛项的Grad 13矩系统。数值上采用IMEX Runge-Kutta格式保证时间高阶精度，空间上用有限差分WENO重构与中心差分实现高阶分辨率，构造渐近保持(AP)框架。

Result: 证明了模型在不可压缩Navier-Stokes极限下的结构稳定性；所提格式在不同物理区制下保持一致稳定性与一致性，当缩放参数趋于零时自动退化为不可压缩热Navier-Stokes极限的一致高阶离散。一维与二维数值实验验证了理论结果，显示计算成本显著降低，并在宽广的Knudsen数与马赫数范围内具有稳健性能。

Conclusion: 该工作通过双曲松弛建模与高阶AP-IMEX数值框架的结合，为低马赫数极限下动理学方程提供了一种结构稳定、跨区制一致且高效的模拟方案，数值实验证实了其理论性质与计算优势。

Abstract: This work introduces a hyperbolic relaxation system designed for the simulation of kinetic equations in the low-Mach number limit. The methodology is built upon a micro-macro decomposition of the scaled BGK model, which reformulates the kinetic distribution function into a coupled system consisting of a macroscopic equilibrium part and a microscopic non-equilibrium remainder. By projecting the microscopic deviations onto a set of orthogonal polynomials, we derive a closed moment relaxation system. The resulting system is a version of Grad's 13 moment system with a linear hyperbolic part and a relaxation adapted to the incompressible limit. We prove the model's structural stability in the incompressible Navier-Stokes limit. Moreover, we develop a high order Asymptotic-Preserving (AP) numerical framework using Implicit-Explicit (IMEX) Runge Kutta schemes for temporal accuracy and finite difference WENO reconstructions as well as central difference approximations for high order spatial resolution. The proposed scheme ensures uniform stability and consistency across different physical regimes, automatically degenerating into a consistent high order discretization of the incompressible thermal Navier-Stokes limit as the scaling parameter vanishes. Numerical experiments in one and two dimensions corroborate the theoretical findings, demonstrating significant reductions in computational cost and robust performance across a wide range of Knudsen and Mach numbers.

</details>


### [122] [Matrix-Free Moment-Matching Method for Reduced-Order Modeling of Quadratic-Bilinear Descriptor Systems with Multiple Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00830)
*Zeyuan Gao,Cheol W. Lee,Oleg Zikanov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种面向多输入二次-双线性描述子系统的矩匹配降阶方法，支持多输入同时作用于微分与代数方程，构建分通道投影空间，采用无矩阵算法，并在二维流动问题中验证了降阶模型对多时变输入瞬态响应的精确复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模计算流体力学等问题中的多输入二次-双线性描述子系统维度过高，传统降阶方法难以高效处理多输入作用于微分与代数方程的情形，且显式装配与存储全阶矩阵/张量代价巨大。

Method: 基于矩匹配的降阶框架，为不同输入通道及输入组合分别构造投影空间；采用无矩阵算法，无需显式装配或存储全阶系统的矩阵与张量即可计算降阶矩阵和张量。

Result: 针对二维流动问题的数值实验表明，所得降阶模型能够准确复现全阶模型在多个时变输入下的瞬态响应。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效处理多输入二次-双线性描述子系统，兼具多输入通道投影空间构造与无矩阵实现的优势，适用于大规模计算流体力学问题。

Abstract: This paper develops a new moment-matching order reduction method for multi-input quadratic-bilinear descriptor systems. The proposed approach accommodates multiple inputs acting on both the differential and algebraic equations and constructs separate projection spaces associated with different input channels and input combinations. The method uses a matrix-free algorithm, allowing the reduced-order matrices and tensors to be computed without explicitly assembling or storing the full-order system matrices and tensors. This feature makes the proposed method particularly suitable for large-scale computational fluid dynamics problems. Numerical experiments carried out for two-dimensional flow problems demonstrate that the resulting reduced-order models accurately reproduce the transient responses of the full-order models under multiple time-varying inputs.

</details>


### [123] [Uncertainty Quantification for Landweber Iteration with Randomized Normal-Operator Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00882)
*Anuj Abhishek,Sean Holman*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对线性不适定反问题中Landweber迭代的不确定性量化，借助广义信念推断将数据噪声传播到重建结果，并结合正则化偏差理论界构造置信区间；同时用随机SVD低秩近似正算子以降低计算成本，数值实验验证了其重建精度与不确定性估计的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代方法虽在求解线性不适定反问题中应用广泛，其确定性收敛（半收敛）已有充分研究，但由观测数据随机噪声导致的重建不确定性量化问题关注不足。本文以Radon变换为例，填补Landweber迭代在不确定性量化方面的空白。

Method: 从统计角度解释Landweber重建，分析测量数据的不确定性如何通过重建映射传播，其视角与广义信念推断密切相关——通过反演观测数据与未知固定量之间的关系诱导参数不确定性。将所得随机不确定性与正则化偏差的理论上界结合，构造重建解的置信区间。为降低大规模问题中反复作用于正算子的高昂代价，采用随机奇异值分解获得Radon变换对应正规算子的低秩近似，并将该近似纳入不确定性量化，同时把随机化技术引入的额外随机误差纳入分析框架。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提方法能够在显著降低计算成本的同时，给出准确的重建结果和可靠的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 本文将Landweber迭代的重建不确定性统计解释与正则化偏差界结合，构建置信区间，并利用随机SVD低秩近似实现高效计算，为线性不适定反问题提供了一种兼顾精度、可靠性与计算效率的不确定性量化方案。

Abstract: Iterative methods are extremely popular for solving linear ill-posed inverse problems. While deterministic convergence, or more precisely, semi-convergence of such methods is widely studied, the problem of quantifying uncertainty in the resulting reconstructions due to random noise in the observed data has received far less attention. In this work, we study uncertainty quantification for the Landweber iteration in linear inverse problems using the Radon transform as a motivating example. We interpret the Landweber reconstruction statistically by analyzing how uncertainty in the measured data is propagated through the reconstruction map. This perspective is closely related to generalized fiducial inference, where uncertainty about the parameter is induced by inverting the relation between the observed data and the unknown (fixed) quantity. We combine the resulting stochastic uncertainty with a theoretical bound on the regularization bias to construct confidence intervals for the reconstructed solution. The evaluation of the resulting uncertainty estimates require repeated operations with the forward operator that can become expensive in large-scale problems. To reduce this cost, we use randomized singular value decomposition to obtain a low-rank approximation of the normal operator associated with the Radon transform and incorporate this approximation in quantifying uncertainty. {In particular, this requires that the additional randomization error introduced due to the use of such randomized techniques be included in our approach for uncertainty quantification.} Numerical results show that the proposed method provides accurate reconstructions and reliable uncertainty estimates at substantially reduced computational cost.

</details>


### [124] [A Deferred Correction, Continuous Galerkin Method for Curvilinear Staggered-Grid Lagrangian Hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00932)
*Steven Walton,Svetlana Tokareva,Nathaniel Morgan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种用于拉格朗日流体动力学的连续Galerkin延迟修正（cG-DeC）方法，结合高阶连续有限元离散与显式DeC时间积分器，避免全局稀疏质量矩阵求逆，并引入兼容的超粘性模型以稳定强激波，同时分析守恒性并与cG-RK方法对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉格朗日流体动力学在强激波和复杂流动下需要高精度且稳健的数值格式。传统高阶连续有限元方法在时间推进中往往需要求解全局稀疏质量矩阵（即每次更新需进行全局求解），计算代价高。同时，显式延迟修正（DeC）方法应用于双曲型偏微分方程时的收敛行为尚未得到充分解释。作者旨在构建一种既保持高阶精度、又避免全局求解、且能处理强激波的格式。

Method: 将高阶连续有限元空间离散与显式延迟修正时间积分器相结合，构造cG-DeC格式，使每次更新无需对全局稀疏质量矩阵求逆。为在强激波下稳定格式，对超粘性模型进行修改，使其与cG-DeC框架兼容，并且构造多调和算子的有限元近似同样无需全局求解。此外给出DeC迭代的一种替代重构形式，具备简单的递归算法结构。分析所得全离散格式的动量与总能量守恒性，并通过数值实验验证精度与稳健性。

Result: 数值实验表明cG-DeC格式具有良好的精度与稳健性，能够处理强激波问题；与连续Galerkin Runge-Kutta（cG-RK）方法的对比进一步展示了该格式的性能特点。所提出的DeC迭代重构形式为显式DeC应用于双曲型偏微分方程时此前观察到的收敛行为提供了理论解释。全离散格式的动量与总能量守恒性得到分析验证。

Conclusion: cG-DeC方法为拉格朗日流体动力学提供了一种高精度、无需全局求解且对强激波稳健的数值框架，其超粘性模型与DeC迭代重构增强了方法的实用性与理论可解释性，守恒性分析与数值对比验证了其有效性。

Abstract: We present a continuous Galerkin, Deferred Correction (cG-DeC) method for the equations of Lagrangian hydrodynamics. The proposed scheme combines a high-order continuous finite element discretization with an explicit Deferred Correction time integrator, yielding a formulation that avoids the inversion of a global sparse mass matrix at each update. To stabilize the method in the presence of strong shocks we introduce a modification of the hyperviscosity model that is compatible with the cG-DeC framework and likewise does not require a global solve to construct the finite element approximation to the polyharmonic operator. We further present an alternative reformulation of the DeC iteration which admits a simple recursive algorithmic structure and provides insight into previously observed convergence behavior of explicit DeC applied to hyperbolic partial differential equations. Conservation of momentum and total energy of the resulting fully discrete scheme is analyzed. A set of numerical experiments illustrates the accuracy and robustness of the cG-DeC scheme. Comparisons with a continuous Galerkin Runge-Kutta (cG-RK) method are provided.

</details>


### [125] [Positivity-preserving scalar auxiliary variable schemes for gradient flows via a quadratic reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01063)
*Qiong-Ao Huang,Zhi-Hao Liu,Ying-Wei Wang,Li-Na Yan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文指出SAV方法中离散标量变量r^{n+1}可能失去正号，违背平方根定义；通过将演化方程由r_t重写为(r²)_t，构造出具有唯一正根的凸二次方程，得到与原始SAV同计算量、无条件能量稳定的新格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAV方法通过正标量r(t)=√(E_N[φ]+C)替换自由能非线性部分，得到线性、无条件能量稳定的梯度流格式。但在离散层面，标准后向欧拉与Crank–Nicolson离散无法保证计算出的r^{n+1}保持为正，这与连续定义中的平方根形式相矛盾，可能损害长时间稳健性。尽管SAV应用广泛，这一微妙却重要的问题却很少受到关注。

Method: 首先定量刻画该失效：推导关于时间步长的尖锐判据与充分条件，并构造显式反例说明在实际相关参数下会发生符号丢失。不同于已有保正变体修改r的定义，本文保留平方根形式，将离散演化从r_t重写为(r²)_t，从而把标量方程转化为常数项严格为负的凸二次方程，保证始终存在唯一正根。对Crank–Nicolson格式，采用乘积形式离散r^{n+1}r^{n}以保持该二次结构，而常规替代格式则不能。

Result: 新格式与原始SAV方法计算代价相同，并被证明具有无条件能量稳定性；针对Cahn–Hilliard方程的数值实验验证了所预测的正性、能量稳定性及收敛阶。

Conclusion: 通过保留平方根定义并重构离散标量方程，可在不增加计算成本的前提下消除SAV离散中的符号丢失问题，获得唯一正根与无条件能量稳定性，为SAV类梯度流格式的长时间稳健性提供了简单而有效的修正。

Abstract: The scalar auxiliary variable (SAV) method replaces the nonlinear part of the free energy by a positive scalar $r(t)=\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{_\mathcal{N}}[φ]+C}>0$, thereby yielding linear, unconditionally energy-stable schemes for gradient flows. At the discrete level, however, the standard backward Euler and Crank--Nicolson discretizations provide no guarantee that the computed $r^{n+1}$ remains positive, an inconsistency with the continuous definition that contradicts the square-root ansatz and may compromise long-time robustness. Although the SAV method has been widely applied, this subtle but consequential issue has received little attention. We first characterize this failure quantitatively by deriving a sharp criterion and a sufficient condition on the time step size, and construct an explicit counterexample showing that sign loss occurs for parameters of practical relevance. Rather than modifying the definition of $r$ as in existing positivity-preserving variants, we retain the square-root form and reformulate the discrete evolution from $r_t$ to $(r^{2})_t$, which converts the scalar equation into a convex quadratic with a strictly negative constant term, always yielding a unique positive root. For the Crank--Nicolson scheme, the product-form discretization $r^{n+1}r^{n}$ preserves this quadratic structure, while conventional alternatives do not. The resulting schemes incur the same computational cost as the original SAV method and are proved unconditionally energy-stable. Numerical experiments for the Cahn--Hilliard equation confirm the predicted positivity, energy stability, and convergence rates.

</details>


### [126] [A higher order lattice-based method for high-dimensional numerical integration using periodization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01113)
*Felix Bartel,Alexander D. Gilbert,Michael Griebel,Frances Y. Kuo,Ian H. Sloan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对高维单位立方体上非周期函数的新型数值积分方法，采用变换格点构造（非等）加权求积规则，并可选子采样。在混合导数平方可积的假设下，收敛率接近 |J|^{-(α-1/2)} 或 |J|^{-α}(1.110721)^s，且可在两者间插值权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维非周期函数的数值积分通常面临维数灾难与端点奇异性问题。标准格点规则适用于周期函数，而直接应用于非周期被积函数时收敛率下降。本文希望构造一种既能保持高维格点求积效率、又能处理非周期被积函数并精确处理非光滑密度的加权求积方法。

Method: 方法包含三个核心步骤：(i) 将被积函数周期化；(ii) 在格点处用核插值逼近光滑周期函数；(iii) 精确积分核插值与（非光滑）乘积密度，并对步骤(ii)和(iii)提供子采样替代方案。整体规则为基于变换格点的（非等）加权求积。

Result: 以 |J| 表示函数求值次数，方法达到接近 |J|^{-(α-1/2)} 的收敛率；另一形式为 |J|^{-α}(1.110721)^s。在有利条件下隐含常数与维数 s 无关。可通过插值两种结果在收敛率与维数增长之间进行权衡。数值实验验证了理论。

Conclusion: 该方法为高维非周期函数积分提供了一种兼具格点求积效率与良好收敛阶的加权求积框架，且能在收敛率与维数依赖之间灵活权衡，理论分析与数值实验一致。

Abstract: We present a novel numerical integration method for non-periodic functions over the high-dimensional unit cube $[0,1]^s$ by a specially crafted (unequally-)weighted quadrature rule using transformed lattice points, with the additional option of subsampling. The method is designed for integrands whose mixed derivatives up to order $α$ are square-integrable with respect to a product Chebyshev density. The ingredients are (i) periodizing the integrand, (ii) approximating the smooth periodic function by a kernel interpolant at lattice points, (iii) integrating exactly the product of the kernel interpolant and the nonsmooth density, together with subsampling alternatives for (ii) and~(iii). With $|J|$ corresponding to the number of function evaluations, we achieve the convergence rate close to the order $|J|^{-(α-1/2)}$ and $|J|^{-α}\,($1.110721$)^s$, where the implied constants are independent of $s$ under favorable conditions. We can interpolate these two results to trade between the convergence rate and the growth in $s$. We provide numerical experiments to demonstrate our theory.

</details>


### [127] [Sign-Switched Antithetic Coupling for Signed Gradient-Particle Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01120)
*Stephen Abkin,Prabir Daripa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出"符号切换配对"(sign-switched pairing)的梯度随机游走方差缩减方法：按位置顺序配对两次模拟，使同号粒子获得相反位移、异号粒子获得相同位移，从而在守恒期望与偏差的前提下显著降低方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度随机游走法用带符号粒子表示解的梯度、通过求和粒子质量恢复解，其采样误差较大。作者希望在保持单次模拟期望与偏差不变的前提下，以低计算成本降低重复场估计的方差。

Method: 对一维对流–扩散方程成对运行模拟，并按粒子位置顺序配对。反射配对(标准对偶抽样)给匹配粒子相反位移；符号切换配对让同号匹配粒子获得相反位移、异号粒子获得相同位移。采用固定配对方式，在最后一步扩散下证明符号切换配对使每对匹配粒子的方差贡献最小，并推导其相对反射配对所消除方差的精确公式。对热方程结果推广至每一步，并数值测量累积方差缩减；在Burgers测试中用试运行确定样本量并在新运行上验证。

Result: 在Burgers测试中，符号切换配对以4次模拟达到均方误差目标，而独立采样需65次、反射配对需34次；与仅匹配同号粒子的同号内配对相比，在8192粒子下方差约低26%；在2048粒子下达到第二个目标所需时间约少40%。该增益在更多剖面、更晚时刻、其他黏度及三次通量下依然保持。

Conclusion: 符号切换配对是一种低成本的方差缩减手段，可有效改进异号粒子相遇处的重复场估计，并在多种设置下稳定优于独立采样、反射配对和同号内配对。

Abstract: Gradient random-walk methods represent the spatial derivative of a solution by signed particles and recover the solution by summing their masses. We reduce the sampling error of such simulations for one-dimensional convection-diffusion equations by running them in pairs with particles matched in order of position. Reflected pairing, the standard antithetic choice, gives matched particles opposite displacements. In sign-switched pairing, matched particles of equal sign receive opposite displacements and those of opposite sign the same displacement. Each simulation keeps the probability distribution of the solver, so the pair average has the expectation and bias of one simulation. For a fixed matching, we prove that sign-switched pairing minimizes the variance contribution of each matched pair at the last diffusion step. We also derive an exact formula for the variance it removes relative to reflected pairing. For the heat equation both results extend to every step, and we measure the accumulated variance reduction numerically. In Burgers tests, with sample sizes chosen from pilot runs and checked on fresh runs, sign-switched pairing meets a mean-squared-error target with 4 simulations, compared with 65 for independent sampling and 34 for reflected pairing. Against within-sign pairing, which matches only particles of equal sign, it has about 26% lower variance with 8192 particles. With 2048 particles it meets a second target in about 40% less time. The gain persists for further profiles, later times, other viscosities, and a cubic flux, supporting sign-switched pairing as an inexpensive way to improve repeated field estimates where particles of opposite sign meet.

</details>


### [128] [Dirichlet--Neumann waveform relaxation for heterogeneous heat equations: fully discrete $l^2$ analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01475)
*Philipp Birken,Niklas Kotarsky*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对不同空间域上通过低维界面耦合的两类线性热方程（共轭传热模型），采用Dirichlet–Neumann波形松弛方法进行分区求解，利用指数加权Fourier技术在全离散情形下推导出有限时间$T$的$l^2$误差估计，并验证其准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标在于发展更高效的分区式（partitioned）求解方法，为此需要可靠的误差估计。现有研究已有时离散估计，但缺乏全离散情形下对网格尺寸、时间步长依赖性的严格刻画。

Method: 对低维界面耦合的两个线性热方程，采用Dirichlet–Neumann波形松弛实现分区求解（各子问题可用独立代码求解）；使用指数加权Fourier技术，在全离散设置下推导$l^2$范数下有限时间$T$的误差估计。

Result: 新估计同时描述了线性与超线性收敛行为；当子求解器CFL数较大时，全离散估计接近已有的时间离散估计，且与$Δx_1$、$Δx_2$无关；当CFL数较小时，收敛行为依赖于网格比$Δx_1/Δx_2$。数值实验表明该估计在广泛的网格尺寸、时间步长与$T$范围内均准确。

Conclusion: 所提全离散误差估计可靠且具有良好鲁棒性，为分析和发展更高效的分区耦合传热求解方法提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We consider two coupled linear heat equations on different spatial domains that interact through a lower dimensional interface. This models conjugate heat transfer. The problem is solved using Dirichlet--Neumann waveform relaxation. This allows the subproblems to be solved using separate codes, a so called partitioned approach. Our overall goal is to develop more efficient partitioned methods, and to this end, we want reliable error estimates.
  Here, we use an exponentially weighted Fourier technique to derive new error estimates in $l^2$ for finite time $T$ in the fully discrete setting. These describe both linear and superlinear behavior. We show that the fully discrete estimate is close to a previously obtained time discrete estimate and independent of $Δx_1$ and $Δx_2$ when the CFL number in the subsolvers is large. We also show that the convergence behaviour depends on the ratio $Δx_1/Δx_2$ when the CFL number is small.
  Our numerical experiments show that the fully discrete estimate is accurate across a wide range of grid sizes $Δx_1, Δx_2$, time step sizes $Δt$ and $T$.

</details>


### [129] [Finite element exterior calculus for spectra and pseudospectra of advection-diffusion of differential forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01501)
*Daniele Boffi,Kaibo Hu,Yizhou Liang,Umberto Zerbinati*

Main category: math.NA

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值发电机研究数十年来一直是流体力学中的活跃方向，存在诸多挑战与开放问题；与此同时，保结构方法与有限元外微积分（FEEC）的发展，为重新审视数值发电机研究并探索计算中的开放问题提供了契机。

Method: 提出面向发电机问题的FEEC方法，构建保结构有限元格式，用于计算微分形式对流-扩散算子的谱与伪谱，格式及其理论分析基于有限元de Rham复形。

Result: 该摘要未给出具体数值结果，仅说明所提出的保结构有限元格式可用于计算相关算子的谱与伪谱，并给出了基于de Rham复形的理论分析框架。

Conclusion: 保结构有限元外微积分方法为数值发电机研究提供了新的计算途径，有望用于探讨该领域既有的开放计算问题。

Abstract: Numerical investigations of dynamo action have remained active in fluid mechanics over the past decades, presenting numerous challenges and open problems. Meanwhile, the development of structure-preserving methods and finite element exterior calculus (FEEC) inspires a revisit of numerical dynamo studies and an exploration of existing open questions in computation. In this paper, we present a FEEC approach for dynamo problems. In particular, we investigate structure-preserving finite element schemes for computing the spectra and pseudospectra of advection-diffusion operators of differential forms. The schemes and their analysis are based on finite element de Rham complexes.

</details>


### [130] [An Unfitted Hybrid High-Order Method for the Elastodynamics Problem with Imperfect Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01504)
*Peiqi Huang,Erik Burman*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文设计并分析了一种用于含线滑移型不完美界面弹性波方程的非拟合混合高阶(HHO)方法。网格不拟合界面，切胞中未知量加倍，采用胞凝聚处理小切，界面处不设未知量。方法核心是局部对称应变重构与界面稳定化。半离散问题证明了双线性形式的强制性与连续性，得到能量误差 $h^{k+1}$ 阶和 $L^2$ 误差 $h^{k+2}$ 阶估计，常数与柔度参数及界面切割方式无关。结合Newmark或SDIRK格式进行时间离散，二维数值实验验证了收敛率、对柔度的鲁棒性及弹性波穿越未分辨滑移界面的传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对由两组件组成、被线滑移型不完美界面分隔的介质中的弹性波方程，界面处牵引力连续而位移跳跃通过柔度张量 $\bK=α\bI+(β-α)\bn\otimes\bn$ 与牵引力成正比。目标是在网格不拟合界面的条件下构建高阶数值方法，同时保证对柔度参数和界面切割方式不敏感。

Method: 采用非拟合混合高阶(HHO)方法。网格不与界面拟合：在切胞中离散未知量加倍，通过胞凝聚过程处理小切，界面处不附加未知量。方法两个关键：一是在每个切割子胞内进行局部对称应变重构，借助正则化界面刚度 $\bS_h=(h_Tδ^{-1}\bI+\bK)^{-1}$ 引入界面条件(遵循Hansbo-Hansbo思想)；二是由同一矩阵构造界面稳定化项。

Result: 对空间半离散问题证明了离散双线性形式的强制性与连续性，推导出能量误差 $h^{k+1}$ 阶估计和 $L^2$ 误差 $h^{k+2}$ 阶估计，且常数与柔度参数及界面切割网格的方式无关。二维数值实验验证了 $k\in\{1,2,3\}$ 的收敛率、对柔度跨越十六个数量级的鲁棒性，并展示了弹性波穿越未分辨滑移界面的传播。

Conclusion: 该非拟合HHO方法结合Newmark格式(精确守恒离散能量)或最高四阶单对角隐式Runge-Kutta(SDIRK)格式，可有效处理含不完美界面的弹性波问题，兼具高阶精度、参数鲁棒性与对界面未分辨情形的适应能力。

Abstract: We design and analyse an unfitted hybrid high-order (HHO) method for the elastic wave equation in a medium made of two components separated by an imperfect interface of linear slip type, across which the traction is continuous and the displacement jump is proportional to the traction through a compliancy tensor $\bK=α\bI+(β-α)\bn\otimes\bn$. The mesh is not fitted to the interface: the discrete unknowns are doubled in the cut cells, the small cuts are cured by a cell agglomeration procedure, and no unknown is attached to the interface. The two specific ingredients of the method are a local symmetric strain reconstruction in each cut subcell, which incorporates the interface condition through the regularised interface stiffness $\bS_h=(h_Tδ^{-1}\bI+\bK)^{-1}$ in the spirit of Hansbo and Hansbo {\em{A finite element method for the simulation of strong and weak discontinuities in solid mechanics.}} {Comput. Methods Appl. Mech. Engrg.}, 193, 2004, and an interface stabilisation built from the same matrix. For the space semi-discrete problem we prove that the discrete bilinear form is coercive and continuous, and we derive an energy-error estimate of order $h^{k+1}$ and an $L^2$-error estimate of order $h^{k+2}$, with constants independent of the compliancy parameters and of how the interface cuts the mesh. The scheme is combined either with the Newmark scheme, which conserves a discrete energy exactly, or with singly diagonally implicit Runge--Kutta schemes of order up to four. Numerical experiments in two dimensions confirm the predicted convergence rates for $k\in\{1,2,3\}$, the robustness with respect to the compliancy over sixteen orders of magnitude, and illustrate the propagation of elastic waves across an unresolved slipping interface.

</details>


### [131] [Deterministic and stochastic particle methods for the Fokker-Planck equation in S--formulation with application to point set registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01700)
*Klaas Willems,Angelo Iollo,Giovanni Russo,Tommaso Taddei*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于Fokker-Planck方程的有界域点集配准粒子方法：一种是移动最小二乘离散的S-公式法，另一种是可并行扩展的Langevin随机微分方程蒙特卡洛求解器。
