  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有界域内点集配准问题，尤其是粒子云在配准过程中出现强烈压缩/膨胀以及障碍物处理的问题，并追求适用于高维配准的高可扩展方法。

Method: 方法一：基于S-公式的移动最小二乘离散化，移动粒子由带目标分布的漂移项对流，扩散在动态演化粒子云上求解；通过新颖的鬼点法施加反射边界条件处理障碍物。方法二：求解相关Langevin随机微分方程的蒙特卡洛求解器，利用演化粒子密度对数梯度的局部近似，从个体随机轨迹中提取宏观渗透路径。

Result: 通过大量数值实验展示了两种方法的主要特性和性能；蒙特卡洛方法因内在并行性具有优异的可扩展性，适合高维配准问题。

Conclusion: 两种粒子方法均可用于有界域点集配准，分别适用于不同场景；蒙特卡洛方法在高维和大规模配准中更具扩展性优势。

Abstract: We present two particle methods for point set registration in bounded domains based on the Fokker-Planck equation. The first method relies on a moving least squares discretization of the S--formulation, in which moving grid points (particles) are advected by the drift with the target distribution, diffusion is resolved on a dynamically evolving particle cloud. This setting naturally leads to strong compression and expansion of the particle cloud. Obstacles are handled by enforcing reflective boundary conditions through a novel ghost point method. The second method is a Monte Carlo solver for the associated Langevin stochastic differential equation. It relies on a local approximation of the logarithmic gradient of the evolving particle density to extract macroscopic osmotic paths from individual stochastic trajectories. Owing to its inherent parallelism, this method exhibits excellent scalability and is well suited for high-dimensional registration problems. We illustrate the main features and performance of both approaches through extensive numerical experiments.

</details>


### [132] [Toeplitz Infinite GMRES for Parameterized Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01740)
*Weiguo Gao,Feiyang Jiang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出Toeplitz infinite GMRES方法，用于求解大量参数取值下的参数化稀疏线性系统，通过利用伴随Krylov序列中的块上三角Toeplitz结构避免存储完整Arnoldi向量，并提供动态生成元刷新策略与隐式重基方法，理论与实验验证了其效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解形如 A(s)x(s)=z 的参数化系统在大量参数取值下计算代价高昂，标准GMRES需存储完整Arnoldi向量，内存与计算开销大。作者希望利用参数化系统伴随Krylov序列的Toeplitz结构，降低存储与算术复杂度。

Method: 提出Toeplitz infinite GMRES：利用伴随Krylov序列中的块上三角Toeplitz结构构建Arnoldi过程并恢复解近似，无需存储完整Arnoldi向量；推导增量递推，p步Arnoldi需 O(np^2+p^3) 算术量与 O(np+p^2) 存储；引入动态生成元刷新策略抑制基本递推中的消去误差；对具有固定分离表示的矩阵函数，发展基于紧凑收缩幂零矩阵的隐式重基方法；并对平方可和Taylor系数给出残差下界以解释归一化单位圆外的缩放障碍。

Result: 推导出增量递推的复杂度为 O(np^2+p^3) 算术量与 O(np+p^2) 存储（不含分解建立，假设系数作用与三角求解为线性代价）；两种变体在精确算术下均保持Arnoldi过程，重基方法需精确矩阵函数作用；残差下界解释了归一化单位圆外的缩放障碍。理论分析与数值实验展示了方法的计算效率。

Conclusion: Toeplitz infinite GMRES通过挖掘结构显著降低参数化系统求解的存储与计算复杂度，动态刷新策略与隐式重基方法增强了数值稳定性，残差下界刻画了适用范围的缩放限制，数值实验验证了其高效性。

Abstract: We develop Toeplitz infinite GMRES for solving large sparse analytic parameterized systems $A(s)x(s)=z$ at many parameter values. The method exploits a block upper triangular Toeplitz structure in the companion Krylov sequence to construct the Arnoldi process and recover solution approximations without storing full Arnoldi vectors. We derive incremental recurrences requiring $\mathcal{O}(np^2+p^3)$ arithmetic and $\mathcal{O}(np+p^2)$ storage for $p$ Arnoldi steps, excluding factorization setup and assuming linear-cost coefficient actions and triangular solves. A dynamic generator-refreshing strategy addresses cancellation in the basic recurrence. For matrix functions admitting a fixed separated representation, we further develop an implicitly rebased method based on a compact, contractive nilpotent matrix. Both variants preserve the Arnoldi process in exact arithmetic, with exact matrix-function actions required for the rebased method. A residual lower bound for square-summable Taylor coefficients explains a scaling obstruction outside the normalized unit disk. Theoretical analysis and numerical experiments illustrate the computational efficiency of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [133] [Adaptive Multiresolution Diffusion Operators: A Variational Theory on Evolving Multiresolution Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01809)
*Christian Tantardini,Stig Rune Jensen,Roberto Di Remigio Eikås,Joakim Henrik Beck*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出自适应多分辨率下的状态依赖扩散变分框架，扩散算子由自适应状态本身生成，并用于正则化反问题与去噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典自适应小波方法中算子是预先给定的，仅在演化逼近空间上表示；本文希望让扩散算子与表示、交互图随自适应状态共同演化。

Method: 以可容许多分辨率树、活跃逼近空间与基、系数表示构成状态，导出对称非负交互形式与半正定内蕴扩散算子；因层次结构经离散拓扑变化演化，采用时间离散变分原理建立耦合动力学，并给出离散更新存在性、离散能量不等式、系数空间零模及冻结状态下收缩性；构造AMDI，将数据保真、内蕴扩散、系数稀疏性与树复杂度纳入状态依赖能量。

Result: 数值实验验证算子恒等式、细化交换子衰减与离散能量耗散；自适应Haar及高阶多小波计算显示分辨率在异质数据上的局部化；去噪中AMDI以不足10%的全活跃表示保持高结构重建质量，且在留出噪声实现上表现稳定。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了自适应表示演化与扩散算子演化，为状态依赖扩散正则化反问题提供理论基础，并在去噪上验证了高效性与稳定性。

Abstract: We develop a variational framework for state-dependent diffusion on adaptive multiresolution representations in which the diffusion operator is generated by the adaptive state itself. The state consists of an admissible multiresolution tree, its active approximation space and basis, and the corresponding coefficient representation. It determines a symmetric nonnegative interaction form and an associated positive semidefinite intrinsic diffusion operator. In contrast to classical adaptive wavelet methods, where a prescribed operator is represented on an evolving approximation space, refinement and coarsening here modify simultaneously the representation, interaction graph, and operator. Because the adaptive hierarchy evolves through discrete topological changes, the coupled dynamics are formulated through a time-discrete variational principle rather than a differential evolution on a fixed space. We establish existence of the discrete updates, a discrete energy inequality, the coefficient-space null mode, and contractivity for frozen adaptive states. For regularized inverse problems, the construction yields Adaptive Multiresolution Diffusion Imaging (AMDI), combining data fidelity, intrinsic diffusion, coefficient sparsity, and tree complexity in a state-dependent energy. Numerical experiments verify the assembled operator identities, examine refinement-commutator decay, and confirm discrete energy dissipation. Adaptive Haar and higher-order multiwavelet calculations demonstrate localization of resolution on heterogeneous data. In denoising, AMDI retains high structural reconstruction quality with less than 10\% of the full active representation, with stable behavior across held-out noise realizations.

</details>


### [134] [Approximation and computation of the geodesic Sinkhorn distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02007)
*Hugo Lavenant,Jonas Luckhardt,Bernhard Schmitzer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究如何逼近与计算由Sinkhorn散度导出的概率分布空间上的Riemannian度量 d_S，从空间与时间两个角度证明收敛性并给出数值方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作在概率分布空间上基于熵正则最优传输（Sinkhorn散度 S_ε）引入了Riemannian度量 d_S，但如何有效地逼近和计算该度量尚不清楚，本文旨在解决其可计算性问题。

Method: 空间上，对底空间采用越来越细的Eulerian离散化，证明度量的Gromov–Hausdorff收敛以及测地线的收敛；时间上，证明链式离散化 N Σ_{k=0}^{N-1} S_ε(μ_k, μ_{k+1}) 到定义 d_S 的能量泛函的 Γ-收敛。

Result: 建立了d_S在空间离散下的Gromov–Hausdorff收敛与测地线收敛，以及时间离散能量泛函的Γ-收敛，并据此推导并实现了计算 d_S 近似值的数值格式。

Conclusion: 该工作为Sinkhorn散度诱导的Riemannian度量提供了理论保证的离散逼近框架与可实现的数值算法。

Abstract: In [H. Lavenant, J. Luckhardt, G. Mordant, B. Schmitzer, L. Tamanini, The Riemannian geometry of Sinkhorn divergences. Ann. Inst. H. Poincaré Anal. Non Linéaire 43 (2026)] we introduced a Riemannian metric $\mathsf{d}_S$ on the space of probability distributions obtained from entropic optimal transport, specifically from the Sinkhorn divergence $S_\varepsilon$. In the present work we discuss how to approximate and compute $\mathsf{d}_S$. Spatially, we prove Gromov--Hausdorff convergence of the metric and convergence of geodesics for increasingly fine Eulerian discretization of the base space. Temporally, we show $Γ$-convergence of the chain discretization $N \sum_{k=0}^{N-1} S_\varepsilon(μ_k, μ_{k+1})$ to the energy functional defining $\mathsf{d}_S$. We deduce and implement numerical schemes to compute approximations of $\mathsf{d}_S$.

</details>


### [135] [Entropy dissipative high order schemes for a hyperbolic model of two-layer thin film flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02059)
*Rahul Barthwal,Philipp Öffner,Chetan Singh,Jan Zawallich*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对描述两层不相溶流体薄膜流动（含完全可溶溶质颗粒）的一阶双曲守恒律系统，构造了高阶熵耗散格式，比较了精确与近似两种熵守恒通量构造方式，并在有限差分与DG谱元两种框架下进行了数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两层薄膜流动的守恒律系统需要高阶且满足熵稳定性的数值格式，而熵守恒通量的构造方式（精确形式与常用简化近似）对格式的结构性、实现成本和求解效率有何影响尚需系统比较。

Method: 1) 基于Tadmor意义下的熵守恒通量，给出两种构造流程：精确公式与常用的简化近似，并加以对比；2) 将两类通量分别嵌入熵耗散有限差分框架和熵耗散间断Galerkin谱元法（DGSEM）中；3) 采用两种不同空间离散进行数值实验。

Result: 数值实验评估了所提方法的性能与鲁棒性，表明数值通量的选取对求解器的效率有显著影响：近似方法构造简单、应用广泛，但结构性较差，实现计算成本反而高于推导更为复杂的精确形式。

Conclusion: 在两类空间离散下，精确熵守恒通量构造虽推导复杂，却具有更好的结构与实现效率；通量选择是影响求解器效率的关键因素，应在实际应用中予以重视。

Abstract: In this article, we develop high-order, entropy-dissipative schemes for a hyperbolic system of conservation laws describing the first-order dynamics of two-layer thin-film flows of immiscible fluids with perfectly soluble solute particles. The key aspect is to construct entropy-conservative fluxes in the sense of Tadmor. Here, we consider and compare two different construction processes: an exact formulation and a commonly used simplified approximation in the flux evaluation. While the approximate approach is simpler at the level of construction and is frequently employed in practice (also in other contexts), it is less structured, leading to increased computational cost in the implementation compared to the exact formulation, which has been much more complicated to derive. We employ these fluxes within both an entropy-dissipative finite-difference framework and an entropy-dissipative discontinuous Galerkin spectral element method. Through numerical experiments using two distinct spatial discretizations, we investigate the performance and robustness of the proposed methods and demonstrate that the choice of numerical flux has a noticeable impact on the efficiency of the resulting solvers.

</details>


### [136] [Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Free-Boundary Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02084)
*Tan Phuong Dong Le*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在物理信息框架下使用Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）求解自由边界问题，通过残差型损失函数融合障碍约束、PDE不等式、互补条件与边界条件，并在椭圆障碍问题、p-Laplacian障碍问题及瞬态单相Stefan问题上与PINN和ResNet基线对比，KAN取得较低相对L2与L∞误差，能准确刻画接触区域与移动界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由边界问题涉及未知接触区域或移动界面，其变分不等式与互补条件结构给传统数值方法带来网格与离散化困难。物理信息神经网络虽已被用于此类PDE，但表示能力与精度仍有提升空间，因此作者探索KAN作为替代表示求解自由边界PDE的可行性。

Method: 在物理信息框架下，将障碍约束、PDE不等式、互补条件及边界条件统一转化为残差型损失函数；采用KAN近似解函数。算例包括线性椭圆障碍问题、非线性p-Laplacian障碍问题以及时间相关单相Stefan问题。以PINN和残差网络（ResNet）作为对比基线，通过相对L2与L∞误差评估精度，并考察接触区域与移动界面的分辨能力。

Result: 数值实验表明，KAN求解器在上述三类问题上均获得较低的相对L2与L∞误差，能准确解析接触区域和移动界面，整体表现与PINN、ResNet基线相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: KAN表示为求解自由边界PDE提供了一种有效替代方案，验证了其在物理信息框架下处理变分不等式与移动边界问题的潜力。

Abstract: We study free-boundary problems within a physics-informed framework using Kolmogorov-Arnold network (KAN) approximations. The proposed approach incorporates obstacle constraints, partial differential equation (PDE) inequalities, complementarity conditions, and boundary conditions through residual-based loss functions. We consider a linear elliptic obstacle problem, a nonlinear $p$-Laplacian obstacle problem, and a time-dependent one-phase Stefan problem. The proposed KAN solver is compared with physics-informed neural network (PINN) and residual-network baselines. Numerical experiments show that KANs achieve low relative $L^2$ and $L^\infty$ errors while accurately resolving contact regions and moving interfaces. The results indicate that KAN representations provide an effective alternative for solving free-boundary PDEs.

</details>

### [137] [Emergence of electronic modes and triplet pairing from spin-1 antiferromagnetic insulators in the Kanamori-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00110)
*Masanori Kohno*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过双轨道Kanamori-Hubbard模型，揭示自旋-1反铁磁绝缘体的能带结构在掺杂和温度变化下如何产生具有动量偏移自旋色散关系的涌现电子模式，并阐明其物理机制与轨道简并特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统能带绝缘体在掺杂或升温下能带结构基本不变，而Mott与Kondo绝缘体中已发现带边涌现出新电子模式。但大自旋（自旋-1）反铁磁绝缘体中，自旋、电荷与轨道自由度相互作用如何导致能带随掺杂和温度变化，尚缺乏系统理解。

Method: 采用双轨道Kanamori-Hubbard模型，结合弱hopping有效理论、选择定则分析与数值计算，研究掺杂和温度诱导的涌现电子模式；并探讨小轨道间排斥下自旋三重态配对及非平衡态自旋/电荷扰动下的模式特征。

Result: 典型参数下，掺杂诱导的模式在各轨道反映不同类型的自旋模式，温度诱导模式在两轨道主要反映低能自旋模式，均呈现动量偏移的自旋模式色散；若hopping参数符号不同，涌现模式的动量区间在轨道间可不同。小轨道间排斥时掺杂后可形成onsite自旋三重态对，产生对破缺gap，而温度诱导模式无该gap。非平衡扰动下模式的涌现与特征也被阐明。

Conclusion: 研究全面揭示了自旋-1反铁磁绝缘体中自旋、电荷与轨道自由度相互作用导致涌现电子模式的机制，为探索强关联体系中的非常规量子现象提供了新途径。

Abstract: The electronic band structures of conventional band insulators generally remain unchanged under doping via chemical-potential shifts or increasing temperature. However, in Mott and Kondo insulators described by models such as the Hubbard and Kondo lattice models, distinct electronic modes with momentum-shifted magnetic dispersion relations have been shown to emerge from band edges into the gap. Here, by investigating the two-orbital Kanamori-Hubbard model, this study elucidates how and why the band structures of spin-1 antiferromagnetic insulators change with doping and temperature, revealing emergent spectral features characteristic of orbitally degenerate systems. The nature of the emergent electronic modes is clarified using weak-hopping effective theory, selection-rule analysis, and numerical calculations. In a typical parameter regime, the doping-induced modes in each orbital reflect distinct types of spin modes, while the temperature-induced modes primarily reflect the low-energy spin mode in both orbitals; in both cases, the emergent modes exhibit momentum-shifted spin-mode dispersion relations. The momentum regimes of these emergent modes can differ between orbitals if signs of the hopping parameters differ. For small inter-orbital repulsion, onsite spin-triplet pairs can form after doping, leading to a pair-breaking gap, while the temperature-induced electronic modes continue to reflect the low-energy spin mode without a pair-breaking gap. Moreover, the emergence and characteristics of electronic modes in nonequilibrium states by spin or charge perturbations are elucidated. These findings provide a comprehensive understanding of how the interplay among the spin, charge, and orbital degrees of freedom in large-spin antiferromagnetic insulators leads to emergent electronic modes, opening avenues for exploring unconventional quantum phenomena in strongly correlated systems.

</details>


### [138] [Causality violations in cluster GW+DMFT: Exact Lehmann moments and the necessity of non-local vertex corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00324)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文以最小Hubbard团簇（二聚体与三格点环）的精确对角化为基准，严格检验了GW与GW+DMFT自能的解析结构，证明两者在阻挫团簇上均违反Matsubara因果性条件，表明仅做局域自能修正不足以恢复因果性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW近似已知会破坏因果性，产生位于复频率平面上半平面的非物理极点。GW+DMFT通过注入精确局域自能试图修正这一问题，但其能否真正恢复因果性尚缺乏严格验证。作者希望用可精确求解的最小模型对这一修正策略进行严格基准测试。

Method: 对最小Hubbard团簇做精确对角化：先在二体二聚体上考察对称性对非局域自能的影响，再转入非二分（阻挫）的三格点Hubbard环；解析计算精确Lehmann矩，导出自能高频尾的闭式表达式；对比GW与GW+DMFT在必要高频条件与有限频率Matsubara符号条件上的表现；以无插值的实轴精确自能评估作为主要因果性判据，有理延拓作为辅助证据；并分析完全不可约顶点Λ的高频渐近行为。

Result: 发现“二聚体反常”：二体二聚体上反演对称性迫使非局域自能为纯实数，从而掩盖了因果性破坏。在三格点阻挫环上，跳跃阻挫使非局域自能出现有限虚部，GW与GW+DMFT均违反Matsubara因果性条件。高频尾闭式结果为C = U²·Var(d)·I。标准方法无法捕捉用于强制该条件的精确等时双粒子关联函数，而这类关联函数恰由完全不可约顶点Λ的高频渐近编码。两个诊断逻辑独立：GW+DMFT满足必要高频条件C⪰0，却在有限频率上仍违反Matsubara符号条件；局域截断的Λ使非局域RPA共振未被修正。

Conclusion: 局域自能修正不足以保证阻挫团簇上的因果解析结构。高频条件与有限频率符号条件相互独立，仅靠GW+DMFT无法消除非局域RPA共振导致的因果性破坏，需要包含非局域顶点修正。

Abstract: The $GW$ approximation is known to violate causality, yielding self-energies with unphysical poles in the upper half of the complex frequency plane. The combined $GW$+DMFT approach attempts to cure this by injecting the exact local self-energy. Using exact diagonalization on minimal Hubbard clusters, we rigorously benchmark the analytic structure of the $GW$ and $GW$+DMFT self-energies. We demonstrate a ``dimer anomaly'': on the two-site dimer, inversion symmetry forces the non-local self-energy to be purely real, masking the causality violation. Moving to the non-bipartite three-site Hubbard ring, where hopping frustration yields a finite imaginary part in the non-local self-energy, we prove that both $GW$ and $GW$+DMFT violate the Matsubara causality condition. By analytically evaluating the exact Lehmann moments, we derive a closed-form expression for the high-frequency tail of the self-energy, $C = U^2\,\mathrm{Var}(d)\,I$, and show that standard methods fail to capture the exact equal-time two-particle correlators required to enforce it. The equal-time correlators that govern this tail are precisely those encoded in the high-frequency asymptotics of the fully irreducible vertex $Λ$; nevertheless, our intermediate-frequency analysis demonstrates that the local-only truncation of $Λ$ in $GW$+DMFT leaves non-local RPA resonances uncorrected. The two diagnostics are logically independent: $GW$+DMFT satisfies the necessary high-frequency condition $C\succeq0$ yet still violates the Matsubara sign condition at finite frequencies. Taking the interpolation-free real-axis evaluation of the exact self-energy as the primary causality certificate, with rational continuation as supporting evidence, we establish a rigorous benchmark demonstrating that local self-energy corrections are insufficient to guarantee a causal analytic structure on frustrated clusters.

</details>


### [139] [Anyon Proliferation and Anyon Superconductivity in Higgsing Transitions via Conformal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00452)
*Diego García-Sepúlveda,Da-Chuan Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于共形嵌入研究(2+1)维Chern-Simons Higgsing相变及其与任意子增殖的关系，分析了拓扑序保持或扩大的情形，提出SO(N)₂↔SU(N)₁族与Spin(16)₁→Spin(9)₂等相变中可凝聚任意子的候选结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Chern-Simons Higgsing相变如何在减少规范群的同时保持或扩大本征拓扑序，并厘清任意子增殖、任意子凝聚与非阿贝尔拓扑序出现之间的联系，特别是标量表示对UV Chern-Simons能级非整可积性带来的任意子标记困难。

Method: 采用共形嵌入方法讨论(2+1)维Chern-Simons Higgsing相变；分析SO(N)₂↔SU(N)₁族（N≥3）中两半经典区域的拓扑序与全局U(1)对称性实现；利用adjoint screening启发式提出未Higgs相中的候选任意子；通过Higgs子群下的分支分解确定Higgs相的可凝聚任意子通道；并构造Spin(16)₁→Spin(9)₂相变及其可凝聚代数。

Result: 在SO(N)₂↔SU(N)₁族中，两相具有等价本征拓扑序但全局U(1)对称性实现不同；奇数N时对应从费米子1/N Laughlin态到电荷2e任意子超导与相同手征Z_N拓扑序共存的转变。标量表示对UV Chern-Simons能级非整可积，故不直接标记未Higgs相的任意子。通过adjoint screening给出候选任意子，分支分解给出Higgs相可凝聚任意子通道。构造的Spin(16)₁→Spin(9)₂相变可凝聚代数包含非阿贝尔任意子，但不含所有允许可规范化通道的自融合。

Conclusion: Chern-Simons Higgsing相变可由共形嵌入刻画，拓扑序保持或经任意子凝聚而扩大；标量表示的非整可积性要求借助启发式筛选确定UV任意子；共形嵌入与分支规则为识别可凝聚任意子提供系统框架，并可将阿贝尔拓扑序引导至非阿贝尔拓扑序。

Abstract: We discuss (2+1)d Chern-Simons Higgsing transitions based on conformal embeddings and their relation to the proliferation of anyons. These transitions preserve or enlarge the intrinsic topological order despite reducing the gauge group. When the topological order is enlarged, the two phases differ by anyon condensation, and the transition takes an Abelian topological order to a non-Abelian one. In the $SO(N)_{2} \hookrightarrow SU(N)_{1}$ family with $N \geq 3$, the two semiclassical regimes have equivalent intrinsic topological orders but distinct realizations of a global $U(1)$ symmetry. For odd $N$, these are non-Abelian realizations of transitions from fermionic $1/N$ Laughlin states to charge-$2e$ anyon superconductivity coexisting with the same chiral $\mathbb{Z}_{N}$ topological order. The scalar representations are always non-integrable with respect to the UV Chern-Simons level and therefore do not directly label anyons of the un-Higgsed phase, despite defining Wilson lines in the UV. Using heuristic adjoint screening, we propose candidate anyons in the un-Higgsed phase, while branching under the Higgs subgroup reveals channels corresponding to condensable anyons of the Higgs phase. We further construct a $\mathrm{Spin}(16)_{1} \to \mathrm{Spin}(9)_{2}$ transition admitting a condensable algebra that contains a non-Abelian anyon but not all of the allowed gaugable channels in its self-fusion.

</details>


### [140] [Near-infrared magnetic linear and non-reciprocal directional dichroism in the chiral antiferromagnet SmFe3(BO3)4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00534)
*B. Beke,B. Szász,I. A. Gudim,D. Szaller,S. Bordács*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过极化分辨磁光光谱研究SmFe3(BO3)4中反铁磁序对近红外光学响应的影响，观测到磁线性二色性与非互易方向二色性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究反铁磁序如何影响稀土铁硼酸盐的近红外光学响应，并寻找探测反铁磁序参量面内取向的光学手段。

Method: 采用极化分辨磁光光谱，在尼尔温度以下测量Sm3+的4f-4f跃迁；利用磁场旋转易平面反铁磁基态的序参量，推导偏振选择定则，并在环面几何下检测非互易方向二色性。

Result: 在尼尔温度以下观测到Sm3+的4f-4f跃迁处显著的磁线性二色性；通过磁场实现了线性二色性的调控并确定偏振选择定则；在面内磁场垂直于二重旋转轴时，于对称性允许的环面几何中检测到若干4f-4f跃迁的非互易吸收。

Conclusion: 非互易吸收表明电偶极与磁偶极激发之间存在强干涉；所观测的光学各向异性为探测反铁磁序的面内取向提供了途径，可用于稀土铁硼酸盐中反铁磁序参量的成像与时间分辨研究。

Abstract: We investigated the influence of antiferromagnetic (AFM) order on the near-infrared optical re- sponse of SmFe3(BO3)4 by polarization-resolved, magneto-optical spectroscopy. Below the Néel temperature, we observed pronounced magnetic linear dichroism at the 4f -4f transitions of Sm3+. By rotating the order parameter of the easy-plane AFM ground state using magnetic fields, we demonstrated the magnetic field control of linear dichroism, and deduced polarization selection rules. In addition, we detected non-reciprocal directional dichroism for several 4f -4f transitions. The non-reciprocal absorption appears in the symmetry-allowed toroidal geometry when the in- plane magnetic field is normal to one of the two-fold rotation axes. These results indicate a strong interference between electric- and magnetic-dipole excitations for transitions showing non-reciprocal absorption. The observed optical anisotropies provide a promising path to detect the in-plane orien- tation of the AFM order, enabling imaging and time-resolved studies of the AFM order parameter in rare-earth ferroborates.

</details>


### [141] [Mechanism of Incommensurate Magnetic Order in $\mathrm{BaCo_2(AsO_4)_2}$: Interplay of Frustrated Further-Neighbor Exchanges and Bond-Directional Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00626)
*Mohammad-Hossein Zare,Mehdi Biderang,Hamid Mosadeq*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展的J1-K-Γ-Γ'-J2-J3模型结合Luttinger-Tisza解析与扭转边界条件精确对角化，揭示BaCo2(AsO4)2中零场非公度螺旋磁序源于交换阻挫与键各向异性的协同作用，而非异常强的Kitaev耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜂窝状钴酸盐BaCo2(AsO4)2的零场非公度磁序微观机制尚不明确，尤其是键向Kitaev型相互作用与交换阻挫各自的相对贡献存在争议。本文旨在澄清其磁相互作用的微观起源。

Method: 构建含XXZ型交换各向异性的扩展J1-K-Γ-Γ'-J2-J3模型，最近邻参数由从头算电子结构计算约束；结合解析Luttinger-Tisza方法与扭转边界条件下的精确对角化，绘制(J2,J3)参数空间的经典与量子相图。

Result: 实验中沿Γ→M方向传播的非公度螺旋相并不需要异常占优的Kitaev耦合，而是由远邻耦合(J2,J3)驱动的交换阻挫与中等大小的非对角键各向异性(Γ,Γ')协同产生；量子区域中该非公度流形与面外铁磁(FMz)相竞争，后者通过量子无序选择机制被选择性稳定。

Conclusion: 研究调和了关于BaCo2(AsO4)2磁相互作用相互矛盾的解释，确立了其非公度基态的微观起源与稳定范围。

Abstract: The microscopic mechanism governing the zero-field incommensurate magnetic order in the honeycomb cobaltate $\mathrm{BaCo}_2(\mathrm{AsO}_4)_2$ remains a significant unresolved problem, particularly concerning the relative contributions of bond-directional Kitaev-type interactions and exchange frustration. This study investigates an extended $J_1\text{--}K\text{--}Γ\text{--}Γ'\text{--}J_2\text{--}J_3$ model, which incorporates XXZ-type exchange anisotropies on the honeycomb lattice. Nearest-neighbor parameters are constrained by ab initio electronic structure calculations. By integrating the analytical Luttinger-Tisza approach with exact diagonalization calculations under twisted boundary conditions, we delineate the classical and quantum phase diagrams across the $(J_2, J_3)$ parameter space. We demonstrate that the stabilization of the experimentally observed incommensurate spiral phase, propagating along the $Γ\to M$ direction, does not necessitate an anomalously dominant Kitaev coupling. Instead, this phase arises naturally from the synergistic interplay between exchange frustration, driven by further-neighbor couplings ($J_2, J_3$), and intermediate off-diagonal bond anisotropies ($Γ, Γ'$). In the quantum regime, we elucidate the competition between this incommensurate manifold and an out-of-plane ferromagnetic ($\mathrm{FM}_z$) phase, which is selectively stabilized via a quantum order-by-disorder mechanism. Our findings reconcile conflicting interpretations of the magnetic interactions in $\mathrm{BaCo}_2(\mathrm{AsO}_4)_2$ and establish the microscopic origin and stability range of its incommensurate ground state.

</details>


### [142] [The half-filled optical Su-Schrieffer-Heeger-Hubbard model with uniaxial strain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00681)
*Jonah Huang,James Neuhaus,Benjamin Cohen-Stead,Steven Johnston,Richard Scalettar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文通过各向异性跃迁模拟单轴应变，研究SSH电子-声子耦合与Hubbard相互作用共同作用下半满二分晶格的相图与键序波(BOW)关联，并揭示BOW相中方向依赖的能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应变可通过改变键长、跃迁振幅与晶格对称性直接调控电子相，使电子-声子耦合体系成为研究应变效应的天然平台。SSH模型描述电子与调制跃迁的晶格自由度之间的耦合，预期对 strain 场敏感，但其在各向异性应变与相互作用共同作用下的相行为尚需系统刻画。

Method: 采用各向异性跃迁来建模单轴应变，破坏90°旋转对称性；在SSH电子-声子耦合基础上加入在位Hubbard U；在半满条件下于电子-声子耦合与Hubbard耦合构成的参数平面内绘制相图，确定BOW形成的临界温度；并通过解析延拓计算谱函数。

Result: 单轴应变将BOW相的四重简并降低为两重；Hubbard U促进反铁磁关联，且该关联也受SSH相互作用弱驱动；获得了半满相图及BOW形成的临界温度；谱函数显示BOW相中出现方向依赖的能隙形成。

Conclusion: 单轴应变通过打破旋转对称性有效调控SSH体系中的键序波相，与Hubbard相互作用协同决定相图；BOW相表现出各向异性的能隙特征，表明应变是调控电子-声子耦合体系有序态的有效手段。

Abstract: Strain offers a direct route to control electronic phases by altering bond lengths, hopping amplitudes, and lattice symmetries. These aspects make electron-phonon (e-ph) coupled systems a natural setting to study strain-induced effects. The Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) Hamiltonian describes the coupling between itinerant electrons and lattice degrees of freedom that modulate hopping and is thus expected to be strongly affected by strain fields. At half-filling and on a bipartite lattice, the SSH interactions drive dominant bond-order-wave (BOW) correlations at low- temperature in which short bonds with high kinetic energy alternate with long bonds with low kinetic energy along four possible patterns. We model uniaxial strain through anisotropic hopping, which breaks the $90^\circ$ rotational symmetry and reduces the BOW phase degeneracy to two. We also include an on-site Hubbard U that promotes antiferromagnetic correlations that are also (weakly) driven by the SSH interaction itself. We map out the half-filled phase diagram in the plane of the e-ph and Hubbard couplings, and determine the critical temperature of BOW formation. Using analytic continuation, we also evaluate the spectral function and demonstrate directionally dependent gap formation in the BOW phase

</details>


### [143] [Local geometry of the Fermi surface and its effect on the electronic characteristics of normal metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00810)
*N. A. Zimbovskaya*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文考察费米面几何中的精细特征（近圆柱形、近抛物面形条带及局部平坦化）对金属电子学性质（主要是电流磁效应）的影响，指出这些特征在特定条件下显著改变常规正常金属或层状金属导体的高频响应，且效应强烈依赖扰动传播方向和外磁场方向，并讨论了实验观测可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米面几何中的精细结构（如近圆柱形/近抛物面形条带、局部平坦化）通常被忽略，但其对电子输运，尤其高频响应的影响尚不明确，需要系统考察其作用及可观测性。

Method: 以费米面精细几何特征为出发点，理论分析其对电子学性质（主要是电流磁效应/galvanomagnetic properties）的影响，考察高频外场扰动下常规金属与层状金属导体的响应，并分析对扰动传播方向和外磁场方向的敏感性。

Result: 在特定条件下，这些费米面精细特征可显著改变金属或层状导电结构对高频外场扰动的响应方式；所讨论的效应均对扰动传播方向和/或外磁场方向非常敏感。

Conclusion: 费米面精细几何特征对金属高频电子学响应有重要影响，且具有方向敏感性，文中进一步讨论了这些效应的实验观测可能性。

Abstract: Fine features in the Fermi surface geometry, such as nearly cylindrical or nearly paraboloidal strips or local flattenings, are examined as regards their effect on the electronic, mainly galvanomagnetic, properties of metals. It is shown that under certain conditions, these features may significantly change the way a conventional normal metal or a layered structure with metallic conductivity responds to high- frequency external disturbances. All of the effects considered appear to be very sensitive to the disturbance propagation direction and/or to that of the external magnetic field. Experimental possibilities of observing the described effects are discussed.

</details>


### [144] [Robustness of the Verwey transition against remanent strain-induced defects in magnetite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01337)
*M. A. Gala,K. J. Grzywa,K. Komędera,R. Zalecki,A. Baczmański,Z. Kąkol,A. Pacanowska,C. M. N. Kumar,R. Rodriguez Lamas,C. Detlefs,C. Yildirim,C. Marin,J. Debray,G. Beutier,L. Ortega,A. Kozłowski,N. Barišić,J. E. Lorenzo,W. Tabiś*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文结合暗场X射线显微镜(DFXM)与交流磁化率测量，研究单轴压缩及卸载产生的应变诱导缺陷对化学计量比单晶磁铁矿Verwey转变的影响，发现线状扩展缺陷与残余应变场不改变转变温度$T_{\rm V}$及其锐度，与点缺陷的强抑制效应形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁铁矿Verwey转变是对晶格缺陷和外部扰动高度敏感的电子驱动相变。已有研究已知掺杂、氧非化学计量和辐照诱导的点缺陷会降低$T_{\rm V}$，但单轴应力产生的残余应变场和扩展缺陷结构的作用几乎未被探索，因此需要系统研究扩展缺陷与点缺陷在该转变中的不同影响。

Method: 对化学计量比单晶磁铁矿施加单轴压缩并卸载：(1) 对沿同一$[011]$方向压缩的两个样品，分别加载至$200~\mathrm{MPa}$（产生稳定线状缺陷与残余应变场）和超过断裂阈值（产生更致密缺陷网络与机械断裂）；(2) 另测一沿$[001]$压缩断裂样品的交流磁化率以考察不同加载几何。采用DFXM进行实空间成像、晶格取向映射与残余应变映射，并结合交流磁化率确定$T_{\rm V}$及转变锐度。

Result: DFXM实空间成像、晶格取向映射和残余应变映射显示压缩后存在显著的残余结构无序；然而在所有压缩或断裂样品中，转变温度$T_{\rm V}$和转变锐度均无可测量变化。这与点缺陷对$T_{\rm V}$的强烈抑制形成反差。不同加载几何（$[011]$与$[001]$）结果一致。

Conclusion: 塑性变形产生的线状扩展缺陷和残余应变场不会可测量地扰动驱动Verwey转变的宏观电子有序，从而在磁铁矿中区分了点缺陷与扩展滑移相关缺陷的不同效应：扩展缺陷虽引入显著结构无序，却不影响宏观电子有序转变。

Abstract: The Verwey transition in magnetite is a benchmark electronically driven phase transition that is highly sensitive to lattice imperfections and external perturbations. Doping, deviations from oxygen stoichiometry, and irradiation-induced point defects are known to lower the Verwey transition temperature, $T_{\rm V}$. By contrast, the role of remanent strain fields and extended defect structures generated by uniaxial stress has remained largely unexplored. Here we combine dark-field x-ray microscopy (DFXM) and ac magnetic susceptibility to determine how strain-induced defects affect the Verwey transition in stoichiometric single-crystalline magnetite after uniaxial compression and unloading. The DFXM measurements were performed on two samples compressed along the same $[011]$ direction: loading to $200~\mathrm{MPa}$ generated stable line-like defects and remanent strain fields, whereas loading beyond the fracture threshold produced denser defect networks and mechanical fracture. To test the effect of a different loading geometry, we also measured the ac susceptibility of a sample fractured by compression along $[001]$. Real-space DFXM imaging, lattice-orientation mapping, and residual strain mapping show substantial remanent structural disorder after compression. Nevertheless, neither the transition temperature $T_{\rm V}$ nor the sharpness of the transition changes measurably in any of the compressed or fractured samples. This behavior contrasts with the strong suppression of $T_{\rm V}$ by point-like disorder. Our results show that extended line-like defects and remanent strain fields produced by plastic deformation do not measurably perturb the macroscopic electronic ordering responsible for the Verwey transition, thereby distinguishing the effects of point defects from those of extended slip-related defects in magnetite.

</details>


### [145] [Na$_2$Ir$^\text{IV}$Cl$_6$: The Missing Member of the Perfectly Cubic Vacancy Ordered $A_2$IrCl$_6$ Family, Another Potential $J_{eff}$ = $\frac{1}{2}$ Ground State Candidate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01371)
*Siddhartha Sankar Soren,Aditya Patra,Rounak Das,Ritwik Das,Tanmay Chanda,Jens Buck,Indra Dasgupta,Sugata Ray*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 首次合成并全面表征了无水Na2IrCl6单晶，具fcc结构与1.91 K反铁磁转变，纠正了此前"无水"单斜相的认识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空位有序双钙钛矿（VODP）铱酸盐是研究在位库仑排斥、晶体场、自旋轨道耦合及Heisenberg-Kitaev竞争相互作用的理想平台。其中Na基体系存在水合依赖的结构与磁性不确定性，具有fcc反铁磁性的立方Na2IrCl6一直无法稳定，因此需要实验上实现并验证该相。

Method: 合成了无水Na2IrCl6单晶，并采用综合表征手段（含热重分析TGA）确定晶体结构、晶格水含量与热稳定性，测量其磁转变行为。

Result: 获得fcc结构的无水Na2IrCl6单晶，反铁磁转变温度为1.91 K；TGA证实无晶格水，晶体热稳定至453 K。所报道立方相与先前"无水"单斜Na2IrCl6形成对比。

Conclusion: 该工作首次稳定了立方Na2IrCl6相，凸显Na基VODP铱酸盐中结构与物性的强关联，并为探索理想Jeff=1/2八面体及最近邻Ir离子间强Kitaev相互作用提供了新平台。

Abstract: Vacancy ordered double perovskites (VODPs) have been known for more than 100 years, but their exact crystal structures and magnetic ground state remained controversial till date. Anhydrous VODP iridates ($A_2$Ir$X_6$; $A$ = K$^+$, (NH$_4$)$^+$, Cs$^+$, Rb$^+$, and $X$ = F$^-$, Cl$^-$, Br$^-$), having $face-centered~cubic$ (fcc) antiferromagnetic ground state, offer a chemically well-defined platform to investigate the influence of on-site Coulomb repulsion, crystal field, spin-orbit coupling, and competing Heisenberg and Kitaev interactions. Interestingly, among these, the Na-based VODP iridates seem to possess an unusual hydration dependent structural and magnetic uncertainty. As a result, a cubic Na$_2$IrCl$_6$ system having fcc antiferromagnetism could not be stabilized ever. Here, for the first time, we report the synthesis and comprehensive characterization of anhydrous Na$_2$IrCl$_6$ single crystals having fcc structure with antiferromagnetic transition temperature of 1.91 K. Thermogravimetric analysis establishes the absence of lattice water and also demonstrates thermal stability of the crystals up to 453 K. The cubic phase reported here contrasts with previously described 'anhydrous' monoclinic Na$_2$IrCl$_6$ and highlights the strong structural and physical property relationship in Na-based VODP iridates. This new material offers an exciting playing ground for exploring another potential perfect $J_{eff}$ = $\frac{1}{2}$ octahedra and the consequent strong Kitaev interaction within the nearest-neighbor (nn) pairs of Ir ions.

</details>


### [146] [Room-temperature Magnetoelastic Coupling in UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01551)
*Mingyu Xu,Tomasz Klimczuk,M. Brian Maple,Weiwei Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文报道铀基金属间化合物UIr₄Al₁₅在略高于室温时呈现巨大磁弹耦合：虽无晶体学对称性破缺，但磁转变附近晶格参数与部分化学键键长出现显著异常，表明晶体结构对磁取向异常敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属体系中电子与结构自由度的耦合支撑众多量子材料涌现现象，但磁弹耦合通常较弱，且局限于低温或微观尺度。作者希望寻找在室温附近具有强磁弹耦合的金属体系。

Method: 采用变温单晶X射线衍射，并结合各向异性磁化率测量，对UIr₄Al₁₅单晶进行结构-磁性联合表征，分辨与磁有序耦合的细微可重复结构畸变。

Result: 在略高于室温处观察到巨大磁弹耦合：虽无晶体学对称性破缺，但磁转变区域附近晶格参数与特定化学键键长出现明显异常，揭示晶体结构对磁取向的异常敏感性。

Conclusion: UIr₄Al₁₅是磁性与晶格畸变在室温附近强纠缠的罕见平台，为磁响应量子材料及功能化磁传感应用提供新机遇，并可借原子间距与局域化学键合探究块体金属间化合物的电子与磁态。

Abstract: The interplay between electronic and structural degrees of freedom underpins many emergent phenomena in quantum materials, yet magnetoelastic coupling in metallic systems is typically weak and confined to low temperatures or microscopic length scales. Here, we report giant magnetoelastic coupling slightly above room temperature in the uranium-based intermetallic compound UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$. Using temperature-dependent single-crystal X-ray diffraction together with anisotropic magnetic susceptibility measurements, we directly resolve subtle but reproducible structural distortions coupled to magnetic alignment. Despite the absence of crystallographic symmetry breaking, pronounced anomalies emerge in lattice parameters and selected chemical bond distances near the magnetic transition region, revealing an unusual sensitivity of the crystal structure to magnetic orientation. The coupling enables direct probing of how atomic distances and local chemical bonding govern the electronic and magnetic states in a bulk intermetallic material. Our results establish UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$ as a rare platform in which magnetism and lattice distortions are strongly intertwined near room temperature, opening new opportunities for magnetically responsive quantum materials and functional magnetic sensing applications.

</details>


### [147] [Mass-asymmetry-controlled exciton dressing and dissociation in a quantum lattice model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01594)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过Holstein-激子模型精确对角化，本文揭示了极性材料中电子-空穴干涉对激子-声子耦合的非微扰调控机制，建立了包含内部极化交叉与解离边界的相图，并证明质量对称时交换选择定则保护激子免受声子极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极性材料中，中性激子通过电子与空穴形变势之和与声子耦合，但电荷中性使总源项为零，激子-声子弹性顶点由电子-空穴干涉正则化。这一干涉的非微扰命运尚不清楚，需明确其如何影响激子的极化修饰与稳定性。

Method: 采用Holstein-激子模型的精确对角化方法；将质量不对称性作为参数以与小平极化子原子极限解耦；构建相图并解析证明、数值验证交换选择定则；分析解离边界与全局能量平衡（库仑束缚与极化稳定化）的关系。

Result: 相图包含内部极化交叉和解离边界。等质量时，只要声子源在电子-空穴交换下为奇（中性情形），对称激子基态因精确交换选择定则而免受声子极化修饰。质量不对称增大时，选择定则被破坏，激子获得强局域极化云。解离边界由库仑束缚与极化稳定化的全局能量平衡决定，几乎独立于内部极化修饰。

Conclusion: 保护激子免受极化的对称性同时剥夺了其极化稳定化，从而在强耦合下驱动其解离。研究结果与晶格激子-极化子模型相呼应，并对质量对称与不对称极性半导体中激子谱线的锐化或展宽具有启示意义。

Abstract: In a polar material, a neutral exciton couples to phonons through the sum of the electron and hole deformation potentials. Because the total source vanishes by charge neutrality, the elastic exciton-phonon vertex is regularized by electron-hole interference. Here we determine the non-perturbative fate of this interference by exact diagonalization of a Holstein-exciton model. By parameterizing the mass asymmetry to decouple it from the small-polaron atomic limit, we map a regime map comprising an internal dressing crossover and a dissociation boundary. We prove analytically and verify numerically that for equal masses, the symmetric exciton ground state is protected from phonon dressing by an exact exchange selection rule, provided the phonon source is odd under electron-hole exchange (the neutral case). As mass asymmetry increases, this selection rule is broken and the exciton acquires a strong local polaronic cloud. We show that the dissociation boundary, conversely, is set by a global energy balance between Coulomb binding and polaronic stabilization, and is nearly independent of the internal dressing. Paradoxically, the very symmetry that protects the exciton from dressing denies it polaronic stabilization, driving it toward dissociation at strong coupling. We discuss these results in the context of lattice exciton-polaron models and their implications for sharp versus broad excitonic lines in mass-symmetric versus mass-asymmetric polar semiconductors.

</details>


### [148] [Why polar excitons stay sharp: parity protection of the center-of-mass recoil channel in exciton-phonon scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01600)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文指出激子-声子问题中忽略质心反冲会遗漏关键运动学自由度；恢复精确质心反冲后出现普适、无参数的1s→1s吸收通道（反冲动量q*，速率∝N_LO(T)），该通道受电子-空穴相消干涉控制，并给出弹性缀饰的严格抑制律S_X/S_ind=η²(6-η²)/5。据此建立了散射regime层级：质量不对称材料（GaAs，η=-0.74）通道活跃（2.2 meV），质量对称材料（FAPbI₃、MAPbI₃）被干涉抑制（0.00、0.12 meV），说明钙钛矿27–40 meV线宽不能由质心反冲解释，需内部态改变等非弹性通道，其中1s→np共振是主要候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卤化物钙钛矿等极性半导体中Fröhlich相互作用应是主导的电子-声子耦合，但实验观察到激子共振异常尖锐。现有激子-声子处理采用"冻结质心"近似，可能遗漏了决定性的运动学自由度，导致对线宽来源的判断失准，故需重新审视质心反冲的作用。

Method: 在氢型Fröhlich模型下恢复精确的激子质心（COM）反冲，推导反冲动量q*=√(2M_ex ħω_LO)/ħ处的1s→1s吸收通道及其速率（∝N_LO(T)）；用电子-空穴干涉分析反冲线宽随质量不对称度的依赖γ_LO^recoil∝F_{1s,1s}(q*)²，并解析导出弹性缀饰抑制律S_X/S_ind=η²(6-η²)/5。以质量不对称参数η为控制量，比较GaAs（η=-0.74）、MAPbI₃（η=-0.11）、FAPbI₃（η=0）三类材料。

Result: 1s→1s反冲通道受相消干涉控制：γ_LO^recoil随质量不对称度以F_{1s,1s}(q*)²消失。GaAs（η=-0.74）反冲通道活跃，γ_LO^recoil=2.2 meV；MAPbI₃（η=-0.11）为0.12 meV；FAPbI₃（η=0）为0.00 meV，被干涉完全抑制。钙钛矿实测27–40 meV线宽无法由质心反冲解释。投影到关联激子后，控制参数为η、q*a_X与Rydberg失谐，而非仅Fröhlich常数α。1s→np共振因构造性且对η稳健，是模型内主要候选机制。

Conclusion: 质心反冲是激子-声子问题中不可忽略的运动学自由度，但其贡献受电子-空穴干涉强烈调制：仅质量不对称体系显著，质量对称的钙钛矿体系被抑制。因此钙钛矿的异常线宽需归因于内部态改变等非弹性通道（1s→np共振为首选）。Fröhlich常数α本身不足以作为品质因子，真正的控制参数是质量不对称度η、q*a_X和Rydberg失谐。

Abstract: In polar semiconductors the Fröhlich interaction is the dominant electron--phonon coupling, yet excitonic resonances in materials such as halide perovskites remain anomalously sharp. We show that standard frozen-center-of-mass treatments of the exciton--phonon problem miss the decisive kinematic degree of freedom: restoring the exact center-of-mass (COM) recoil reveals a universally open, parameter-free $1s\to1s$ absorption channel at recoil momentum $q_*=\sqrt{2M_{\rm ex}\hbarω_{\rm LO}}/\hbar$, whose rate scales as $N_{\rm LO}(T)$. We prove that this recoil channel is controlled by destructive electron--hole interference: the recoil linewidth vanishes with the mass asymmetry as $γ_{\rm LO}^{\rm recoil}\propto\mathcal{F}_{1s,1s}(q_*)^2$, and the elastic dressing obeys the exact suppression law $S_X/S_{\rm ind}=η^2(6-η^2)/5$ within the hydrogenic Fröhlich model. The theory establishes a hierarchy of scattering regimes. In mass-asymmetric materials (GaAs, $η=-0.74$) the recoil channel is active ($γ_{\rm LO}^{\rm recoil}=2.2$~meV); in mass-symmetric materials (FAPbI$_3$, $η=0$; MAPbI$_3$, $η=-0.11$) it is killed by interference (0.00 and 0.12 meV), showing that the observed 27--40 meV perovskite linewidths cannot be accounted for by COM recoil and therefore require internal-state-changing and other inelastic channels, of which the constructive, $η$-robust $1s\to np$ resonance is the leading candidate within the present model. The Fröhlich constant $α$ alone is therefore insufficient as a figure of merit: after projection onto the correlated exciton, the controlling parameters are $η$, $q_*a_X$, and the Rydberg detuning.

</details>


### [149] [Frustration-induced multiferroicity in hauerite MnS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01990)
*Vilmos Kocsis,Harish K. Singh,Kranthi K. Bestha,Yaqian Guo,Maxim Mostovoy,Jeroen van den Brink,Oleg Janson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: MnS₂在48 K以下形成共线反铁磁序，伴随晶胞加倍与反演对称性破缺，产生高达360 μC/m²的铁电极化，从而成为第二类多铁材料；其电极化微观上源于磁性阻挫诱导的S₂二聚体偶极矩，且铁电畴翻转需克服磁交换能级尺度的势垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究硫铁矿型磁性绝缘体MnS₂中反铁磁序导致的对称性破缺能否产生铁电极化，从而揭示其多铁性质及磁电耦合机制。

Method: 结合对称性分析与微观电子机制分析：一方面考察磁序引起的晶胞加倍和反演对称性破缺，另一方面追踪S₂二聚体偶极矩的来源及其与邻近Mn位点磁性阻挫的关系；并分析时间反演对称性对铁电畴的影响及畴翻转的能垒。

Result: MnS₂呈现高达360 μC/m²的铁电极化，属于第二类多铁材料；电极化由磁性阻挫诱导的S₂二聚体偶极矩产生；时间反演对称性保持铁电畴完整；畴翻转需克服由磁交换能级设定的高能垒。

Conclusion: MnS₂是一种由反铁磁序驱动、具有强磁电耦合的第二类多铁材料，其铁电极化与磁性紧密关联，畴调控受磁交换能级限制。

Abstract: Pyrite-type mineral hauerite MnS$_2$ is a magnetic insulator with localized $S=\frac52$ moments. Below 48 K, it develops a collinear antiferromagnetic order, accompanied by unit-cell doubling and breaking the inversion symmetry. We demonstrate that the latter gives rise to a ferroelectric polarization of up to 360 $μ$C/m$^2$ rendering MnS$_2$ a type-II multiferroic. Microscopically, the electronic polarization originates from S$_2$ dimers that acquire a dipole moment induced by the frustrated magnetic ordering of neighboring Mn sites. We show that time-reversal symmetry leaves ferroelectric domains intact, and that domain switching necessitates overcoming a high energy barrier set by the magnetic exchange energy scale.

</details>


### [150] [Magnetic Phase Diagrams and Spin Hamiltonian of Monoclinic $α$-RuCl$_3$ from Angle-Dependent Torque Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02006)
*Daniel Antoniou,Danrui Ni,John S. Pearce,Robert J. Cava,Amalia I. Coldea,Radu Coldea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究亚100微米高质量α-RuCl₃单晶（低温保持单斜结构）的磁性，通过压电悬臂扭矩测量构建三个正交晶面内的磁场相图，发现其与菱方样品存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-RuCl₃是自旋轨道纠缠jeff=1/2的Ru³⁺磁矩在强阻挫Kitaev及键依赖各向异性相互作用下展现非常规协同磁性的候选材料。以往研究多集中于较大单晶（150 K以下发生单斜→菱方结构相变，约7 K进入zigzag磁序），而小尺寸单晶的磁行为尚不明确。

Method: 采用高灵敏度压电悬臂磁扭矩测量技术，在最高16 T磁场下对亚100微米单晶进行测量；通过在三个正交晶面内连续改变磁场角度与强度，利用原始扭矩数据及其对角和场强的高阶导数异常来检测相变；并将扭矩数据与考虑单斜晶格对称性的α-RuCl₃磁哈密顿量平均场计算进行直接对比。

Result: 小尺寸单晶冷却至低温仍保持单斜结构，并在14 K出现尖锐磁相变。所获磁相图与菱方样品相比存在诸多差异：旋转对称性降低、抑制自发磁序所需磁场显著更高、并出现不同的场诱导相。基于对比提出了各相区的最小磁结构模型。

Conclusion: 单斜对称性对α-RuCl₃的磁性相图有决定性影响，小尺寸单晶为研究该体系中结构-磁性耦合及Kitaev物理提供了独特平台。

Abstract: The layered honeycomb $α$-RuCl$_3$ has been much explored as a candidate to display unconventional cooperative magnetism between spin-orbit entangled $j_{\rm eff}=1/2$ Ru$^{3+}$ magnetic moments stabilized by strongly-frustrated, Kitaev and other bond-dependent anisotropic interactions. Most studies so far have focused on relatively large single crystals that undergo a structural phase transition from monoclinic to rhombohedral upon cooling below 150 K and a magnetic transition to zigzag order around 7 K. Here we study the magnetism of high-quality, very small (sub 100 $μ$m diameter) single crystals, which remain monoclinic upon cooling to low temperatures and display a sharp magnetic transition at 14 K. Using highly-sensitive piezo-cantilever magnetic torque measurements in fields up to 16 T, we report a comprehensive study of the magnetic phase diagrams for magnetic field rotated in three orthogonal crystallographic planes. Our extensive torque data collected upon varying almost continuously the angular orientation of the field as well as the field magnitude, allows clear detection of phase transitions via anomalies in the raw torque data and its higher order derivatives with respect to both angle and field magnitude. The obtained magnetic phase diagrams show many differences compared to rhombohedral samples, displaying reduced rotational symmetry, substantially higher fields required to suppress the spontaneous magnetic order, and distinct field-induced phases. We provide a direct comparison of the torque data with mean-field calculations for magnetic Hamiltonians appropriate for $α$-RuCl$_3$ allowing for the monoclinic lattice symmetry and propose minimal magnetic structure models for all explored regions of the magnetic phase diagrams.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Closed-form evaluation of the cyclic four-electron atomic correlation integral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00036)
*Jonas Matuzas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了Bonham 1965分类中case V（四电子环状Slater积分）在正指数下的有限实表达式，权重不超过三，由18个对数、二重对数、三重对数块组成，并附带等指数特例、数值验证、极限律与对称性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环四电子Slater积分（case V拓扑）长期缺乏无需根式、广义多重对数、围道约定或未求极限的完整闭式求值，作者希望给出一个纯对数与（多）对数、所有自变量实且落在可控区间内的显式公式，并系统验证其正确性。

Method: 将一般正指数的case V积分表示为18个仅含对数、二重对数、三重对数（自变量为指数有理函数）的块之和，并把非平凡多重对数自变量映射到(-1,1)；对等指数取极限化简；用三条独立数值路径、经典公式的精确二进求值、以及已发表标定值做交叉验证；同时推导小/大指数极限、二面体对称与重排律。

Result: 得到有限实表达式，不含根式、广义多重对数、围道约定或未求极限；等指数时化为I(λ,λ,λ,λ)=(4π)^4λ^{-4}[Li_3(1/4)/2−7ζ(3)/24+π^2 log2/12−2log^3 2/3]，为仅在2和3处分歧的权三周期；三条数值路径在四组指数元组上至少吻合32位小数，精确二进求值给出8个100位凭证，三个已发表四电子标定值逐位复现；并证明小/大指数极限律（系数闭式）、二面体对称性与三种不等价循环放置的严格重排律。公式适用于四环拓扑，不处理两个带弦拓扑。

Conclusion: 作者完成了case V型循环四电子Slater积分的显式权三求值，提供了可复现的高精度数值验证、等指数特例的周期结构以及极限与对称/重排性质；方法明确限定于四环拓扑，不覆盖带弦情形。

Abstract: For positive exponents $α_1,\ldots,α_4$ we evaluate the cyclic four-electron Slater integral, the topology recorded as case V in Bonham's 1965 classification, in a finite real expression of weight at most three. The general answer is an explicit sum of eighteen blocks involving only logarithms, dilogarithms and trilogarithms of rational functions of the exponents; all logarithm arguments are positive and all nonconstant polylogarithm arguments may be placed in $(-1,1)$, so no radical, generalized polylogarithm, contour prescription or unevaluated limit is required. At equal exponents the value collapses to $I(λ,λ,λ,λ)=(4π)^4λ^{-4}[\mathrm{Li}_3(1/4)/2-7ζ(3)/24+π^2\log 2/12-2\log^3 2/3]$, a weight-three period over $\mathbb{Q}$ ramified only at 2 and 3. Three independently implemented numerical routes agree at four exponent tuples to at least 32 decimal places, an exact-dyadic evaluation of the classical formula gives eight 100-place certificates, and three published four-electron calibration values are reproduced to every printed digit. We also prove the small- and large-exponent limit laws with closed-form coefficients, dihedral symmetry, and a strict rearrangement law for the three inequivalent cyclic placements of four exponents. The formula applies to the four-cycle and does not evaluate the two chorded topologies.

</details>


### [152] [Quantum Reverse Hypercontractivity for KMS-Symmetric Quantum Markov Semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00242)
*Jinsong Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构造了原初KMS对称量子Markov半群的反例，证明p-对数Sobolev常数在p=1/2处消失、并在p→0+时趋于负无穷，从而在(0,1)上排除了统一的逆向超压缩速率；同时给出了1/2<p≤q<1及去相位扰动下的正结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Markov半群的逆向超压缩性（reverse hypercontractivity）在小p区间是否具有统一定量速率尚不清楚。本文旨在通过构造反例与给出充分条件，厘清p∈(0,1)尤其是p→0+时的行为边界。

Method: 构造满足KMS对称性的原初量子Markov半群及其基于图的关联实例，计算其p-对数Sobolev常数随p的变化；并结合去相位（dephasing）扰动与显式充分时间估计，分析逆向超压缩性的成立范围。

Result: 1) 存在原初KMS对称量子Markov半群，其p-对数Sobolev常数在p=1/2处消失；2) 存在相关图实例，其常数在p→0+时趋于负无穷，从而阻碍(0,1)上正的统一逆向超压缩速率；3) 任意原初KMS对称量子Markov半群在1/2<p≤q<1上满足量子逆向超压缩，并给出显式充分时间；4) 对任意固定δ>0，足够强的去相位扰动满足[δ,1]上统一正的对数Sobolev不等式，因而对δ<p≤q<1成立逆向超压缩；5) 对n≥3的连通图，图基KMS对称量子Markov半群在δ<p≤q<1（固定0<δ<1）上成立逆向超压缩。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了量子逆向超压缩在p→0+区间缺乏统一正速率的内在障碍，同时说明在1/2<p<1以及经去相位扰动或图结构条件下仍可获得逆向超压缩性，刻画了该性质的适用边界。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct primitive KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroups for which the $p$-logarithmic Sobolev constant vanishes at $p=1/2$ and a correlated graph-based example for which the $p$-logarithmic Sobolev constant tends to $-\infty$ as $p\to 0^+$. These examples obstruct a positive uniform reverse-hypercontractivity rate on $(0,1)$. By contrast, we show that every primitive KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroup satisfies quantum reverse hypercontractivity for $1/2< p\leq q < 1$ with explicit sufficient times. For each fixed $δ>0$, a sufficiently strong dephasing perturbation satisfies a uniform positive logarithmic Sobolev inequality on $[δ, 1]$ and hence quantum reverse hypercontractivity for $δ< p\leq q<1$ with fixed $0<δ<1/2$. Furthermore, we establish reverse hypercontractivity for graph-based KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroups for $δ< p\leq q< 1$ with fixed $0<δ<1$ for connected graphs when $n \geq 3$.

</details>


### [153] [Complete Factorial Asymptotics and Integrable Structure of Generalized Charlier Recurrence Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00396)
*Mahouton Norbert Hounkonnou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究广义Charlier多项式（离散权重ρ_μ(k)=μ^k/(k!)^2）的首项递推系数，证明偏差β_n-n与μ-γ_n具有超出所有代数阶的阶乘渐近，量级为μ^n/(n!)^2，并给出精确常数系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Charlier多项式的递推系数偏离其渐近主项（β_n→n, γ_n→μ）的修正项此前缺乏完整的阶乘级渐近刻画；作者希望建立精确的偏差渐近公式并揭示其与可积结构的联系。

Method: 引入参数化β_n-n=√μ c_n c_{n+1}、γ_n=μ(1-c_n^2)，将问题化为满足离散Painlevé II方程的正序列(c_n)；在c_n=0附近线性化得到离散Bessel方程，其劣解为J_n(2√μ)。通过Bessel比较与基于Hankel行列式扇区分解的全局分析证明比值c_n/J_n(2√μ)→1（连接公式L(μ)=1）。另一路方法将Hankel行列式Δ_n(μ)视为离散对数气体，分解为Δ_n(μ)=μ^{n(n-1)/2}T_n(μ)，并作扇区分解T_n(μ)=1+∑_{q=1}^n S_q(μ)，其中S_q与n无关且S_q(μ)∼e^μ μ^q/(q!)^2。

Result: 证明c_n∼J_n(2√μ)（n→∞），从而得到精确渐近β_n-n∼μ^{n+1}/(n!(n+1)!)、μ-γ_n∼μ^{n+1}/(n!)^2，即偏差超出所有代数阶、出现在μ^n/(n!)^2尺度。Hankel行列式的扇区分解给出阶乘修正的独立推导。

Conclusion: Bessel比较与扇区分解共同揭示了离散Painlevé动力学、Toda形变与Hankel–分拆表示之间的联系，展现出广义Charlier多项式背后丰富的可积结构。

Abstract: We study the monic recurrence coefficients of the generalized Charlier polynomials associated with the discrete weight
  \[
  ρ_μ(k)
  =
  \frac{μ^k}{(k!)^2},
  \qquad
  k\in\mathbb N_0,
  \qquad
  μ>0.
  \]
  We establish complete factorial asymptotics for the deviations $β_n-n$ and $μ-γ_n$, proving that they are beyond all algebraic orders and appear at the scale $μ^{n}/(n!)^2$. The key step is a parametrization $β_n-n=\sqrtμ\,c_nc_{n+1}$, $γ_n=μ(1-c_n^2)$, which reduces the problem to a positive sequence $(c_n)$ satisfying a discrete Painlevé II equation.
  Linearization around $c_n=0$ yields a discrete Bessel equation whose recessive solution is $J_n(2\sqrtμ)$. We prove that the ratio $c_n/J_n(2\sqrtμ)$ converges to a finite limit $L(μ)$, which we identify exactly as $L(μ)=1$ by combining Bessel comparison with a global analysis based on the sector decomposition of the associated Hankel determinant. This yields the exact connection formula
  \[
  c_n \sim J_n(2\sqrtμ), \qquad n\to\infty,
  \]
  and consequently the precise asymptotics
  \[
  β_n-n\sim \frac{μ^{n+1}}{n!(n+1)!}, \qquad
  μ-γ_n\sim \frac{μ^{n+1}}{(n!)^2}.
  \]
  We further develop a complementary approach based on Hankel determinants interpreted as a discrete logarithmic gas. We show that the Hankel determinant factorizes as $Δ_n(μ)=μ^{n(n-1)/2}T_n(μ)$ and admits a sector decomposition $T_n(μ)=1+\sum_{q=1}^{n}S_q(μ)$ with sector contributions independent of $n$. We establish $S_q(μ)\sim e^μμ^q/(q!)^2$, providing an independent derivation of the factorial corrections. Together, the Bessel comparison and sector decomposition reveal a rich integrable structure linking discrete Painlevé dynamics, Toda deformations, and Hankel--partition representations.

</details>


### [154] [On spectrum of the harmonic Kronig-Penney model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00879)
*J. Gibbons,A. P. Veselov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究经"穿衣链"理论启发的量子力学Kronig-Penney模型之谐振修正的谱性质，证明在特定情形下基态可显式给出，且所有谱隙长度相等，其端点可表示为相应Weber-Hermite函数的零点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Kronig-Penney模型是周期势中量子粒子的经典可解模型，但其谱结构相对刚性。作者希望借助穿衣链（dressing chain）理论对模型进行"谐振修正"，以获得更丰富、仍可解析处理的谱结构，从而深入理解周期势与谐振项耦合下的能带与谱隙行为。

Method: 以穿衣链理论为出发点，对量子力学Kronig-Penney模型施加谐振（harmonic）修正，构造相应的一维周期Schrödinger型算子，并对其谱（能带结构、基态、谱隙）进行解析分析，借助Weber-Hermite函数刻画谱隙端点。

Result: 在若干特殊情形下，修正模型的基态可被显式求得；进一步证明所有谱隙具有相同的长度，且这些谱隙的端点可表示为相应Weber-Hermite函数的零点。

Conclusion: 谐振修正的Kronig-Penney模型在特定参数下具有显式可解的基态与等长谱隙结构，揭示了穿衣链理论与周期量子模型之间的深刻联系，并为相关可积谱问题的研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study the spectral properties of the harmonic modification of the quantum mechanical Kronig-Penney model motivated by the theory of the dressing chain. In particular, we show that in the special cases the corresponding ground states can be given explicitly and all spectral gaps have the same length with the ends expressed as zeros of the corresponding Weber-Hermite functions.

</details>


### [155] [The Buchholz Algebra from the Universal Resolvent Algebra--Particle Structure, Gibbs States, and Closed-Path Expansions without Fock Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01622)
*Yoshitsugu Sekine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在不依赖Fock表示的情况下构造了Buchholz代数，将其实现为规范固定预解代数内禀粒子截断商的有界逆向极限，并给出迹、KMS态及路径积分预备结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Buchholz代数依赖Fock空间表示，限制了对粒子数截断结构、非微扰路径积分表述及准局域性质的内禀刻画。作者希望摆脱Fock表示，从预解代数出发给出更本征的构造。

Method: 以规范固定预解代数的内禀粒子截断商为基本构件，取带等距粒子数度坐标的有界逆向极限构造Buchholz代数；在有限矩阵角（corner）上分析其代数与态结构。

Result: 有限矩阵角上存在典范迹、KMS态与迹范数乘积公式；并建立闭路展开，为路径积分表示奠定基础。已知的Fock空间描述与一个恰当准局域子代数作为实现（realization）出现。

Conclusion: 该构造给出Buchholz代数的不依赖Fock表示的内禀框架，统一了迹、KMS态、乘积公式、闭路展开与Fock实现，并为路径积分表示提供出发点。

Abstract: We construct the Buchholz algebra without a Fock representation, as the bounded inverse limit of intrinsic particle-cutoff quotients of the gauge-fixed resolvent algebra, with isometric particle-degree coordinates. Its finite matrix corners carry canonical traces, KMS states, and trace-norm product formulas, together with closed-path expansions that lay the groundwork for path-integral representations. The known Fock-space description and a proper quasilocal subalgebra arise as realizations.

</details>


### [156] [Quantum hierarchies of the ILW and BO equations and the DR cycles on the moduli spaces of curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01841)
*Alexandr Buryak,Abner Hernandez Rodriguez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子ILW可积层级的一个极限与Nazarov–Sklyanin量子BO层级之间的关系，提出联系二者的猜想并给出多种验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前利用任意上同调场论和模空间上的双分歧（DR）循环可构造一大类量子层级，其中特例是对中间长波（ILW）方程层级的量子化。ILW方程有两个著名极限：KdV方程与Benjamin–Ono（BO）方程，而BO方程的量子层级已由Nazarov与Sklyanin构造。因此自然的问题是：量子ILW层级的相应极限是否与Nazarov–Sklyanin层级一致，二者之间的精确关系此前尚不清楚。

Method: 基于上同调场论与DR循环的构造框架，考察量子ILW层级在BO极限下的行为，并将其与Nazarov–Sklyanin量子层级进行比较；通过提出刻画二者关系的猜想，并给出多个具体检验来支持该猜想。

Result: 提出了一个描述量子ILW层级某一极限与Nazarov–Sklyanin层级之间关系的猜想，并给出了多种检验以支持该猜想。

Conclusion: 量子ILW层级与Nazarov–Sklyanin量子BO层级之间存在由所提猜想刻画的联系，初步验证支持该猜想，但其严格证明仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: In an earlier paper of P. Rossi and the first author, a construction of a wide class of quantum hierarchies was presented, with the input data given by an arbitrary cohomological field theory. A crucial role in the construction is played by the double ramification (DR) cycles on the moduli spaces of stable curves. As a special case, one gets a quantization of the hierarchy of the Intermediate Long Wave (ILW) equation. The ILW equation has two famous limits: the Korteweg--de Vries equation and the Benjamin--Ono (BO) equation, and for the latter one a quantum hierarchy was constructed by Nazarov and Sklyanin. In our paper, we propose a conjecture describing a relation between a limit of the quantum ILW hierarchy and the Nazarov--Sklyanin hierarchy and present various checks to support it.

</details>


### [157] [Quadratic symmetry algebras and Sturm algebraization of superintegrable systems with magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01869)
*Shams Ara,Md Fazlul Hoque,Ian Marquette*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究两个在非零轴对称磁场中的三维量子超可积系统，揭示其在原始哈密顿量、抛物分离后及完全Sturm问题三个层次上分别存在的二次代数、$sl(2)$与$sl(3)$代数化结构，并解释了两磁系统共享$sl(3)$结构但不等价的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望理解多分离超可积系统在不同处理阶段可能涌现的隐藏代数结构与(准)精确可解性，特别关注两个在原始分类中不同的轴对称磁场哈密顿量为何会呈现共同的代数特征。

Method: 对两个三维量子超可积系统，利用积分构造带Casimir的二次代数并通过有限维形变振子表示获得代数谱；在圆抛物坐标下将分离方程化为具有限多项式扇区的六次双合流Heun方程，并表述为以物理能量为参数、分离常数为对角的Sturm本征值问题；经规范变换实现$sl(2)$代数化并约化为有限三对角矩阵；进一步构造分离前的Sturm表示，取逆平方耦合为Sturm本征值。

Result: 分离方程在规范变换后各约化为有限三对角矩阵；固定轴向积分、变量替换与规范旋转后，两变量Sturm哈密顿量及非中心积分属于普适包络代数$U(sl(3))$。尽管两磁场哈密顿量在原始分类中不同，固定轴向积分后它们约化为同一奇异2:1振子正规形，仅有效参数嵌入不同。

Conclusion: 研究给出三种互补的代数描述：原哈密顿量的二次代数/形变振子表示、抛物分离后的$sl(2)$代数化、以及分离前完整Sturm问题的$sl(3)$代数化。共享的$sl(3)$结构源于约化到同一正规形，并不意味着原磁系统等价，说明不同阶段可涌现不同的隐藏代数结构与精确或准精确可解性。

Abstract: We study two three-dimensional quantum superintegrable systems in nonvanishing axially symmetric magnetic fields. For each model the integrals generate a quadratic algebra with a Casimir, and finite-dimensional deformed-oscillator representations yield algebraic spectra. The same spectra are recovered by separation of variables. In circular parabolic coordinates the separated equations are sextic biconfluent-Heun equations with finite polynomial sectors. We formulate them as Sturm eigenvalue problems with the physical energy as a parameter and the separation constant diagonalized. After gauge transformation, each equation admits an $sl(2)$ algebraization and reduces to a finite tridiagonal matrix. We also construct a pre-separation Sturm representation of each complete Hamiltonian, taking the inverse-square coupling as the Sturm eigenvalue. The transformed integrals remain commuting and, after fixing the axial integral, changing variables and gauge rotating, the two-variable Sturm Hamiltonian and its non-central integrals belong to the universal enveloping algebra $U(sl(3))$. We thus obtain three complementary algebraic descriptions: the quadratic-algebra/deformed-oscillator representation of the original Hamiltonian, the $sl(2)$ algebraization after parabolic separation, and the $sl(3)$ algebraization of the complete Sturm problem before separation. Although the two magnetic Hamiltonians are distinct in the original classification, fixing the axial integral reduces them to the same singular 2:1 oscillator normal form with different effective parameter embeddings. This explains the common $sl(3)$ structure without implying equivalence of the original magnetic systems. These results show how distinct hidden algebraic structures and exact or quasi-exact solvability can emerge at different stages of a multiseparable superintegrable system.

</details>


### [158] [Superintegrability of a resonant ghostly tri-Hamiltonian model: polynomial first integrals and transcendental completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01901)
*Andreas Fring,Ian Marquette*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一个具有不定动能的三维共振哈密顿系统的经典与量子超可积性，并给出其三种Poisson描述。结果表明该系统在多项式与有理函数类中为极小超可积，在允许超越积分时可达到极大超可积，并通过Weyl量子化给出精确高阶微分对称与非局域量子对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究同一流的不同Poisson描述下的超可积性，有助于厘清多项式、有理函数与超越函数层级上不变环与首次积分域的结构差异，尤其是当动能符号不定时系统的可积性层级与其量子对应。

Method: 以共振三维哈密顿系统为对象，结合三种Poisson描述；分析三个对合二次哈密顿量以及由奇偶动量宇称的两个四次原始生成元I_4^o、I_4^e构成的多项式首次积分环；通过一个八次关系确定超越次数为四的情形；求解完整首次积分方程；并利用Weyl量子化构造精确高阶微分对称及非局域量子对应物。

Result: 多项式首次积分环除三个对合二次哈密顿量外还含两个四次原始生成元，八次关系使该环的超越次数为四；一切有理首次积分均为H_1,H_2,H_3,I_4^o的有理函数，故有理不变量域的一般函数秩为四；在不变开集Δ>0上引入无分支超越不变量后，一般光滑函数秩升至五。Weyl量子化在三种Poisson实现中均给出精确高阶微分对称，且无分支第五经典不变量存在稠定非局域量子对应。

Conclusion: 该系统在多项式与有理函数类中为极小超可积，而在Δ>0的不变域上若允许超越积分则为极大超可积；量子化后可由非局域算符实现该超越完备，从而给出超越完备的量子实现。

Abstract: We study classical and quantum superintegrability of a resonant three-dimensional Hamiltonian with indefinite kinetic energy and three Poisson descriptions of the same flow. Besides three commuting quadratic Hamiltonians, the polynomial first-integral ring contains two primitive degree-four generators, $I_4^{\rm o}$ and $I_4^{\rm e}$, of odd and even momentum parity. A single octic relation leaves the polynomial invariant ring with transcendence degree four. Solving the complete first-integral equation shows that every rational first integral is a rational function of $H_1,H_2,H_3,I_4^{\rm o}$, so that the rational invariant field likewise has generic functional rank four. A branch-free transcendental invariant raises the generic smooth functional rank to five on the invariant open set $Δ>0$. The system is therefore minimally superintegrable in the polynomial and rational classes and maximally superintegrable on this invariant domain when transcendental integrals are admitted. Weyl quantisation yields exact higher-order differential symmetries in all three Poisson realisations. The branch-free fifth classical invariant also admits a densely defined nonlocal quantum counterpart, providing a concrete quantum realisation of the transcendental completion.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Topologically Relevant Landmark Selection on Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00019)
*Kaifeng Zhang,Kai Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 提出基于Hodge Laplacian的地标选取方法，通过调和参与度评分恢复原始点云的持续同调特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续同调（PH）在大规模点云上计算代价过高，常用做法是在代表性子集（地标）上近似计算PH。现有地标选取方法主要解决离群点污染问题，而非恢复全数据集的拓扑特征。

Method: 提出一种基于Hodge Laplacian的地标选取方法。为每个点赋予基于其调和参与度（harmonic participation）的拓扑相关性分数，并选取高分点作为地标，从而量化每个点对全局同调结构的参与程度。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上，该方法恢复原始点云PH的准确度优于几何基线和一种基于局部PH的方法。

Conclusion: 基于Hodge Laplacian的拓扑相关性评分能有效选取拓扑相关地标，比几何与局部PH基线更准确地恢复原始点云的持续同调。

Abstract: Persistent Homology (PH) is an important tool in Topological Data Analysis for point clouds, but can be prohibitively expensive for large datasets. A practical approximation is to compute PH on a smaller subset of representative points, known as landmarks. Existing landmark selection methods mainly address challenges such as outlier contamination rather than restoring the topological features of the full dataset. To select topologically relevant landmarks, we propose a landmark selection method based on Hodge Laplacian. It assigns each point a topological relevance score based on its harmonic participation and selects points with high scores as landmarks. By quantifying each point's participation in global homological structures, our method restores the PH of the original point cloud more accurately than geometric baselines and a method built upon local PH on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [160] [Minimum Spanning Trees for Square Crop Plots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00807)
*Mingyang Gong,Adiesha Liyanage,Braeden Sopp,Muzhou Chen,Binhai Zhu*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究单位正方形集合在"crop plot距离"下的最小生成树问题，证明该问题NP难，并给出4-因子近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机来源于农田中访问作物地块的实际场景：每个作物地块只能由给定的一对入口/出口点访问（共三种类型，每种有四种情况）。因此需要研究这类平面轴对齐单位正方形集合对应的最小生成树问题。

Method: 作者针对单位正方形集合P定义"crop plot距离"，指出该距离即使在全几何设定下也不满足三角不等式，需额外处理；随后分析在该距离下计算MST的复杂度，并设计近似算法。

Result: (1) 证明在crop plot距离下计算单位正方形集合P的最小生成树是NP难的；(2) 给出该MST的4-因子近似算法。

Conclusion: 在crop plot距离下，单位正方形集合的MST问题计算上困难（NP难），但可通过4-因子近似算法有效近似求解。

Abstract: Motivated by accessing crop plots in a field, where each crop plot can only be visited by a given pair of entry/exit points (we have three different types, each having four cases), we study the corresponding Minimum Spanning Tree (MST) problem of such a planar set of axis-aligned unit squares. It turns out that this crop plot distance does not satisfy triangle inequality, even though the whole setup of the problem is geometric. Hence additional care must be taken. The main results of this paper are as follows: (1) we prove that computing the MST of a set $P$ of unit squares under the crop plot distance is NP-hard, (2) the MST of $P$ can be approximated with a factor-4 approximation.

</details>


### [161] [Optimal Coresets for Hyperbolic Farthest-Point Queries via Ideal-Boundary Envelopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01130)
*Eunku Park*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究双曲空间中最近点查询（farthest-point queries）coreset 的最坏情况最优规模，证明对任意固定维度 $D\ge2$，最优 coreset 规模为 $\Theta(\varepsilon^{-(D-1)/2})$，并给出匹配的上下界构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲空间中的 farthest-point 查询在低维表示（如层次数据、树嵌入）中具有天然适配性，但其 coreset 理论基础尚不完善。作者希望确定双曲 farthest-point coreset 的最坏情况最优规模，并刻画其几何本质。

Method: 核心几何工具是将双曲查询精确归约为理想边界（ideal boundary）上的上包络：在 hyperboloid 模型中，每个输入点诱导一个边界得分函数，其对数为沿测地射线的渐近距离偏移；定义 ideal-boundary envelope 为这些函数的逐点最大值，并证明所有查询上的最坏加性损失等于输入包络与 coreset 包络之间的最大对数间隙。上界构造：将最小包围球中心移至原点并归一化空间坐标，得到有界欧氏点集，其边界包络有正下界，再用标准欧氏 kernel 同时逼近所有方向得分最大值，结合结构定理得到两条保证。下界构造：在固定半径双曲球面上做球面填充（spherical packing），配合对跖查询与双曲余弦定理，使每个输入点都不可或缺。

Result: 对每个固定 $D\ge2$，最优最坏情况 coreset 规模为 $\Theta(\varepsilon^{-(D-1)/2})$；下界甚至对两条保证（加性误差与 $1-\varepsilon$ 比例保持）分别成立，均与上界匹配。

Conclusion: 双曲 farthest-point coreset 的最优规模由维度指数 $(D-1)/2$ 决定，理想边界包络归约为双曲几何与欧氏核方法之间架起了精确桥梁。

Abstract: We study coresets for farthest-point queries in hyperbolic space. Given a nonempty finite set $P \subset \mathbb{H}^D$ and $0<\varepsilon \le 1$, we seek a coreset $P_{\varepsilon} \subseteq P$ whose farthest distance from every query point underestimates that of $P$ by at most an additive $\varepsilon$ and retains at least a $1-\varepsilon$ fraction of it. For every fixed $D \ge 2$, we prove that the optimal worst-case coreset size is $Θ\bigl(\varepsilon^{-(D-1)/2}\bigr)$.
  Our main geometric ingredient is an exact reduction from hyperbolic queries to an upper envelope on the ideal boundary. In the hyperboloid model, each input point induces a positive boundary-score function whose logarithm gives its asymptotic distance offset along geodesic rays. We define the \emph{ideal-boundary envelope} as the pointwise maximum of these functions and prove that the supremum additive loss over all queries equals the maximum logarithmic gap between the input and coreset envelopes.
  For the upper bound, we move the minimum-enclosing-ball center to the origin and normalize the spatial coordinates, obtaining a bounded Euclidean point set whose boundary envelope is bounded away from zero. A standard Euclidean kernel then approximates all directional score maxima simultaneously, and the structure theorem yields both guarantees. For the lower bound, a spherical packing on a fixed-radius hyperbolic sphere, together with antipodal queries and the hyperbolic cosine law, makes every input point indispensable, matching the upper bound even for either guarantee separately.

</details>


### [162] [Hyperbolic Sphericity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01336)
*Thomas Bläsius,Lennart Großkreutz,Jean-Pierre von der Heydt*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出双曲球面性概念，并系统研究其与欧氏球面性及球半径的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面性（sphericity）此前仅在欧氏空间中被研究，即用等半径d维球相交表示图的最小维度d。作者希望将这一概念推广到双曲空间，因为双曲球面性可能显著小于欧氏球面性，且与欧氏情形不同，它强烈依赖于球半径，这一特性在理论上和机器学习中的图嵌入应用上都值得探索。

Method: 作者定义了图的双曲球面性：使图具有d维等半径球相交表示的最小维度d。随后分三种情形展开分析：（1）半径可依图选择；（2）半径固定不依赖于图；（3）研究球面性随半径变化的行为，并构造具体图例。

Result: 若半径可依图选择，双曲球面性上界为欧氏球面性，这将二维双曲空间（即均匀圆盘图）的已有结果推广到任意维度，且证明显著更简单；若固定半径，双曲球面性可能大于欧氏球面性，但最多大1；增大半径可大幅降低球面性，但至多增加1；并构造了一个球面性随半径增大而在不同值之间振荡的图。

Conclusion: 双曲球面性与欧氏球面性存在紧密而有规律的定量关系，且对半径高度敏感。这些结果不仅具有理论意义，也对机器学习中图的低维数值表示（图嵌入）具有实际参考价值。

Abstract: The sphericity of a graph is the minimum dimension d such that the graph has an intersection representation of d-dimensional balls of equal radius. While sphericity has been studied in Euclidean space, we initiate the study of hyperbolic sphericity. The hyperbolic sphericity of a graph can be significantly smaller than its Euclidean counterpart, but, contrary to the Euclidean setting, depends strongly on the radius of the balls.
  We show that, if the radius of the balls can be chosen depending on the graph, the hyperbolic sphericity is upper bounded by the Euclidean sphericity. This extends a previous result for 2-dimensional hyperbolic space, i.e., uniform disk graphs, to arbitrary dimensions. Moreover, our proof is significantly simpler. If we fix the radius, i.e., do not make it dependent on the graph, we show that hyperbolic sphericity can be larger than Euclidean sphericity, but by at most 1. Additionally, we study how hyperbolic sphericity changes with the ball radius. We show that choosing a larger radius can substantially decrease the sphericity while increasing it by at most 1. We also provide a construction of a graph where the sphericity oscillates between different values as the radius increases.
  Besides being theoretically interesting, we note that these results are relevant for graph embeddings in machine learning, where one is interested in low-dimensional numeric representations of symbolic data like graphs.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Inverse Optimal Control with Convex Features and Box Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00033)
*Jiguang Yu,Louis Shuo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究凸特征下层目标中非负权重的逆最优控制问题，建立乐观双层最优控制模型，证明响应映射的全局单值性与Lipschitz估计，得到值函数凹性与渐近精确松弛，并给出局部可辨识性、噪声稳定性及C-平稳性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解逆最优控制问题，即通过观测数据识别下层凸特征目标中的非负权重，此类问题具有双层最优控制结构，且需在缺乏活跃集可微性假设下刻画解映射的正则性与稳定性。

Method: 将问题建模为乐观双层最优控制问题，下层为L^2中带箱约束的强凸线性约束控制问题；对约化响应映射 x↦(y_x,u_x) 证明全局单值性与显式Lipschitz估计；利用下层泛函对x的仿射依赖性，分析值函数并构造渐近精确的最优值松弛；通过二阶增长的无梯度论证建立局部可辨识性与O(δ)噪声稳定性；用KKT重构为函数空间MPCC，并借助Scholtes松弛在乘子一致有界下导出C-平稳性。

Result: 证明了响应映射的全局单值性和显式Lipschitz估计（无需活跃集可微性）；值函数是凹且Lipschitz的，并得到渐近精确的最优值松弛；在二阶增长条件下获得局部可辨识性与O(δ)噪声稳定性；KKT重构给出函数空间MPCC，Scholtes松弛在一致乘子有界下给出C-平稳性；解耦标量例子通过阈值接触的有限性给出显式严格互补证书。

Conclusion: 该工作为凸特征逆最优控制提供了非光滑解映射的正则性、值函数凹性、渐近精确松弛及噪声稳定性等理论保证，并通过M P C C与Scholtes松弛建立最优性条件，标量算例验证了严格互补性。

Abstract: We study an inverse optimal-control problem for identifying nonnegative weights in a convex-feature lower-level objective. The resulting model is an optimistic bilevel optimal-control problem whose lower level is a strongly convex, linearly constrained control problem with box constraints in \(L^2\). For the reduced response map \(x\mapsto (y_x,u_x)\), we prove global single-valuedness and an explicit Lipschitz estimate, without assuming differentiability of active sets. Exploiting the affine dependence of the lower-level functional on \(x\), we show that the value function is concave and Lipschitz and derive an asymptotically exact optimal-value relaxation. We further establish local identifiability and \(O(δ)\) noise stability by a gradient-free argument based on second-order growth. Finally, a KKT reformulation yields a function-space MPCC; Scholtes relaxation gives C-stationarity under uniform multiplier boundedness. A decoupled scalar example provides an explicit strict-complementarity certificate via finiteness of threshold contacts.

</details>


### [164] [Improved upper bounds for five IHTC-2024 benchmark instances via exact reoptimization of the nurse-to-room layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00044)
*Alfonso Gippini Requeijo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 报告IHTC-2024基准上5个改进上界，通过对护士-房间-班次层的精确重优化获得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IHTC-2024基准的已知最优上界表仍开放提交，且现有已发表解在护士层可能未达最优，存在改进空间。

Method: 固定已发表解的患者入院日、房间分配与手术室分配，仅对护士-房间-班次层重新优化至证明最优。

Result: 在i02、i05、m01、m03、m04五个实例上改进上界（如i02由1264降至1263），改进幅度0.075%–0.125%，均通过官方IHTP验证器且无硬约束违反。

Conclusion: 护士层可精确求解的实例中大多在数秒内闭合，说明若干已发表最优解在该层并未真正最优，护士层是易被忽视的改进来源。

Abstract: The Integrated Healthcare Timetabling Competition 2024 (IHTC-2024) defined a benchmark that integrates surgical case planning, patient admission scheduling and nurse-to-room assignment. Its instance set remains live: the organizers keep a public table of best-known upper bounds that is open to new submissions. We report five improved upper bounds on that table, for instances i02 (1263, previously 1264), i05 (12744, previously 12760), m01 (3380, previously 3384), m03 (6692, previously 6697) and m04 (3315, previously 3318). All five solutions were checked with the official IHTP validator and carry no hard-constraint violations. The solutions are not built from scratch: each is derived from the corresponding solution published on the benchmark page, holding its admission days, room assignments and operating-theater assignments fixed, and reoptimizing only the nurse-to-room-and-shift layer to proven optimality. The improvements are small in magnitude, between 0.075% and 0.125%, but they carry a structural observation: on the instances where the nurse layer can be closed exactly, it closes within seconds on all but one of them, and several of the published state-of-the-art solutions fall short of optimality in that layer.

</details>


### [165] [Predictor-Based Exponential Tracking of Turbulent Solutions for the Kuramoto--Sivashinsky Equation with Input Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00048)
*Amadou Cissé*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对带常输入时滞的非线性Kuramoto–Sivashinsky方程，利用预测器–反步变换补偿时滞，实现参考轨迹的一致指数跟踪，并给出适定性与稳定性证明及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究受常输入时滞影响的非线性Kuramoto–Sivashinsky方程对非定常轨迹的跟踪问题。参考轨迹取自全局吸引子中的完整轨迹族，不要求其静止、周期、慢变或由有限维外系统生成，因而比常规跟踪设定更具一般性，同时时滞使指令生成与执行之间存在时间失配，需要专门补偿。

Method: 将时滞输入用一阶输运方程建模，与四阶跟踪误差动力学耦合构成增广系统；采用预测器–反步变换把增广闭环系统映射为无时滞的名义误差动力学，该动力学由齐次输运子系统的出射迹驱动，并在一个时滞区间后消失；借助一致吸引子界选择反馈参数与稳定性常数，使其不依赖于初始时刻和参考轨迹。

Result: 在增广状态空间中建立了全局适定性与一致指数稳定性。数值结果表明：在所考虑构型下，未补偿的时滞反馈会放大跟踪误差，而预测器补偿可恢复持续衰减；预测器一致性、有限时间熄灭和离散细化等诊断支持数值实现的有效性。

Conclusion: 预测器–反步变换能够有效补偿常输入时滞，实现对全局吸引子内非定常参考轨迹的一致指数跟踪，且参数选择与初始时刻、参考轨迹无关，数值实验验证了补偿的必要性与方法的可实现性。

Abstract: Uniform exponential tracking is addressed for nonstationary trajectories of the nonlinear Kuramoto--Sivashinsky equation subject to a constant input delay. The reference belongs to a family of complete trajectories contained in the global attractor and need not be stationary, periodic, slowly varying, or generated by a finite-dimensional exosystem. The delayed input is represented by a first-order transport equation coupled with the fourth-order tracking-error dynamics. A predictor--backstepping transformation compensates for the temporal mismatch between command generation and actuation by mapping the augmented closed-loop system into the nominal delay-free error dynamics driven by the outgoing trace of a homogeneous transport subsystem. This subsystem vanishes after one delay interval. Uniform attractor bounds permit the feedback parameters and stability constants to be selected independently of the initial time and the reference trajectory. Global well-posedness and uniform exponential stability are established in the augmented state space. Numerical results show that, for the considered configuration, uncompensated delayed feedback amplifies the tracking error, whereas predictor compensation restores sustained decay. Predictor-consistency, finite-time-extinction, and discretization-refinement diagnostics support the numerical implementation.

</details>


### [166] [New Pareto Z-eigenvalue localization theorems for tensors with applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00113)
*Licai Shao,Xingong Shao,Yisheng Song*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为张量特征值互补问题的Pareto Z-特征值建立新的定位集，讨论其与已有定位集的关系，并应用于TCP与TGEiCP解的存在性判定及标量势真空稳定性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量特征值互补问题的解存在性判定需要有效的特征值定位工具，而现有定位集之间存在关系不明、覆盖范围有限的问题，因此有必要构造新的定位集并厘清其与已有结果的联系。

Method: 针对张量特征值互补问题的Pareto Z-特征值，构造若干新的定位集，并分析这些定位集与现有定位集之间的包含或强弱关系；随后将其作为工具推导TCP与TGEiCP的解存在性充分条件。

Result: 得到了Pareto Z-特征值的新定位集及其与已有定位集的关系；由此给出张量互补问题（TCP）和张量广义特征值互补问题（TGEiCP）解存在性的一个充分条件。

Conclusion: 新的定位集为张量互补类问题的解存在性提供了有效判据，该充分条件还可用于检验一般标量势的真空稳定性，体现了方法的可迁移性。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish some new localization sets for Pareto Z-eigenvalues of tensor eigenvalue complementarity problems and discuss the relationships among existing ones. As applications, a sufficient condition for the solution existence of the tensor complementarity problem (TCP) and the tensor generalized eigenvalue complementarity problem (TGEiCP) are presented. In addition, this condition is also used to test the vacuum stability of a general scalar potential

</details>


### [167] [Control of 1D first-order linear hyperbolic systems: a brief survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00139)
*Long Hu,Guillaume Olive*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 这是一篇综述性文章，回顾了一维一阶线性双曲系统控制方面的文献，以纪念Jean-Michel Coron 70岁诞辰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该文为纪念Jean-Michel Coron 70岁诞辰而作，旨在系统梳理他在一维一阶线性双曲系统控制领域所做出的重要贡献及相关文献。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，围绕一维一阶线性双曲系统的控制问题进行归纳与评述。

Result: 对一维一阶线性双曲系统控制领域的文献进行了系统回顾。

Conclusion: 该综述总结了Jean-Michel Coron在此领域的贡献，并为相关研究提供了文献参考。

Abstract: At the occasion of the 70th birthday anniversary of Jean-Michel Coron, we review the literature on the control of 1D first-order linear hyperbolic systems, a topic where he has made significant contributions.

</details>


### [168] [Minimal control time of some 1D first-order linear hyperbolic systems with row rank deficient boundary coupling matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00140)
*Long Hu,Guillaume Olive,Zengzhi Xin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一维一阶线性双曲系统在边界耦合矩阵行秩亏缺情形下的最小控制时间刻画问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界耦合矩阵满行秩时，一维一阶线性双曲系统的最小控制时间已有明确结论；但对于行秩亏缺的边界耦合矩阵，其最小控制时间尚缺乏刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对一类边界耦合矩阵行秩亏缺的一维一阶线性双曲系统，通过分析边界耦合结构对可控性的影响，刻画其最小控制时间。

Result: 给出了该类行秩亏缺边界耦合矩阵系统最小控制时间的刻画结果。

Conclusion: 将最小控制时间的结果从满行秩边界耦合矩阵推广到行秩亏缺的一类系统。

Abstract: The minimal control time of one-dimensional first-order linear hyperbolic systems is by now well-known when the boundary coupling matrix is full row rank. The goal of this article is to characterize the minimal control time for a class of systems with row rank deficient boundary coupling matrices.

</details>


### [169] [Sharpness and Rigidity of the Two-Factor Recht-Re Lower Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00155)
*Jinze Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Lai 与 Lim 关于半正定矩阵乘积和负特征值下界常数紧性的猜想，给出达到该界的显式构造，并完整分类等号情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机优化方法在连续步骤使用不同数据点时，会产生半正定矩阵的乘积；即使每个矩阵均半正定，其平均仍可能出现负特征值。Lai 与 Lim 在 ICML 2020 给出下界并猜想常数对所有 n≥2 都紧，但缺乏构造性证明，本文旨在验证该猜想并刻画等号结构。

Method: 构造一族 n 维空间中的 n 个秩一矩阵，通过将一组标准正交基的某一方向压缩 1/√2 倍来实现；同时给出已知不等式的简短自洽证明，并据此分类全部等号情形。

Result: 所构造的秩一矩阵族对每个 n 均达到下界，证明紧性猜想成立；所有极值矩阵都包含一个与构造等价的公共不变 n 维块；等号要求维数恰为 n；正定矩阵只能趋近而无法达到该下端点；强凸二次目标可将极端负特征值实现为两步期望梯度下降更新。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了 Lai–Lim 猜想，确认了下界常数的紧性并给出等号分类，从优化角度解释了极值负特征值的可实现性。

Abstract: Products of positive semidefinite matrices arise when successive steps of a randomized optimization method use different data points. Their average can have a negative eigenvalue even though every individual matrix is positive semidefinite. Lai and Lim proved at ICML 2020 that $\sum_{i\ne j}A_iA_j\succeq-\frac{1}{4}n(n-1)I$ whenever $A_i\succeq0$ and $\sum_i A_i\preceq nI$, and conjectured that the constant is sharp for every $n\ge2$. We give an explicit family of $n$ rank-one matrices in dimension $n$ that attains this bound for every $n$, proving that sharpness statement. The construction compresses one direction of an orthonormal basis by a factor $1/\sqrt{2}$. We also give a short, self-contained proof of the known inequality and classify all equality cases: every extremizer contains a common invariant $n$-dimensional block equivalent to the construction. Consequently, dimension $n$ is necessary for equality, and positive definite matrices approach but never attain the lower endpoint. Finally, strongly convex quadratic objectives realize the extremal negative eigenvalue as a two-step expected gradient-descent update.

</details>


### [170] [A Two-Dimensional Extremizer for Relatively Inexact Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00156)
*Jinze Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出梯度下降一步、梯度误差相对于真实梯度有界时的显式极值函数：一个坐标为二次函数、另一坐标为Huber函数之和，并在中间步长区域达到对最终平方梯度/初始目标间隙和最终平方梯度/目标下降两套最坏情形常数，证明了Vernimmen与Glineur的猜想2.6，并通过变量替换将参数刻画归约为标量三次方程，证明所有参数不等式、给出上界的非负平方证书并验证匹配构造，同时刻画一维最坏值并证明中间区域需要两维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究梯度误差相对于真实梯度有界时梯度下降一步的最坏情形行为，并证明Vernimmen与Glineur于2025年6月提出的猜想2.6，给出显式极值函数与相应常数。

Method: 构造显式极值函数：一个坐标为二次函数、另一个坐标为Huber函数；通过变量替换将参数描述归约为具有严格指定根的标量三次方程；证明所有参数不等式，推导上界的非负平方证书，并直接验证匹配构造。
