Result: 在避免平均光滑性假设的前提下，找到 ε-稳定点的样本复杂度达到最优 O(ε^{-4})；归一化技术使相同速率无需问题相关常数设置超参数；无批次方法亦达到最优速率。在风险规避投资组合优化和分层倾斜经验风险最小化实验中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法在更弱假设下实现最优样本复杂度，并摆脱了对问题相关超参数的依赖，实验证明其实际有效性。

Abstract: This paper investigates stochastic multi-level optimization where the objective is a nested composition of several smooth non-convex functions. We assume that only stochastic estimates of the gradient and function values for each level are accessible. Consequently, obtaining an accurate estimate of the overall gradient is challenging due to the nested structure. To address this, we employ a momentum-based estimator with mini-batches to track the function values of each level, which are subsequently used to construct momentum gradient estimators. We establish an optimal sample complexity of $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$ for finding an $ε$-stationary point, avoiding the stronger average smoothness assumption commonly relied upon in prior literature. Furthermore, by employing a normalization technique, we attain the same rate without requiring problem-dependent constants to set hyperparameters. To achieve the optimal rate without mini-batches, we further develop a batch-free method that incorporates a first-order approximation and a clipping technique for function value estimation. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our proposed methods through experiments on risk-averse portfolio optimization and hierarchical tilted empirical risk minimization.

</details>


### [207] [Convergence Analysis of STORM Under Different Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01599)
*Wei Jiang,Yibo Wang,Wenhao Yang,Rui Yan,Lijun Zhang,Zechao Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在放弃平均光滑性假设的前提下，系统分析了随机递归动量（STORM）方法的收敛性，通过引入辅助序列并与其比较，证明了STORM在非凸、凸及强凸目标下均可达最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STORM的收敛分析依赖于较强的平均光滑性假设，限制了其适用范围，因此需要研究在无平均光滑性假设下STORM的收敛行为。

Method: 首先在平均光滑性下重新审视STORM的结果；随后在无平均光滑性时，设计辅助序列并将其与STORM更新进行对比分析，以推导不同目标函数下的收敛界。

Result: 在平均光滑性下，得到非凸目标的$O(T^{-1/3})$界以及μ-PL条件下最后迭代输出的$O(σ^2/(μT))$界；无平均光滑性时，证明非凸目标仍可达到标准光滑性下最优的$O(T^{-1/4})$率；对凸与λ-强凸目标，分别证明平均和最后迭代的最优率$O(σR/\sqrt T)$与$O(σ^2/(λT))$。

Conclusion: 所有结果均基于同一STORM递归式，仅通过不同超参数选取实现，表明STORM在更弱假设下仍具最优收敛保证。

Abstract: Stochastic recursive momentum (STORM) achieves fast convergence for nonconvex optimization via the variance reduction effect, but existing analyses rely on the strong average smoothness assumption. In this paper, we study the convergence of STORM for different objectives without average smoothness. We first revisit the results under average smoothness, obtaining the $O(T^{-1/3})$ bound for nonconvex objectives and the $O(σ^2/(μT))$ bound for last-iterate output under the $μ$-Polyak--Łojasiewicz~(PL) condition. Without average smoothness, we design an auxiliary sequence and compare the STORM update with it in the analysis. With the help of this sequence, we prove that STORM still attains an $O(T^{-1/4})$ rate for nonconvex objectives, which is optimal under standard smoothness. For convex and $λ$-strongly convex objectives, we further prove averaged and last-iterate bounds with optimal rates of $O(σR/\sqrt T)$ and $O(σ^2/(λT))$, respectively. All the obtained results use the same STORM recursion with different hyperparameter choices.

</details>


### [208] [Structural stability of systems and cycle covers in random graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01607)
*Mohamed Ali Belabbas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文研究有向step-graphon抽样网络拓扑能否维持线性系统渐近稳定性的概率问题，给出性质 N（必要）与 S（充分）成立概率趋于1的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构系统理论研究哪些网络拓扑能维持可控性或稳定性等性质。当拓扑本身是随机的，问题转为概率性：从随机模型抽取的图维持该性质的概率有多大。此类概率度量该性质在拓扑中的普遍性与鲁棒性，并指示依赖该性质的系统能否在不确定环境中可靠部署。作者针对有向graphon情形下的线性系统渐近稳定性回答该问题。

Method: 考虑两个图论性质：N 要求对每个 k≤n，D 的某 k 顶点诱导子有向图存在圈覆盖；S 要求这些子图可选得使其节点集构成从单个带自环顶点开始的嵌套序列 V_1⊂…⊂V_n=V(D)。已证 N 是结构稳定的必要条件、S 是充分条件。从有向step-graphon W 抽样 D。对骨架有向图 S（q个节点）和浓度向量 x* 的step-graphon W，关联圈多面体 X(S)⊆Δ_q，用 x* 在其中的位置、多面体维数、W 的自环密度，以及对 S 还需骨架圈的排序条件来表述条件。

Result: 主要结果给出当 n→∞ 时 Pr(N)→1 与 Pr(S)→1 的充要条件。

Conclusion: 对有向step-graphon，这些结果刻画了抽样拓扑极可能或极不可能维持稳定动态的区域。

Abstract: Structural system theory studies which network topologies can sustain a prescribed system property such as controllability or stability. When the topology is itself random, the relevant question becomes probabilistic: how likely is a graph drawn from a stochastic model to sustain the property? Such probabilities measure the abundance and robustness of the property across topologies, and indicate whether systems requiring it can be reliably deployed in uncertain environments. We address this question for asymptotic stability of linear systems in the directed graphon setting.
  We consider two graph-theoretic properties. The first is $\mathcal N$, and it requires that for every $k\leq n$ some $k$-vertex induced subdigraph of $D$ admits a cycle cover, and the second is $\mathcal S$, which requires that these subdigraphs can be chosen so that their node sets form a nested sequence $V_1\subset\cdots\subset V_n=V(D)$ starting from a single vertex with a loop. We have shown that $\mathcal N$ is necessary and $\mathcal S$ is sufficient for structural stability. We sample $D$ from a directed step-graphon $W$. Our main results give necessary and sufficient conditions for $\Pr(\mathcal N)\to 1$ and $\Pr(\mathcal S)\to 1$ as $n\to\infty$. In more detail, to a step-graphon $W$ with skeleton digraph $S$ on $q$ nodes and concentration vector $x^*$ we associate a cycle polytope $\vec{\mathcal X}(S)\subseteqΔ_q$. The conditions are then formulated in terms of the position of $x^*$ within $\vec{\mathcal X}(S)$, the dimension of the polytope, the loop density of $W$ and, for $\mathcal S$, an ordering condition on the cycles of the skeleton. Together these results identify, for directed step-graphons, the regime in which a sampled topology is overwhelmingly likely or unlikely to sustain stable dynamics.

</details>


### [209] [Lower Bounds for Stochastic First-Order Algorithms with Variance Reduction in Nonconvex--Concave Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01662)
*Jiayi Song,Zi Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了允许使用方差缩减的随机一阶算法在非凸-凹及非凸-强凹极小极大优化中的复杂度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下界对算法类别施加了较严格的限制（如禁止方差缩减），无法刻画允许方差缩减时的真实复杂度壁垒，因此需要一个更一般化的零尊重（zero-respecting）算法类下界。

Method: 针对具有L-Lipschitz连续联合梯度的目标、欧氏半径不超过D_Y的紧凸对偶域，以及初始次优性不超过Δ的原值函数，以参数1/(2L)的Moreau包络梯度范数度量精度ε；在方差不超过σ²且均方光滑的无偏随机一阶oracle下，构造零尊重算法类的下界，并给出非凸-强凹（强凹参数μ、条件数κ=L/μ）的补充下界。

Result: 非凸-凹情形下界为Ω(L²D_YΔε⁻³+L³D_Y²Δσ²ε⁻⁶)；非凸-强凹在有限方差oracle下为Ω(LΔ√κ ε⁻²+LΔκσ²ε⁻⁴)，在额外均方光滑（常数L̄）下为Ω(LΔ√κ ε⁻²+ΔL̄σκ^{3/2}ε⁻³)。

Conclusion: 结果明确了对于允许方差缩减的算法在凹与强凹两种情形下的复杂度壁垒，主要非凸-凹下界依然对方差缩减算法成立。

Abstract: We establish complexity lower bounds for stochastic first-order algorithms in nonconvex--concave minimax optimization, allowing algorithms to use variance reduction. Our main contribution is a lower bound for a zero-respecting algorithm class that permits variance reduction, extending beyond the algorithmic restrictions imposed by some existing lower bounds. We consider objectives with an $L$-Lipschitz continuous joint gradient, a compact convex dual domain of Euclidean radius at most $D_Y$, and a primal value function, defined by maximizing the objective over the dual variable, with initial suboptimality at most $Δ$. The target accuracy $\varepsilon$ is measured by the gradient norm of the Moreau envelope of the constrained primal value function with parameter $1/(2L)$. Under an unbiased stochastic first-order oracle with variance at most $σ^2$ and mean-square smoothness, we prove the lower bound $Ω\!\left(L^2D_YΔ\varepsilon^{-3}+L^3D_Y^2Δσ^2\varepsilon^{-6}\right)$. This result quantifies the dependence on accuracy, dual-domain radius, and oracle noise even when variance reduction is allowed. We also establish complementary lower bounds for nonconvex--strongly-concave minimax optimization. With dual strong-concavity parameter $μ>0$ and condition number $κ:=L/μ$, we obtain $Ω\!\left(LΔ\sqrtκ\,\varepsilon^{-2}+LΔκσ^2\varepsilon^{-4}\right)$ under the bounded-variance oracle model. Under the additional mean-square smoothness condition with constant $\bar L$, we obtain $Ω\!\left(LΔ\sqrtκ\,\varepsilon^{-2}+Δ\bar Lσκ^{3/2}\varepsilon^{-3}\right)$. Together, these results identify complexity barriers across the concave and strongly concave regimes, with the main nonconvex--concave bound remaining valid for algorithms that use variance reduction.

</details>


### [210] [Routing in Line Networks with Handling Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01666)
*Gabriel Deza,Michal Tzur,Tal Raviv*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究固定线路路由问题：车辆沿预定义的循环服务点序列（线路）运行，包裹可在不同线路间转运。转运带来装卸操作，使车辆在服务点停留，且停留时间依赖于路由决策，具有内生性。作者提出RLN-HT问题，建立双线性广义容量流分配模型，刻画最优停留时间与频率，导出仅依赖货流变量的重构形式，进而给出两种二阶锥松弛及残差前沿框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超连接城市包裹网络面向当日达和时效敏感配送，评估候选的重复性枢纽间车辆服务集合时需要求解固定线路路由问题。线路间转运虽扩展路径选择，但装卸操作会产生停留时间；由于处理量与路由相关，停留时间内生，进而影响线路周期、有效服务频率与转运等待时间，这一耦合在既有模型中常被忽略。

Method: 提出Routing in Line Networks with Handling Times (RLN-HT)问题，并将其表述为双线性广义容量流分配模型。刻画最优停留时间及由此产生的频率与处理工作量之间的函数关系，从而得到仅以货流为变量的重构模型。利用其凸子结构，推导两种二阶锥松弛，保持原可行域不变，从而同时给出可行解与有效下界。进一步提出残差前沿框架，刻画凸松弛目标与剩余非凸残差之间的权衡，为构造下界证书提供通用机制。

Result: 计算实验表明：忽略处理时间会导致较差甚至不可行的路由；考虑内生停留时间可将平均包裹运输时间最多改善26.5%。在最多257个节点和25条线路的算例中，松弛求解迅速，得到接近最优的路由并取得较小的最优性间隙，而通用空间分支定界方法难以给出有用下界。

Conclusion: 内生停留时间对固定线路网络路由至关重要，忽略它可能产生不可行或劣质方案。基于货流重构的二阶锥松弛与残差前沿框架可高效求解大规模实例，提供近优路由与紧下界证书，优于现有通用求解方法。

Abstract: Motivated by hyperconnected urban parcel networks for same-day and time-sensitive delivery, we study the fixed-line routing problem that arises when evaluating a candidate set of recurring inter-hub vehicle services. Vehicles follow predefined cyclic sequences of service points, or lines, and parcels may transfer between lines. Such transfers expand path choice but require loading and unloading operations that make vehicles dwell at service points. Because handled volumes are routing-dependent, dwell times become endogenous, linking routing decisions to line cycle times, effective service frequencies, and transfer waiting times. We introduce the Routing in Line Networks with Handling Times (RLN-HT) problem and formulate it as a bilinear generalized capacity-flow assignment model. We characterize optimal dwell times and the resulting frequencies as functions of handling workloads, enabling a reformulation solely in terms of commodity flows. We exploit its convex substructure to derive two second-order conic relaxations that preserve the original feasible region and therefore yield feasible incumbent solutions along with valid lower bounds. We also develop a residual-frontier framework that characterizes the tradeoff between the convex relaxation objective and the remaining nonconvex residual, yielding a general mechanism for constructing lower-bound certificates. Computational experiments show that ignoring handling times can yield poor or infeasible routing, whereas accounting for endogenous dwell times can improve average parcel travel times by up to 26.5%. In instances with up to 257 nodes and 25 lines, the relaxations solve quickly, yield near-optimal routing, and obtain small optimality gaps, whereas off-the-shelf spatial branch-and-bound struggles to produce useful bounds.

</details>


### [211] [Simplifying the computation of weak-$\star$ second subderivatives of convex functionals via $Γ$-convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01673)
*Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在温和假设下证明了凸泛函的强-强二次Epi可微性与其共轭泛函的弱-⋆二次Epi可微性等价，且相应次导数（相差一个缩放）互为共轭；该结果有助于计算弱-⋆二次次导数，并证明其在适当的函数空间改变下不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱-⋆二次次导数是无间隙二阶最优性条件的关键要素，但其计算通常较困难。作者希望借助共轭对偶关系简化这类计算，并厘清其在不同函数空间设定下的稳定性。

Method: 在温和假设下，建立凸泛函的强-强二次Epi可微性与共轭泛函的弱-⋆二次Epi可微性之间的等价关系；进一步分析对应次导数的共轭对应（相差一个缩放因子），并研究弱-⋆二次次导数在适当函数空间变换下的不变性。

Result: 证明了强-强二次Epi可微性与弱-⋆二次Epi可微性的等价性；给出相应次导数互为共轭（相差缩放）的结论；表明该结果可用于计算弱-⋆二次次导数；并证明弱-⋆二次次导数在适当的函数空间设定改变下保持不变。

Conclusion: 该工作为弱-⋆二次次导数提供了一种基于共轭对偶的计算途径，并说明其在合适的空间变换下具有不变性，从而有助于无间隙二阶最优性条件的建立与应用。

Abstract: We prove (under some mild assumptions) that the strong-strong twice epidifferentiability of a convex functional is equivalent to the weak-$\star$ twice epidifferentiability of its conjugate functional. Further, the corresponding subderivatives are (up to scaling) conjugates of each other. This result can be used to facilitate the computation of weak-$\star$ second subderivatives, which are crucial ingredients in no-gap second-order optimality conditions. We also show that weak-$\star$ second subderivatives are invariant under suitable changes of the underlying function space setting.

</details>


### [212] [Recognizing Signomial Convexity is Hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01812)
*Rui Zheng,Iosif Sakos,Antonios Varvitsiotis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明：判定符号多项式（signomial）在三种凸性形式（原始变量凸、对数变换后凸、正值取对数后凸）下是否凸，均为强NP难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号多项式是广义单项式（实指数）的有限和，能紧凑表示幂律、反比关系与乘法交互，兼具万能逼近与可解释参数，是自然的机器学习模型；AI Feynman基准100个方程中有45个可写为符号多项式形式。符号优化在工程设计、通信、经济与机器学习中广泛使用，但一般情形计算不可解。凸性是高效优化的黄金标准，为符号多项式提供三条可解路径，因此识别这些凸性结构本身的可计算性（复杂度）至关重要。

Method: 从多项式凸性的"间隙承诺"（gap-promise）变体出发，构造多项式到符号多项式的归约，使曲率间隙在相应对数变换下得以保持；分别针对三种凸性判定形式在紧致域与全局两种设定下进行归约证明。

Result: 三种凸性形式的判定问题（原始变量凸、对数变量变换后凸、正值再对取值取对数后凸，即纪律化几何规划所依赖的结构）在紧致域上和全局上均被证明为强NP难。

Conclusion: 识别符号多项式的可用凸性结构本质上计算困难，说明即便存在三条通向可解性的凸性路径，判定某符号多项式属于哪条路径（或是否属于）本身是强NP难的，这对符号优化的理论与算法设计具有根本性含义。

Abstract: Signomials---finite sums of generalized monomials with real exponents---are a natural machine learning model, combining parsimonious representations of power laws, inverse relationships, and multiplicative interactions with universal approximation and interpretable parameters. In fact, 45 of the 100 equations in the AI Feynman benchmark admit signomial representations. Moreover, signomial optimization is widely used in engineering design, communications, economics, and machine learning, but is computationally intractable in general. Convexity, the gold standard for efficient optimization, offers three routes to tractability for signomials: a signomial may be convex in its original variables, become convex after a logarithmic change of variables, or, when positive, become convex after additionally taking the logarithm of its value---the structure underlying disciplined geometric programming. We show that recognizing each form is strongly NP-hard, both on compact domains and globally. Our proofs start from gap-promise variants of polynomial convexity and develop polynomial-to-signomial reductions that preserve curvature gaps under the relevant logarithmic transformations.

</details>


### [213] [Optimal Stochastic Bilevel Optimization with First-Order Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01843)
*Linxuan Pan,Junchi Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究随机一阶oracle下的非凸-强凸双层优化问题，提出单循环一阶方法MRT-FD，并给出与之匹配的oracle复杂度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层优化中上层非凸、下层强凸的情形在机器学习和超参数优化中广泛存在，但在随机一阶oracle设定下，其最优复杂度（尤其是对ε的依赖）尚不清楚，上下界之间存在差距。作者希望厘清该设定下可达的复杂度并给出匹配下界。

Method: 提出MRT-FD：单循环一阶方法，每轮迭代同时更新上层变量、下层解以及由超目标隐式微分产生的辅助响应变量；三者每轮各更新一次。其中二阶导数作用采用阶数为p的有限差分进行近似。下界方面，从带有更强随机oracle的困难非凸最小化链出发，通过引入标量下层变量并以正弦耦合方式将其提升为双层问题，从而构造出Ω(ε^{-4-2/p})的oracle下界。

Result: 对任意固定有限光滑阶p≥1，MRT-FD以𝒪(ε^{-4-2/p})次随机梯度查询找到ε-稳定点；同时证明匹配的Ω(ε^{-4-2/p}) oracle下界。

Conclusion: 对每个固定的有限p，复杂度对ε的依赖是最优的，从而在该随机一阶oracle设定下闭合了双层优化的上下复杂度差距。

Abstract: We study nonconvex--strongly-convex bilevel optimization under a stochastic first-order oracle. We introduce MRT-FD, a single-loop first-order method that simultaneously tracks the upper-level variable, the lower-level solution, and the auxiliary response arising from implicit differentiation of the hyperobjective. MRT-FD performs one update of each variable per iteration and approximates the second-order derivative actions using order-$p$ finite differences. For any fixed finite smoothness order $p\ge1$ in the lower-level variable, MRT-FD finds an $\varepsilon$-stationary point using $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-4-2/p})$ stochastic gradient queries. We also prove a matching $Ω(\varepsilon^{-4-2/p})$ oracle lower bound. The lower-bound construction starts from a hard nonconvex minimization chain with a stronger stochastic oracle, and lifts it to a bilevel problem through a sinusoidal coupling with a scalar lower-level variable. Consequently, the dependence on $\varepsilon$ is optimal for every fixed finite $p$, closing the upper--lower complexity gap in this stochastic first-order oracle setting.

</details>


### [214] [Randomized Matvec Lower Bounds for Simplex-Based Matrix Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02095)
*Wendao Wu,Cong Fang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了矩阵-向量查询模型下两类规范化矩阵博弈的随机化下界，分别为球-单纯形博弈的 Ω(ε^{-2/3}/(log²(1/ε)loglog(1/ε))) 和单纯形-单纯形博弈的 Ω(ε^{-2/3}/(log^{7/3}(1/ε)loglog(1/ε)))，与已知确定性上界仅差对数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵博弈（如球-单纯形、单纯形-单纯形几何）的求解复杂度在确定性算法方面已有上界，但其随机化查询下界尚不明确。作者希望确定在允许随机化、且每次查询可返回 (Ax, A^⊤y) 的矩阵-向量查询模型下，达到 ε 鞍点间隙所需的最坏情况查询次数下界，以判断确定性上界是否在随机化设定下仍然接近最优。

Method: 证明采用随机化矩阵-向量查询下界技术：在自适应双侧查询后提取一个新的高斯核心（fresh Gaussian core），利用其最小奇异值的不确定性建立线性系统求解的困难性；再通过两个归约将这种困难性传递到矩阵博弈，将小的完整间隙转换为小的残差，其中单纯形-单纯形博弈额外产生对数级归一化损失。

Result: 对充分小的 ε，球-单纯形博弈的最坏情况查询复杂度为 Ω(ε^{-2/3}/(log²(1/ε)loglog(1/ε)))，单纯形-单纯形博弈为 Ω(ε^{-2/3}/(log^{7/3}(1/ε)loglog(1/ε)))。对应困难实例维度分别约为 ε^{-2/3} 与 ε^{-2/3}/log^{1/3}(1/ε)，且下界可推广到更大维度。这些下界与 Karmarkar、O'Carroll 和 Sidford 的确定性上界在忽略对数因子意义下相匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了两类规范化矩阵博弈在随机化矩阵-向量查询模型下的下界，证明其与确定性上界仅相差对数因子，从而表明现有确定性算法在随机化设定下已接近最优；核心证明工具是高斯核心提取与最小奇异值不确定性归约，为矩阵博弈复杂度研究提供了新的下界框架。

Abstract: We prove randomized matrix-vector query lower bounds for two normalized matrix-game geometries: a Euclidean unit ball against a probability simplex, with row norms at most one, and two probability simplices, with entries of absolute value at most one. Each query returns $(Ax,A^\top y)$ for arbitrary real vectors. The algorithm must return a feasible pair with full saddle-point gap at most $\varepsilon$, with probability at least $2/3$ on every admissible matrix. For sufficiently small $\varepsilon$, the worst-case query complexities are $Ω(\varepsilon^{-2/3}/(\log^2(1/\varepsilon)\log\log(1/\varepsilon)))$ for ball-simplex games and $Ω(\varepsilon^{-2/3}/(\log^{7/3}(1/\varepsilon)\log\log(1/\varepsilon)))$ for simplex-simplex games. The hard instances have dimensions of order $\varepsilon^{-2/3}$ and $\varepsilon^{-2/3}/\log^{1/3}(1/\varepsilon)$, respectively, and the bounds extend to larger dimensions. These lower bounds match the deterministic upper bounds of Karmarkar, O'Carroll, and Sidford up to logarithmic factors. The proof extracts a fresh Gaussian core after adaptive two-sided queries and uses uncertainty in its smallest singular value to establish linear-system solve hardness. Two reductions transfer this hardness to matrix games by converting a small full gap into a small residual, with an additional logarithmic normalization loss only for simplex-simplex games.

</details>

### [215] [A multi-scale immuno-epidemiological behavioral model for influenza-like illness: connecting scales through symptom scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00862)
*Binod Pant,Summer Atkins,Necibe Tuncer,Hana M. Dobrovolny*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 提出一种以系统性症状评分为桥梁的新型多尺度模型，发现疾病驱动的行为改变会推迟并压低流行高峰，且疫情轨迹在定性上与标准SEIR模型相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数模型将行为改变视为对病例数上升的群体级反应，但许多传染病中行为改变其实是个体级反应，由个体自身的患病感受触发；而现有模型难以刻画疾病严重程度如何驱动这一反应。传统多尺度模型多以病毒载量为桥梁连接宿主内与群体尺度，但病毒载量常与症状严重程度不一致，可能不适合用于在群体层面驱动行为。

Method: 构建一种新型多尺度模型：有症状个体基于系统性症状评分（由肌肉酸痛、疲劳、头痛、发热感的复合指标，并由宿主内免疫动力学信息决定）动态调整其传播率，无症状个体则不改变行为；并比较多种连接两个尺度的连接函数（linking functions）。

Result: 更强的疾病驱动行为改变会推迟并降低流行高峰。无论采用几种不同的连接函数，所得疫情轨迹在定性上仍与不含行为改变的标准SEIR模型相似，这与由群体级反馈（如报告病例或死亡数）驱动的行为改变模型形成对比——后者可产生平台期、肩峰或疫情下降期延长等定性不同的特征。当采用症状评分的指数函数作为连接函数时，标准SEIR模型能很好地复现多尺度模型在给定时间点的总感染人数。

Conclusion: 以症状评分（而非病毒载量）作为行为驱动桥梁的多尺度建模更能反映个体疾病感受驱动的行为改变；疾病驱动的行为改变主要起到推迟并压低高峰的作用，而不改变疫情轨迹的定性形态，在指数连接函数下甚至可被标准SEIR模型近似复现。

Abstract: For many infectious diseases, behavior change is not a population-level reaction to rising case counts, but a personal one, triggered by how sick an individual feels. Yet most models fail to capture how illness severity drives this response. Multi-scale models that link the immunological scale to the epidemiological scale seem well-suited for capturing this behavior. Most multi-scale models link within-host viral load to population-level transmission rates through linking functions that are a function of viral load. However, viral load is often misaligned with symptom severity and thus possibly ill-suited for the purpose of informing behavior at a population level. We present a novel multi-scale model in which symptomatic individuals dynamically modify their transmission based on systemic symptom scores (a composite measure of muscle ache, fatigue, headache, and feverishness informed by within-host immune dynamics) while asymptomatic individuals do not change behavior. We find that stronger illness-driven behavior change delays and lowers the epidemic peak. Moreover, the resulting epidemic trajectory remains qualitatively similar to that of a standard SEIR model without behavior change, across several distinct choices of linking functions connecting the two scales. This stands in contrast to models where behavior change is driven by population-level feedback (e.g., reported cases or deaths), which can produce qualitatively distinct signatures such as plateaus, shoulders, or an elongated epidemic decline. In the case when an exponential function of symptom score is used as a linking function, we show that the standard SEIR model can closely reproduce the total number of infectious individuals at a given point in time generated through the multi-scale level.

</details>

### [216] [Integrating a Novel Kumaraswamy-Teissier Distribution with VARMA: A Hybrid Framework for Rainfall Modeling and Forecasting in the Northwest Himalayas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00060)
*Kamana Mishra,Neeraj Poonia,Tanmay Kayal,Sarita Azad*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究提出KTD-VARMA混合框架：以Kumaraswamy-Teissier分布刻画极端降雨，再经KTD变换结合VARMA建模时空依赖，实现山区多站点降雨的极值分析与更精准预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 山区降雨具有强偏态与极端性，传统统计工具难以同时兼顾极值刻画与多站点时空依赖建模，需更灵活的统计框架用于水文风险评估。

Method: 构建三参数Kumaraswamy-Teissier分布（KTD，基于Kumaraswamy-G生成元），推导其性质，采用极大似然、最大乘积间距及贝叶斯（MCMC）估计参数并给出可信区间与HPD区间；进行重现期分析；将KTD作为正态化变换，对变换后数据建立VARMA模型以捕捉时空依赖，并与原始数据及单变量ARIMA模型对比。

Result: KTD拟合优于其子模型；重现期分析显示明显空间差异：Dehradun极端事件最频繁，Mandi和Kangra呈中等极端性，Shimla和Nainital极端月总量重现期较长；经季节调整后，KTD-VARMA预测精度优于原始数据模型和单变量ARIMA，VARMA的RMSE最低。

Conclusion: KTD-VARMA框架可同时用于极值分析与改进的多站点预测，为山区水文风险评估提供了稳健方法。

Abstract: Rainfall modeling in mountainous regions requires flexible statistical tools capable of capturing strong skewness and extremes. This study proposes a novel hybrid KTD-VARMA framework to address this challenge. The framework first introduces the Kumaraswamy-Teissier Distribution (KTD), a new three-parameter model derived via the Kumaraswamy-G generator, to statistically characterize extreme rainfall. Its properties are derived, and parameters are estimated via maximum likelihood, maximum product spacing, and a Bayesian (MCMC) approach along with credible and HPD intervals. The KTD provides a superior fit compared to its sub-models. The return-period analysis highlights clear spatial contrasts: Dehradun experiences the most frequent extremes, Mandi and Kangra exhibit moderate extremal behavior, while Shimla and Nainital display long return periods for extreme monthly totals. Further, the KTD serves as a normalizing transformation for the skewed rainfall series and KTD-transformed data are then modeled using a Vector Autoregressive Moving Average (VARMA) model to capture spatio-temporal dependencies. This integrated methodology, presented here for the first time, yields more accurate forecasts after seasonal adjustment than models using raw data or univariate ARIMA, with VARMA achieving the lowest RMSE. The work establishes the KTD-VARMA framework as a comprehensive tool for both extreme value analysis and improved multi-site forecasting, providing a robust approach for hydrological risk assessment in mountainous regions.

</details>


### [217] [Counting the Uncounted: Population-Level Surveillance of Documented Pregnancy and Fetal Harm in Police Crash Narratives with a System One Model (Jev)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00213)
*Amir Rafe,Subasish Das*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究利用大语言模型对德克萨斯州2017–2025年全部501万余条警方碰撞叙述进行全量筛查与判读，估计出有记录孕妇的碰撞事故约为5,467起（95%区间5,306–5,577），为交通碰撞中孕期结局提供了首个基于警方叙述的人口级估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机动车碰撞是美国创伤性胎儿死亡的主要原因，但没有碰撞数据库记录妊娠状态。现有估计或来自区间极宽的国家级调查样本，或来自只覆盖受伤者的创伤登记，或局限于少数州的记录链接，均存在代表性或精度不足的问题。警方叙述在事故现场只要与妊娠相关就会记录，但此前无人对其进行全人口规模的阅读，因此需要一种可扩展的方法来填补这一数据空白。

Method: 研究读取德克萨斯州2017至2025年全部5,018,079条警方碰撞叙述。首先用正则表达式筛查出6,840条候选记录；再由"Jev"——一个经校准的System One决策模型——按八问题模式判读候选记录，仅返回概率而不返回文本，确认其中5,333条记录涉及孕妇及其角色、孕期阶段和碰撞后状况。随后对剩余记录中的499,306条进行"是否存在"问题的判读，以估计筛查漏检量。通过概率分层抽样进行盲法人工裁定，得到加权灵敏度0.999、特异度1.000，再用Rogan-Gladen估计量与自助法（bootstrap）进行总量校正。

Result: 校正后估计共有5,467起碰撞记录在案有孕妇参与，95%置信区间为5,306至5,577，其中58起记录了胎儿伤害。个体层面记录显示，15至49岁女性驾驶员的碰撞率为每千人1.13至1.45。与生命统计的活产预期相比，叙述仅记录了预期怀孕驾驶员的2.56%至3.09%。有记录病例状态与驾驶员自身被记录的受伤关联最强，优势比为21.7。

Conclusion: 警方碰撞叙述可在人口规模上用于识别孕期相关的碰撞事故，但仅能捕捉到预期怀孕驾驶员中很小一部分（约3%），且记录与否强烈依赖于驾驶员本人是否受伤。这表明基于警方叙述的孕期碰撞数据存在显著的选择性漏报，需结合其他数据源才能全面评估孕期交通伤害负担。

Abstract: Motor-vehicle crashes are a leading cause of traumatic fetal death in the United States, yet no crash database records pregnancy. Existing estimates come from national investigation samples with wide intervals, trauma registries seeing only the injured, or record linkage in a few states. Police narratives record pregnancy whenever it matters at the scene, and no study has read them at population scale. We read every police crash narrative in Texas from 2017 through 2025, 5,018,079 in total. A regular-expression screen flags 6,840 candidates, which Jev, a calibrated System One decision model, reads under an eight-question schema, returning probabilities and no text. It confirms 5,333 as documenting a pregnant person involved in the crash, with role, stage and post-crash condition. The same model reads 499,306 of the remainder under the presence question, to estimate what the screen missed. Blind human adjudication of a probability-stratified sample gives weighted sensitivity 0.999 and specificity 1.000, from which the Rogan-Gladen estimator and a bootstrap give 5,467 crashes with a documented pregnancy, with a 95% interval of 5,306 to 5,577, including 58 crashes documenting fetal harm. Person-level records give a rate of 1.13 to 1.45 per 1,000 female drivers aged 15 to 49. Measured against a live-birth expectation from vital statistics, narratives document 2.56% to 3.09% of expected pregnant drivers, and documented-case status is most strongly associated with the driver's own recorded injury, at an odds ratio of 21.7.

</details>


### [218] [Efficient Convex Optimization Methods for State-Space Heartbeat Dynamics Models with Gamma Generalized Linear Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00884)
*Sabrina Liu,Andrew S. Perley,Todd P. Coleman*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种心跳动力学的动态状态空间统计模型，其最优状态估计可通过凸优化高效求解，用于评估自主神经与心血管健康。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统局部平均方法难以刻画心率模式中的急剧瞬变，需要一种能高效求解且具生理意义的建模框架来评估心跳动力学与自主神经健康。

Method: 将Gauss-Markov过程先验与带log链接函数的gamma发射分布相结合构建状态空间模型；用静态版本估计参数，采用交替方向乘子法（ADMM）高效求解最大后验潜状态估计；通过偏自相关函数在模型规模与拟合之间权衡进行模型选择。

Result: 在合成心跳数据上能准确恢复潜在权重；在倾斜床数据集上比传统局部平均方法更好地捕捉心率模式的急剧瞬变；结合呼吸相位等其他器官信息，可提取信息论度量以量化器官间耦合（如呼吸性窦性心律不齐）。

Conclusion: 该心跳动力学统计框架高效且能提供具生理意义的洞见，拓宽了评估心跳动力学的潜在工具。

Abstract: Objective: This work proposes a dynamic state-space statistical model of heartbeat dynamics whose optimal state estimate can be solved efficiently with convex optimization and used to assess autonomic and cardiovascular health. Methods: This state-space model combines a Gauss-Markov process prior with gamma emissions using a log-link function. Parameters are estimated using a static version of the model, and alternating direction method of multipliers is used to efficiently find the optimal maximum a priori latent state estimate. Model selection is performed by finding the tradeoff between model size and model fit using the partial autocorrelation function. Results: We demonstrate in synthetically generated heartbeats that this model is able to closely recover the underlying latent weights. Then, we show using a tilt table dataset that this statistical model is able to better capture sharp transients in heart rate patterns than traditional local averaging methods. In addition, combining heart beat timings with information from other organs such as the respiratory phase, we show that our statistical modeling framework enables the extraction of information-theoretic measures to quantify inter-organ coupling such as respiratory sinus arrythmia. Conclusion: This statistical framework for modeling heartbeat dynamics provides an efficient framework that can provide physiologically meaningful insights. Significance: This work broadens potential tools that can be used to assess heartbeat dynamics.

</details>


### [219] [On relations between the Cochran--Mantel--Haenszel test and Augmented Inverse Probability Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01362)
*Ekkehard Glimm*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文将Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel (CMH)检验统计量重新解释为增广逆概率加权(AIPW)因果平均处理效应估计量的标准化形式，并比较条件方差与无条件方差，指出在等比例分组下CMH检验对弱零假设具有渐近保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着因果推断方法在临床试验中的应用日益广泛，传统CMH检验通常被局限于分层logistic回归中共同优势比的检验解释。作者希望摆脱这一常被用来motivate CMH检验的共同优势比模型依赖，从因果推断视角重新审视CMH检验，厘清其与Wald型AIPW检验之间的关系。

Method: 针对二分类终点、各层处理分配比例相同的分层随机试验，作者证明CMH统计量的分子正比于总体平均因果风险差的AIPW估计量。在此基础上，讨论该估计量的条件方差与无条件方差概念，以及方差估计及其对检验的影响，并将其与经典条件CMH检验和Wald型AIPW检验进行比较。

Result: 结果显示CMH检验统计量可被解释为AIPW因果平均处理效应估计量的标准化版本，因而不必局限于共同优势比的解释。同时结果显示，在等比例处理分配下，CMH检验对零平均处理效应的弱零假设是渐近保守的。

Conclusion: 本文澄清了经典条件CMH检验与Wald型AIPW检验之间的关系，为CMH检验提供了不依赖共同优势比模型的因果推断解释，并表明在等比例分配下CMH检验对弱零假设渐近保守。

Abstract: This note revisits the Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel (CMH) test in light of the increasing use of causal inference methods in clinical trials. It shows that the CMH test statistic can be interpreted as a standardized version of the augmented inverse probability weighted (AIPW) estimate of the causal average treatment effect. Consequently, it is not restricted to an interpretation as a test of the common-odds ratio in a stratified logistic regression model. The note then discusses conditional and unconditional variances for this estimator as well as variance estimation and its implications for testing. The results clarify the relationship between the classical conditional CMH test and Wald-type AIPW tests, and show that, under equal treatment allocation, the CMH test is asymptotically conservative for the weak null hypothesis of zero average treatment effect. %This note revisits the Cochran--Mantel--Haenszel (CMH) test in light of the increasing use of causal inference methods in clinical trials. For stratified randomized trials with binary endpoints and a common treatment allocation fraction across strata, we show that the numerator of the CMH statistic is proportional to an augmented inverse probability weighted estimator of the population-average causal risk difference. This provides an interpretation of the CMH test that does not depend on the common-odds-ratio model often used to motivate it. We then compare conditional and unconditional variance concepts for this estimator and discuss their implications for testing. The results clarify the relationship between the classical conditional CMH test and Wald-type AIPW tests, and show that, under equal treatment allocation, the CMH test is asymptotically conservative for the weak null hypothesis of zero average treatment effect.

</details>
